# VNB catalog: theorems, axioms, definitions, assertions

Auto-generated by `(catalog)`.  4349 results — 3479 proven (3440 of them installed from a certificate, their proofs not run in this load), 63 support (PSS), 807 stated without proof (197 axioms, 563 definitions, 47 assertions).

> **Trust.**  This catalog lists *what* is proven; how much each
> proof still leans on unproved facts is the companion ledger
> [`PROOF-DEBT.md`](PROOF-DEBT.md).  In short: a proof's *bill*
> is the set of `asserted` facts it rests on; an empty bill prints
> `proven modulo 0` (unconditional relative to the kernel -- the
> strongest report), and a non-empty bill carries a `[trust: K]`
> tier equal to its **weakest** leaf's warrant, where `none`
> (a leaf with no warrant at all) is weakest and `proof` strongest.
> The ledger heads with the full glossary and tier table.

## Proven theorems

- `abelian-group-as-monoid-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-abelian-group(a, b, f) implies is-hom-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(a), abelian-group-as-monoid(b), f))
- `abelian-group-assoc` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, a) = a implies a = iden(s)))
- `abelian-group-inv-iden` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies (inv(g))(iden(g)) = iden(g))
- `abelian-group-inv-inv` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (inv(s))((inv(s))(a)) = a))
- `abelian-group-inv-opr` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (inv(s))((opr(s))(a, b)) = (opr(s))((inv(s))(a), (inv(s))(b))))
- `abelian-group-inverse-unique` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) = iden(s) implies b = (inv(s))(a)))
- `abelian-group-is-group` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies is-group(s))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) = (opr(s))(b, a)))
- `abelian-group-opr-interchange` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s), d in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), (opr(s))(c, d)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, c), (opr(s))(b, d))))
- `abelian-group-right-id` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, iden(s)) = a))
- `abelian-group-right-inv` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, (inv(s))(a)) = iden(s)))
- `abs-nonneg-le` — forall([a, c], a in rr implies 0 <= a implies abs(a) <= c implies a <= c)
- `abs-seq-in-fun` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], vnb-lambda(n_, nn, abs(f(n_))) in fun(nn, rr))
- `affine-closed-road-integral` — forall([gbp_ in cc, gbq_ in cc, gbw_ in cc, gbg_ in fun(cc, cc)], forall([gbz_ in cc], gbg_(gbz_) == gbp_ + gbq_ * (gbz_ - gbw_)) implies forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies pgam(b) = pgam(a) implies line-int(gbg_, pgam, dgam, a, b) = 0))
- `affine-continuous-at` — forall([c, lam, t_], c in rr and lam in rr and t_ in rr implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, c + lam * x), t_))
- `affine-has-primitive-cc` — forall([gbp_ in cc, gbq_ in cc, gbw_ in cc, gbf_ in fun(cc, cc)], forall([gbz_ in cc], gbf_(gbz_) == gbp_ * gbz_ + (gbq_ * ((gbz_ - gbw_) * (gbz_ - gbw_))) * recip(2)) implies holomorphic-on(cc, gbf_) and forall([gbz_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, gbf_, gbz_, gbp_ + gbq_ * (gbz_ - gbw_))))

- `affine-reparam-continuous-at-sub` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies forall([f, lrpg_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), rr)], forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrpg_(lrpz_) == f(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies forall([lrpt_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(a, b)), rr-ms, f, lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpt_) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)), rr-ms, lrpg_, lrpt_))))

- `affine-reparam-inverse` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(a, b)], (lrpz_ - lrpm_) * recip(lrpl_) in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_) and lrpm_ + lrpl_ * ((lrpz_ - lrpm_) * recip(lrpl_)) = lrpz_))
- `affine-reparam-inverse-value` — forall([lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr, lrpz_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies (lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_ - lrpm_) * recip(lrpl_) = lrpz_)
- `affine-reparam-not-in-image` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies forall([lrpe_], lrpe_ subset ccint(a, b) implies forall([lrpt_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], not(lrpt_ in image(vnb-lambda(lrpv_, ccint(a, b), (lrpv_ - lrpm_) * recip(lrpl_)), lrpe_)) implies not(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpt_ in lrpe_))))
- `affine-reparam-ooint-in` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies forall([lrpz_ in ooint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_ in ooint(a, b)))
- `affine-reparam-regulated` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies lrpc_ < lrpd_ implies forall([f, lrpg_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), rr)], is-regulated-on(f, a, b) implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrpg_(lrpz_) == f(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies is-regulated-on(lrpg_, lrpc_, lrpd_)))
- `affine-reparam-solve` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(a, b), lrpq_ in rr], lrpz_ - lrpm_ = lrpq_ * lrpl_ implies lrpq_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_) and lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpq_ = lrpz_))

- `ag-cancel-right` — forall([s], is-abelian-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, c) = (opr(s))(b, c) implies a = b))
- `ag-mpow-diff-neg` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([p_ in nn, m_ in nn, n_ in nn], p_ + m_ = n_ implies forall([a in carr(g)], (opr(g))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, m_), (inv(g))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, n_))) = (inv(g))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, p_)))))
- `ag-mpow-diff-nonneg` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([d_ in nn, n_ in nn, m_ in nn], d_ + n_ = m_ implies forall([a in carr(g)], (opr(g))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, m_), (inv(g))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, n_))) = mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, d_))))
- `ag-norm-metric-is-metric-space` — forall([g, nm], is-abelian-group(g) implies is-group-norm(nm, opr(g), inv(g), iden(g), carr(g)) implies is-metric-space([carr(g), vnb-lambda([u, v], cartesian(carr(g), carr(g)), nm((opr(g))(u, (inv(g))(v))))]))
- `alg-shift-diff` — forall([alga_ in cc, algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies forall([algw_ in ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_)], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_), vnb-lambda(algx_, ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_), algx_ - alga_), algw_, 1)))

- `alg-zero-deriv-ball` — forall([alga_ in cc, algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies forall([algh_ in fun(ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_), cc)], forall([hbz_ in ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_)], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_), algh_, hbz_, 0)) implies forall([algz_ in ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_)], algh_(algz_) = algh_(alga_))))
- `analytic-at-intro` — forall([algf_, algu_], algf_ in fun(algu_, cc) implies forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc), alga_ in cc, algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ < cps-radius(algc_) implies ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_) subset algu_ implies forall([algz_ in ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_)], algf_(algz_) = cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * (algz_ - alga_) ^ cpsk_))) implies is-open(cc-ms, algu_) implies is-analytic-at(algf_, algu_, alga_)))
- `analytic-deriv` — forall([algf_, algu_], algf_ in fun(algu_, cc) implies forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc), alga_ in cc, algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ < cps-radius(algc_) implies ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_) subset algu_ implies forall([algz_ in ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_)], algf_(algz_) = cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * (algz_ - alga_) ^ cpsk_))) implies is-open(cc-ms, algu_) implies is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, algu_, algf_, alga_, algc_(1))))
- `analytic-holomorphic-ball` — forall([algf_, algu_, alga_], is-analytic-at(algf_, algu_, alga_) implies forsome([algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ and ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_) subset algu_ and holomorphic-on(ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_), restrict(algf_, ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_)))))
- `analytic-rep-holomorphic` — forall([algf_, algu_], algf_ in fun(algu_, cc) implies forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc), alga_ in cc, algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ < cps-radius(algc_) implies ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_) subset algu_ implies forall([algz_ in ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_)], algf_(algz_) = cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * (algz_ - alga_) ^ cpsk_))) implies holomorphic-on(ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_), restrict(algf_, ball(cc-ms, alga_, algr_)))))
- `anti-lam-in-fun` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr), m in nn], vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, (recip(succ(k)) * cf(k)) * x ^ succ(k)), m)) in fun(rr, rr))

- `anti-term-lam-in-fun` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr), x in rr], vnb-lambda(k, nn, (recip(succ(k)) * cf(k)) * x ^ succ(k)) in fun(nn, rr))
- `antiderivable-add` — forall([phi, psi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(psi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) + psi(x)), a, b))
- `antiderivable-affine-subst` — forall([phi, ck, lam, p_, q_, aa, bb], phi in fun(rr, rr) implies ck in rr implies lam in rr implies not(lam = 0) implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivable(phi, aa, bb) implies forall([t_ in ccint(p_, q_)], ck + lam * t_ in ccint(aa, bb)) implies forall([t_], t_ in rr and p_ < t_ and t_ < q_ implies aa < ck + lam * t_ and ck + lam * t_ < bb) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(ck + lam * x)), p_, q_))
- `antiderivable-affine-subst-pos` — forall([phi, ck, lam, p_, q_], phi in fun(rr, rr) implies ck in rr implies lam in rr implies 0 < lam implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivable(phi, ck + lam * p_, ck + lam * q_) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(ck + lam * x)), p_, q_))
- `antiderivable-affine-subst-pos-transfer` — forall([phi, psi, ck, lam, p_, q_], phi in fun(rr, rr) implies psi in fun(rr, rr) implies ck in rr implies lam in rr implies 0 < lam implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivable(phi, ck + lam * p_, ck + lam * q_) implies forall([x_ in rr], psi(x_) == phi(ck + lam * x_)) implies is-antiderivable(psi, p_, q_))
- `antiderivable-endpoints` — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b)
- `antiderivable-family-choice` — forall([phifam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), a in rr, b in rr], forall([k_ in nn], is-antiderivable(phifam(k_), a, b)) implies forsome([fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr))], forall([k_ in nn], is-antiderivative(fam(k_), phifam(k_), a, b))))
- `antiderivable-family-normalized` — forall([phifam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), a in rr, b in rr], forall([k_ in nn], is-antiderivable(phifam(k_), a, b)) implies forsome([fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr))], forall([k_ in nn], is-antiderivative(fam(k_), phifam(k_), a, b) and (fam(k_))(a) = 0)))
- `antiderivable-fn-in-fun` — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies phi in fun(rr, rr))
- `antiderivable-from-deriv` — forall([f, phi, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies phi in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x_ in rr], is-diff-at(f, x_, phi(x_))) implies is-antiderivable(phi, a, b))
- `antiderivable-integrand-transfer` — forall([phi, psi, a, b], psi in fun(rr, rr) implies is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], psi(x_) == phi(x_)) implies is-antiderivable(psi, a, b))
- `antiderivable-not-degenerate` — forall([phi, a], not(is-antiderivable(phi, a, a)))
- `antiderivable-not-reverse` — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies not(is-antiderivable(phi, b, a)))
- `antiderivable-scale` — forall([c, phi, a, b], c in rr implies is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * phi(x)), a, b))
- `antiderivable-sub` — forall([phi, psi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(psi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) - psi(x)), a, b))
- `antiderivable-subinterval` — forall([phi, a, b, lo, hi], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies lo in rr implies hi in rr implies a <= lo implies lo < hi implies hi <= b implies is-antiderivable(phi, lo, hi))
- `antiderivable-uniform-limit` — forall([phifam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), psi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr], forall([k_ in nn], is-antiderivable(phifam(k_), a, b)) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(psi(x_) - (phifam(k_))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies is-antiderivable(psi, a, b))
- `antiderivative-add` — forall([f, g, phi, psi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(g, psi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) + g(x)), vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) + psi(x)), a, b))
- `antiderivative-differ-by-constant` — forall([f, g, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(g, phi, a, b) implies forsome([cst_ in rr], forall([s_ in ccint(a, b)], f(s_) = g(s_) + cst_)))
- `antiderivative-endpoint-difference` — forall([f, g, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(g, phi, a, b) implies f(b) - f(a) = g(b) - g(a))
- `antiderivative-endpoints` — forall([f, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b)
- `antiderivative-family-uniform-limit` — forall([fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), phifam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), psi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([k_ in nn], is-antiderivative(fam(k_), phifam(k_), a, b) and (fam(k_))(a) = 0) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(psi(x_) - (phifam(k_))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies forsome([f in fun(rr, rr)], is-antiderivative(f, psi, a, b) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(f(x_) - (fam(k_))(x_)) <= eps))))))
- `antiderivative-fn-in-fun` — forall([f, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies phi in fun(rr, rr))
- `antiderivative-increment-bound` — forall([h in fun(rr, rr), phi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, m_ in rr], is-antiderivative(h, phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(phi(x_)) <= m_) implies forall([u in rr, v in rr], u in ccint(a, b) implies v in ccint(a, b) implies u < v implies abs(h(v) - h(u)) <= m_ * (v - u)))
- `antiderivative-integrand-transfer` — forall([f, phi, psi, a, b], psi in fun(rr, rr) implies is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], psi(x_) == phi(x_)) implies is-antiderivative(f, psi, a, b))
- `antiderivative-lipschitz` — forall([h in fun(rr, rr), phi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, m_ in rr], is-antiderivative(h, phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(phi(x_)) <= m_) implies forall([u in rr, v in rr], u in ccint(a, b) implies v in ccint(a, b) implies abs(h(v) - h(u)) <= m_ * abs(v - u)))
- `antiderivative-map-in-fun` — forall([f, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies f in fun(rr, rr))
- `antiderivative-pair-close` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), g in fun(rr, rr), phi in fun(rr, rr), psi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, m_ in rr], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(g, psi, a, b) implies f(a) = g(a) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(phi(x_) - psi(x_)) <= m_) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(f(x_) - g(x_)) <= m_ * (b - a)))
- `antiderivative-scale` — forall([c, f, phi, a, b], c in rr implies is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * f(x)), vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * phi(x)), a, b))
- `antiderivative-shift-const` — forall([f, phi, a, b, c], f in fun(rr, rr) implies phi in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies c in rr implies is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(vnb-lambda(x_, rr, f(x_) - c), phi, a, b))
- `antiderivative-sub` — forall([f, g, phi, psi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(g, psi, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) - g(x)), vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) - psi(x)), a, b))
- `antiderivative-subinterval` — forall([f, phi, a, b, lo, hi], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies lo in rr implies hi in rr implies a <= lo implies lo < hi implies hi <= b implies is-antiderivative(f, phi, lo, hi))
- `antiderivative-transfer-ptwise-eq` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), psi in fun(rr, rr), g, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(g, phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], f(x_) = g(x_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], psi(x_) = phi(x_)) implies is-antiderivative(f, psi, a, b))
- `apply-congruence-1` — forall([f, g], f == g implies forall([u], f(u) == g(u)))
- `apply-congruence-2` — forall([f, g], f == g implies forall([u, v], f(u, v) == g(u, v)))
- `ascoli-arzela-sequential` — forall([s, fam], is-compact(s) implies forsome([x], x in pts(s)) implies fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies pointwise-bounded(s, fam) implies forsome([del], strictly-mono-nn(del) and forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g) and converges-uniformly(s, subseq(fam, del), g))))
- `ascoli-dense-bridge` — forall([s, fam], is-compact(s) implies fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies forsome([dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) and converges-on(s, fam, dseq)) implies forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g) and converges-uniformly(s, fam, g)))
- `ascoli-pointwise-diagonal` — forall([s, fam], pointwise-bounded(s, fam) implies forall([dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) implies forsome([del], strictly-mono-nn(del) and converges-on(s, subseq(fam, del), dseq))))
- `ascoli-sequential-from-diagonal` — forall([s, fam], is-compact(s) implies forsome([x], x in pts(s)) implies fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies pointwise-bounded(s, fam) implies forall([dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) implies forsome([del], strictly-mono-nn(del) and converges-on(s, subseq(fam, del), dseq))) implies forsome([del], strictly-mono-nn(del) and forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g) and converges-uniformly(s, subseq(fam, del), g))))
- `baire-category` — forall([s], is-complete(s) implies forsome([x], x in pts(s)) implies is-nonmeager(s, pts(s)))
- `ball-2r-triangle` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forall([y in ball(s, x, r), z in ball(s, x, r)], (dist(s))(y, z) <= r + r and not((dist(s))(y, z) = r + r))))
- `ball-center-in` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies x in ball(s, x, r)))
- `ball-cover-is-open-cover` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies is-open-cover(s, ball-cover(s, r))))
- `ball-cover-mem-fwd` — forall([s, r, u in ball-cover(s, r)], forsome([c in pts(s)], ball(s, c, r) = u))
- `ball-cover-unfold` — forall([s, r], ball-cover(s, r) == image(vnb-lambda(c, pts(s), ball(s, c, r)), pts(s)))
- `ball-in-closed-ball` — forall([s, c, r], ball(s, c, r) subset closed-ball(s, c, r))
- `ball-is-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies is-open(s, ball(s, x, r))))
- `ball-is-set` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x, r], ball(s, x, r) in set))
- `ball-mem-from-le` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y, d, r], y in pts(s) and d in rr and r in rr and (dist(s))(x, y) <= d and d < r implies y in ball(s, x, r)))
- `ball-membership` — forall([s, x, r, y], y in ball(s, x, r) iff y in pts(s) and (dist(s))(x, y) <= r and not((dist(s))(x, y) = r))
- `ball-point-le` — forall([s, c, r, w, p in w], ball(s, c, r) = w implies (dist(s))(c, p) <= r)
- `ball-point-ne` — forall([s, c, r, w, p in w], ball(s, c, r) = w implies not((dist(s))(c, p) = r))
- `ball-sep-unfold` — forall([s, c, r], ball(s, c, r) == {y in pts(s): (dist(s))(c, y) <= r and not((dist(s))(c, y) = r)})
- `bary-in-conv3` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc, goux_ in cc], 0 <= real-part(goux_) implies 0 <= imag-part(goux_) implies real-part(goux_) + imag-part(goux_) <= 1 implies (real-part(goux_) * goua_ + imag-part(goux_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(goux_) - imag-part(goux_)) * gouc_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_))
- `bary-lip` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc, goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc], magnitude((real-part(goux_) * goua_ + imag-part(goux_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(goux_) - imag-part(goux_)) * gouc_ - ((real-part(gouy_) * goua_ + imag-part(gouy_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(gouy_) - imag-part(gouy_)) * gouc_)) <= (magnitude(goua_ - gouc_) + magnitude(goub_ - gouc_)) * magnitude(goux_ - gouy_))
- `bary-mid` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc, goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc], (real-part(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) * goua_ + imag-part(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) - imag-part(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_))) * gouc_ = cc-mid((real-part(goux_) * goua_ + imag-part(goux_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(goux_) - imag-part(goux_)) * gouc_, (real-part(gouy_) * goua_ + imag-part(gouy_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(gouy_) - imag-part(gouy_)) * gouc_))
- `bary-side-near` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc, goup_ in cc, gouq_ in cc, goux_ in cc, gouw_ in cc, gour_ in rr, goum_ in rr], magnitude(goup_ - goux_) <= gour_ implies magnitude(gouq_ - goup_) <= gour_ implies gour_ <= 6 * goum_ implies magnitude(goux_ - gouw_) <= 12 * goum_ implies forall([gouz_ in trace(seg-path((real-part(goup_) * goua_ + imag-part(goup_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(goup_) - imag-part(goup_)) * gouc_, (real-part(gouq_) * goua_ + imag-part(gouq_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(gouq_) - imag-part(gouq_)) * gouc_), 0, 1)], magnitude(gouz_ - ((real-part(gouw_) * goua_ + imag-part(gouw_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(gouw_) - imag-part(gouw_)) * gouc_)) <= (24 * (magnitude(goua_ - gouc_) + magnitude(goub_ - gouc_))) * goum_))
- `bary-vertices` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], (real-part(1) * goua_ + imag-part(1) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(1) - imag-part(1)) * gouc_ = goua_ and (real-part(1i) * goua_ + imag-part(1i) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(1i) - imag-part(1i)) * gouc_ = goub_ and (real-part(0) * goua_ + imag-part(0) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(0) - imag-part(0)) * gouc_ = gouc_)
- `bdd-fn-le-arg` — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies (/)(t, 1 + t) <= t)
- `bdd-fn-lt-one` — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies (/)(t, 1 + t) < 1)
- `bdd-fn-mono` — forall([s in rr, t in rr], 0 <= s and s <= t implies (/)(s, 1 + s) <= (/)(t, 1 + t))
- `bdd-fn-nonneg` — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies 0 <= (/)(t, 1 + t))
- `bdd-fn-reflect` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], 0 <= a implies 0 <= b implies (/)(a, 1 + a) <= (/)(b, 1 + b) implies a <= b)
- `bdd-fn-subadd` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], 0 <= a and 0 <= b implies (/)(a + b, 1 + (a + b)) <= (/)(a, 1 + a) + (/)(b, 1 + b))
- `bdd-fn-zero` — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies t = 0 implies (/)(t, 1 + t) = 0)
- `bdd-fn-zero-eq` — forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies (/)(t, 1 + t) = 0 implies t = 0)
- `bdd-linfun-abs-real` — forall([m, f, x], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) implies x in vec(m) implies abs(f(x)) in rr)
- `bdd-metric-bounded` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x, y) < 1))
- `bdd-metric-carrier` — forall([s], pts(bdd-metric(s)) == pts(s))
- `bdd-metric-converges-bwd` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), lm_ in pts(s)], converges-to(bdd-metric(s), f, lm_) implies converges-to(s, f, lm_)))
- `bdd-metric-converges-fwd` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), lm_ in pts(s)], converges-to(s, f, lm_) implies converges-to(bdd-metric(s), f, lm_)))
- `bdd-metric-converges-iff` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), lm_ in pts(s)], converges-to(bdd-metric(s), f, lm_) iff converges-to(s, f, lm_)))
- `bdd-metric-dist-le` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x_ in pts(s), y_ in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x_, y_) <= (dist(s))(x_, y_)))
- `bdd-metric-dist-reflect` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x_ in pts(s), y_ in pts(s), e_ in rr], 0 <= e_ implies (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x_, y_) <= (/)(e_, 1 + e_) implies (dist(s))(x_, y_) <= e_))
- `bdd-metric-distance` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([x in pts(s), y in pts(s)], (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(x, y) = (/)((dist(s))(x, y), 1 + (dist(s))(x, y))))
- `bdd-metric-id-bicontinuous` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-continuous(s, bdd-metric(s), vnb-lambda(x, pts(s), x)) and is-continuous(bdd-metric(s), s, vnb-lambda(x, pts(bdd-metric(s)), x)))
- `bdd-metric-is-bounded-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-bounded-metric-space(bdd-metric(s)))
- `bdd-metric-is-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(bdd-metric(s)))
- `bdd-metric-open-bwd` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([uu], is-open(bdd-metric(s), uu) implies is-open(s, uu)))
- `bdd-metric-open-fwd` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([uu], is-open(s, uu) implies is-open(bdd-metric(s), uu)))
- `bdd-metric-preserves-metric-top` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies metric-top(bdd-metric(s)) == metric-top(s))
- `bdd-metric-weight-bound` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([u_ in pts(s), v_ in pts(s), wn in rr], 0 <= wn implies wn * (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(u_, v_) in rr and 0 <= wn * (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(u_, v_) and wn * (dist(bdd-metric(s)))(u_, v_) <= wn))
- `bdd-rr-equivalent` — is-continuous(rr-ms, bdd-metric(rr-ms), vnb-lambda(x, rr, x)) and is-continuous(bdd-metric(rr-ms), rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, x))
- `bdd-rr-ms-bounded` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], (dist(bdd-metric(rr-ms)))(x, y) < 1)
- `bdd-rr-ms-is-metric-space` — is-metric-space(bdd-metric(rr-ms))
- `bernstein-approx-bound` — forall([f_, n_, x_, hh_, delta_, mm_, cc_], f_ in fun(rr, rr) implies n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies x_ in ccint(0, 1) implies hh_ in rr implies 0 < hh_ implies delta_ in rr implies 0 < delta_ implies mm_ in rr implies 0 <= mm_ implies cc_ in rr implies 0 <= cc_ implies (n_ * n_) * ((delta_ * delta_) * cc_) = mm_ + mm_ implies cc_ * n_ <= hh_ implies forall([p_ in ccint(0, 1), q_ in ccint(0, 1)], abs(p_ - q_) <= delta_ implies abs(f_(p_) - f_(q_)) <= hh_) implies forall([z_ in ccint(0, 1)], abs(f_(z_)) <= mm_) implies abs(f_(x_) - bernstein-poly(f_, n_, x_)) <= hh_ + hh_)
- `bernstein-basis-above` — forall([n_, x_, k_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies k_ in zz implies n_ < k_ implies (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_) = 0)
- `bernstein-basis-antiderivable` — forall([n_, k_, a, b], n_ in nn implies k_ in nn implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, (bernstein-basis(n_, x))(k_)), a, b))
- `bernstein-basis-degree-zero-diag` — forall([x_, k_], x_ in rr implies k_ in nn implies k_ = 0 implies (bernstein-basis(0, x_))(k_) = 1)
- `bernstein-basis-degree-zero-off` — forall([x_, k_], x_ in rr implies k_ in nn implies not(k_ = 0) implies (bernstein-basis(0, x_))(k_) = 0)
- `bernstein-basis-in-fun` — forall([n_ in nn, x_ in rr], bernstein-basis(n_, x_) in fun(zz, rr))
- `bernstein-basis-nonneg` — forall([n_ in nn, x_, k_], x_ in rr implies 0 <= x_ implies x_ <= 1 implies k_ in nn implies 0 <= (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_))
- `bernstein-basis-null` — forall([n_, x_, k_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies k_ in zz implies k_ < 0 implies (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_) = 0)
- `bernstein-basis-ptwise-in-rr` — forall([n_, x_, k_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies k_ in zz implies (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_) in rr)
- `bernstein-basis-scaled-antiderivable` — forall([c, n_, k_, a, b], c in rr implies n_ in nn implies k_ in nn implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * (bernstein-basis(n_, x))(k_)), a, b))
- `bernstein-basis-succ` — forall([n_, x_, k_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies k_ in nn implies (bernstein-basis(succ(n_), x_))(k_) = x_ * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_ - 1) + (1 - x_) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_))
- `bernstein-basis-unfold` — forall([n_, x_], bernstein-basis(n_, x_) == comb-kk(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), x_, 1 - x_, n_))
- `bernstein-ccint-approximant-antiderivable` — forall([f, n_, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, bernstein-poly(compose(f, vnb-lambda(x_, rr, a + (b - a) * x_)), n_, (x - a) * recip(b - a))), a, b))
- `bernstein-moment-1` — forall([n_ in nn, x_, h_], x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = k_ * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(h_, succ(n_)) = n_ * x_)
- `bernstein-moment-2` — forall([n_ in nn, x_, h_], x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = (k_ * (k_ - 1)) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(h_, succ(n_)) = (n_ * (n_ - 1)) * (x_ * x_))
- `bernstein-partition` — forall([n_ in nn, x_ in rr], series-partial-sum(bernstein-basis(n_, x_), succ(n_)) = 1)
- `bernstein-poly-antiderivable` — forall([f, n_, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, bernstein-poly(f, n_, x)), a, b))
- `bernstein-poly-unit-coordinate-antiderivable` — forall([g, n_, a, b], g in fun(rr, rr) implies n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, bernstein-poly(g, n_, (x - a) * recip(b - a))), a, b))
- `bernstein-term-antiderivable` — forall([n_ in nn, a, b, k_, j_], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies k_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ j_ * (bernstein-basis(n_, x))(k_)), a, b))
- `bernstein-term-bound` — forall([f_, n_, x_, hh_, delta_, mm_, cc_, l_], f_ in fun(rr, rr) implies n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies x_ in ccint(0, 1) implies hh_ in rr implies 0 < hh_ implies delta_ in rr implies 0 < delta_ implies mm_ in rr implies 0 <= mm_ implies cc_ in rr implies 0 <= cc_ implies (n_ * n_) * ((delta_ * delta_) * cc_) = mm_ + mm_ implies forall([p_ in ccint(0, 1), q_ in ccint(0, 1)], abs(p_ - q_) <= delta_ implies abs(f_(p_) - f_(q_)) <= hh_) implies forall([z_ in ccint(0, 1)], abs(f_(z_)) <= mm_) implies l_ in nn implies (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(l_) * (f_(x_) - f_((/)(l_, n_))) <= (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(l_) * (hh_ + cc_ * ((l_ - n_ * x_) * (l_ - n_ * x_))) and (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(l_) * ((0 - 1) * (hh_ + cc_ * ((l_ - n_ * x_) * (l_ - n_ * x_)))) <= (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(l_) * (f_(x_) - f_((/)(l_, n_))))
- `bernstein-uniform-approximation` — forall([f_, eps_], f_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([x in ccint(0, 1)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f_, x)) implies pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([cap_ in nn], forall([n_, x_], n_ in nn implies cap_ <= n_ implies x_ in ccint(0, 1) implies abs(f_(x_) - bernstein-poly(f_, n_, x_)) <= eps_)))
- `bernstein-uniform-approximation-ccint` — forall([fn_, a, b, ep_], fn_ in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, fn_, x_)) implies pos-rr(ep_) implies forsome([cap_ in nn], forall([n_], n_ in nn and cap_ <= n_ implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(fn_(x_) - bernstein-poly(compose(fn_, vnb-lambda(x, rr, a + (b - a) * x)), n_, (x_ - a) * recip(b - a))) <= ep_))))
- `bernstein-variance` — forall([n_, x_, h_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = ((k_ - n_ * x_) * (k_ - n_ * x_)) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(h_, succ(n_)) = (n_ * x_) * (1 - x_))
- `bernstein-variance-bound` — forall([n_, x_, h_], n_ in nn implies not(n_ = 0) implies x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = ((k_ - n_ * x_) * (k_ - n_ * x_)) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies (/)(1, n_) * series-partial-sum(h_, succ(n_)) <= 1)
- `bernstein-weighted-step` — forall([n_, x_, w_, p_, q_, s_], n_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies forall([k_ in zz], w_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], q_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], s_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], p_(k_) = w_(k_) * (bernstein-basis(succ(n_), x_))(k_)) implies forall([k_ in nn], q_(k_) = w_(succ(k_)) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies forall([k_ in nn], s_(k_) = w_(k_) * (bernstein-basis(n_, x_))(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(p_, succ(succ(n_))) = x_ * series-partial-sum(q_, succ(n_)) + (1 - x_) * series-partial-sum(s_, succ(n_)))
- `big-union-mono` — forall([a_, b_], a_ subset b_ implies big-union(u_, a_, u_) subset big-union(u_, b_, u_))
- `bijection-compose` — forall([x, y, z, phi, psi], phi in bijection(x, y) and psi in bijection(y, z) implies vnb-lambda(x_, x, psi(phi(x_))) in bijection(x, z))
- `bijection-from-inverse` — forall([x, y, phi, psi], phi in fun(x, y) implies psi in fun(y, x) implies forall([u_ in x], psi(phi(u_)) = u_) implies forall([v_ in y], phi(psi(v_)) = v_) implies phi in bijection(x, y))
- `bijection-identity` — forall([x in set], vnb-lambda(x_, x, x_) in bijection(x, x))
- `bijection-in-fun` — forall([x, y, phi in bijection(x, y)], phi in fun(x, y))
- `bijection-injective` — forall([x, y, phi in bijection(x, y), a in x, b in x], phi(a) = phi(b) implies a = b)
- `bijection-is-injection` — forall([x, y, phi in bijection(x, y)], phi in injection(x, y))
- `bijection-surjective` — forall([x, y, phi in bijection(x, y), w in y], forsome([z in x], phi(z) = w))
- `binomial-sum-value` — forall([n_, r, x_, y_], n_ in nn implies is-commutative-ring(r) implies x_ in carr(r) implies y_ in carr(r) implies sum(r, comb-kk(r, x_, y_, n_), succ(n_)) = ring-power(r, (add(r))(x_, y_), n_))
- `binomial-theorem` — forall([n in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r)], ring-power(r, (add(r))(x, y), n) = sum(r, comb-kk(r, x, y, n), succ(n))))
- `blf-app-real` — forall([m, g, v], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies v in vec(m) implies g(v) in rr)
- `blf-is-blf-on-vec` — forall([m, f], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f) implies is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, vec(m), f))
- `block-family` — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), rad], null-rr-seq(rad) implies forsome([blk in fun(nn, inf-subsets(nn))], forall([k in nn], blk(succ(k)) subset blk(k)) and forall([k in nn], forsome([c in pts(s)], forall([i in blk(k)], f(i) in ball(s, c, rad(k))))))))
- `block-family-combinatorial` — forall([v in set, f in fun(nn, v), cov], forall([k in nn], is-finite-cover(cov(k), v)) implies forsome([blk in fun(nn, inf-subsets(nn))], forall([k in nn], blk(succ(k)) subset blk(k)) and forall([k in nn], forsome([u in cov(k)], forall([i in blk(k)], f(i) in u)))))
- `block-step-converges` — forall([s], seq-compact(s) implies forall([h in fun(nn, pts(s)), j in inf-subsets(nn)], forsome([b_ in inf-subsets(nn)], b_ subset j and forsome([p in pts(s)], converges-along(s, h, b_, p)))))
- `block-type` — forall([m, n, x, p, k, l], k in nn implies l in nn implies p in mat(m, n, x) implies k <= m implies l <= n implies block(p, k, l) in mat(k, l, x))
- `border-entries-defined` — forall([a, b, m, p, q], is-ring(a) implies p in nn implies q in nn implies b in carr(a) implies m in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies forall([i_ in interval(1, succ(p)), j_ in interval(1, succ(q))], (vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, succ(p)), interval(1, succ(q))), if(i = 1, if(j = 1, b, zero(a)), if(j = 1, zero(a), entry(m, nn-minus(i, 1), nn-minus(j, 1))))))(i_, j_) in set))
- `border-entry-11` — forall([a, b, m, p, q], is-ring(a) implies p in nn implies q in nn implies b in carr(a) implies m in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies entry(border(a, b, m, p, q), 1, 1) = b)
- `border-entry-1j` — forall([a, b, m, p, q, j], is-ring(a) implies p in nn implies q in nn implies b in carr(a) implies m in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies j in interval(1, succ(q)) implies not(j = 1) implies entry(border(a, b, m, p, q), 1, j) = zero(a))
- `border-entry-block` — forall([a, b, m, p, q, i, j], is-ring(a) implies p in nn implies q in nn implies b in carr(a) implies m in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies i in interval(1, p) implies j in interval(1, q) implies entry(border(a, b, m, p, q), succ(i), succ(j)) = entry(m, i, j))
- `border-entry-block2` — forall([a, b, m, p, q, i, j], is-ring(a) implies p in nn implies q in nn implies b in carr(a) implies m in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies i in interval(1, succ(p)) implies not(i = 1) implies j in interval(1, succ(q)) implies not(j = 1) implies entry(border(a, b, m, p, q), i, j) = entry(m, nn-minus(i, 1), nn-minus(j, 1)))
- `border-entry-i1` — forall([a, b, m, p, q, i], is-ring(a) implies p in nn implies q in nn implies b in carr(a) implies m in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies i in interval(1, succ(p)) implies not(i = 1) implies entry(border(a, b, m, p, q), i, 1) = zero(a))
- `border-identity` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p in nn], border(a, one(a), identmat(a, p), p, p) = identmat(a, succ(p))))
- `border-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, u], n in nn implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, u) implies is-invertible-mat(a, succ(n), border(a, one(a), u, n, n))))
- `border-is-diagonal` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([b, e, p, q], p in nn implies q in nn implies is-diagonal(a, p, q, e) implies b in carr(a) implies e in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies is-diagonal(a, succ(p), succ(q), border(a, b, e, p, q))))
- `border-mult` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p, q, r, x, y, b, d], p in nn implies q in nn implies r in nn implies x in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies y in mat(q, r, carr(a)) implies b in carr(a) implies d in carr(a) implies (q = 0 implies p = 0 or r = 0) implies matmul(a, border(a, b, x, p, q), border(a, d, y, q, r)) = border(a, (mul(a))(b, d), matmul(a, x, y), p, r)))
- `border-staircase` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([b, w, p, q, kp], p in nn implies q in nn implies kp in nn implies b in carr(a) implies not(b = zero(a)) implies smith-staircase(a, p, q, w, kp) implies w in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies smith-staircase(a, succ(p), succ(q), border(a, b, w, p, q), succ(kp))))
- `border-type` — forall([a, b, m, p, q], is-ring(a) implies p in nn implies q in nn implies b in carr(a) implies m in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies border(a, b, m, p, q) in mat(succ(p), succ(q), carr(a)))
- `bordered-eq-border` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p, q, c], p in nn implies q in nn implies c in mat(succ(p), succ(q), carr(a)) implies forall([j in interval(1, succ(q))], not(j = 1) implies entry(c, 1, j) = zero(a)) implies forall([i in interval(1, succ(p))], not(i = 1) implies entry(c, i, 1) = zero(a)) implies c = border(a, entry(c, 1, 1), submat(c, p, q), p, q)))
- `bordering` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([p, q, b, w, g, e], p in nn implies q in nn implies b in carr(a) implies w in mat(p, q, carr(a)) implies g in mat(succ(p), succ(q), carr(a)) implies g = border(a, b, w, p, q) implies mat-equiv(a, p, q, w, e) implies mat-equiv(a, succ(p), succ(q), g, border(a, b, e, p, q))))
- `bounded-block-converges` — forall([hqv_ in fun(nn, rr), bdv_ in rr], forall([iqv_ in nn], abs(hqv_(iqv_)) <= bdv_) implies forall([jqv_ in inf-subsets(nn)], forsome([bqv_ in inf-subsets(nn)], bqv_ subset jqv_ and forsome([ptv_ in rr], converges-along(rr-ms, hqv_, bqv_, ptv_)))))
- `bt-add-in-carr` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, b) in carr(r)))
- `bt-lt-succ` — forall([n in nn], n < succ(n))
- `bt-neg1-in-zz` — 0 - 1 in zz
- `bt-neg1-neg` — 0 - 1 < 0
- `bt-nn-in-zz` — forall([n in nn], n in zz)
- `bt-succ-in-nn` — forall([n in nn], succ(n) in nn)
- `bt-succ-minus-1` — forall([n in nn], succ(n) - 1 = n)
- `bt-sum-in-carr-zz` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([g in fun(zz, carr(r)), n in nn], sum(r, g, n) in carr(r)))
- `c-int-add` — forall([phi, psi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies is-antiderivable(psi, a, b) implies c-int(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) + psi(x)), a, b) = c-int(phi, a, b) + c-int(psi, a, b))
- `c-int-add-bounds` — forall([phi, a, b, c], is-antiderivable(phi, a, c) implies b in rr implies a < b implies b < c implies c-int(phi, a, c) = c-int(phi, a, b) + c-int(phi, b, c))
- `c-int-add-continuous` — forall([phi, psi, a, b], phi in fun(rr, rr) implies psi in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, phi, x_)) implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, psi, x_)) implies c-int(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(x) + psi(x)), a, b) = c-int(phi, a, b) + c-int(psi, a, b))
- `c-int-change-of-variable` — forall([phi, amap, ader, p_, q_], amap in fun(rr, rr) implies ader in fun(rr, rr) implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivable(phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, amap, u_)) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies is-diff-at(amap, w_, ader(w_))) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], amap(u_) in ccint(amap(p_), amap(q_))) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies amap(p_) < amap(w_) and amap(w_) < amap(q_)) implies c-int(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(amap(x)) * ader(x)), p_, q_) = c-int(phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)))
- `c-int-change-of-variable-transfer` — forall([phi, psi, amap, ader, p_, q_], amap in fun(rr, rr) implies ader in fun(rr, rr) implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies psi in fun(rr, rr) implies is-antiderivable(phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, amap, u_)) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies is-diff-at(amap, w_, ader(w_))) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], amap(u_) in ccint(amap(p_), amap(q_))) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies amap(p_) < amap(w_) and amap(w_) < amap(q_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], psi(x_) == phi(amap(x_)) * ader(x_)) implies c-int(psi, p_, q_) = c-int(phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)))
- `c-int-defined-for-continuous` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies c-int(f, a, b) in rr)
- `c-int-in-rr` — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies c-int(phi, a, b) in rr)
- `c-int-or-additive` — forall([phi, p, q, a, b, c], is-antiderivable(phi, p, q) implies a in ccint(p, q) implies b in ccint(p, q) implies c in ccint(p, q) implies c-int-or(phi, a, c) = c-int-or(phi, a, b) + c-int-or(phi, b, c))
- `c-int-or-anti` — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies c-int-or(phi, a, b) = c-int(phi, a, b))
- `c-int-or-degenerate` — forall([phi, a], c-int-or(phi, a, a) = 0)
- `c-int-or-flip` — forall([phi, a, b], is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies c-int-or(phi, b, a) = -c-int(phi, a, b))
- `c-int-or-in-rr` — forall([phi, a, b], c-int-or(phi, a, b) in rr)
- `c-int-or-none` — forall([phi, a, b], not(is-antiderivable(phi, a, b)) implies not(is-antiderivable(phi, b, a)) implies c-int-or(phi, a, b) = 0)
- `c-int-or-reverse` — forall([phi, a, b], c-int-or(phi, b, a) = -c-int-or(phi, a, b))
- `c-int-or-scale` — forall([lam, a, b], lam in rr implies a in rr implies b in rr implies 0 < lam implies 0 < a implies 0 < b implies c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), lam * a, lam * b) = c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), a, b))
- `c-int-or-scale-ordered` — forall([lam, a, b], lam in rr implies a in rr implies b in rr implies 0 < lam implies 0 < a implies a < b implies c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), lam * a, lam * b) = c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), a, b))
- `c-int-or-value` — forall([f, phi, p, q, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, p, q) implies a in ccint(p, q) implies b in ccint(p, q) implies c-int-or(phi, a, b) = f(b) - f(a))
- `c-int-scale` — forall([c, phi, a, b], c in rr implies is-antiderivable(phi, a, b) implies c-int(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * phi(x)), a, b) = c * c-int(phi, a, b))
- `c-int-scale-continuous` — forall([c, phi, a, b], c in rr implies phi in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, phi, x_)) implies c-int(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * phi(x)), a, b) = c * c-int(phi, a, b))
- `c-int-value` — forall([f, phi, a, b], is-antiderivative(f, phi, a, b) implies c-int(phi, a, b) = f(b) - f(a))
- `c-int-value-continuous` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies forsome([g_], is-antiderivative(g_, f, a, b) and c-int(f, a, b) = g_(b) - g_(a)))
- `c-metric-canonical-summable` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies series-converges(vnb-lambda(k, nn, (vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))))(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u_, v_))))))
- `c-metric-carrier` — forall([s, w], pts(c-metric-w(s, w)) == pts(s))
- `c-metric-dist-converges` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies series-converges-to(vnb-lambda(k, nn, w(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u_, v_))), (dist(c-metric-w(s, w)))(u_, v_)))))
- `c-metric-dist-real` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([k_ in nn, u_ in pts(s), v_ in pts(s)], ((dists(s))(k_))(u_, v_) in rr))
- `c-metric-distance` — forall([s, w, u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(c-metric-w(s, w)))(u_, v_) == series-limit(vnb-lambda(k, nn, w(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u_, v_)))))
- `c-metric-pseudometric-at` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([k_ in nn], is-pseudometric((dists(s))(k_), pts(s))))
- `c-metric-summable` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies series-converges(vnb-lambda(k, nn, w(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u_, v_)))))))
- `c-metric-term-bound` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([k_, u_, v_, wk], k_ in nn implies u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies wk in rr implies 0 <= wk implies wk * min(1, ((dists(s))(k_))(u_, v_)) in rr and 0 <= wk * min(1, ((dists(s))(k_))(u_, v_)) and wk * min(1, ((dists(s))(k_))(u_, v_)) <= wk))
- `c-metric-term-seq-in-fun` — forall([s], is-c-metric-space(s) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies vnb-lambda(k, nn, w(k) * min(1, ((dists(s))(k))(u_, v_))) in fun(nn, rr))))
- `caratheodory-remainder-small` — forall([gbs_, gbf_, gba_, gbl_], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, gbs_, gbf_, gba_, gbl_) implies forall([gbe_], pos-rr(gbe_) implies forsome([gbr_], pos-rr(gbr_) and forall([gbz_ in gbs_], magnitude(gbz_ - gba_) < gbr_ implies magnitude(gbf_(gbz_) - (gbf_(gba_) + gbl_ * (gbz_ - gba_))) <= gbe_ * magnitude(gbz_ - gba_)))))
- `caratheodory-witness-bound` — forall([h in fun(rr, rr), phi in fun(rr, rr), psi in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, t_ in rr, m_ in rr], is-antiderivative(h, phi, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(phi(x_)) <= m_) implies t_ in ccint(a, b) implies psi(t_) = phi(t_) implies forall([x_ in rr], h(x_) - h(t_) = psi(x_) * (x_ - t_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(psi(x_)) <= m_))
- `caratheodory-witness-pair-bound` — forall([f1 in fun(rr, rr), f2 in fun(rr, rr), p1 in fun(rr, rr), p2 in fun(rr, rr), w1 in fun(rr, rr), w2 in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, t_ in rr, m_ in rr], is-antiderivative(f1, p1, a, b) implies is-antiderivative(f2, p2, a, b) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(p1(x_) - p2(x_)) <= m_) implies t_ in ccint(a, b) implies w1(t_) = p1(t_) implies w2(t_) = p2(t_) implies forall([x_ in rr], f1(x_) - f1(t_) = w1(x_) * (x_ - t_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], f2(x_) - f2(t_) = w2(x_) * (x_ - t_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs(w1(x_) - w2(x_)) <= m_))
- `card-bij` — forall([n_ in nn, a_, ph, et], ph in bijection(a_, ord-segment(n_)) and et in bijection(ord-segment(n_), a_) implies card(a_) = n_)
- `card-bijection-eq` — forall([dm, cod, phi], dm in set implies card(dm) in nn implies phi in bijection(dm, cod) implies card(cod) = card(dm))
- `card-body-exists` — forall([s in set], forsome([al in ord], forsome([phi], phi in bijection(s, ord-segment(al))) and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, al) implies not(forsome([psi], psi in bijection(s, ord-segment(beta)))))))
- `card-cartesian-nn` — forall([x, y], x in set implies card(x) in nn implies y in set implies card(y) in nn implies card(cartesian(x, y)) in nn)
- `card-empty` — card(empty-set) = 0
- `card-empty-le` — forall([x_], x_ = empty-set implies card(x_) <= 0)
- `card-finite-bij` — forall([a], a in set and card(a) in nn implies forsome([phi], phi in bijection(ord-segment(card(a)), a)))
- `card-finite-induction-aux` — forall([n_ in nn, c_], empty-set in c_ implies forall([s_], s_ in set and card(s_) in nn and s_ in c_ implies forall([x_], x_ in set and not(x_ in s_) implies union(s_, pair(x_, x_)) in c_)) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) = n_ implies s_ in c_))
- `card-from-body` — forall([a_, al], al in ord and forsome([phi], phi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(al))) and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, al) implies not(forsome([psi], psi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(beta))))) implies card(a_) = al)
- `card-image-finite` — forall([phi, x in set], card(x) in nn implies card(image(phi, x)) in nn)
- `card-image-injection` — forall([dm, cod, phi], phi in injection(dm, cod) and dm in set and card(dm) in nn implies card(image(phi, dm)) = card(dm))
- `card-image-injection-curried` — forall([dm_ in set], card(dm_) in nn implies forall([cod_, ph_ in injection(dm_, cod_)], card(image(ph_, dm_)) = card(dm_)))
- `card-in-ord` — forall([a in set], card(a) in ord)
- `card-insert` — forall([a in set], card(a) in nn implies forall([x], x in set and not(x in a) implies card(union(a, pair(x, x))) = succ_ord(card(a))))
- `card-insert-converse` — forall([a_ in set, x_], x_ in set and not(x_ in a_) implies forall([k_ in nn], card(union(a_, pair(x_, x_))) = succ(k_) implies card(a_) = k_))
- `card-insert-curried` — forall([a_ in set], card(a_) in nn implies forall([x_ in set], not(x_ in a_) implies card(union(a_, pair(x_, x_))) = succ_ord(card(a_))))
- `card-pair` — forall([a_ in set, b_ in set], not(a_ = b_) implies card({a_, b_}) = 2)
- `card-power-nn` — forall([x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies card(power(x)) in nn)
- `card-seg-image` — forall([n_ in nn, b_, g_ in bijection(ord-segment(succ(n_)), b_)], card(image(g_, ord-segment(n_))) = n_)
- `card-segment` — forall([n_ in nn], card(ord-segment(n_)) = n_)
- `card-singleton` — forall([x in set], card(pair(x, x)) = succ(0))
- `card-subset-mono` — forall([b_ in set], card(b_) in nn implies forall([a_], a_ subset b_ implies card(a_) <= card(b_)))
- `card-subset-nn` — forall([x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies forall([s], s in set and forall([z in s], z in x) implies card(s) in nn))
- `card-union-bound` — forall([a_ in set], card(a_) in nn implies forall([b_ in set], card(b_) in nn implies card(union(a_, b_)) <= card(a_) + card(b_)))
- `card-union-disjoint` — forall([a], a in set and card(a) in nn implies forall([b], b in set and card(b) in nn and intersection(a, b) = empty-set implies card(union(a, b)) = card(a) + card(b)))
- `card-union-disjoint-curried` — forall([a_ in set], card(a_) in nn implies forall([b_ in set], card(b_) in nn implies intersection(a_, b_) = empty-set implies card(union(a_, b_)) = card(a_) + card(b_)))
- `card-union-disjoint-ind` — forall([n_ in nn, a_ in set], card(a_) in nn implies forall([b_ in set], card(b_) = n_ implies intersection(a_, b_) = empty-set implies card(union(a_, b_)) = card(a_) + n_))
- `card-union-nn` — forall([a_ in set], card(a_) in nn implies forall([b_ in set], card(b_) in nn implies card(union(a_, b_)) in nn))
- `card-union-singleton-bound` — forall([s_ in set, y_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies card(union(pair(y_, y_), s_)) in nn and card(union(pair(y_, y_), s_)) <= succ(card(s_)))
- `card-union-singleton-nn` — forall([s_ in set, y_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies card(union(pair(y_, y_), s_)) in nn)
- `card-zermelo` — forall([s in set], card(s) in ord and forsome([phi], phi in bijection(s, ord-segment(card(s)))))
- `card-zero-is-empty` — forall([b_ in set], card(b_) = 0 implies b_ = empty-set)
- `carrier-is-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-open(s, pts(s)))
- `cartesian-nth` — forall([x_, aa, bb], x_ in cartesian(aa, bb) implies nth(1, x_) in aa and nth(2, x_) in bb)
- `cartesian-pair-eq` — forall([x_, aa, bb], x_ in cartesian(aa, bb) implies x_ = [nth(1, x_), nth(2, x_)])
- `cauchy-block-estimate` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c in pts(s), r], pos-rr(r) implies forall([u], u = ball(s, c, r) implies forall([y in u, z in u, d], pos-rr(d) implies forall([eps in rr], r <= d implies d + d = eps implies (dist(s))(y, z) <= eps)))))
- `cauchy-cluster-converges` — forall([s, f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) and forsome([x], cluster-point(s, f, x)) implies converges(s, f))
- `cauchy-convex-closed` — forall([u, f], is-convex(u) implies holomorphic-on(u, f) implies forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies pgam(b) = pgam(a) implies line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b) = 0))
- `cauchy-convex-closed-except` — forall([u, f, goxx_], is-convex(u) implies is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u) implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies goxx_ in u implies holomorphic-on(difference(u, singleton(goxx_)), restrict(f, difference(u, singleton(goxx_)))) implies forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies pgam(b) = pgam(a) implies line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b) = 0))
- `cauchy-convex-primitive` — forall([u, f], is-convex(u) implies holomorphic-on(u, f) implies forall([gouo_ in u], forsome([goug_ in fun(u, cc)], holomorphic-on(u, goug_) and forall([gouz_ in u], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, u, goug_, gouz_, f(gouz_))))))
- `cauchy-convex-primitive-except` — forall([u, f, goxx_], is-convex(u) implies is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u) implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies goxx_ in u implies holomorphic-on(difference(u, singleton(goxx_)), restrict(f, difference(u, singleton(goxx_)))) implies forall([goxo_ in u], forsome([goxg_ in fun(u, cc)], holomorphic-on(u, goxg_) and forall([goxz_ in u], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, u, goxg_, goxz_, f(goxz_))))))
- `cauchy-convex-segments` — forall([u, f], is-convex(u) implies holomorphic-on(u, f) implies forall([goua_ in u, goub_ in u, gouc_ in u], seg-int(f, goua_, gouc_) = seg-int(f, goua_, goub_) + seg-int(f, goub_, gouc_)))
- `cauchy-convex-segments-except` — forall([u, f, goxx_], is-convex(u) implies is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u) implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies goxx_ in u implies holomorphic-on(difference(u, singleton(goxx_)), restrict(f, difference(u, singleton(goxx_)))) implies forall([goxa_ in u, goxb_ in u, goxc_ in u], seg-int(f, goxa_, goxc_) = seg-int(f, goxa_, goxb_) + seg-int(f, goxb_, goxc_)))
- `cauchy-criterion-left` — forall([f, x], f in fun(rr, rr) implies x in rr implies forall([eps_], pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([delta_], pos-rr(delta_) and forall([s_, t_], s_ in rr and x - delta_ <= s_ and s_ < x implies t_ in rr and x - delta_ <= t_ and t_ < x implies abs(f(s_) - f(t_)) <= eps_))) implies forsome([l_], is-left-limit(f, x, l_)))
- `cauchy-criterion-right` — forall([f, x], f in fun(rr, rr) implies x in rr implies forall([eps_], pos-rr(eps_) implies forsome([delta_], pos-rr(delta_) and forall([s_, t_], s_ in rr and x < s_ and s_ <= x + delta_ implies t_ in rr and x < t_ and t_ <= x + delta_ implies abs(f(s_) - f(t_)) <= eps_))) implies forsome([l_], is-right-limit(f, x, l_)))
- `cauchy-rapid-subsequence` — forall([s, f, rad], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) and rad in fun(nn, rr) and forall([k in nn], pos-rr(rad(k))) implies forsome([phi in fun(nn, nn)], forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], m < n_ implies phi(m) < phi(n_)) and forall([k in nn], (dist(s))(f(phi(k)), f(phi(succ(k)))) <= rad(k))))
- `cauchy-seq-is-fun` — forall([s, f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) implies f in fun(nn, pts(s)))
- `cauchy-setoid-is-setoid` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies is-setoid(cauchy-setoid(m)))
- `cc-abs-im-le-magnitude` — forall([z_ in cc], abs(imag-part(z_)) <= magnitude(z_))
- `cc-abs-re-le-magnitude` — forall([z_ in cc], abs(real-part(z_)) <= magnitude(z_))
- `cc-additive-ag-iden` — iden(normed-field-additive-ag(cc-normed-field)) == 0
- `cc-additive-ag-opr` — opr(normed-field-additive-ag(cc-normed-field)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ + y_)
- `cc-affinely-independent` — forall([goxa_ in cc, goxb_ in cc, goxc_ in cc], not(imag-part((goxb_ - goxa_) * conjugate(goxc_ - goxa_)) = 0) implies forall([goxp_ in rr, goxq_ in rr], goxp_ * (goxb_ - goxa_) + goxq_ * (goxc_ - goxa_) = 0 implies goxp_ = 0 and goxq_ = 0))
- `cc-cauchy-of-dominated` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), p in fun(nn, rr)], is-cauchy-seq(cc-ms, f) implies forall([m_ in nn, n_ in nn], abs(p(m_) - p(n_)) <= magnitude(f(m_) - f(n_))) implies is-cauchy-seq(rr-ms, p))
- `cc-circle-arg` — forall([cxsa_ in rr, cxsb_ in rr], cxsa_ ^ 2 + cxsb_ ^ 2 = 1 implies forsome([cxsth_ in rr], -pi <= cxsth_ and cxsth_ < pi and cc-cos(cxsth_) = cxsa_ and cc-sin(cxsth_) = cxsb_))
- `cc-closed-ball-origin` — forall([cpsrad_, cpsz_ in closed-ball(cc-ms, 0, cpsrad_)], cpsz_ in cc and magnitude(cpsz_) <= cpsrad_)
- `cc-complete` — is-complete(cc-ms)
- `cc-compose-continuous-at` — forall([s, u, g, phi, x], is-metric-space(s) implies u subset cc implies is-continuous-at(s, cc-ms, g, x) implies forall([msy_ in pts(s)], g(msy_) in u) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), phi, g(x)) implies is-continuous-at(s, cc-ms, vnb-lambda(msz_, pts(s), phi(g(msz_))), x))
- `cc-conjugate-neg` — forall([a in cc], conjugate(-a) = -conjugate(a))
- `cc-conjugate-power` — forall([cexk_ in nn, cexz_ in cc], conjugate(cexz_ ^ cexk_) = conjugate(cexz_) ^ cexk_)
- `cc-conjugate-sub` — forall([cexa_ in cc, cexb_ in cc], conjugate(cexa_ - cexb_) = conjugate(cexa_) - conjugate(cexb_))
- `cc-continuous-at-add-const` — forall([u], u subset cc implies forall([f in fun(u, cc), goxg_ in fun(u, cc), goxk_ in cc], forall([goxz_ in u], goxg_(goxz_) == f(goxz_) + goxk_) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), goxg_, rgy_))))
- `cc-continuous-at-iff-coords` — forall([s, f, fr, fi, a], is-metric-space(s) implies f in fun(pts(s), cc) implies fr in fun(pts(s), rr) implies fi in fun(pts(s), rr) implies a in pts(s) implies forall([u_ in pts(s)], real-part(f(u_)) = fr(u_)) implies forall([u_ in pts(s)], imag-part(f(u_)) = fi(u_)) implies is-continuous-at(s, cc-ms, f, a) iff is-continuous-at(s, rr-ms, fr, a) and is-continuous-at(s, rr-ms, fi, a))
- `cc-continuous-restrict-rr` — forall([cekff_, cekgg_, cekx_], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), cc), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), cekff_, cekx_) implies cekgg_ in fun(rr, rr) implies cekx_ in rr implies forall([cekv_ in rr], cekgg_(cekv_) = cekff_(cekv_)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, cekgg_, cekx_))
- `cc-converges-iff-coords` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), g in fun(nn, rr), q_ in fun(nn, rr), lv in cc], forall([n_ in nn], real-part(f(n_)) = g(n_)) implies forall([n_ in nn], imag-part(f(n_)) = q_(n_)) implies converges-to(cc-ms, f, lv) iff converges-to(rr-ms, g, real-part(lv)) and converges-to(rr-ms, q_, imag-part(lv)))
- `cc-converges-magnitude-null` — forall([s_ in fun(nn, cc), lv in cc, m in fun(nn, rr)], converges-to(cc-ms, s_, lv) implies forall([j_ in nn], m(j_) = magnitude(s_(j_) - lv)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, m, 0))
- `cc-converges-of-coords` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), g in fun(nn, rr), q_ in fun(nn, rr), lv in cc], forall([n_ in nn], real-part(f(n_)) = g(n_)) implies forall([n_ in nn], imag-part(f(n_)) = q_(n_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, real-part(lv)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, q_, imag-part(lv)) implies converges-to(cc-ms, f, lv))
- `cc-coords-add` — forall([z in cc, w in cc], cc-coords(z + w) = [real-part(z) + real-part(w), imag-part(z) + imag-part(w)])
- `cc-coords-apply` — forall([z in cc], cc-coords(z) == [real-part(z), imag-part(z)])
- `cc-coords-in-fun` — cc-coords in fun(cc, cartesian(rr, rr))
- `cc-coords-is-bijection` — cc-coords in bijection(cc, cartesian(rr, rr))
- `cc-coords-of-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), g in fun(nn, rr), q_ in fun(nn, rr), lv in cc], forall([n_ in nn], real-part(f(n_)) = g(n_)) implies forall([n_ in nn], imag-part(f(n_)) = q_(n_)) implies converges-to(cc-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, real-part(lv)) and converges-to(rr-ms, q_, imag-part(lv)))
- `cc-coords-of-pair` — forall([pr_ in cartesian(rr, rr)], cc-coords(cc-of-pair(pr_)) = pr_)
- `cc-coords-real-mul` — forall([c_ in rr, z in cc], cc-coords(c_ * z) = [c_ * real-part(z), c_ * imag-part(z)])
- `cc-cos-add` — forall([cexz_ in cc, cexw_ in cc], cc-cos(cexz_ + cexw_) = cc-cos(cexz_) * cc-cos(cexw_) - cc-sin(cexz_) * cc-sin(cexw_))
- `cc-cos-as-power-series` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cek_ in nn], cf(cek_) = -1 ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(2 * cek_))) implies forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-series-converges(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, (-1 ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(2 * cek_))) * cexz_ ^ (2 * cek_))) and cc-cos(cexz_) = cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * (cexz_ ^ 2) ^ cpsk_))))
- `cc-cos-converges` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-series-converges(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, (-1 ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(2 * cek_))) * cexz_ ^ (2 * cek_))) and cc-cos(cexz_) in cc)
- `cc-cos-deriv` — forall([cexz_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cc-cos(cexy_)), cexz_, -cc-sin(cexz_)))
- `cc-cos-even` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-cos(-cexz_) = cc-cos(cexz_))
- `cc-cos-exp` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-cos(cexz_) = (cc-exp(1i * cexz_) + cc-exp(-(1i * cexz_))) * recip(2))
- `cc-cos-half-pi` — cc-cos(1/2 * pi) = 0
- `cc-cos-has-zero` — forsome([cekt_ in rr], 0 < cekt_ and cekt_ < 2 and cc-cos(cekt_) = 0)
- `cc-cos-holomorphic` — holomorphic-on(cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cc-cos(cexy_)))
- `cc-cos-least-zero` — forall([clht_ in rr], 0 < clht_ implies clht_ < 1/2 * pi implies not(cc-cos(clht_) = 0))
- `cc-cos-nonpos-far` — forall([clht_ in rr], 1/2 * pi <= clht_ implies clht_ <= pi implies cc-cos(clht_) <= 0)
- `cc-cos-one-bounds` — 5/12 <= cc-cos(1) and cc-cos(1) <= 7/12
- `cc-cos-pi` — cc-cos(pi) = -1
- `cc-cos-pos-half` — forall([clht_ in rr], -(1/2 * pi) < clht_ implies clht_ < 1/2 * pi implies 0 < cc-cos(clht_))
- `cc-cos-pos-right` — forall([clhs_ in rr], 0 < clhs_ implies clhs_ < 1/2 * pi implies 0 < cc-cos(clhs_))
- `cc-cos-pos-small` — forall([clht_ in rr], 0 < clht_ implies clht_ < 1 implies 0 < cc-cos(clht_))
- `cc-cos-radius` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cek_ in nn], cf(cek_) = -1 ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(2 * cek_))) implies cps-radius(cf) = pos-inf)
- `cc-cos-real-continuous` — vnb-lambda(cekv_, rr, cc-cos(cekv_)) in fun(rr, rr) and forall([cekx_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(cekv_, rr, cc-cos(cekv_)), cekx_))
- `cc-cos-real-part` — forall([cekt_ in rr], cc-cos(cekt_) = real-part(cc-exp(1i * cekt_)))
- `cc-cos-sin-half-pi` — cc-cos(1/2 * pi) = 0 and cc-sin(1/2 * pi) ^ 2 = 1
- `cc-cos-sin-one` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-cos(cexz_) ^ 2 + cc-sin(cexz_) ^ 2 = 1)
- `cc-cos-sin-pi` — cc-cos(pi) = -1 and cc-sin(pi) = 0
- `cc-cos-sin-real` — forall([cekt_ in rr], cc-cos(cekt_) in rr and cc-sin(cekt_) in rr)
- `cc-cos-sin-two-pi` — cc-cos(2 * pi) = 1 and cc-sin(2 * pi) = 0
- `cc-cos-three-halves-pos` — 0 < cc-cos(3/2)
- `cc-cos-two-neg` — cc-cos(2) < 0
- `cc-cos-two-pi` — cc-cos(2 * pi) = 1
- `cc-cos-unfold` — forall([cexz_], cc-cos(cexz_) == cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, (-1 ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(2 * cek_))) * cexz_ ^ (2 * cek_))))
- `cc-cos-zero` — cc-cos(0) = 1
- `cc-cross-coords` — forall([goxp_ in cc, goxq_ in cc], imag-part(goxp_ * conjugate(goxq_)) = imag-part(goxp_) * real-part(goxq_) - real-part(goxp_) * imag-part(goxq_))
- `cc-deriv-on-of-is-diff-on` — forall([drou_, drof_, droa_, drol_], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, drou_, drof_, droa_, drol_) implies deriv-on(cc-normed-field, drou_, drof_, droa_) = drol_)
- `cc-diff-at-cc-mul` — forall([cv in cc, g in fun(rr, cc), pd in fun(rr, cc), tv_ in rr, lv in cc], forall([x_ in rr], pd(x_) = cv * g(x_)) implies is-cc-diff-at(g, tv_, lv) implies is-cc-diff-at(pd, tv_, cv * lv))
- `cc-diff-at-chain-real` — forall([g in fun(rr, cc), h in fun(rr, rr), cm in fun(rr, cc), tv_ in rr, kv in rr, lv in cc], forall([x_ in rr], cm(x_) = g(h(x_))) implies is-diff-at(h, tv_, kv) implies is-cc-diff-at(g, h(tv_), lv) implies is-cc-diff-at(cm, tv_, kv * lv))
- `cc-diff-at-const` — forall([cv in cc, g in fun(rr, cc), tv_ in rr], forall([x_ in rr], g(x_) = cv) implies is-cc-diff-at(g, tv_, 0))
- `cc-diff-at-difference` — forall([g in fun(rr, cc), h in fun(rr, cc), sb_ in fun(rr, cc), tv_ in rr, lv in cc, mv in cc], forall([x_ in rr], sb_(x_) = g(x_) - h(x_)) implies is-cc-diff-at(g, tv_, lv) implies is-cc-diff-at(h, tv_, mv) implies is-cc-diff-at(sb_, tv_, lv - mv))
- `cc-diff-at-iff-coords` — forall([g in fun(rr, cc), gr in fun(rr, rr), gi in fun(rr, rr), tv_ in rr, lv in cc], forall([x_ in rr], real-part(g(x_)) = gr(x_)) implies forall([x_ in rr], imag-part(g(x_)) = gi(x_)) implies is-cc-diff-at(g, tv_, lv) iff is-diff-at(gr, tv_, real-part(lv)) and is-diff-at(gi, tv_, imag-part(lv)))
- `cc-diff-at-implies-continuous` — forall([g in fun(rr, cc), tv_ in rr, lv in cc], is-cc-diff-at(g, tv_, lv) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, cc-ms, g, tv_))
- `cc-diff-at-product` — forall([g in fun(rr, cc), h in fun(rr, cc), pr_ in fun(rr, cc), tv_ in rr, lv in cc, mv in cc], forall([x_ in rr], pr_(x_) = g(x_) * h(x_)) implies is-cc-diff-at(g, tv_, lv) implies is-cc-diff-at(h, tv_, mv) implies is-cc-diff-at(pr_, tv_, lv * h(tv_) + g(tv_) * mv))
- `cc-diff-at-real-mul` — forall([c_ in rr, g in fun(rr, cc), pd in fun(rr, cc), tv_ in rr, lv in cc], forall([x_ in rr], pd(x_) = c_ * g(x_)) implies is-cc-diff-at(g, tv_, lv) implies is-cc-diff-at(pd, tv_, c_ * lv))
- `cc-diff-at-sum` — forall([g in fun(rr, cc), h in fun(rr, cc), sm in fun(rr, cc), tv_ in rr, lv in cc, mv in cc], forall([x_ in rr], sm(x_) = g(x_) + h(x_)) implies is-cc-diff-at(g, tv_, lv) implies is-cc-diff-at(h, tv_, mv) implies is-cc-diff-at(sm, tv_, lv + mv))
- `cc-diff-at-unique` — forall([g in fun(rr, cc), tv_ in rr, lv in cc, mv in cc], is-cc-diff-at(g, tv_, lv) implies is-cc-diff-at(g, tv_, mv) implies lv = mv)
- `cc-diff-on-local` — forall([cexu_], is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), cexu_) implies forall([cexf_ in fun(cexu_, cc), cexv_], cexv_ subset cexu_ implies forall([cexg_, cexa_, cexl_], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cexv_, cexg_, cexa_, cexl_) implies forall([cexx_ in cexv_], cexf_(cexx_) = cexg_(cexx_)) implies is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cexu_, cexf_, cexa_, cexl_))))
- `cc-diff-on-local-quadratic` — forall([gdls_], is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), gdls_) implies forall([gdlf_ in fun(gdls_, cc), gdla_ in gdls_, gdll_ in cc, gdlm_ in rr], 0 <= gdlm_ implies forall([gdlr_], pos-rr(gdlr_) implies forall([gdlx_ in gdls_], magnitude(gdlx_ - gdla_) < gdlr_ implies magnitude(gdlf_(gdlx_) - gdlf_(gdla_) - (gdlx_ - gdla_) * gdll_) <= gdlm_ * (magnitude(gdlx_ - gdla_) * magnitude(gdlx_ - gdla_))) implies is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, gdls_, gdlf_, gdla_, gdll_))))
- `cc-diff-on-of-eps-bound` — forall([goxs_], is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), goxs_) implies forall([goxg_ in fun(goxs_, cc), goxa_ in goxs_, goxl_ in cc], forall([goxe_], pos-rr(goxe_) implies forsome([goxd_], pos-rr(goxd_) and forall([goxy_ in goxs_], magnitude(goxy_ - goxa_) < goxd_ implies magnitude(goxg_(goxy_) - goxg_(goxa_) - (goxy_ - goxa_) * goxl_) <= goxe_ * magnitude(goxy_ - goxa_)))) implies is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, goxs_, goxg_, goxa_, goxl_)))
- `cc-diff-on-quadratic` — forall([cpds_], is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), cpds_) implies forall([cpdf_ in fun(cpds_, cc), cpda_ in cpds_, cpdl_ in cc, cpdm_ in rr], 0 <= cpdm_ implies forall([cpdx_ in cpds_], magnitude(cpdf_(cpdx_) - cpdf_(cpda_) - (cpdx_ - cpda_) * cpdl_) <= cpdm_ * (magnitude(cpdx_ - cpda_) * magnitude(cpdx_ - cpda_))) implies is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cpds_, cpdf_, cpda_, cpdl_)))
- `cc-diff-on-restrict-rr` — forall([cxsf_, cxsg_, cxsa_, cxsl_], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, cxsf_, cxsa_, cxsl_) implies cxsg_ in fun(rr, rr) implies cxsa_ in rr implies cxsl_ in rr implies forall([cxsv_ in rr], cxsg_(cxsv_) = cxsf_(cxsv_)) implies is-diff-at(cxsg_, cxsa_, cxsl_))
- `cc-diff-on-scale` — forall([cexu_, cexf_, cexa_, cexl_], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cexu_, cexf_, cexa_, cexl_) implies forall([cexc_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cexu_, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cexu_, cexc_ * cexf_(cexy_)), cexa_, cexc_ * cexl_)))
- `cc-dist-le-coords` — forall([z in cc, w in cc], magnitude(z - w) <= abs(real-part(z) - real-part(w)) + abs(imag-part(z) - imag-part(w)))
- `cc-exp-add` — forall([cexz_ in cc, cexw_ in cc], cc-exp(cexz_ + cexw_) = cc-exp(cexz_) * cc-exp(cexw_))
- `cc-exp-as-power-series` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cek_ in nn], cf(cek_) = recip(factorial(cek_))) implies forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-series-converges(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, recip(factorial(cek_)) * cexz_ ^ cek_)) and cc-exp(cexz_) = cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * cexz_ ^ cpsk_))))
- `cc-exp-bijection-open-strip` — vnb-lambda(cxsz_, {cxsq_ in cc: -pi < imag-part(cxsq_) and imag-part(cxsq_) < pi}, cc-exp(cxsz_)) in bijection({cxsq_ in cc: -pi < imag-part(cxsq_) and imag-part(cxsq_) < pi}, {cxsu_ in cc: not(imag-part(cxsu_) = 0 and real-part(cxsu_) <= 0)})
- `cc-exp-bijection-strip` — vnb-lambda(cxsz_, {cxsq_ in cc: -pi <= imag-part(cxsq_) and imag-part(cxsq_) < pi}, cc-exp(cxsz_)) in bijection({cxsq_ in cc: -pi <= imag-part(cxsq_) and imag-part(cxsq_) < pi}, difference(cc, singleton(0)))
- `cc-exp-conj` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-exp(conjugate(cexz_)) = conjugate(cc-exp(cexz_)))
- `cc-exp-continuous` — forall([cexz_ in cc], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), cc), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cc-exp(cexy_)), cexz_))
- `cc-exp-converges` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-series-converges(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, recip(factorial(cek_)) * cexz_ ^ cek_)) and cc-exp(cexz_) in cc)
- `cc-exp-deriv` — forall([cexz_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cc-exp(cexy_)), cexz_, cc-exp(cexz_)))
- `cc-exp-holomorphic` — holomorphic-on(cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cc-exp(cexy_)))
- `cc-exp-i` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-exp(1i * cexz_) = cc-cos(cexz_) + 1i * cc-sin(cexz_))
- `cc-exp-i-pi` — cc-exp(1i * pi) = -1
- `cc-exp-imaginary-period-exists` — forsome([cekt_ in rr], 0 < cekt_ and cekt_ < 8 and cc-exp(1i * cekt_) = 1)
- `cc-exp-injective-strip` — forall([cekz_ in cc, cekw_ in cc], -pi <= imag-part(cekz_) implies imag-part(cekz_) < pi implies -pi <= imag-part(cekw_) implies imag-part(cekw_) < pi implies cc-exp(cekz_) = cc-exp(cekw_) implies cekz_ = cekw_)
- `cc-exp-kernel-add` — forall([cekz_ in cc, cekw_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 implies cc-exp(cekw_) = 1 implies cc-exp(cekz_ + cekw_) = 1)
- `cc-exp-kernel-discrete` — forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 implies magnitude(cekz_) < 1/2 implies cekz_ = 0)
- `cc-exp-kernel-generator` — forsome([cekg_ in cc], 0 < imag-part(cekg_) and forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * cekg_)))
- `cc-exp-kernel-generator-unique` — forall([cekg_ in cc], 0 < imag-part(cekg_) implies forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * cekg_)) implies cekg_ = (2 * pi) * 1i)
- `cc-exp-kernel-imaginary` — forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 implies real-part(cekz_) = 0)
- `cc-exp-kernel-neg` — forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 implies cc-exp(-cekz_) = 1)
- `cc-exp-kernel-nontrivial` — forsome([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 and not(cekz_ = 0))
- `cc-exp-kernel-on-imaginary-axis` — forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 implies cekz_ = 1i * imag-part(cekz_))
- `cc-exp-kernel-sub` — forall([cekz_ in cc, cekw_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 implies cc-exp(cekw_) = 1 implies cc-exp(cekz_ - cekw_) = 1)
- `cc-exp-kernel-zz-multiple` — forall([cekw_ in cc], cc-exp(cekw_) = 1 implies forall([cekk_ in zz], cc-exp(cekk_ * cekw_) = 1))
- `cc-exp-least-period` — forsome([ceka_ in rr], 0 < ceka_ and cc-exp(1i * ceka_) = 1 and forall([cekt_ in rr], 0 < cekt_ implies cc-exp(1i * cekt_) = 1 implies ceka_ <= cekt_))
- `cc-exp-log` — forall([clgw_ in cc], not(imag-part(clgw_) = 0 and real-part(clgw_) <= 0) implies cc-exp(cc-log(clgw_)) = clgw_)
- `cc-exp-magnitude-imaginary` — forall([cext_ in rr], magnitude(cc-exp(1i * cext_)) = 1)
- `cc-exp-maps-to-nonzero` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-exp(cexz_) in difference(cc, singleton(0)))
- `cc-exp-near-one` — forall([cekz_ in cc], magnitude(cekz_) <= 1 implies magnitude(cc-exp(cekz_) - 1 - cekz_) <= magnitude(cekz_) ^ 2)
- `cc-exp-neg` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-exp(-cexz_) = recip(cc-exp(cexz_)))
- `cc-exp-nonzero` — forall([cexz_ in cc], not(cc-exp(cexz_) = 0))
- `cc-exp-one-iff` — forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * ((2 * pi) * 1i)))
- `cc-exp-open-strip-avoids-negatives` — forall([cxsz_ in cc], -pi < imag-part(cxsz_) implies imag-part(cxsz_) < pi implies not(imag-part(cc-exp(cxsz_)) = 0 and real-part(cc-exp(cxsz_)) <= 0))
- `cc-exp-open-strip-onto` — forall([cxsw_ in cc], not(imag-part(cxsw_) = 0 and real-part(cxsw_) <= 0) implies forsome([cxsz_ in cc], -pi < imag-part(cxsz_) and imag-part(cxsz_) < pi and cc-exp(cxsz_) = cxsw_))
- `cc-exp-radius` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cek_ in nn], cf(cek_) = recip(factorial(cek_))) implies cps-radius(cf) = pos-inf)
- `cc-exp-real` — forall([cext_ in rr], cc-exp(cext_) in rr and 0 < cc-exp(cext_))
- `cc-exp-real-continuous-rr` — vnb-lambda(cxsv_, rr, cc-exp(cxsv_)) in fun(rr, rr) and forall([cxsx_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(cxsv_, rr, cc-exp(cxsv_)), cxsx_))
- `cc-exp-real-deriv` — forall([cxst_ in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(cxsv_, rr, cc-exp(cxsv_)), cxst_, cc-exp(cxst_)))
- `cc-exp-real-gt-one` — forall([cekt_ in rr], 0 < cekt_ implies 1 < cc-exp(cekt_))
- `cc-exp-real-is-r-exp` — forall([cxst_ in rr], cc-exp(cxst_) = r-exp(cxst_))
- `cc-exp-real-one-iff` — forall([cekt_ in rr], cc-exp(cekt_) = 1 iff cekt_ = 0)
- `cc-exp-real-surjective-pos` — forall([cxsr_ in rr], 0 < cxsr_ implies forsome([cxss_ in rr], cc-exp(cxss_) = cxsr_))
- `cc-exp-real-valued` — forall([cext_ in rr], cc-exp(cext_) in rr)
- `cc-exp-scaled-deriv` — forall([cexc_ in cc, cexz_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cc-exp(cexc_ * cexy_)), cexz_, cexc_ * cc-exp(cexc_ * cexz_)))
- `cc-exp-shift` — forall([cexw_ in cc, cexz_ in cc], cc-exp(cexz_) * cc-exp(cexw_ - cexz_) = cc-exp(cexw_))
- `cc-exp-surjective-strip` — forall([cxsw_ in cc], not(cxsw_ = 0) implies forsome([cxsz_ in cc], -pi <= imag-part(cxsz_) and imag-part(cxsz_) < pi and cc-exp(cxsz_) = cxsw_))
- `cc-exp-tail` — forall([cekz_ in cc, cekm_ in nn], 2 * magnitude(cekz_) <= succ(cekm_) implies magnitude(cc-exp(cekz_) - cc-series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, recip(factorial(cek_)) * cekz_ ^ cek_), cekm_)) <= 2 * (recip(factorial(cekm_)) * magnitude(cekz_) ^ cekm_))
- `cc-exp-two-pi-i` — cc-exp((2 * pi) * 1i) = 1
- `cc-exp-unfold` — forall([cexz_], cc-exp(cexz_) == cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, recip(factorial(cek_)) * cexz_ ^ cek_)))
- `cc-exp-zero` — cc-exp(0) = 1
- `cc-geometric-alternating` — forall([algg_ in fun(nn, cc)], forall([algj_ in nn], algg_(algj_) = -1 ^ algj_) implies forall([algz_ in cc], magnitude(algz_) < 1 implies cc-series-converges(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algg_(cpsk_) * algz_ ^ cpsk_)) and cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algg_(cpsk_) * algz_ ^ cpsk_)) = recip(1 + algz_)))
- `cc-im-add` — forall([z in cc, w in cc], imag-part(z + w) = imag-part(z) + imag-part(w))
- `cc-im-dist-le` — forall([z in cc, w in cc], abs(imag-part(z) - imag-part(w)) <= magnitude(z - w))
- `cc-im-mul` — forall([z in cc, w in cc], imag-part(z * w) = real-part(z) * imag-part(w) + imag-part(z) * real-part(w))
- `cc-im-real-mul` — forall([c_ in rr, z in cc], imag-part(c_ * z) = c_ * imag-part(z))
- `cc-im-sub` — forall([z_, w_], z_ in cc implies w_ in cc implies imag-part(z_ - w_) = imag-part(z_) - imag-part(w_))
- `cc-int-abs-bound` — forall([pphi_, pwf_, paw_, pabs_, pbh_, a, b], pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(pbh_, pabs_, a, b) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pabs_(pay_) == magnitude(pphi_(pay_))) implies magnitude(cc-int(pphi_, a, b)) <= pw-int(pabs_, a, b))
- `cc-int-adjacent` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([pcv_ in ooint(a, b), pphi_, pwf_, paw_], pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies cc-int(pphi_, a, b) = cc-int(restrict(pphi_, ccint(a, pcv_)), a, pcv_) + cc-int(restrict(pphi_, ccint(pcv_, b)), pcv_, b)))
