- `cc-int-affine-subst` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies lrpc_ < lrpd_ implies forall([pphi_, lrpb_], pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies lrpb_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), cc) implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrpb_(lrpz_) == lrpl_ * pphi_(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies forall([pwf_, paw_], is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies cc-int(lrpb_, lrpc_, lrpd_) = cc-int(pphi_, a, b))))
- `cc-int-complex-mul` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([pphi_, pchi_, pcw_ in cc], pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies pchi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pchi_(pay_) == pcw_ * pphi_(pay_)) implies forall([pwf_, paw_], is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies cc-int(pchi_, a, b) = pcw_ * cc-int(pphi_, a, b))))
- `cc-int-congruence` — forall([pphi_, ppsi_, a, b, lig1_, lig2_], is-primitive(lig1_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(lig2_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies ppsi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([liy_ in ccint(a, b)], pphi_(liy_) == ppsi_(liy_)) implies cc-int(pphi_, a, b) = cc-int(ppsi_, a, b))
- `cc-int-in-cc` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([pphi_, pwf_, paw_], is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies cc-int(pphi_, a, b) in cc))
- `cc-int-real-mul` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([pphi_, pchi_, pcc_ in rr], pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies pchi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pchi_(pay_) == pcc_ * pphi_(pay_)) implies forall([pwf_, paw_], is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies cc-int(pchi_, a, b) = pcc_ * cc-int(pphi_, a, b))))
- `cc-int-reflect` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([pphi_, prb_], pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies prb_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prb_(pay_) == pphi_(a + b - pay_)) implies forall([pwf_, paw_], is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies cc-int(prb_, a, b) = cc-int(pphi_, a, b))))
- `cc-int-sum` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([pphi_, ppsi_, pchi_], pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies ppsi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies pchi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pchi_(pay_) == pphi_(pay_) + ppsi_(pay_)) implies forall([pwf_, paw_, pauh_, pbw_], is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(pauh_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(ppsi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(pbw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(ppsi_(pat_))), a, b) implies cc-int(pchi_, a, b) = cc-int(pphi_, a, b) + cc-int(ppsi_, a, b))))
- `cc-int-unfold` — forall([pphi_, a, b], cc-int(pphi_, a, b) == pw-int(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) + pw-int(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) * 1i)
- `cc-int-value` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([pphi_, pwf_, paw_], is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pphi_(pat_))), a, b) implies cc-int(pphi_, a, b) = pwf_(b) - pwf_(a) + (paw_(b) - paw_(a)) * 1i))
- `cc-is-metric-space` — is-metric-space(cc-ms)
- `cc-is-normed-field` — is-normed-field(cc-normed-field)
- `cc-limit-lincomb` — forall([s_ in fun(nn, cc), t_ in fun(nn, cc), h in fun(nn, cc), lv in cc, mv in cc, cpdc_ in cc, cpdd_ in cc], forall([cpdj_ in nn], h(cpdj_) = cpdc_ * s_(cpdj_) + cpdd_ * t_(cpdj_)) implies converges-to(cc-ms, s_, lv) implies converges-to(cc-ms, t_, mv) implies converges-to(cc-ms, h, cpdc_ * lv + cpdd_ * mv))
- `cc-limit-magnitude-le` — forall([s_ in fun(nn, cc), lv in cc, bd in rr], converges-to(cc-ms, s_, lv) implies forall([n_ in nn], magnitude(s_(n_)) <= bd) implies magnitude(lv) <= bd)
- `cc-limit-shift` — forall([s_ in fun(nn, cc), h in fun(nn, cc), lv in cc], forall([cpdj_ in nn], h(cpdj_) = s_(succ(cpdj_))) implies converges-to(cc-ms, s_, lv) implies converges-to(cc-ms, h, lv))
- `cc-limit-unique` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), lm1 in cc, lm2 in cc], converges-to(cc-ms, f, lm1) implies converges-to(cc-ms, f, lm2) implies lm1 = lm2)
- `cc-log-char` — forall([clgw_ in cc, clgz_ in cc], -pi < imag-part(clgz_) implies imag-part(clgz_) < pi implies cc-exp(clgz_) = clgw_ implies cc-log(clgw_) = clgz_)
- `cc-log-deriv` — forall([clhb_ in cc], not(imag-part(clhb_) = 0 and real-part(clhb_) <= 0) implies is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, {cxsu_ in cc: not(imag-part(cxsu_) = 0 and real-part(cxsu_) <= 0)}, vnb-lambda(clhw_, {cxsu_ in cc: not(imag-part(cxsu_) = 0 and real-part(cxsu_) <= 0)}, cc-log(clhw_)), clhb_, recip(clhb_)))
- `cc-log-exp` — forall([clgz_ in cc], -pi < imag-part(clgz_) implies imag-part(clgz_) < pi implies cc-log(cc-exp(clgz_)) = clgz_)
- `cc-log-holomorphic` — holomorphic-on({cxsu_ in cc: not(imag-part(cxsu_) = 0 and real-part(cxsu_) <= 0)}, vnb-lambda(clhw_, {cxsu_ in cc: not(imag-part(cxsu_) = 0 and real-part(cxsu_) <= 0)}, cc-log(clhw_)))
- `cc-log-holomorphic-at` — forall([clha_ in cc], not(imag-part(clha_) = 0 and real-part(clha_) <= 0) implies forsome([clhp_ in rr], 0 < clhp_ and ball(cc-ms, clha_, clhp_) subset {cxsu_ in cc: not(imag-part(cxsu_) = 0 and real-part(cxsu_) <= 0)} and holomorphic-on(ball(cc-ms, clha_, clhp_), vnb-lambda(clhx_, ball(cc-ms, clha_, clhp_), cc-log(clhx_)))))
- `cc-log-holomorphic-near-one` — forall([algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ * (1 + pi) <= pi implies holomorphic-on(ball(cc-ms, 1, algr_), vnb-lambda(algz_, ball(cc-ms, 1, algr_), cc-log(algz_))))
- `cc-log-holomorphic-scale` — forall([algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ * (1 + pi) <= pi implies forall([algs_ in rr], 0 < algs_ implies holomorphic-on(ball(cc-ms, algs_, algs_ * algr_), vnb-lambda(algz_, ball(cc-ms, algs_, algs_ * algr_), cc-log(algz_)))))
- `cc-log-im-near-one` — forall([clhd_ in rr], 0 < clhd_ implies forall([clhr_ in rr], 0 < clhr_ implies clhr_ * (1 + pi) <= pi implies clhr_ * (1 + clhd_) <= clhd_ implies forall([clhz_ in ball(cc-ms, 1, clhr_)], not(imag-part(clhz_) = 0 and real-part(clhz_) <= 0) and -clhd_ < imag-part(cc-log(clhz_)) and imag-part(cc-log(clhz_)) < clhd_)))
- `cc-log-in-slit` — forall([clgw_ in cc], not(imag-part(clgw_) = 0 and real-part(clgw_) <= 0) implies cc-log(clgw_) in cc and -pi < imag-part(cc-log(clgw_)) and imag-part(cc-log(clgw_)) < pi)
- `cc-log-mul` — forall([clhu_ in cc], not(imag-part(clhu_) = 0 and real-part(clhu_) <= 0) implies forall([clhv_ in cc], not(imag-part(clhv_) = 0 and real-part(clhv_) <= 0) implies -pi < imag-part(cc-log(clhu_)) + imag-part(cc-log(clhv_)) implies imag-part(cc-log(clhu_)) + imag-part(cc-log(clhv_)) < pi implies cc-log(clhu_ * clhv_) = cc-log(clhu_) + cc-log(clhv_)))
- `cc-log-one` — cc-log(1) = 0
- `cc-log-one-minus` — forall([clhr_ in rr], 0 < clhr_ implies clhr_ * (1 + pi) <= pi implies forall([clhz_ in cc], magnitude(clhz_) < clhr_ implies cc-log(1 - clhz_) = -cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(clhk_, nn, recip-star(clhk_) * clhz_ ^ clhk_))))
- `cc-log-one-plus` — forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc)], algc_(0) = 0 implies forall([algj_ in nn], algc_(succ(algj_)) = -1 ^ algj_ * recip(succ(algj_))) implies forall([algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ * (1 + pi) <= pi implies forall([algz_ in cc], magnitude(algz_) < algr_ implies cc-log(1 + algz_) = cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * algz_ ^ cpsk_)))))
- `cc-log-polar` — forall([clgr_ in rr, clgth_ in rr], 0 < clgr_ implies -pi < clgth_ implies clgth_ < pi implies cc-log(clgr_ * cc-exp(1i * clgth_)) = log(clgr_) + 1i * clgth_)
- `cc-log-real` — forall([clgt_ in rr], 0 < clgt_ implies cc-log(clgt_) = log(clgt_))
- `cc-log-right-half` — forall([clhw_ in cc], 0 < real-part(clhw_) implies -(1/2 * pi) < imag-part(cc-log(clhw_)) and imag-part(cc-log(clhw_)) < 1/2 * pi)
- `cc-log-rotate` — forall([clhw_ in cc], 0 < real-part(clhw_) implies cc-log(1i * clhw_) = cc-log(clhw_) + 1i * (1/2 * pi))
- `cc-log-rotate-neg` — forall([clhw_ in cc], 0 < real-part(clhw_) implies cc-log(-1i * clhw_) = cc-log(clhw_) - 1i * (1/2 * pi))
- `cc-log-scale` — forall([clgs_ in rr], 0 < clgs_ implies forall([clgw_ in cc], not(imag-part(clgw_) = 0 and real-part(clgw_) <= 0) implies cc-log(clgs_ * clgw_) = log(clgs_) + cc-log(clgw_)))
- `cc-log-spec` — forall([clgw_ in cc], not(imag-part(clgw_) = 0 and real-part(clgw_) <= 0) implies cc-log(clgw_) in cc and -pi < imag-part(cc-log(clgw_)) and imag-part(cc-log(clgw_)) < pi and cc-exp(cc-log(clgw_)) = clgw_)
- `cc-log-unique` — forall([clga_ in cc, clgb_ in cc], -pi < imag-part(clga_) implies imag-part(clga_) < pi implies -pi < imag-part(clgb_) implies imag-part(clgb_) < pi implies cc-exp(clga_) = cc-exp(clgb_) implies clga_ = clgb_)
- `cc-magnitude-closed` — forall([z in cc], magnitude(z) in rr)
- `cc-magnitude-conjugate` — forall([cexz_ in cc], magnitude(conjugate(cexz_)) = magnitude(cexz_))
- `cc-magnitude-le-add` — forall([p_ in cc, q_ in cc], magnitude(p_) <= magnitude(p_ - q_) + magnitude(q_))
- `cc-magnitude-le-re-im` — forall([z_ in cc], magnitude(z_) <= abs(real-part(z_)) + abs(imag-part(z_)))
- `cc-magnitude-mul` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies magnitude(a * b) = magnitude(a) * magnitude(b))
- `cc-magnitude-neg` — forall([z in cc], magnitude(-z) = magnitude(z))
- `cc-magnitude-nonneg` — forall([z in cc], 0 <= magnitude(z))
- `cc-magnitude-power` — forall([cpsk_ in nn, cpsz_ in cc], magnitude(cpsz_ ^ cpsk_) = magnitude(cpsz_) ^ cpsk_)
- `cc-magnitude-sq` — forall([cqz_ in cc], magnitude(cqz_) * magnitude(cqz_) = real-part(cqz_) * real-part(cqz_) + imag-part(cqz_) * imag-part(cqz_))
- `cc-magnitude-sq-add-le` — forall([p_ in cc, q_ in cc], magnitude(p_ + q_) * magnitude(p_ + q_) <= (magnitude(p_) * magnitude(p_) + magnitude(p_) * magnitude(p_)) + (magnitude(q_) * magnitude(q_) + magnitude(q_) * magnitude(q_)))
- `cc-magnitude-sq-conjugate` — forall([cexz_ in cc], magnitude(cexz_) * magnitude(cexz_) = cexz_ * conjugate(cexz_))
- `cc-magnitude-sub-sym` — forall([p_ in cc, q_ in cc], magnitude(p_ - q_) = magnitude(q_ - p_))
- `cc-magnitude-sub-triangle` — forall([p_ in cc, q_ in cc], magnitude(p_ - q_) <= magnitude(p_) + magnitude(q_))
- `cc-magnitude-triangle` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies magnitude(a + b) <= magnitude(a) + magnitude(b))
- `cc-magnitude-zero` — magnitude(0) = 0
- `cc-magnitude-zero-iff` — forall([z in cc], magnitude(z) = 0 iff z = 0)
- `cc-mid-affine` — forall([goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc], cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) = goux_ + 1/2 * (gouy_ - goux_) and cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) = gouy_ + 1/2 * (goux_ - gouy_))
- `cc-mid-coords` — forall([goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc], real-part(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) = 1/2 * (real-part(goux_) + real-part(gouy_)) and imag-part(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) = 1/2 * (imag-part(goux_) + imag-part(gouy_)))
- `cc-mid-in-seg-trace` — forall([goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc], cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) in trace(seg-path(goux_, gouy_), 0, 1) and cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) in trace(seg-path(gouy_, goux_), 0, 1))
- `cc-mid-unfold` — forall([goux_, gouy_], cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) == (goux_ + gouy_) * recip(2))
- `cc-ms-dist` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in cc implies v_ in cc implies (dist(cc-ms))(u_, v_) == magnitude(u_ - v_))
- `cc-ms-open-iff` — forall([hbw_], is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), hbw_) iff is-open(cc-ms, hbw_))
- `cc-mul-nonzero` — forall([ccu_ in cc, ccv_ in cc], not(ccu_ = 0) implies not(ccv_ = 0) implies not(ccu_ * ccv_ = 0))
- `cc-neg-cos-continuous-rr` — vnb-lambda(cekv_, rr, -1 * cc-cos(cekv_)) in fun(rr, rr) and forall([cekx_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(cekv_, rr, -1 * cc-cos(cekv_)), cekx_))
- `cc-nf-add-apply` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in cc implies v_ in cc implies (add(cc-normed-field))(u_, v_) == u_ + v_)
- `cc-nf-carr` — carr(cc-normed-field) == cc
- `cc-nf-fnrm` — fnrm(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, cc, magnitude(x_))
- `cc-nf-fnrm-apply` — forall([u_ in cc], (fnrm(cc-normed-field))(u_) == magnitude(u_))
- `cc-nf-mul-apply` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in cc implies v_ in cc implies (mul(cc-normed-field))(u_, v_) == u_ * v_)
- `cc-nf-neg-apply` — forall([u_ in cc], (neg(cc-normed-field))(u_) == -u_)
- `cc-nf-small-elements` — forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([h in carr(cc-normed-field)], not(h = zero(cc-normed-field)) and (fnrm(cc-normed-field))(h) < eps))
- `cc-nf-sub` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in cc implies v_ in cc implies (add(cc-normed-field))(u_, (neg(cc-normed-field))(v_)) == u_ - v_)
- `cc-nf-zero` — zero(cc-normed-field) == 0
- `cc-nonzero-is-set` — difference(cc, singleton(0)) in set
- `cc-nonzero-membership` — forall([crw_], crw_ in difference(cc, singleton(0)) iff crw_ in cc and not(crw_ = 0))
- `cc-nonzero-open` — is-open(cc-ms, difference(cc, singleton(0)))
- `cc-nonzero-open-nf` — is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), difference(cc, singleton(0)))
- `cc-nonzero-subset` — difference(cc, singleton(0)) subset cc
- `cc-of-pair-add` — forall([a1_ in rr, b1_ in rr, a2_ in rr, b2_ in rr], cc-of-pair([a1_ + a2_, b1_ + b2_]) = cc-of-pair([a1_, b1_]) + cc-of-pair([a2_, b2_]))
- `cc-of-pair-apply` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], cc-of-pair([a, b]) == a + b * 1i)
- `cc-of-pair-in-fun` — cc-of-pair in fun(cartesian(rr, rr), cc)
- `cc-of-pair-is-bijection` — cc-of-pair in bijection(cartesian(rr, rr), cc)
- `cc-of-pair-of-coords` — forall([z in cc], cc-of-pair(cc-coords(z)) = z)
- `cc-of-pair-real-mul` — forall([c_ in rr, a_ in rr, b_ in rr], cc-of-pair([c_ * a_, c_ * b_]) = c_ * cc-of-pair([a_, b_]))
- `cc-open-subset-cc` — forall([u], is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u) implies u subset cc)
- `cc-partial-error-limit` — forall([cpdff_ in fun(nn, cc), cpdgg_ in fun(nn, cc), cpdhh_ in fun(nn, cc), cpdw_ in cc], cc-series-converges(cpdff_) implies cc-series-converges(cpdgg_) implies cc-series-converges(cpdhh_) implies converges-to(cc-ms, vnb-lambda(cpdj_, nn, cc-series-partial-sum(cpdff_, succ(cpdj_)) - cc-series-partial-sum(cpdgg_, succ(cpdj_)) - cpdw_ * cc-series-partial-sum(cpdhh_, cpdj_)), cc-series-limit(cpdff_) - cc-series-limit(cpdgg_) - cpdw_ * cc-series-limit(cpdhh_)))
- `cc-pi-exists` — forsome([cekp_ in rr], 0 < cekp_ and forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * ((2 * cekp_) * 1i))))
- `cc-pi-gt-three` — 3 < pi
- `cc-pi-gt-two` — 2 < pi
- `cc-pi-lt-four` — pi < 4
- `cc-pi-unique` — forall([cekp_, cekq_], cekp_ in rr and 0 < cekp_ and forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * ((2 * cekp_) * 1i))) implies cekq_ in rr and 0 < cekq_ and forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * ((2 * cekq_) * 1i))) implies cekp_ = cekq_)
- `cc-plus-conj-is-2re` — forall([z in cc], z + conjugate(z) = 2 * real-part(z))
- `cc-polar-form` — forall([cxsw_ in cc], not(cxsw_ = 0) implies forsome([cxsr_ in rr, cxsth_ in rr], 0 < cxsr_ and -pi <= cxsth_ and cxsth_ < pi and cxsw_ = cxsr_ * cc-exp(1i * cxsth_)))
- `cc-power-add` — forall([cexn_ in nn, cexm_ in nn, cexz_ in cc], cexz_ ^ (cexm_ + cexn_) = cexz_ ^ cexm_ * cexz_ ^ cexn_)
- `cc-power-diff-bound` — forall([cpdk_ in nn, cpdz_ in cc, cpdu_ in cc, cpdr_ in rr], magnitude(cpdz_) <= cpdr_ implies magnitude(cpdu_) <= cpdr_ implies magnitude(cpdz_ ^ succ(cpdk_) - cpdu_ ^ succ(cpdk_)) <= (succ(cpdk_) * cpdr_ ^ cpdk_) * magnitude(cpdz_ - cpdu_))
- `cc-power-mul` — forall([cexk_ in nn, cexm_ in nn, cexz_ in cc], cexz_ ^ (cexm_ * cexk_) = (cexz_ ^ cexm_) ^ cexk_)
- `cc-power-mul-base` — forall([cexk_ in nn, cexa_ in cc, cexb_ in cc], (cexa_ * cexb_) ^ cexk_ = cexa_ ^ cexk_ * cexb_ ^ cexk_)
- `cc-product-continuous-at` — forall([s, p, q, x], is-continuous-at(s, cc-ms, p, x) implies is-continuous-at(s, cc-ms, q, x) implies is-continuous-at(s, cc-ms, vnb-lambda(msz_, pts(s), p(msz_) * q(msz_)), x))
- `cc-re-add` — forall([z in cc, w in cc], real-part(z + w) = real-part(z) + real-part(w))
- `cc-re-dist-le` — forall([z in cc, w in cc], abs(real-part(z) - real-part(w)) <= magnitude(z - w))
- `cc-re-im-decompose` — forall([z in cc], z = real-part(z) + 1i * imag-part(z))
- `cc-re-im-of` — forall([z in cc, a in rr, b in rr], z = a + b * 1i implies real-part(z) = a and imag-part(z) = b)
- `cc-re-inner-bound` — forall([cqw_ in cc, cqz_ in cc], real-part(cqw_) * real-part(cqz_) + imag-part(cqw_) * imag-part(cqz_) <= magnitude(cqw_) * magnitude(cqz_))
- `cc-re-mul` — forall([z in cc, w in cc], real-part(z * w) = real-part(z) * real-part(w) - imag-part(z) * imag-part(w))
- `cc-re-real-mul` — forall([c_ in rr, z in cc], real-part(c_ * z) = c_ * real-part(z))
- `cc-re-sq-le-mod-sq` — forall([z in cc], real-part(z) * real-part(z) <= z * conjugate(z))
- `cc-re-sub` — forall([z_, w_], z_ in cc implies w_ in cc implies real-part(z_ - w_) = real-part(z_) - real-part(w_))
- `cc-recip-continuous-at` — forall([cra_ in difference(cc, singleton(0))], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), difference(cc, singleton(0))), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), vnb-lambda(crz_, difference(cc, singleton(0)), recip(crz_)), cra_))
- `cc-recip-diff-on` — forall([cra_ in difference(cc, singleton(0))], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, difference(cc, singleton(0)), vnb-lambda(crz_, difference(cc, singleton(0)), recip(crz_)), cra_, -recip(cra_ * cra_)))
- `cc-recip-lam-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(crz_, difference(cc, singleton(0)), recip(crz_)) in fun(difference(cc, singleton(0)), cc)
- `cc-recip-mul` — forall([ccu_ in cc, ccv_ in cc], not(ccu_ = 0) implies not(ccv_ = 0) implies recip(ccu_ * ccv_) = recip(ccu_) * recip(ccv_))
- `cc-series-abs-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsj_ in nn], g(cpsj_) = magnitude(f(cpsj_))) implies series-converges(g) implies cc-series-converges(f))
- `cc-series-block-le` — forall([cpsk_ in nn, cpsm_ in nn, f in fun(nn, cc), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsj_ in nn], g(cpsj_) = magnitude(f(cpsj_))) implies cpsm_ <= cpsk_ implies magnitude(cc-series-partial-sum(f, cpsk_) - cc-series-partial-sum(f, cpsm_)) <= series-partial-sum(g, cpsk_) - series-partial-sum(g, cpsm_))
- `cc-series-even-odd` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), cexfe_ in fun(nn, cc), cexfo_ in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cexj_ in nn], cexfe_(cexj_) = f(2 * cexj_)) implies forall([cexj_ in nn], cexfo_(cexj_) = f(succ(2 * cexj_))) implies cc-series-converges(f) implies cc-series-converges(cexfe_) implies cc-series-converges(cexfo_) implies cc-series-limit(f) = cc-series-limit(cexfe_) + cc-series-limit(cexfo_))
- `cc-series-limit-conjugate` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), g in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cexj_ in nn], g(cexj_) = conjugate(f(cexj_))) implies cc-series-converges(f) implies cc-series-converges(g) and cc-series-limit(g) = conjugate(cc-series-limit(f)))
- `cc-series-limit-converges-to` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc)], cc-series-converges(f) implies cc-series-converges-to(f, cc-series-limit(f)))
- `cc-series-limit-in-cc` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc)], cc-series-converges(f) implies cc-series-limit(f) in cc)
- `cc-series-limit-linear` — forall([f in fun(nn, cc), g in fun(nn, cc), h in fun(nn, cc), cpdc_ in cc, cpdd_ in cc], forall([cpdj_ in nn], h(cpdj_) = cpdc_ * f(cpdj_) + cpdd_ * g(cpdj_)) implies cc-series-converges(f) implies cc-series-converges(g) implies cc-series-converges(h) and cc-series-limit(h) = cpdc_ * cc-series-limit(f) + cpdd_ * cc-series-limit(g))
- `cc-series-partial-sum-in-cc` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, cc)], cc-series-partial-sum(f, k) in cc)
- `cc-series-partial-sum-lincomb` — forall([cpdk_ in nn, f in fun(nn, cc), g in fun(nn, cc), h in fun(nn, cc), cpdc_ in cc, cpdd_ in cc], forall([cpdj_ in nn], h(cpdj_) = cpdc_ * f(cpdj_) + cpdd_ * g(cpdj_)) implies cc-series-partial-sum(h, cpdk_) = cpdc_ * cc-series-partial-sum(f, cpdk_) + cpdd_ * cc-series-partial-sum(g, cpdk_))
- `cc-series-partial-sum-magnitude-le` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, cc), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([j in nn], g(j) = magnitude(f(j))) implies magnitude(cc-series-partial-sum(f, k)) <= series-partial-sum(g, k))
- `cc-series-partial-sum-succ` — forall([f, k in nn], cc-series-partial-sum(f, k) in cc implies f(k) in cc implies cc-series-partial-sum(f, succ(k)) == cc-series-partial-sum(f, k) + f(k))
- `cc-series-partial-sum-unfold` — forall([f, k], cc-series-partial-sum(f, k) == sum-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(cc-normed-field), f, k))
- `cc-series-partial-sum-zero` — forall([f], cc-series-partial-sum(f, 0) == 0)
- `cc-series-ratio-tail` — forall([cekf_ in fun(nn, cc), cekm_ in nn], cc-series-converges(cekf_) implies forall([cekj_ in nn], cekm_ <= cekj_ implies 2 * magnitude(cekf_(succ(cekj_))) <= magnitude(cekf_(cekj_))) implies magnitude(cc-series-limit(cekf_) - cc-series-partial-sum(cekf_, cekm_)) <= 2 * magnitude(cekf_(cekm_)))
- `cc-series-terms-small` — forall([clhf_ in fun(nn, cc)], cc-series-converges(clhf_) implies forall([clhe_], pos-rr(clhe_) implies forsome([clhn_ in nn], forall([clhm_ in nn], clhn_ <= clhm_ implies magnitude(clhf_(clhm_)) <= clhe_))))
- `cc-sin-add` — forall([cexz_ in cc, cexw_ in cc], cc-sin(cexz_ + cexw_) = cc-cos(cexw_) * cc-sin(cexz_) + cc-cos(cexz_) * cc-sin(cexw_))
- `cc-sin-as-power-series` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cek_ in nn], cf(cek_) = -1 ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(succ(2 * cek_)))) implies forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-series-converges(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, (-1 ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(succ(2 * cek_)))) * cexz_ ^ succ(2 * cek_))) and cc-sin(cexz_) = cexz_ * cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * (cexz_ ^ 2) ^ cpsk_))))
- `cc-sin-converges` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-series-converges(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, (-1 ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(succ(2 * cek_)))) * cexz_ ^ succ(2 * cek_))) and cc-sin(cexz_) in cc)
- `cc-sin-deriv` — forall([cexz_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cc-sin(cexy_)), cexz_, cc-cos(cexz_)))
- `cc-sin-exp` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-sin(cexz_) = (cc-exp(1i * cexz_) - cc-exp(-(1i * cexz_))) * recip(2 * 1i))
- `cc-sin-half-pi` — cc-sin(1/2 * pi) = 1
- `cc-sin-holomorphic` — holomorphic-on(cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cc-sin(cexy_)))
- `cc-sin-odd` — forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-sin(-cexz_) = -cc-sin(cexz_))
- `cc-sin-pi` — cc-sin(pi) = 0
- `cc-sin-pos-half` — forall([clht_ in rr], 0 < clht_ implies clht_ < pi implies 0 < cc-sin(clht_))
- `cc-sin-radius` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cek_ in nn], cf(cek_) = -1 ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(succ(2 * cek_)))) implies cps-radius(cf) = pos-inf)
- `cc-sin-real-part` — forall([cekt_ in rr], cc-sin(cekt_) = imag-part(cc-exp(1i * cekt_)))
- `cc-sin-two-pi` — cc-sin(2 * pi) = 0
- `cc-sin-unfold` — forall([cexz_], cc-sin(cexz_) == cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cek_, nn, (-1 ^ cek_ * recip(factorial(succ(2 * cek_)))) * cexz_ ^ succ(2 * cek_))))
- `cc-sub-in-cc` — forall([a, b], a in cc implies b in cc implies a - b in cc)
- `cc-sub-zero-eq` — forall([a, b], a in cc implies b in cc implies a - b = 0 implies a = b)
- `cc-sup-norm-bound` — forall([u, cpsxs_, cpsm_ in rr], 0 <= cpsm_ implies forall([cpsx_ in cpsxs_], u(cpsx_) in cc) implies forall([cpsx_ in cpsxs_], magnitude(u(cpsx_)) <= cpsm_) implies cc-sup-norm(u, cpsxs_) in rr and 0 <= cc-sup-norm(u, cpsxs_) and cc-sup-norm(u, cpsxs_) <= cpsm_)
- `cc-telescoping-limit-bound` — forall([gbs_ in fun(nn, cc), gbc_ in rr], 0 <= gbc_ implies forall([gbk_ in nn], magnitude(gbs_(succ(gbk_)) - gbs_(gbk_)) <= gbc_ * recip(2 ^ gbk_)) implies forsome([gbv_ in cc], converges-to(cc-ms, gbs_, gbv_) and forall([gbn_ in nn], magnitude(gbs_(gbn_) - gbv_) <= (2 * gbc_) * recip(2 ^ gbn_))))
- `ccint-affine-in` — forall([a, b, t_], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies t_ in ccint(0, 1) implies a + (b - a) * t_ in ccint(a, b))
- `ccint-affine-inverse` — forall([a, b, x_], a in rr and b in rr and a < b and x_ in rr implies a + (b - a) * ((x_ - a) * recip(b - a)) = x_)
- `ccint-coaffine-in` — forall([a, b, x_], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies x_ in ccint(a, b) implies (x_ - a) * recip(b - a) in ccint(0, 1))
- `ccint-creep` — forall([a, b, g_], a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies g_ subset rr implies a in g_ implies rr-upper-bound(g_, b) implies forall([t_ in ccint(a, b)], forsome([d_ in rr], 0 < d_ and (forsome([w_ in g_], t_ - d_ < w_) implies forall([y_], y_ in ccint(a, b) and y_ <= t_ + d_ implies y_ in g_)))) implies b in g_)
- `ccint-elt-in-rr` — forall([a, b, x in ccint(a, b)], x in rr)
- `ccint-eps-grid-cover` — forall([av_ in rr, bv_ in rr, ev_], pos-rr(ev_) implies forsome([cv_], is-finite-cover(cv_, ccint(av_, bv_)) and forall([uv_ in cv_, pv_ in uv_, qv_ in uv_], pv_ in rr and qv_ in rr and abs(pv_ - qv_) <= ev_)))
- `ccint-eps-grid-cover-at` — forall([nv_ in nn, av_ in rr, bv_ in rr, ev_], pos-rr(ev_) implies bv_ <= av_ + nv_ * ev_ implies forsome([cv_], is-finite-cover(cv_, ccint(av_, bv_)) and forall([uv_ in cv_, pv_ in uv_, qv_ in uv_], pv_ in rr and qv_ in rr and abs(pv_ - qv_) <= ev_)))
- `ccint-grid-cover-seq` — forall([av_ in rr, bv_ in rr, rd_], forall([kv_ in nn], pos-rr(rd_(kv_))) implies forsome([cw_], forall([kv_ in nn], is-finite-cover(cw_(kv_), ccint(av_, bv_)) and forall([uv_ in cw_(kv_), pv_ in uv_, qv_ in uv_], pv_ in rr and qv_ in rr and abs(pv_ - qv_) <= rd_(kv_)))))
- `ccint-left-approach` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, rqx_ in rr], a < rqx_ implies rqx_ <= b implies forall([rqr_], pos-rr(rqr_) implies forsome([rqt_ in ccint(a, b)], rqx_ - rqr_ < rqt_ and rqt_ < rqx_)))
- `ccint-membership` — forall([a, b, x], x in ccint(a, b) iff x in rr and a <= x and x <= b)
- `ccint-parts` — forall([lo_, hi_, pt_ in ccint(lo_, hi_)], pt_ in rr and lo_ <= pt_ and pt_ <= hi_)
- `ccint-reflect-in` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pay_ in ccint(a, b)], a + b - pay_ in ccint(a, b))
- `ccint-right-approach` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, rqx_ in rr], a <= rqx_ implies rqx_ < b implies forall([rqr_], pos-rr(rqr_) implies forsome([rqt_ in ccint(a, b)], rqx_ < rqt_ and rqt_ < rqx_ + rqr_)))
- `ccint-subset-ccint` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pcv_ in rr, pdv_ in rr], a <= pcv_ implies pdv_ <= b implies ccint(pcv_, pdv_) subset ccint(a, b))
- `ccint-subset-rr` — forall([a, b], ccint(a, b) subset rr)
- `cd-body-unique` — forall([a_, al, be], al in ord and forsome([phi], phi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(al))) and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, al) implies not(forsome([psi], psi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(beta))))) implies be in ord and forsome([phi], phi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(be))) and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, be) implies not(forsome([psi], psi in bijection(a_, ord-segment(beta))))) implies al = be)
- `cd-seg-body` — forall([n_ in nn], n_ in ord and forsome([phi], phi in bijection(ord-segment(n_), ord-segment(n_))) and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, n_) implies not(forsome([psi], psi in bijection(ord-segment(n_), ord-segment(beta))))))
- `cek-exp-gt-one-n` — forall([cekn_ in nn, cekt_ in rr], 0 < cekt_ implies cekt_ <= cekn_ * 1/2 implies 1 < cc-exp(cekt_))
- `cek-exp-gt-one-small` — forall([cekt_ in rr], 0 < cekt_ implies cekt_ <= 1/2 implies 1 < cc-exp(cekt_))
- `cek-half-ratio-partial` — forall([cekn_ in nn, cekg_ in fun(nn, rr), cekm_ in nn], forall([cekj_ in nn], cekm_ <= cekj_ implies 2 * cekg_(succ(cekj_)) <= cekg_(cekj_)) implies series-partial-sum(cekg_, cekm_ + cekn_) - series-partial-sum(cekg_, cekm_) + 2 * cekg_(cekm_ + cekn_) <= 2 * cekg_(cekm_))
- `cek-kernel-nn-multiple` — forall([cekn_ in nn, cekw_ in cc], cc-exp(cekw_) = 1 implies cc-exp(cekn_ * cekw_) = 1)
- `cek-limit-tail-le` — forall([cekts_ in fun(nn, cc), ceklv_ in cc, cekc_ in cc, cekbd_ in rr, cekm_ in nn], converges-to(cc-ms, cekts_, ceklv_) implies forall([cekn_ in nn], cekm_ <= cekn_ implies magnitude(cekts_(cekn_) - cekc_) <= cekbd_) implies magnitude(ceklv_ - cekc_) <= cekbd_)
- `cek-period-ge-half` — forall([cekt_ in rr], 0 < cekt_ implies cc-exp(1i * cekt_) = 1 implies 1/2 <= cekt_)
- `cek-period-multiple-nn` — forall([cekn_ in nn, ceka_ in rr], 0 < ceka_ implies cc-exp(1i * ceka_) = 1 implies forall([cekt_ in rr], 0 < cekt_ implies cc-exp(1i * cekt_) = 1 implies ceka_ <= cekt_) implies forall([cekr_ in rr], 0 <= cekr_ implies cekr_ < cekn_ * ceka_ implies cc-exp(1i * cekr_) = 1 implies forsome([cekk_ in nn], cekr_ = cekk_ * ceka_)))
- `cek-period-multiple-zz` — forall([ceka_ in rr], 0 < ceka_ implies cc-exp(1i * ceka_) = 1 implies forall([cekt_ in rr], 0 < cekt_ implies cc-exp(1i * cekt_) = 1 implies ceka_ <= cekt_) implies forall([cekr_ in rr], cc-exp(1i * cekr_) = 1 implies forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekr_ = cekk_ * ceka_)))
- `cek-pi-le` — forall([cekp_, cekq_], cekp_ in rr and 0 < cekp_ and forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * ((2 * cekp_) * 1i))) implies cekq_ in rr and 0 < cekq_ and forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * ((2 * cekq_) * 1i))) implies cekq_ <= cekp_)
- `centre-set-contains-choice` — forall([s, r, f, u], u in f and forsome([c], c in centres(s, u, r)) implies choice(centres(s, u, r)) in centre-set(s, r, f))
- `centre-set-finite-guarded` — forall([s, r, f in set], card(f) in nn implies card(centre-set(s, r, f)) in nn)
- `centre-set-unfold` — forall([s, r, f], centre-set(s, r, f) == image(vnb-lambda(b, f, choice(centres(s, b, r))), f))
- `centres-ball-eq` — forall([s, b, r, c in centres(s, b, r)], ball(s, c, r) = b)
- `centres-in-carrier` — forall([s, b, r, c in centres(s, b, r)], c in pts(s))
- `centres-mem-build` — forall([s, b, r, c in pts(s)], ball(s, c, r) = b implies c in centres(s, b, r))
- `centres-unfold` — forall([s, b, r], centres(s, b, r) == {c in pts(s): ball(s, c, r) = b})
- `cex-exp-comb-deriv` — forall([cexa_ in cc, cexb_ in cc, cexc_ in cc, cexd_ in cc, cexz_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cexa_ * cc-exp(cexc_ * cexy_) + cexb_ * cc-exp(cexd_ * cexy_)), cexz_, cexa_ * (cexc_ * cc-exp(cexc_ * cexz_)) + cexb_ * (cexd_ * cc-exp(cexd_ * cexz_))))
- `cex-exp-series-real-converges` — forall([cext_ in rr], 0 < cext_ implies series-converges(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, recip(factorial(cpsk_)) * cext_ ^ cpsk_)))
- `cex-lin-deriv` — forall([cexc_ in cc, cexz_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cexc_ * cexy_), cexz_, cexc_))
- `cex-shift-deriv-zero` — forall([cexw_ in cc, cexz_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cc-exp(cexy_) * cc-exp(cexw_ - cexy_)), cexz_, 0))
- `cex-sign-magnitude` — forall([cexk_ in nn, cexa_ in rr], 0 <= cexa_ implies magnitude(-1 ^ cexk_ * cexa_) = cexa_)
- `cex-sub-deriv` — forall([cexw_ in cc, cexz_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, vnb-lambda(cexy_, cc, cexw_ - cexy_), cexz_, -1))
- `change-of-variable-antiderivable` — forall([phi, amap, ader, p_, q_], amap in fun(rr, rr) implies ader in fun(rr, rr) implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivable(phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, amap, u_)) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies is-diff-at(amap, w_, ader(w_))) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], amap(u_) in ccint(amap(p_), amap(q_))) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies amap(p_) < amap(w_) and amap(w_) < amap(q_)) implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(amap(x)) * ader(x)), p_, q_))
- `change-of-variable-antiderivative` — forall([fw, phi, amap, ader, p_, q_], amap in fun(rr, rr) implies ader in fun(rr, rr) implies p_ in rr implies q_ in rr implies p_ < q_ implies is-antiderivative(fw, phi, amap(p_), amap(q_)) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, amap, u_)) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies is-diff-at(amap, w_, ader(w_))) implies forall([u_ in ccint(p_, q_)], amap(u_) in ccint(amap(p_), amap(q_))) implies forall([w_], w_ in rr and p_ < w_ and w_ < q_ implies amap(p_) < amap(w_) and amap(w_) < amap(q_)) implies is-antiderivative(compose(fw, amap), vnb-lambda(x, rr, phi(amap(x)) * ader(x)), p_, q_))
- `choose-0-succ` — forall([k in nn], choose(0, succ(k)) = 0)
- `choose-card-unfold` — forall([n_, m_], card(choose-set(n_, m_)) == choose(n_, m_))
- `choose-in-nn` — forall([n in nn, m in nn], choose(n, m) in nn)
- `choose-n-0` — forall([n in nn], choose(n, 0) = succ(0))
- `choose-set-avoid-in` — forall([n_ in nn, m_, a_], a_ in choose-set(succ(n_), m_) and not(n_ in a_) implies a_ in choose-set(n_, m_))
- `choose-set-avoids-point` — forall([n_ in nn, m_, a_ in choose-set(n_, m_)], not(n_ in a_))
- `choose-set-insert-in` — forall([n_ in nn, k_ in nn, s_ in choose-set(n_, k_)], union(s_, pair(n_, n_)) in choose-set(succ(n_), succ(k_)))
- `choose-set-insert-injective` — forall([n_ in nn, m_, s_ in choose-set(n_, m_), t_ in choose-set(n_, m_)], union(s_, pair(n_, n_)) = union(t_, pair(n_, n_)) implies s_ = t_)
- `choose-set-mem-iff` — forall([n_, m_, a_], a_ in choose-set(n_, m_) iff a_ in power(ord-segment(n_)) and card(a_) = m_)
- `choose-set-remove-in` — forall([n_ in nn, k_ in nn, a_], a_ in choose-set(succ(n_), succ(k_)) and n_ in a_ implies difference(a_, pair(n_, n_)) in choose-set(n_, k_))
- `choose-set-remove-restores` — forall([n_ in set, a_], a_ in set and n_ in a_ implies union(difference(a_, pair(n_, n_)), pair(n_, n_)) = a_)
- `choose-set-split` — forall([n_ in nn, k_ in nn], choose-set(succ(n_), succ(k_)) = union(choose-set(n_, succ(k_)), image(vnb-lambda(s_, choose-set(n_, k_), union(s_, pair(n_, n_))), choose-set(n_, k_))))
- `choose-set-split-disjoint` — forall([n_ in nn, k_ in nn], intersection(choose-set(n_, succ(k_)), image(vnb-lambda(s_, choose-set(n_, k_), union(s_, pair(n_, n_))), choose-set(n_, k_))) = empty-set)
- `choose-set-unfold` — forall([n_, m_], choose-set(n_, m_) == {a in power(ord-segment(n_)): card(a) = m_})
- `choose-set-widen` — forall([n_ in nn, m_, a_ in choose-set(n_, m_)], a_ in choose-set(succ(n_), m_))
- `choose-succ` — forall([n in nn, k in nn], choose(succ(n), succ(k)) = choose(n, k) + choose(n, succ(k)))
- `chosen-centre-is-centre` — forall([s, r, u in ball-cover(s, r)], choice(centres(s, u, r)) in pts(s) and ball(s, choice(centres(s, u, r)), r) = u)
- `cips-expand-sum` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))((vadd(v))(x_, y_), (vadd(v))(x_, y_)) = ((ip(v))(x_, x_) + (ip(v))(x_, y_)) + (conjugate((ip(v))(x_, y_)) + (ip(v))(y_, y_))))
- `cips-expand-two` — forall([v, a_, b_, x_, y_], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies a_ in cc implies b_ in cc implies x_ in vec(v) implies y_ in vec(v) implies (ip(v))((vadd(v))((act(v))(a_, x_), (act(v))(b_, y_)), (vadd(v))((act(v))(a_, x_), (act(v))(b_, y_))) = ((a_ * conjugate(a_)) * (ip(v))(x_, x_) + (a_ * conjugate(b_)) * (ip(v))(x_, y_)) + ((b_ * conjugate(a_)) * conjugate((ip(v))(x_, y_)) + (b_ * conjugate(b_)) * (ip(v))(y_, y_)))
- `cips-ip-add-right` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, (vadd(v))(y_, z_)) = (ip(v))(x_, y_) + (ip(v))(x_, z_)))
- `cips-ip-conj-homog-right` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, (act(v))(a_, y_)) = conjugate(a_) * (ip(v))(x_, y_)))
- `cips-ip-neg-left` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))((vneg(v))(x_), z_) = -(ip(v))(x_, z_)))
- `cips-ip-neg-self` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))((vneg(v))(x_), (vneg(v))(x_)) = (ip(v))(x_, x_)))
- `cips-ip-zero-left` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([z_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(vzero(v), z_) = 0))
- `cips-ip-zero-right` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, vzero(v)) = 0))
- `cips-minkowski` — forall([v, x_, y_], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies x_ in vec(v) implies y_ in vec(v) implies ip-norm(v, (vadd(v))(x_, y_)) <= ip-norm(v, x_) + ip-norm(v, y_))
- `cips-schwarz` — forall([v, x_, y_], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies x_ in vec(v) implies y_ in vec(v) implies (ip(v))(x_, y_) * conjugate((ip(v))(x_, y_)) <= (ip(v))(x_, x_) * (ip(v))(y_, y_))
- `cips-vadd-vneg` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (vadd(v))(x_, (vneg(v))(x_)) = vzero(v)))
- `cips-vneg-type` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (vneg(v))(x_) in vec(v)))
- `clamp-compose-continuous-at` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, t_ in rr], a <= b implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z_, rr, f(clamp(a, b, z_))), t_))
- `clamp-compose-fixes` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr, x in rr], a <= x implies x <= b implies (vnb-lambda(z_, rr, f(clamp(a, b, z_))))(x) = f(x))
- `clamp-compose-lam-in-fun` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, b in rr], vnb-lambda(z_, rr, f(clamp(a, b, z_))) in fun(rr, rr))
- `clamp-fixes` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, x in rr], a <= x implies x <= b implies clamp(a, b, x) = x)
- `clamp-in-ccint` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, x in rr], a <= b implies clamp(a, b, x) in ccint(a, b))
- `clamp-in-rr` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, x in rr], clamp(a, b, x) in rr)
- `clamp-left-const` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, x in rr], x <= a implies a <= b implies clamp(a, b, x) = a)
- `clamp-lipschitz` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, y in rr, x in rr], abs(clamp(a, b, y) - clamp(a, b, x)) <= abs(y - x))
- `clamp-right-const` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, x in rr], a <= b implies b <= x implies clamp(a, b, x) = b)
- `class-disjoint` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], class(s, a) = class(s, b) or intersection(class(s, a), class(s, b)) = empty-set))
- `class-eq-iff` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s), b in pts(s)], class(s, a) = class(s, b) iff related(s, a, b)))
- `class-in-quotient` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) in quotient(s)))
- `class-is-set` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) in set))
- `class-min-pivot` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies forsome([i0, j0], i0 in interval(1, m) and j0 in interval(1, n) and not(entry(p, i0, j0) = zero(a))) implies forsome([b], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, b) and not(entry(b, 1, 1) = zero(a)) and forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([i, j], i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, i, j) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(b, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, i, j)))))))
- `class-self` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], a in class(s, a)))
- `class-subset-carrier` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a in pts(s)], class(s, a) subset pts(s)))
- `class-unfold` — forall([s, a], class(s, a) == {b_ in pts(s): related(s, a, b_)})
- `classmin-transport` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, q], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) implies entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, q, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(q, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj))))))
- `clear-col-upto` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forall([k in nn], forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and forall([i in interval(1, m)], not(i = 1) implies i <= k implies entry(q, i, 1) = zero(a)) and forall([c in interval(1, n)], entry(q, 1, c) = entry(p, 1, c))))))
- `clear-first-col` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], m in nn implies n in nn implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and forall([i in interval(1, m)], not(i = 1) implies entry(q, i, 1) = zero(a)) and forall([c in interval(1, n)], entry(q, 1, c) = entry(p, 1, c)))))
- `clear-first-row` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], m in nn implies n in nn implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and forall([j in interval(1, n)], not(j = 1) implies entry(q, 1, j) = zero(a)))))
- `clear-pivot-cross` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([p, q, m], p in nn implies q in nn implies m in mat(succ(p), succ(q), carr(a)) implies forsome([i0, j0], i0 in interval(1, succ(p)) and j0 in interval(1, succ(q)) and not(entry(m, i0, j0) = zero(a))) implies forsome([c2], mat-equiv(a, succ(p), succ(q), m, c2) and c2 = border(a, entry(c2, 1, 1), submat(c2, p, q), p, q) and not(entry(c2, 1, 1) = zero(a)))))
- `clear-row-upto` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forall([k in nn], forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and forall([j in interval(1, n)], not(j = 1) implies j <= k implies entry(q, 1, j) = zero(a))))))
- `closed-ball-center-in` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([cv_ in pts(s), qv_ in rr], 0 <= qv_ implies cv_ in closed-ball(s, cv_, qv_)))
- `closed-ball-in-ball` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c in pts(s), r in rr, q in rr], r < q implies closed-ball(s, c, r) subset ball(s, c, q)))
- `closed-ball-in-carrier` — forall([s, cv_, qv_], closed-ball(s, cv_, qv_) subset pts(s))
- `closed-ball-in-power` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([cv_, qv_], closed-ball(s, cv_, qv_) in power(pts(s))))
- `closed-ball-is-closed` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c in pts(s), r in rr], is-closed(s, closed-ball(s, c, r))))
- `closed-ball-is-set` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c, r], closed-ball(s, c, r) in set))
- `closed-ball-membership` — forall([s, c, r, y], y in closed-ball(s, c, r) iff y in pts(s) and (dist(s))(c, y) <= r)
- `closed-ball-sep-unfold` — forall([s, c, r], closed-ball(s, c, r) == {y in pts(s): (dist(s))(c, y) <= r})
- `closed-limit-in` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], is-closed(s, a) implies forall([f in fun(nn, a), l], converges-to(s, f, l) implies l in a)))
- `closed-preimage-implies-continuous` — forall([s, t, f], is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) implies forall([a], is-closed(t, a) implies is-closed(s, preimage(s, f, a))) implies is-continuous(s, t, f))
- `closed-set-limit-in` — forall([s, a, f, l], is-closed(s, a) implies converges-to(s, f, l) implies forall([n in nn], f(n) in a) implies l in a)
- `closed-set-limit-in-tail` — forall([s, av_, fv_, lv_, nb_], is-closed(s, av_) implies converges-to(s, fv_, lv_) implies nb_ in nn implies forall([n in nn], nb_ <= n implies fv_(n) in av_) implies lv_ in av_)
- `closed-subset-of-compact-is-compact` — forall([s], is-compact(s) implies forall([a], is-closed(s, a) implies is-compact(subspace-ms(s, a))))
- `closure-contains` — forall([s, a], a subset pts(s) implies a subset closure(s, a))
- `closure-in-carrier` — forall([s, a], closure(s, a) subset pts(s))
- `closure-in-closed` — forall([s, a, cst], is-closed(s, cst) implies a subset cst implies closure(s, a) subset cst)
- `closure-is-closed` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], is-closed(s, closure(s, a))))
- `closure-is-set` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], closure(s, a) in set))
- `closure-membership` — forall([s, a, x], x in closure(s, a) iff x in pts(s) and forall([u], is-open(s, u) and x in u implies forsome([y in u], y in a)))
- `closure-of-ball-in-closed-ball` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c in pts(s), r in rr], closure(s, ball(s, c, r)) subset closed-ball(s, c, r)))
- `closure-sep-unfold` — forall([s, a], closure(s, a) == {x in pts(s): forall([u], is-open(s, u) and x in u implies forsome([y in u], y in a))})
- `cluster-point-has-convergent-subseq` — forall([s, f, x], cluster-point(s, f, x) implies forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and converges-to(s, subseq(f, phi), x)))
- `co-eq-le-trans` — forall([a, b, c], a = b implies b <= c implies a <= c)
- `co-eq-lt-trans` — forall([a, b, c], a = b implies b < c implies a < c)
- `co-le-eq-trans` — forall([a, b, c], a <= b implies b = c implies a <= c)
- `co-le-lt-trans` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, c in rr], a <= b implies b < c implies a < c)
- `co-lt-eq-trans` — forall([a, b, c], a < b implies b = c implies a < c)
- `co-lt-le-trans` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, c in rr], a < b implies b <= c implies a < c)
- `co-lt-trans` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, c in rr], a < b implies b < c implies a < c)
- `coint-elt-in-rr` — forall([a, b, osy_ in coint(a, b)], osy_ in rr)
- `coint-left-endpoint` — forall([osx_, osr_], osx_ in rr implies pos-rr(osr_) implies osx_ in coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_))
- `coint-left-limit-point` — forall([osx_, osd_], osx_ in rr implies pos-rr(osd_) implies forall([osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forsome([osb_ in coint(osx_, osx_ + osd_)], not(osb_ = osx_) and abs(osx_ - osb_) <= osr_)))
- `coint-membership` — forall([a, b, osy_], osy_ in coint(a, b) iff osy_ in rr and a <= osy_ and osy_ < b)
- `coint-of-ooint` — forall([osx_, osr_], osx_ in rr implies pos-rr(osr_) implies forall([osy_ in ooint(osx_ - osr_, osx_ + osr_)], osx_ <= osy_ implies osy_ in coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_)))
- `coint-shrink` — forall([osx_, osr_, osv_], osx_ in rr implies pos-rr(osr_) implies pos-rr(osv_) implies osv_ <= osr_ implies coint(osx_, osx_ + osv_) subset coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_))
- `coint-subset-ooint` — forall([osx_, osr_], osx_ in rr implies pos-rr(osr_) implies coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_) subset ooint(osx_ - osr_, osx_ + osr_))
- `coint-subset-rr` — forall([a, b], coint(a, b) subset rr)
- `collapse-at-hi` — forall([k_ in nn, j_ in nn], k_ < j_ implies (collapse-at(k_))(j_) = pred(j_))
- `collapse-at-in-nn` — forall([k_ in nn, j_ in nn], (collapse-at(k_))(j_) in nn)
- `collapse-at-in-seg` — forall([n_, k_, j_], n_ in nn implies k_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies k_ in ord-segment(succ(n_)) implies j_ in ord-segment(succ(n_)) implies not(j_ = k_) implies (collapse-at(k_))(j_) in ord-segment(n_))
- `collapse-at-inj` — forall([k_, a_, b_], k_ in nn implies a_ in nn implies b_ in nn implies not(a_ = k_) implies not(b_ = k_) implies (collapse-at(k_))(a_) = (collapse-at(k_))(b_) implies a_ = b_)
- `collapse-at-lo` — forall([k_ in nn, j_ in nn], not(k_ < j_) implies (collapse-at(k_))(j_) = j_)
- `collapse-at-lt` — forall([n_, k_, j_], n_ in nn implies k_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies k_ <= n_ implies j_ <= n_ implies not(j_ = k_) implies (collapse-at(k_))(j_) < n_)
- `collinear-cases` — forall([goxa_ in cc, goxb_ in cc, goxc_ in cc], imag-part((goxb_ - goxa_) * conjugate(goxc_ - goxa_)) = 0 implies goxc_ in trace(seg-path(goxa_, goxb_), 0, 1) or goxa_ in trace(seg-path(goxb_, goxc_), 0, 1) or goxb_ in trace(seg-path(goxc_, goxa_), 0, 1))
- `comb-kk-0-0` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r)], (comb-kk(r, x, y, 0))(0) = one(r)))
- `comb-kk-above` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r), m in nn, k in zz], m < k implies (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k) = zero(r)))
- `comb-kk-above-ind` — forall([m_ in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r), k_ in zz], m_ < k_ implies (comb-kk(r, x, y, m_))(k_) = zero(r)))
- `comb-kk-in-fun` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r), m in nn], comb-kk(r, x, y, m) in fun(zz, carr(r))))
- `comb-kk-in-fun-ind` — forall([m_ in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r)], comb-kk(r, x, y, m_) in fun(zz, carr(r))))
- `comb-kk-null` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r), m in nn, k in zz], k < 0 implies (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k) = zero(r)))
- `comb-kk-null-ind` — forall([m_ in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r), k_ in zz], k_ < 0 implies (comb-kk(r, x, y, m_))(k_) = zero(r)))
- `comm-monoid-assoc` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `comm-monoid-carrier-closed-opr` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) in carr(s)))
- `comm-monoid-identity-in-carr` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies iden(s) in carr(s))
- `comm-monoid-is-monoid` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies is-monoid(s))
- `comm-monoid-left-id` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(iden(s), a) = a))
- `comm-monoid-opr-comm` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) = (opr(s))(b, a)))
- `comm-monoid-opr-interchange` — forall([s], is-comm-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s), d in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), (opr(s))(c, d)) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, c), (opr(s))(b, d))))
- `commutative-ring-additive-ag-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-commutative-ring(a, b, f) implies is-hom-abelian-group(commutative-ring-additive-ag(a), commutative-ring-additive-ag(b), f))
- `commutative-ring-is-ring` — forall([s], is-commutative-ring(s) implies is-ring(s))
- `commutative-ring-mul-comm` — forall([s], is-commutative-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = (mul(s))(b, a)))
- `commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-commutative-ring(a, b, f) implies is-hom-comm-monoid(commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(a), commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(b), f))
- `compact-countable-product` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([n in nn], is-compact(ms(n))) implies is-compact(product-metric(ms)))
- `compact-iff-cluster-point` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) iff forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s))], forsome([x], cluster-point(s, f, x))))
- `compact-iff-fip` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) iff forall([c], has-fip(s, c) implies forsome([p], p in intersection-of(c))))
- `compact-iff-seq-compact` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) iff seq-compact(s))
- `compact-iff-tb-complete` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) iff totally-bounded(s) and is-complete(s))
- `compact-image` — forall([s, t, fqf_], is-continuous(s, t, fqf_) implies is-compact(s) implies is-compact(subspace-ms(t, image(fqf_, pts(s)))))
- `compact-implies-complete` — forall([s], is-compact(s) implies is-complete(s))
- `compact-implies-fip` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) implies forall([c], has-fip(s, c) implies forsome([p], p in intersection-of(c))))
- `compact-implies-seq-compact` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-compact(s) implies seq-compact(s))
- `compact-implies-totally-bounded` — forall([s], is-compact(s) implies totally-bounded(s))
- `compact-metric-is-separable` — forall([s], is-compact(s) implies forsome([x], x in pts(s)) implies is-separable(s))
- `compact-seq-has-cluster` — forall([s, f], is-compact(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) implies forsome([x], cluster-point(s, f, x)))
- `comparison-test` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n in nn], 0 <= f(n) and f(n) <= g(n)) and series-converges(g) implies series-converges(f))
- `complement-in-big-union` — forall([ioy_, c_, iox_], iox_ in complement-in(ioy_, big-union(iow_, c_, iow_)) iff iox_ in ioy_ and forall([iou_ in c_], not(iox_ in iou_)))
- `complement-in-double` — forall([rcy_, rca_], rca_ subset rcy_ implies complement-in(rcy_, complement-in(rcy_, rca_)) = rca_)
- `complement-in-intersection-of` — forall([ioy_, c_], forsome([iou_], iou_ in c_) implies complement-in(ioy_, intersection-of(c_)) = big-union(iow_, c_, complement-in(ioy_, iow_)))
- `complement-in-subset` — forall([a_, b_], complement-in(b_, a_) subset b_)
- `completion-is-complete` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies is-complete(completion(m)))
- `completion-is-metric-space` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies is-metric-space(completion(m)))
- `complex-inner-product-space-as-module-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-complex-inner-product-space(a, b, f) implies is-hom-module(complex-inner-product-space-as-module(a), complex-inner-product-space-as-module(b), f))
- `compose-apply` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], g in fun(a, b) and f in fun(b, c) implies forall([x in a], (compose(f, g))(x) = f(g(x))))
- `compose-assoc` — forall([a, b, c, u, f, g, h], a in set implies b in set implies f in fun(a, b) implies g in fun(b, c) implies h in fun(c, u) implies compose(h, compose(g, f)) = compose(compose(h, g), f))
- `compose-continuous-at` — forall([g, f, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, f(a)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, compose(g, f), a))
- `compose-id-left` — forall([a, b, f], a in set implies b in set implies f in fun(a, b) implies compose(id-fun(b), f) = f)
- `compose-id-right` — forall([a, b, f], a in set implies f in fun(a, b) implies compose(f, id-fun(a)) = f)
- `compose-type` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], a in set implies g in fun(a, b) and f in fun(b, c) implies compose(f, g) in fun(a, c))
- `compose-type-2` — forall([x, f, g, a], f in fun(x) implies g in fun(ran(f)) implies a in x implies g(f(a)) in ran(g))
- `compose-type-3` — forall([x, f, g, h, a], f in fun(x) implies g in fun(ran(f)) implies h in fun(ran(g)) implies a in x implies h(g(f(a))) in ran(h))
- `compose-type-4` — forall([x, f, g, h, k, a], f in fun(x) implies g in fun(ran(f)) implies h in fun(ran(g)) implies k in fun(ran(h)) implies a in x implies k(h(g(f(a)))) in ran(k))
- `compose-type-5` — forall([x, f, g, h, k, m, a], f in fun(x) implies g in fun(ran(f)) implies h in fun(ran(g)) implies k in fun(ran(h)) implies m in fun(ran(k)) implies a in x implies m(k(h(g(f(a))))) in ran(m))
- `const-continuous-at` — forall([c in rr, a in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, c), a))
- `const-lam-in-fun` — forall([c in rr], vnb-lambda(x, rr, c) in fun(rr, rr))
- `cont-agree-off-pt` — forall([fa, fb, pt in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, fa, pt) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, fb, pt) implies forall([x in rr], not(x = pt) implies fa(x) = fb(x)) implies fa(pt) = fb(pt))
- `cont-on-in-fun` — forall([f, itdm_], is-continuous-on(f, itdm_) implies f in fun(itdm_, rr))
- `cont-on-of-total` — forall([itdm_], itdm_ subset rr implies forall([itg_ in fun(rr, rr)], forall([ity_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, itg_, ity_)) implies forall([f in fun(itdm_, rr)], forall([itz_ in itdm_], f(itz_) = itg_(itz_)) implies is-continuous-on(f, itdm_))))
- `cont-on-transfer-ptwise-eq` — forall([itdm_], itdm_ subset rr implies forall([f, itg_], f in fun(itdm_, rr) implies is-continuous-on(itg_, itdm_) implies forall([itz_ in itdm_], f(itz_) = itg_(itz_)) implies is-continuous-on(f, itdm_)))
- `cont-transfer-ptwise-eq` — forall([f, g, a], f in fun(rr, rr) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, a) implies forall([x in rr], f(x) = g(x)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, a))
- `continuous-abs-bounded-on-ccint` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forsome([m_ in rr], 0 <= m_ and forall([z_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(f(z_)) <= m_)))
- `continuous-at-iff-sequential` — forall([s, t, f, a], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(t) implies f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) implies a in pts(s) implies is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a) iff forall([sq in sqn(pts(s))], converges-to(s, sq, a) implies converges-to(t, compose(f, sq), f(a))))
- `continuous-at-implies-sequential` — forall([s, t, f, a], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(t) implies f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) implies a in pts(s) implies is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a) implies forall([sq in sqn(pts(s))], converges-to(s, sq, a) implies converges-to(t, compose(f, sq), f(a))))
- `continuous-at-left-limit` — forall([f, x], f in fun(rr, rr) implies x in rr implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x) implies is-left-limit(f, x, f(x)))
- `continuous-at-local` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), g in fun(rr, rr), t_ in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, t_) implies forall([x_ in rr], abs(x_ - t_) <= rho implies f(x_) = g(x_)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, t_))
- `continuous-at-right-limit` — forall([f, x], f in fun(rr, rr) implies x in rr implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x) implies is-right-limit(f, x, f(x)))
- `continuous-bounded-above-on-ccint` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forsome([k_ in rr], forall([z_ in ccint(a, b)], f(z_) <= k_)))
- `continuous-image-of-compact` — forall([s, t, fqf_], is-continuous(s, t, fqf_) implies forall([aqa_], aqa_ subset pts(s) implies is-compact(subspace-ms(s, aqa_)) implies is-compact(subspace-ms(t, image(fqf_, aqa_)))))
- `continuous-implies-closed-preimage` — forall([s, t, f], is-continuous(s, t, f) implies forall([a], is-closed(t, a) implies is-closed(s, preimage(s, f, a))))
- `continuous-implies-open-preimage` — forall([s, t, f], is-continuous(s, t, f) implies forall([v], is-open(t, v) implies is-open(s, preimage(s, f, v))))
- `continuous-is-antiderivable` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies is-antiderivable(f, a, b))
- `continuous-is-continuous-at` — forall([s, t, f], is-continuous(s, t, f) implies forall([a in pts(s)], is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a)))
- `continuous-is-regulated` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies is-regulated(f, a, b))
- `continuous-nonneg-left` — forall([g, aa, th], g in fun(rr, rr) implies aa in rr implies th in rr implies aa < th implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, th) implies forall([x], x in rr and aa < x and x < th implies 0 <= g(x)) implies 0 <= g(th))
- `continuous-nonpos-right` — forall([g, th, bb], g in fun(rr, rr) implies th in rr implies bb in rr implies th < bb implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, th) implies forall([x], x in rr and th < x and x < bb implies g(x) <= 0) implies g(th) <= 0)
- `continuous-on-compose-path` — forall([u, f, pgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-path(pgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies is-continuous-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(f(pgam(pat_)))), ccint(a, b)) and is-continuous-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(f(pgam(pat_)))), ccint(a, b)))
- `continuous-on-compose-regulated` — forall([u, f, pgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-path(pgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(f(pgam(pat_)))), a, b) and is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(f(pgam(pat_)))), a, b))
- `continuous-on-implies-regulated-on` — forall([f, a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies a < b implies is-continuous-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies is-regulated-on(f, a, b))
- `continuous-on-left-limit-within` — forall([f, rqw_], rqw_ subset rr implies is-continuous-on(f, rqw_) implies forall([rqx_ in rqw_], is-left-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, f(rqx_))))
- `continuous-on-right-limit-within` — forall([f, rqw_], rqw_ subset rr implies is-continuous-on(f, rqw_) implies forall([rqx_ in rqw_], is-right-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, f(rqx_))))
- `continuous-uniformly-continuous-on-ccint` — forall([f, a, b, eps], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies pos-rr(eps) implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forsome([d_ in rr], 0 < d_ and forall([p_ in ccint(a, b), q_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(p_ - q_) <= d_ implies abs(f(p_) - f(q_)) <= eps)))
- `conv3-avoids-vertex` — forall([goxa_ in cc, goxb_ in cc, goxc_ in cc], not(imag-part((goxb_ - goxa_) * conjugate(goxc_ - goxa_)) = 0) implies forall([goxt_ in rr], 0 < goxt_ implies goxt_ <= 1 implies not(goxa_ in conv3(goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxb_ - goxa_), goxb_, goxc_)) and not(goxa_ in conv3(goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxb_ - goxa_), goxc_, goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxc_ - goxa_)))))
- `conv3-in-convex` — forall([u], is-convex(u) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], goua_ in u implies goub_ in u implies gouc_ in u implies conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) subset u))
- `conv3-intro` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc, goul_ in ccint(0, 1), goum_ in ccint(0, 1), goun_ in ccint(0, 1), gouz_], (goul_ + goum_) + goun_ = 1 implies gouz_ = (goul_ * goua_ + goum_ * goub_) + goun_ * gouc_ implies gouz_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_))
- `conv3-is-convex` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], is-convex(conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_)))
- `conv3-member-iff` — forall([goua_, goub_, gouc_, gouz_], gouz_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) iff gouz_ in cc and forsome([goul_ in ccint(0, 1), goum_ in ccint(0, 1), goun_ in ccint(0, 1)], (goul_ + goum_) + goun_ = 1 and gouz_ = (goul_ * goua_ + goum_ * goub_) + goun_ * gouc_))
- `conv3-mid-in` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc, goux_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_), gouy_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_)], cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_))
- `conv3-near-vertex` — forall([goxa_ in cc, goxb_ in cc, goxc_ in cc, goxz_ in conv3(goxa_, goxb_, goxc_)], magnitude(goxz_ - goxa_) <= magnitude(goxb_ - goxa_) + magnitude(goxc_ - goxa_))
- `conv3-seg-trace` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc, goux_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_), gouy_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_)], trace(seg-path(goux_, gouy_), 0, 1) subset conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_))
- `conv3-sub-hull` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc, goux_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_), gouy_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_), gouz_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_)], conv3(goux_, gouy_, gouz_) subset conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_))
- `conv3-sub-triangle` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], conv3(goua_, cc-mid(goua_, goub_), cc-mid(goua_, gouc_)) subset conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) and conv3(cc-mid(goua_, goub_), goub_, cc-mid(goub_, gouc_)) subset conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) and conv3(cc-mid(goua_, gouc_), cc-mid(goub_, gouc_), gouc_) subset conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) and conv3(cc-mid(goua_, goub_), cc-mid(goub_, gouc_), cc-mid(goua_, gouc_)) subset conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_))
- `conv3-vertex-shrink` — forall([goxa_ in cc, goxb_ in cc, goxc_ in cc, goxt_ in ccint(0, 1)], conv3(goxa_, goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxb_ - goxa_), goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxc_ - goxa_)) subset conv3(goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) and conv3(goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxb_ - goxa_), goxb_, goxc_) subset conv3(goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) and conv3(goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxb_ - goxa_), goxc_, goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxc_ - goxa_)) subset conv3(goxa_, goxb_, goxc_))
- `conv3-vertices-in` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], goua_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) and goub_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) and gouc_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_))
- `convergence-block-tower` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([n in nn], seq-compact(ms(n))) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms))], forsome([s in fun(nn, inf-subsets(nn))], forall([k in nn], s(succ(k)) subset s(k)) and forsome([l in product-carrier(ms)], forall([n in nn], converges-along(ms(n), vnb-lambda(i, nn, (seq(i))(n)), s(n), l(n)))))))
- `converges-dist-null-bwd` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)), 0) implies converges-to(t, f, lv)))
- `converges-dist-null-fwd` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(t, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)), 0)))
- `converges-iff-dist-null` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(t, f, lv) iff converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (dist(t))(f(j_), lv)), 0)))
- `converges-to-transfer` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(t)), h in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(t, f, lv) implies forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_)) implies converges-to(t, h, lv)))
- `coord-block-estimate` — forall([s, g, b, p, delta, m0], converges-along(s, g, b, p) implies strictly-mono-nn(delta) implies m0 in nn implies forall([j in nn], m0 <= j implies delta(j) in b) implies converges-to(s, subseq(g, delta), p))
- `coordinatewise-diagonal-subseq` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([n in nn], seq-compact(ms(n))) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms))], forsome([delta], strictly-mono-nn(delta) and forsome([l in product-carrier(ms)], forall([n in nn], converges-to(ms(n), vnb-lambda(k, nn, ((subseq(seq, delta))(k))(n)), l(n)))))))
- `countable-cases` — forall([rqd_], is-countable(rqd_) implies rqd_ in set and (rqd_ = empty-set or forsome([rqe_ in fun(nn, rqd_)], forall([rqy_ in rqd_], forsome([rqn_ in nn], rqy_ = rqe_(rqn_))))))
- `countable-empty` — is-countable(empty-set)
- `countable-image` — forall([rqd_, rqh_, rqa_, rqb_], is-countable(rqd_) implies rqh_ in fun(rqa_, rqb_) implies rqd_ subset rqa_ implies is-countable(image(rqh_, rqd_)))
- `countable-image-avoids-interval` — forall([zc_, zdm_, zph_], is-countable(zc_) implies zc_ subset zdm_ implies zph_ in fun(zdm_, rr) implies forall([zp_ in rr, zq_ in rr], zp_ < zq_ implies forsome([zy_ in rr], zp_ < zy_ and zy_ < zq_ and forall([zx_ in zc_], not(zph_(zx_) = zy_)))))
- `countable-of-enum` — forall([rqd_, rqe_], rqd_ in set implies rqe_ in fun(nn, rqd_) implies forall([rqy_ in rqd_], forsome([rqn_ in nn], rqy_ = rqe_(rqn_))) implies is-countable(rqd_))
- `countable-of-two-enum` — forall([rqu_, rqf_, rqg_], rqu_ in set implies rqf_ in fun(nn, rqu_) implies rqg_ in fun(nn, rqu_) implies forall([rqy_ in rqu_], forsome([rqj_ in nn], rqy_ = rqf_(rqj_)) or forsome([rqj_ in nn], rqy_ = rqg_(rqj_))) implies is-countable(rqu_))
- `countable-singleton` — forall([rqx_ in set], is-countable(pair(rqx_, rqx_)))
- `countable-subset` — forall([rqa_, rqd_], is-countable(rqd_) implies rqa_ subset rqd_ implies is-countable(rqa_))
- `countable-union-2` — forall([rqa_, rqb_], is-countable(rqa_) implies is-countable(rqb_) implies is-countable(union(rqa_, rqb_)))
- `countable-union-nn` — forall([pud_, puu_ in set], forall([puk_ in nn], is-countable(pud_(puk_))) implies forall([puy_ in puu_], forsome([puk_ in nn], puy_ in pud_(puk_))) implies is-countable(puu_))
- `cover-block-step` — forall([v in set, f in fun(nn, v), c], is-finite-cover(c, v) implies forall([j in inf-subsets(nn)], forsome([j_ in inf-subsets(nn)], j_ subset j and forsome([u in c], forall([i in j_], f(i) in u)))))
- `cpd-cc-squeeze` — forall([s_ in fun(nn, cc), lv in cc, m in fun(nn, rr)], converges-to(rr-ms, m, 0) implies forall([cpdj_ in nn], magnitude(s_(cpdj_) - lv) <= m(cpdj_)) implies converges-to(cc-ms, s_, lv))
- `cpd-power-second-order` — forall([cpdk_ in nn, cpdz_ in cc, cpdu_ in cc, cpdr_ in rr], magnitude(cpdz_) <= cpdr_ implies magnitude(cpdu_) <= cpdr_ implies cpdr_ * magnitude(cpdz_ ^ succ(cpdk_) - cpdu_ ^ succ(cpdk_) - (succ(cpdk_) * cpdu_ ^ cpdk_) * (cpdz_ - cpdu_)) <= ((succ(cpdk_) * succ(cpdk_)) * cpdr_ ^ cpdk_) * (magnitude(cpdz_ - cpdu_) * magnitude(cpdz_ - cpdu_)))
- `cps-abs-conv-closed-ball` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), cpsrad_ in rr], 0 <= cpsrad_ implies cpsrad_ < cps-radius(cf) implies forall([cpsz_ in closed-ball(cc-ms, 0, cpsrad_)], series-converges(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, magnitude(cf(cpsk_) * cpsz_ ^ cpsk_)))))
- `cps-coefficient-formula` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), ctck_ in nn, ctcg_ in fun(nn, cc)], forall([ctcn_ in nn], ctcg_(ctcn_) = (factorial(ctcn_ + ctck_) * recip(factorial(ctcn_))) * cf(ctcn_ + ctck_)) implies forall([cpdr_ in rr], 0 < cpdr_ implies cf(ctck_) = (vnb-lambda(cpdz_, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, ctcg_(cpsk_) * cpdz_ ^ cpsk_))))(0) * recip(factorial(ctck_))))
- `cps-converges-closed-ball` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), cpsrad_ in rr], 0 <= cpsrad_ implies cpsrad_ < cps-radius(cf) implies forall([cpsz_ in closed-ball(cc-ms, 0, cpsrad_)], cc-series-converges(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * cpsz_ ^ cpsk_))))
- `cps-diff-on-ball` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), dcf in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cpdj_ in nn], dcf(cpdj_) = succ(cpdj_) * cf(succ(cpdj_))) implies forall([cpdr_ in rr], 0 < cpdr_ implies cpdr_ < cps-radius(cf) implies forall([cpda_ in ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_)], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), vnb-lambda(cpdz_, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * cpdz_ ^ cpsk_))), cpda_, cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, dcf(cpsk_) * cpda_ ^ cpsk_))))))
- `cps-entire-converges` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc)], cps-radius(cf) = pos-inf implies forall([cexz_ in cc], cc-series-converges(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * cexz_ ^ cpsk_))))
- `cps-entire-diff` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), dcf in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cpdj_ in nn], dcf(cpdj_) = succ(cpdj_) * cf(succ(cpdj_))) implies cps-radius(cf) = pos-inf implies forall([cpda_ in cc], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cc, vnb-lambda(cpdz_, cc, cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * cpdz_ ^ cpsk_))), cpda_, cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, dcf(cpsk_) * cpda_ ^ cpsk_)))))
- `cps-factor-conv` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), cg in fun(nn, cc), cfg in fun(nn, cc)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], cfg(cpsk_) = cf(cpsk_) * cg(cpsk_)) implies forall([cpsc_ in rr, cpsm_ in nn], forall([cpsk_ in nn], magnitude(cg(cpsk_)) <= cpsc_ * cpsk_ ^ cpsm_) implies forall([cpst_ in rr, cpsrad_ in rr], 0 <= cpsrad_ implies cpsrad_ < cpst_ implies series-converges(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, magnitude(cf(cpsk_)) * cpst_ ^ cpsk_)) implies series-converges(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, magnitude(cfg(cpsk_)) * cpsrad_ ^ cpsk_)))))
- `cps-holomorphic-ball` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), cpdr_ in rr], 0 < cpdr_ implies cpdr_ < cps-radius(cf) implies holomorphic-on(ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), vnb-lambda(cpdz_, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * cpdz_ ^ cpsk_)))) and cps-radius(vnb-lambda(cpdn_, nn, succ(cpdn_) * cf(succ(cpdn_)))) = cps-radius(cf) and forall([cpda_ in ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_)], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), vnb-lambda(cpdz_, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * cpdz_ ^ cpsk_))), cpda_, cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, (succ(cpsk_) * cf(succ(cpsk_))) * cpda_ ^ cpsk_)))))
- `cps-kth-derived-radius` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), ctck_ in nn, ctcg_ in fun(nn, cc)], forall([ctcn_ in nn], ctcg_(ctcn_) = (factorial(ctcn_ + ctck_) * recip(factorial(ctcn_))) * cf(ctcn_ + ctck_)) implies cps-radius(ctcg_) = cps-radius(cf))
- `cps-kth-derived-step` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), ctck_ in nn, ctcg_ in fun(nn, cc), ctch_ in fun(nn, cc)], forall([ctcn_ in nn], ctcg_(ctcn_) = (factorial(ctcn_ + ctck_) * recip(factorial(ctcn_))) * cf(ctcn_ + ctck_)) implies forall([ctcn_ in nn], ctch_(ctcn_) = (factorial(ctcn_ + succ(ctck_)) * recip(factorial(ctcn_))) * cf(ctcn_ + succ(ctck_))) implies forall([cpdj_ in nn], ctch_(cpdj_) = succ(cpdj_) * ctcg_(succ(cpdj_))))
- `cps-log-deriv-at-one` — forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc)], algc_(0) = 0 implies forall([algj_ in nn], algc_(succ(algj_)) = -1 ^ algj_ * recip(succ(algj_))) implies forall([algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ < 1 implies forall([algw_ in ball(cc-ms, 1, algr_)], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, ball(cc-ms, 1, algr_), vnb-lambda(algz_, ball(cc-ms, 1, algr_), cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * (algz_ - 1) ^ cpsk_))), algw_, recip(algw_)))))
- `cps-log-radius` — forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc)], algc_(0) = 0 implies forall([algj_ in nn], algc_(succ(algj_)) = -1 ^ algj_ * recip(succ(algj_))) implies forall([algr_ in rr], 0 <= algr_ implies algr_ < 1 implies algr_ < cps-radius(algc_)))
- `cps-log-radius-le-one` — forall([clhc_ in fun(nn, cc)], clhc_(0) = 0 implies forall([clhj_ in nn], clhc_(succ(clhj_)) = -1 ^ clhj_ * recip(succ(clhj_))) implies forall([clhr_ in rr], 1 < clhr_ implies not(clhr_ < cps-radius(clhc_))))
- `cps-log-radius-one` — forall([clhc_ in fun(nn, cc)], clhc_(0) = 0 implies forall([clhj_ in nn], clhc_(succ(clhj_)) = -1 ^ clhj_ * recip(succ(clhj_))) implies cps-radius(clhc_) = 1)
- `cps-log-series` — forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc)], algc_(0) = 0 implies forall([algj_ in nn], algc_(succ(algj_)) = -1 ^ algj_ * recip(succ(algj_))) implies forall([algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ < 1 implies forall([algz_ in ball(cc-ms, 0, algr_)], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, ball(cc-ms, 0, algr_), vnb-lambda(cpdz_, ball(cc-ms, 0, algr_), cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * cpdz_ ^ cpsk_))), algz_, recip(1 + algz_)))))
- `cps-normally-convergent` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), cpsrad_ in rr], 0 <= cpsrad_ implies cpsrad_ < cps-radius(cf) implies is-normally-convergent(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, vnb-lambda(cpsz_, closed-ball(cc-ms, 0, cpsrad_), cf(cpsk_) * cpsz_ ^ cpsk_)), closed-ball(cc-ms, 0, cpsrad_)))
- `cps-nth-deriv` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), cpdr_ in rr], 0 < cpdr_ implies cpdr_ < cps-radius(cf) implies forall([ndlk_ in nn, ndlg_ in fun(nn, cc)], forall([ctcn_ in nn], ndlg_(ctcn_) = (factorial(ctcn_ + ndlk_) * recip(factorial(ctcn_))) * cf(ctcn_ + ndlk_)) implies nth-deriv-on(cc-normed-field, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), vnb-lambda(cpdz_, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * cpdz_ ^ cpsk_))), ndlk_) = vnb-lambda(cpdz_, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, ndlg_(cpsk_) * cpdz_ ^ cpsk_)))))
