- `daf-swap` — forall([r, n, d, a, b, p, q], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies d in fun(mat(n, n, carr(r)), carr(r)) implies forall([dfa_, dfb_, dfc_, dfp_, dfs_], dfa_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfb_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfc_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfp_ in interval(1, n) implies dfs_ in carr(r) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = dfp_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfb_, dri_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = dfp_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfc_, dri_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfc_, dfp_, drk_) = (add(r))((mul(r))(dfs_, entry(dfa_, dfp_, drk_)), entry(dfb_, dfp_, drk_))) implies d(dfc_) = (add(r))((mul(r))(dfs_, d(dfa_)), d(dfb_))) implies forall([dfa_, dfp_, dfq_], dfa_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfp_ in interval(1, n) implies dfq_ in interval(1, n) implies not(dfp_ = dfq_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfa_, dfp_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dfq_, drk_)) implies d(dfa_) = zero(r)) implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies b in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies p in interval(1, n) implies q in interval(1, n) implies not(p = q) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = p) implies not(dri_ = q) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, dri_, drk_) = entry(a, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, p, drk_) = entry(a, q, drk_)) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, q, drk_) = entry(a, p, drk_)) implies d(b) = (neg(r))(d(a)))
- `daf-zero-row` — forall([r, n, d, a, dfk_], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies d in fun(mat(n, n, carr(r)), carr(r)) implies forall([dfa_, dfb_, dfc_, dfp_, dfs_], dfa_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfb_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfc_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfp_ in interval(1, n) implies dfs_ in carr(r) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = dfp_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfb_, dri_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = dfp_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfc_, dri_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfc_, dfp_, drk_) = (add(r))((mul(r))(dfs_, entry(dfa_, dfp_, drk_)), entry(dfb_, dfp_, drk_))) implies d(dfc_) = (add(r))((mul(r))(dfs_, d(dfa_)), d(dfb_))) implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfk_ in interval(1, n) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(a, dfk_, drk_) = zero(r)) implies d(a) = zero(r))
- `dc-iter-in` — forall([n_ in nn, s_, a_ in s_, nx_], forall([k in nn, u in s_], forsome([y in s_], y in nx_(k, u))) implies dc-iter(s_, a_, nx_, n_) in s_)
- `dc-iter-step` — forall([n_ in nn, s_, a_ in s_, nx_], forall([k in nn, u in s_], forsome([y in s_], y in nx_(k, u))) implies dc-iter(s_, a_, nx_, succ(n_)) in nx_(n_, dc-iter(s_, a_, nx_, n_)))
- `dc-on-nn` — forall([x in set, a in x, r in set], forall([k in nn, u in x], forsome([y in x], [k, u, y] in r)) implies forsome([f in fun(nn, x)], f(0) = a and forall([k in nn], [k, f(k), f(succ(k))] in r)))
- `dc-on-nn-pred` — forall([x in set, a in x, nxt], forall([k in nn, u in x], forsome([y in x], y in nxt(k, u))) implies forsome([f in fun(nn, x)], f(0) = a and forall([k in nn], f(succ(k)) in nxt(k, f(k)))))
- `deg-add-le` — forall([a_, f_, g_, n_], is-ring(a_) implies f_ in carr(poly(a_)) implies g_ in carr(poly(a_)) implies n_ in nn implies deg(a_, f_) <= n_ implies deg(a_, g_) <= n_ implies deg(a_, monalg-add(a_, nn-add-monoid, f_, g_)) <= n_)
- `deg-bound-apply` — forall([a_, p_, n_, k_], deg-bound(a_, p_, n_) implies k_ in nn implies n_ < k_ implies p_(k_) = zero(a_))
- `deg-is` — forall([a_, p_, m_], p_ in carr(poly(a_)) implies m_ in nn implies deg-bound(a_, p_, m_) implies forall([j_ in nn], deg-bound(a_, p_, j_) implies m_ <= j_) implies deg(a_, p_) = m_)
- `deg-unfold` — forall([a_, p_], deg(a_, p_) == iota(n_, n_ in nn and deg-bound(a_, p_, n_) and forall([j_ in nn], deg-bound(a_, p_, j_) implies n_ <= j_)))
- `deg-well-defined` — forall([a_, p_ in carr(poly(a_))], deg(a_, p_) in nn and deg-bound(a_, p_, deg(a_, p_)) and forall([j_ in nn], deg-bound(a_, p_, j_) implies deg(a_, p_) <= j_))
- `deriv-affine` — forall([c, lam, a], c in rr and lam in rr and a in rr implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c + lam * x), a, lam))
- `deriv-anti-monomial` — forall([c in rr, m in nn, pt in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, (recip(succ(m)) * c) * x ^ succ(m)), pt, c * pt ^ m))
- `deriv-chain` — forall([f, g, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) implies is-diff-at(g, f(a), m) implies is-diff-at(compose(g, f), a, m * l))
- `deriv-chain-value` — forall([f, g, a, dl, dm], is-diff-at(f, a, dl) implies is-diff-at(g, f(a), dm) implies deriv(compose(g, f), a) = deriv(g, f(a)) * deriv(f, a))
- `deriv-coef-monomial` — forall([c in rr, dg in nn, pt in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * x ^ succ(dg)), pt, (succ(dg) * c) * pt ^ dg))
- `deriv-const` — forall([c, a], c in rr and a in rr implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c), a, 0))
- `deriv-difference` — forall([f, g, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) implies is-diff-at(g, a, m) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) - g(x)), a, l - m))
- `deriv-identity` — forall([a in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x), a, 1))
- `deriv-inverse` — forall([f, g, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) implies not(l = 0) implies g in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([x in rr], g(f(x)) = x) implies forall([y in rr], f(g(y)) = y) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, f(a)) implies is-diff-at(g, f(a), recip(l)))
- `deriv-neg` — forall([f, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, -f(z)), a, -l))
- `deriv-of-is-diff-at` — forall([f, a, dv], is-diff-at(f, a, dv) implies deriv(f, a) = dv)
- `deriv-on-of-is-diff-on` — forall([drok_], is-normed-field(drok_) implies forall([d2e_], pos-rr(d2e_) implies forsome([d2h_ in carr(drok_)], not(d2h_ = zero(drok_)) and (fnrm(drok_))(d2h_) < d2e_)) implies forall([drou_, drof_, droa_, drol_], is-diff-on(drok_, drou_, drof_, droa_, drol_) implies deriv-on(drok_, drou_, drof_, droa_) = drol_))
- `deriv-on-unfold` — forall([k, u, f, a], deriv-on(k, u, f, a) == iota(l, is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l)))
- `deriv-polynomial` — forall([n in nn, cf in fun(nn, rr), pt in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k), succ(n))), pt, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(j, nn, (succ(j) * cf(succ(j))) * pt ^ j), n)))
- `deriv-pos-strictly-increasing` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, x, l) and 0 < l)) implies forall([u, v], u in ccint(a, b) and v in ccint(a, b) and u < v implies f(u) < f(v)))
- `deriv-power` — forall([n in nn, a in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ succ(n)), a, succ(n) * a ^ n))
- `deriv-product` — forall([f, g, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) and is-diff-at(g, a, m) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) * g(x)), a, l * g(a) + f(a) * m))
- `deriv-right-inverse` — forall([fm_, gm_, am_, lv_], is-diff-at(fm_, am_, lv_) implies not(lv_ = 0) implies gm_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([z_ in rr], fm_(gm_(z_)) = z_) implies gm_(fm_(am_)) = am_ implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, gm_, fm_(am_)) implies is-diff-at(gm_, fm_(am_), recip(lv_)))
- `deriv-scalar-mult` — forall([c in rr, f, pt, dl], is-diff-at(f, pt, dl) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * f(x)), pt, c * dl))
- `deriv-sub` — forall([f, g, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) and is-diff-at(g, a, m) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) - g(x)), a, l - m))
- `deriv-sum` — forall([f, g, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) and is-diff-at(g, a, m) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) + g(x)), a, l + m))
- `deriv-within-in-rr` — forall([f, osw_, osx_, osl_], has-deriv-at-within(f, osw_, osx_, osl_) implies osl_ in rr)
- `deriv-within-local` — forall([f, g, osw_, osx_, osl_], restrict(f, osw_) in fun(osw_, rr) implies forall([osy_ in osw_], f(osy_) == g(osy_)) implies has-deriv-at-within(g, osw_, osx_, osl_) implies has-deriv-at-within(f, osw_, osx_, osl_))
- `deriv-within-pt-in` — forall([f, osw_, osx_, osl_], has-deriv-at-within(f, osw_, osx_, osl_) implies osx_ in osw_)
- `deriv-within-restrict-in-fun` — forall([f, osw_, osx_, osl_], has-deriv-at-within(f, osw_, osx_, osl_) implies restrict(f, osw_) in fun(osw_, rr))
- `deriv-within-shrink` — forall([f, osw_, osv_, osx_, osl_], osw_ subset rr implies has-deriv-at-within(f, osw_, osx_, osl_) implies osv_ subset osw_ implies osx_ in osv_ implies has-deriv-at-within(f, osv_, osx_, osl_))
- `deriv-within-sum` — forall([f, g, h, osw_, osx_, osl_, osm_], osw_ subset rr implies restrict(h, osw_) in fun(osw_, rr) implies forall([osy_ in osw_], h(osy_) == f(osy_) + g(osy_)) implies has-deriv-at-within(f, osw_, osx_, osl_) implies has-deriv-at-within(g, osw_, osx_, osm_) implies has-deriv-at-within(h, osw_, osx_, osl_ + osm_))
- `deriv-within-unique` — forall([f, osw_, osx_, osl_, osm_], osw_ subset rr implies forall([osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forsome([osb_ in osw_], not(osb_ = osx_) and abs(osx_ - osb_) <= osr_)) implies has-deriv-at-within(f, osw_, osx_, osl_) implies has-deriv-at-within(f, osw_, osx_, osm_) implies osl_ = osm_)
- `deriv-zero-const-up` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies is-diff-at(f, x, 0)) implies forall([u, v], u in ccint(a, b) and v in ccint(a, b) and u < v implies f(u) = f(v)))
- `deriv-zero-constant-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([h in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(h, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([pat_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(h, pat_, 0)) implies forall([pau_ in ccint(a, b), pav_ in ccint(a, b)], h(pau_) = h(pav_))))
- `deriv-zero-implies-constant` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies is-diff-at(f, x, 0)) implies forall([u, v], u in ccint(a, b) and v in ccint(a, b) implies f(u) = f(v)))
- `derivative-unique` — forall([f, a, l, m], is-diff-at(f, a, l) and is-diff-at(f, a, m) implies l = m)
- `descend-computes` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies forall([a in pts(s)], (descend(s, f))(class(s, a)) = f(a))))
- `descend-in-fun` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies descend(s, f) in fun(quotient(s), z)))
- `descend2-computes` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), z)], respects2(s, f) implies forall([a, b], a in pts(s) implies b in pts(s) implies (descend2(s, f))(class(s, a), class(s, b)) = f(a, b))))
- `descend2-in-fun` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f in fun(cartesian(pts(s), pts(s)), z)], respects2(s, f) implies descend2(s, f) in fun(cartesian(quotient(s), quotient(s)), z)))
- `descent-remainder` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-commutative-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(succ(n), 1, vec(md)), c in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(md))), c0 in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(md))), q in carr(scal(md)), b in carr(scal(md))], entry(c0, 1, succ(n)) = b implies entry(c, 1, succ(n)) = (mul(scal(md)))(q, b) implies forsome([y in span(md, n, block(u, n, 1))], lincomb(md, succ(n), c, u) = (vadd(md))(y, (act(md))(q, lincomb(md, succ(n), c0, u))) and y = (vadd(md))(lincomb(md, succ(n), c, u), (act(md))((neg(scal(md)))(q), lincomb(md, succ(n), c0, u))))))
- `det-1x1` — forall([r, a], is-ring(r) implies a in mat(1, 1, carr(r)) implies det(r, 1, a) = entry(a, 1, 1))
- `det-2x2` — forall([r, a], is-ring(r) implies a in mat(2, 2, carr(r)) implies det(r, 2, a) = (add(r))((mul(r))(entry(a, 1, 1), entry(a, 2, 2)), (neg(r))((mul(r))(entry(a, 1, 2), entry(a, 2, 1)))))
- `det-alternating-form` — forall([r, n, d, a], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies d in fun(mat(n, n, carr(r)), carr(r)) implies forall([dfa_, dfb_, dfc_, dfp_, dfs_], dfa_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfb_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfc_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfp_ in interval(1, n) implies dfs_ in carr(r) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = dfp_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfb_, dri_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = dfp_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfc_, dri_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfc_, dfp_, drk_) = (add(r))((mul(r))(dfs_, entry(dfa_, dfp_, drk_)), entry(dfb_, dfp_, drk_))) implies d(dfc_) = (add(r))((mul(r))(dfs_, d(dfa_)), d(dfb_))) implies forall([dfa_, dfp_, dfq_], dfa_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfp_ in interval(1, n) implies dfq_ in interval(1, n) implies not(dfp_ = dfq_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfa_, dfp_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dfq_, drk_)) implies d(dfa_) = zero(r)) implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies d(a) = (mul(r))(det(r, n, a), d(identmat(r, n))))
- `det-alternating-form-ind` — forall([n in nn, r, d], is-commutative-ring(r) implies d in fun(mat(n, n, carr(r)), carr(r)) implies forall([dfa_, dfb_, dfc_, dfp_, dfs_], dfa_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfb_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfc_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfp_ in interval(1, n) implies dfs_ in carr(r) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = dfp_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfb_, dri_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = dfp_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfc_, dri_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfc_, dfp_, drk_) = (add(r))((mul(r))(dfs_, entry(dfa_, dfp_, drk_)), entry(dfb_, dfp_, drk_))) implies d(dfc_) = (add(r))((mul(r))(dfs_, d(dfa_)), d(dfb_))) implies forall([dfa_, dfp_, dfq_], dfa_ in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies dfp_ in interval(1, n) implies dfq_ in interval(1, n) implies not(dfp_ = dfq_) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(dfa_, dfp_, drk_) = entry(dfa_, dfq_, drk_)) implies d(dfa_) = zero(r)) implies forall([dfx_ in mat(n, n, carr(r))], d(dfx_) = (mul(r))(det(r, n, dfx_), d(identmat(r, n)))))
- `det-alternating-ind` — forall([n in nn, r, a, p, q], is-commutative-ring(r) implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies p in interval(1, n) implies q in interval(1, n) implies not(p = q) implies forall([k in interval(1, n)], entry(a, p, k) = entry(a, q, k)) implies det(r, n, a) = zero(r))
- `det-alternating-rows` — forall([r, n, a, p, q], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies p in interval(1, n) implies q in interval(1, n) implies not(p = q) implies forall([k in interval(1, n)], entry(a, p, k) = entry(a, q, k)) implies det(r, n, a) = zero(r))
- `det-col-linear` — forall([r, n, a, b, c, p, sc], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies b in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies c in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies p in interval(1, n) implies sc in carr(r) implies forall([dcj_ in interval(1, n)], not(dcj_ = p) implies forall([dci_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, dci_, dcj_) = entry(a, dci_, dcj_))) implies forall([dcj_ in interval(1, n)], not(dcj_ = p) implies forall([dci_ in interval(1, n)], entry(c, dci_, dcj_) = entry(a, dci_, dcj_))) implies forall([dci_ in interval(1, n)], entry(c, dci_, p) = (add(r))((mul(r))(sc, entry(a, dci_, p)), entry(b, dci_, p))) implies det(r, n, c) = (add(r))((mul(r))(sc, det(r, n, a)), det(r, n, b)))
- `det-elem-f` — forall([r, n, k, l], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies det(r, n, elem-f(r, n, k, l)) = (neg(r))(one(r)))

- `det-elem-h` — forall([r, n, sc, k], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies sc in carr(r) implies k in interval(1, n) implies det(r, n, elem-h(r, n, sc, k)) = sc)

- `det-elem-h-row` — forall([r, n, a, sc, k], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies sc in carr(r) implies k in interval(1, n) implies det(r, n, matmul(r, elem-h(r, n, sc, k), a)) = (mul(r))(sc, det(r, n, a)))
- `det-equal-cols-zero` — forall([r, n, a, p, q], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies p in interval(1, n) implies q in interval(1, n) implies not(p = q) implies forall([dci_ in interval(1, n)], entry(a, dci_, p) = entry(a, dci_, q)) implies det(r, n, a) = zero(r))
- `det-expand-col` — forall([r, n, a, j], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(succ(n), succ(n), carr(r)) implies j in interval(1, succ(n)) implies det(r, succ(n), a) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(dce_, interval(1, succ(n)), (mul(r))(mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), (neg(r))(one(r)), dce_ + j), (mul(r))(entry(a, dce_, j), det(r, n, minor(a, dce_, j, n))))), interval(1, succ(n))))
- `det-expand-row` — forall([r, n, a, i], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(succ(n), succ(n), carr(r)) implies i in interval(1, succ(n)) implies det(r, succ(n), a) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(dxj_, interval(1, succ(n)), (mul(r))(mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), (neg(r))(one(r)), i + dxj_), (mul(r))(entry(a, i, dxj_), det(r, n, minor(a, i, dxj_, n))))), interval(1, succ(n))))
- `det-expand-row-ind` — forall([i in nn, n, r, a], n in nn implies is-commutative-ring(r) implies a in mat(succ(n), succ(n), carr(r)) implies 1 <= i implies i <= succ(n) implies det(r, succ(n), a) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(dxj_, interval(1, succ(n)), (mul(r))(mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), (neg(r))(one(r)), i + dxj_), (mul(r))(entry(a, i, dxj_), det(r, n, minor(a, i, dxj_, n))))), interval(1, succ(n))))

- `det-identity-ind` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([n in nn], det(r, n, identmat(r, n)) = one(r)))

- `det-mul-elem-f` — forall([r, n, a, k, l], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies det(r, n, matmul(r, elem-f(r, n, k, l), a)) = (mul(r))(det(r, n, elem-f(r, n, k, l)), det(r, n, a)))
- `det-mul-elem-f-col` — forall([r, n, a, k, l], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies det(r, n, matmul(r, a, elem-f(r, n, k, l))) = (mul(r))(det(r, n, a), det(r, n, elem-f(r, n, k, l))))
- `det-mul-elem-g` — forall([r, n, a, sc, k, l], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies sc in carr(r) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies det(r, n, matmul(r, elem-g(r, n, sc, k, l), a)) = (mul(r))(det(r, n, elem-g(r, n, sc, k, l)), det(r, n, a)))
- `det-mul-elem-g-col` — forall([r, n, a, sc, k, l], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies sc in carr(r) implies k in interval(1, n) implies l in interval(1, n) implies not(k = l) implies det(r, n, matmul(r, a, elem-g(r, n, sc, k, l))) = (mul(r))(det(r, n, a), det(r, n, elem-g(r, n, sc, k, l))))
- `det-mul-elem-h` — forall([r, n, a, sc, k], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies sc in carr(r) implies k in interval(1, n) implies det(r, n, matmul(r, elem-h(r, n, sc, k), a)) = (mul(r))(det(r, n, elem-h(r, n, sc, k)), det(r, n, a)))
- `det-mul-elem-h-col` — forall([r, n, a, sc, k], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies sc in carr(r) implies k in interval(1, n) implies det(r, n, matmul(r, a, elem-h(r, n, sc, k))) = (mul(r))(det(r, n, a), det(r, n, elem-h(r, n, sc, k))))
- `det-mul-invertible` — forall([r, n, a, v], is-field-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies is-invertible-mat(r, n, v) implies det(r, n, matmul(r, a, v)) = (mul(r))(det(r, n, a), det(r, n, v)))
- `det-multiplicative` — forall([r, n, p, q], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies p in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies q in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies det(r, n, matmul(r, p, q)) = (mul(r))(det(r, n, p), det(r, n, q)))
- `det-row-linear` — forall([r, n, a, b, c, p, sc], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies b in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies c in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies p in interval(1, n) implies sc in carr(r) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = p) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, dri_, drk_) = entry(a, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = p) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(c, dri_, drk_) = entry(a, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(c, p, drk_) = (add(r))((mul(r))(sc, entry(a, p, drk_)), entry(b, p, drk_))) implies det(r, n, c) = (add(r))((mul(r))(sc, det(r, n, a)), det(r, n, b)))
- `det-row-linear-ind` — forall([n in nn, r, a, b, c, p, dsc_], is-commutative-ring(r) implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies b in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies c in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies p in interval(1, n) implies dsc_ in carr(r) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = p) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, dri_, drk_) = entry(a, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = p) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(c, dri_, drk_) = entry(a, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(c, p, drk_) = (add(r))((mul(r))(dsc_, entry(a, p, drk_)), entry(b, p, drk_))) implies det(r, n, c) = (add(r))((mul(r))(dsc_, det(r, n, a)), det(r, n, b)))
- `det-swap-cols` — forall([r, n, a, b, p, q], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies b in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies p in interval(1, n) implies q in interval(1, n) implies not(p = q) implies forall([dcj_ in interval(1, n)], not(dcj_ = p) implies not(dcj_ = q) implies forall([dci_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, dci_, dcj_) = entry(a, dci_, dcj_))) implies forall([dci_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, dci_, p) = entry(a, dci_, q)) implies forall([dci_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, dci_, q) = entry(a, dci_, p)) implies det(r, n, b) = (neg(r))(det(r, n, a)))
- `det-swap-rows` — forall([r, n, a, b, p, q], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies b in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies p in interval(1, n) implies q in interval(1, n) implies not(p = q) implies forall([dri_ in interval(1, n)], not(dri_ = p) implies not(dri_ = q) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, dri_, drk_) = entry(a, dri_, drk_))) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, p, drk_) = entry(a, q, drk_)) implies forall([drk_ in interval(1, n)], entry(b, q, drk_) = entry(a, p, drk_)) implies det(r, n, b) = (neg(r))(det(r, n, a)))
- `det-transpose` — forall([r, n, a], is-commutative-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(n, n, carr(r)) implies det(r, n, transpose(a, n, n)) = det(r, n, a))
- `dfun-v-mono` — forall([m, f, n, k], forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= n implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))) implies n in nn implies k in nn implies k <= n implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= k implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))))
- `diagonal-off-entry` — forall([a, m, n, d], is-diagonal(a, m, n, d) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), j in interval(1, n)], not(i = j) implies entry(d, i, j) = zero(a)))
- `diagonalization` — forall([s in fun(nn, inf-subsets(nn))], forall([k in nn], s(succ(k)) subset s(k)) implies forsome([f], strictly-mono-nn(f) and forall([k in nn, j in nn], k <= j implies f(j) in s(k))))
- `diff-at-iff-diff-on` — forall([f, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) iff is-diff-on(rr-normed-field, rr, f, a, l))
- `diff-at-in-fun` — forall([f, a, dl], is-diff-at(f, a, dl) implies f in fun(rr, rr))
- `diff-at-local` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), psi in fun(rr, rr), t_ in rr, lv in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, psi, t_) implies psi(t_) = lv implies forall([x_ in rr], abs(x_ - t_) <= rho implies f(x_) - f(t_) = psi(x_) * (x_ - t_)) implies is-diff-at(f, t_, lv))
- `diff-at-ptwise-limit` — forall([fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), phifam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), f in fun(rr, rr), phi in fun(rr, rr), t_ in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, phi, t_) implies forall([k in nn, y_ in rr], abs(y_ - t_) <= rho implies (fam(k))(y_) - (fam(k))(t_) = (phifam(k))(y_) * (y_ - t_)) implies forall([y_ in rr], abs(y_ - t_) <= rho implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (fam(k_))(y_)), f(y_))) implies forall([y_ in rr], abs(y_ - t_) <= rho implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (phifam(k_))(y_)), phi(y_))) implies is-diff-at(f, t_, phi(t_)))
- `diff-at-shift-back` — forall([f, g, c, dl], f in fun(rr, rr) implies c in rr implies is-diff-at(g, 0, dl) implies forall([x_ in rr], g(x_) == f(c + x_)) implies is-diff-at(f, c, dl))
- `diff-at-witness-family` — forall([fam, lfam, t_], fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)) implies lfam in fun(nn, rr) implies t_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], is-diff-at(fam(k_), t_, lfam(k_))) implies forsome([wfam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr))], forall([k_ in nn], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, wfam(k_), t_) and (wfam(k_))(t_) = lfam(k_) and forall([x_ in rr], (fam(k_))(x_) - (fam(k_))(t_) = (wfam(k_))(x_) * (x_ - t_)))))
- `diff-iff-little-o` — forall([f, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) iff f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and l in rr and little-o-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) - f(a) - l * (x - a)), a))
- `diff-implies-continuous` — forall([f, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, a))
- `diff-on-chain` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u, d2s_, d2f_, d2g_, d2a_, d2l_, d2m_], is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_) implies is-diff-on(k, d2s_, d2g_, d2f_(d2a_), d2m_) implies forall([d2y_ in u], d2f_(d2y_) in d2s_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, d2g_(d2f_(d2x_))), d2a_, (mul(k))(d2m_, d2l_))))
- `diff-on-const` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u], is-open(nf-metric-space(k), u) implies forall([d2c_ in carr(k), d2a_ in u], is-diff-on(k, u, vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, d2c_), d2a_, zero(k)))))
- `diff-on-deriv-in-carr` — forall([k, u, f, a, l], is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l) implies l in carr(k))
- `diff-on-identity` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u], is-open(nf-metric-space(k), u) implies forall([d2a_ in u], is-diff-on(k, u, vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, d2x_), d2a_, one(k)))))
- `diff-on-implies-continuous` — forall([k, u, f, a, l], is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(k), u), nf-metric-space(k), f, a))
- `diff-on-in-fun` — forall([k, u, f, a, l], is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l) implies f in fun(u, carr(k)))
- `diff-on-nbhd-implies-diff-at` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, ice_], pos-rr(ice_) implies forall([icl_], is-diff-on(rr-normed-field, ooint(a - ice_, a + ice_), restrict(f, ooint(a - ice_, a + ice_)), a, icl_) implies is-diff-at(f, a, icl_)))
- `diff-on-normed-field` — forall([k, u, f, a, l], is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l) implies is-normed-field(k))
- `diff-on-ooint-shrink` — forall([a in rr, ice_], pos-rr(ice_) implies forall([icw_], pos-rr(icw_) implies icw_ <= ice_ implies forall([f, icl_], is-diff-on(rr-normed-field, ooint(a - ice_, a + ice_), restrict(f, ooint(a - ice_, a + ice_)), a, icl_) implies is-diff-on(rr-normed-field, ooint(a - icw_, a + icw_), restrict(f, ooint(a - icw_, a + icw_)), a, icl_))))
- `diff-on-open` — forall([k, u, f, a, l], is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l) implies is-open(nf-metric-space(k), u))
- `diff-on-product` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u, d2f_, d2g_, d2a_, d2l_, d2m_], is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, d2g_, d2a_, d2m_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, (mul(k))(d2f_(d2x_), d2g_(d2x_))), d2a_, (add(k))((mul(k))(d2l_, d2g_(d2a_)), (mul(k))(d2m_, d2f_(d2a_))))))
- `diff-on-pt-in` — forall([k, u, f, a, l], is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l) implies a in u)
- `diff-on-recip-cc` — forall([cru_, crf_, cra_, crl_, crq_], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cru_, crf_, cra_, crl_) implies forall([cry_ in cru_], not(crf_(cry_) = 0)) implies crq_ in fun(cru_, cc) implies forall([cry_ in cru_], crq_(cry_) == recip(crf_(cry_))) implies is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, cru_, crq_, cra_, -(crl_ * recip(crf_(cra_) * crf_(cra_)))))
- `diff-on-shrink` — forall([k, u, f, a, icl_], is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, icl_) implies forall([icv_], is-open(nf-metric-space(k), icv_) implies icv_ subset u implies a in icv_ implies is-diff-on(k, icv_, restrict(f, icv_), a, icl_)))
- `diff-on-shrink-restrict` — forall([k, u, f, a, icl_], is-diff-on(k, u, restrict(f, u), a, icl_) implies forall([icv_], is-open(nf-metric-space(k), icv_) implies icv_ subset u implies a in icv_ implies is-diff-on(k, icv_, restrict(f, icv_), a, icl_)))
- `diff-on-sum` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u, d2f_, d2g_, d2a_, d2l_, d2m_], is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, d2g_, d2a_, d2m_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, (add(k))(d2f_(d2x_), d2g_(d2x_))), d2a_, (add(k))(d2l_, d2m_))))
- `diff-on-transfer-ptwise-eq` — forall([k, u, f, g, a, dl], f in fun(u, carr(k)) implies is-diff-on(k, u, g, a, dl) implies forall([hbx_ in u], f(hbx_) == g(hbx_)) implies is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, dl))
- `diff-on-unique` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([d2e_], pos-rr(d2e_) implies forsome([d2h_ in carr(k)], not(d2h_ = zero(k)) and (fnrm(k))(d2h_) < d2e_)) implies forall([u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_, d2m_], is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2l_) implies is-diff-on(k, u, d2f_, d2a_, d2m_) implies d2l_ = d2m_))
- `diff-on-value-in-carr` — forall([k, u, f, a, l], is-diff-on(k, u, f, a, l) implies forall([dfx_ in u], f(dfx_) in carr(k)))
- `diff-transfer-ptwise-eq` — forall([f, g, a, dl], f in fun(rr, rr) implies is-diff-at(g, a, dl) implies forall([x_ in rr], f(x_) == g(x_)) implies is-diff-at(f, a, dl))
- `diff-v-value-in-vec` — forall([m, f, a, l], is-diff-at-v(m, f, a, l) implies l in vec(m))
- `diff-value-real` — forall([f, a, l], is-diff-at(f, a, l) implies l in rr)
- `difference-empty-left` — forall([s_], difference(empty-set, s_) = empty-set)
- `difference-in-power` — forall([w_ in set, s_], difference(w_, s_) in power(w_))
- `difference-membership` — forall([u, b, x], x in difference(u, b) iff x in u and not(x in b))
