- `elem-g-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, r, k, l], n in nn implies r in carr(a) implies elem-g(a, n, r, k, l) in mat(n, n, carr(a))))
- `elem-h-action` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, r, k], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies r in carr(a) implies k in interval(1, n) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], entry(matmul(a, p, elem-h(a, n, r, k)), i, c) = if(c = k, (mul(a))(entry(p, i, k), r), entry(p, i, c)))))
- `elem-h-entries-defined` — forall([a, n, r, k], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies r in carr(a) implies forall([i_ in interval(1, n), j_ in interval(1, n)], (vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = j, if(i = k, r, one(a)), zero(a))))(i_, j_) in set))
- `elem-h-entry-diag` — forall([a, n, r, k, c], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies r in carr(a) implies c in interval(1, n) implies entry(elem-h(a, n, r, k), c, c) = if(c = k, r, one(a)))
- `elem-h-entry-off-diag` — forall([a, n, r, k, j, c], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies r in carr(a) implies j in interval(1, n) implies c in interval(1, n) implies not(j = c) implies entry(elem-h(a, n, r, k), j, c) = zero(a))
- `elem-h-inverse` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, r, s, k], n in nn implies r in carr(a) implies s in carr(a) implies (mul(a))(r, s) = one(a) implies k in interval(1, n) implies matmul(a, elem-h(a, n, r, k), elem-h(a, n, s, k)) = identmat(a, n)))
- `elem-h-ro-off` — forall([a, n, r, k, i, j], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies r in carr(a) implies i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(j = i) implies entry(elem-h(a, n, r, k), i, j) = zero(a))
- `elem-h-row-action` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, r, k], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies r in carr(a) implies k in interval(1, m) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), c in interval(1, n)], entry(matmul(a, elem-h(a, m, r, k), p), i, c) = if(i = k, (mul(a))(r, entry(p, k, c)), entry(p, i, c)))))
- `elem-h-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, r, k], n in nn implies r in carr(a) implies elem-h(a, n, r, k) in mat(n, n, carr(a))))
- `eliminf-eventually-above` — forall([f], forall([cpsk_ in nn], f(cpsk_) in rr) implies forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpsk_)) implies forall([cpsr_ in rr], 0 <= cpsr_ implies cpsr_ < eliminf(f) implies forsome([cpsn_ in nn], forall([cpsk_ in nn], cpsn_ <= cpsk_ implies cpsr_ < f(cpsk_)))))
- `eliminf-in-rr-pos-star` — forall([f], forall([cpsk_ in nn], f(cpsk_) in rr) implies forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpsk_)) implies eliminf(f) in rr-pos-star)
- `elimsup-eventually-below` — forall([f], forall([cpsk_ in nn], f(cpsk_) in rr) implies forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpsk_)) implies forall([cpsr_ in rr], elimsup(f) < cpsr_ implies forsome([cpsn_ in nn], forall([cpsk_ in nn], cpsn_ <= cpsk_ implies f(cpsk_) < cpsr_))))
- `elimsup-eventually-le` — forall([f], forall([cpsk_ in nn], f(cpsk_) in rr) implies forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpsk_)) implies forall([rtpr_ in rr, rtpn_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], rtpn_ <= k_ implies f(k_) <= rtpr_) implies elimsup(f) <= rtpr_))
- `elimsup-frequently-above` — forall([f], forall([cpsk_ in nn], f(cpsk_) in rr) implies forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpsk_)) implies forall([cpsr_ in rr], 0 <= cpsr_ implies cpsr_ < elimsup(f) implies forall([cpsn_ in nn], forsome([cpsk_ in nn], cpsn_ <= cpsk_ and cpsr_ < f(cpsk_)))))
- `elimsup-in-rr-pos-star` — forall([f], forall([cpsk_ in nn], f(cpsk_) in rr) implies forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpsk_)) implies elimsup(f) in rr-pos-star)
- `ell-one-dominated-convergence` — forall([a_ in fun(nn, rr), x in sqn(cc), e in fun(nn, rr), xs], forall([j_ in nn], xs(j_) in sqn(cc)) implies series-converges(a_) implies forall([j_ in nn, k_ in nn], magnitude((xs(j_))(k_)) <= a_(k_)) implies forall([k_ in nn], converges-to(cc-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (xs(j_))(k_)), x(k_))) implies forall([j_ in nn], e(j_) = series-limit(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude((xs(j_))(k_) - x(k_))))) implies x in ell-one and converges-to(rr-ms, e, 0))
- `ell-one-membership` — forall([y_], y_ in ell-one iff y_ in sqn(cc) and series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(y_(k_)))))
- `ell-two-add-closed` — forall([x_ in ell-two, y_ in ell-two, z_ in sqn(cc)], forall([k_ in nn], z_(k_) = x_(k_) + y_(k_)) implies z_ in ell-two)
- `ell-two-membership` — forall([y_], y_ in ell-two iff y_ in sqn(cc) and series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(y_(k_)) * magnitude(y_(k_)))))
- `ell-two-neg-closed` — forall([x_ in ell-two, z_ in sqn(cc)], forall([k_ in nn], z_(k_) = -x_(k_)) implies z_ in ell-two)
- `ell-two-zero-in` — vnb-lambda(n_, nn, 0) in ell-two
- `embed-in-fun` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies embed(m) in fun(pts(m), pts(completion(m))))
- `embed-isometry` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([u in pts(m), v in pts(m)], (dist(completion(m)))((embed(m))(u), (embed(m))(v)) = (dist(m))(u, v)))
- `empty-is-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-open(s, empty-set))
- `empty-subset-any` — forall([b_], empty-set subset b_)
- `entry-in-carrier` — forall([m, n, x, p, i, j], p in mat(m, n, x) implies i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies entry(p, i, j) in x)
- `entry-of-block` — forall([p, k, l, i, j, m, n, x], k in nn implies l in nn implies p in mat(m, n, x) implies k <= m implies l <= n implies i in interval(1, k) implies j in interval(1, l) implies entry(block(p, k, l), i, j) = entry(p, i, j))
- `entry-of-elem-f` — forall([a, n, k, l, i, j], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies entry(elem-f(a, n, k, l), i, j) = if(i = k and j = l or i = l and j = k or i = j and not(i = k) and not(i = l), one(a), zero(a)))
- `entry-of-elem-g` — forall([a, n, r, k, l, i, j], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies r in carr(a) implies i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies entry(elem-g(a, n, r, k, l), i, j) = if(i = j, one(a), if(i = k and j = l, r, zero(a))))
- `entry-of-elem-h` — forall([a, n, r, k, i, j], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies r in carr(a) implies i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies entry(elem-h(a, n, r, k), i, j) = if(i = j, if(i = k, r, one(a)), zero(a)))
- `entry-of-identmat` — forall([a, n, i, j], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies entry(identmat(a, n), i, j) = if(i = j, one(a), zero(a)))
- `entry-of-matof` — forall([m, n, g, i, j], m in nn implies n in nn implies forall([i_ in interval(1, m), j_ in interval(1, n)], g(i_, j_) in set) implies i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies entry(matof(m, n, g), i, j) = g(i, j))
- `entry-of-matof-guarded` — forall([m, n, g], m in nn implies n in nn implies 1 <= m implies forall([i_ in interval(1, m), j_ in interval(1, n)], g(i_, j_) in set) implies forall([u_ in interval(1, m), v_ in interval(1, n)], entry(matof(m, n, g), u_, v_) = g(u_, v_)))
- `entry-of-matunit` — forall([a, n, k, l, i, j], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies entry(matunit(a, n, k, l), i, j) = if(i = k and j = l, one(a), zero(a)))
- `entry-of-snoc-col` — forall([w, n, v, i, x], n in nn implies w in mat(n, 1, x) implies v in x implies i in interval(1, n) implies entry(snoc-col(w, n, v), i, 1) = entry(w, i, 1))
- `entry-of-snoc-row` — forall([c, n, r, j, x], n in nn implies c in mat(1, n, x) implies r in x implies j in interval(1, n) implies entry(snoc-row(c, n, r), 1, j) = entry(c, 1, j))
- `entry-of-submat` — forall([s, p, q, i, j, x], p in nn implies q in nn implies s in mat(succ(p), succ(q), x) implies i in interval(1, p) implies j in interval(1, q) implies entry(submat(s, p, q), i, j) = entry(s, succ(i), succ(j)))
- `entry-of-zeromat` — forall([a, m, n, i, j], is-ring(a) implies m in nn implies n in nn implies i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies entry(zeromat(a, m, n), i, j) = zero(a))
- `entry-unfold` — forall([m, i, j], entry(m, i, j) == nth(j, nth(i, m)))
- `enum-append-is-bijection` — forall([n in nn, sg in set, pt in set], not(pt in sg) implies forall([enm in bijection(ord-segment(n), sg)], vnb-lambda(i, ord-segment(succ(n)), if(i in ord-segment(n), enm(i), pt)) in bijection(ord-segment(succ(n)), union(sg, pair(pt, pt)))))
- `enum-fam-comm-monoid-in-fun` — forall([n in nn, m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s, phi in fun(ord-segment(n), s), f in fun(s, carr(m))], enum-fam(m, f, phi, n) in fun(nn, carr(m))))
- `enum-fam-in-fun` — forall([n in nn, ag], is-group(ag) implies forall([s, phi in fun(ord-segment(n), s), f in fun(s, carr(ag))], enum-fam(ag, f, phi, n) in fun(nn, carr(ag))))
- `enum-fam-value` — forall([n in nn, ag, u, phi, i in ord-segment(n)], (enum-fam(ag, u, phi, n))(i) == u(phi(i)))
- `eplus-assoc` — forall([x, y, z], x in rr-pos-star and y in rr-pos-star and z in rr-pos-star implies eplus(eplus(x, y), z) = eplus(x, eplus(y, z)))
- `eplus-closed` — forall([x, y], x in rr-pos-star and y in rr-pos-star implies eplus(x, y) in rr-pos-star)
- `eplus-comm` — forall([x, y], x in rr-pos-star and y in rr-pos-star implies eplus(x, y) = eplus(y, x))
- `eplus-in-fun` — eplus in fun(cartesian(rr-pos-star, rr-pos-star), rr-pos-star)
- `eplus-le-left` — forall([x, y], x in rr-pos-star and y in rr-pos-star implies x <= eplus(x, y))
- `eplus-mono` — forall([x, y, u, v], x in rr-pos-star and y in rr-pos-star and u in rr-pos-star and v in rr-pos-star implies x <= u and y <= v implies eplus(x, y) <= eplus(u, v))
- `eplus-pos-inf-left` — forall([y in rr-pos-star], eplus(pos-inf, y) = pos-inf)
- `eplus-pos-inf-right` — forall([x in rr-pos-star], eplus(x, pos-inf) = pos-inf)
- `eplus-real-defined` — forall([x, y], x in rr implies 0 <= x implies y in rr implies 0 <= y implies eplus(x, y) = binplus(x, y))
- `eplus-zero-left` — forall([x in rr-pos-star], eplus(0, x) = x)
- `eplus-zero-right` — forall([x in rr-pos-star], eplus(x, 0) = x)
- `eq-sym` — forall([a, b], a = b implies b = a)
- `eq-trans` — forall([a, b, c], a = b implies b = c implies a = c)
- `equicont-dense-conv-implies-unif-cauchy` — forall([s, fam], is-compact(s) implies fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies forsome([dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) and converges-on(s, fam, dseq)) implies is-unif-cauchy(s, fam))
- `equicont-dense-conv-ptwise-cauchy` — forall([s, fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr))], is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies forsome([dseq], is-dense-seq(s, dseq) and converges-on(s, fam, dseq)) implies is-ptwise-cauchy(s, fam))
- `equicont-subseq` — forall([s, t, fam, phi], is-equicontinuous(s, t, fam) implies strictly-mono-nn(phi) implies is-equicontinuous(s, t, subseq(fam, phi)))
- `equiv-mul-both` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([m, n, a, u, v], a in mat(m, n, carr(r)) implies is-invertible-mat(r, m, u) implies is-invertible-mat(r, n, v) implies mat-equiv(r, m, n, a, matmul(r, matmul(r, u, a), v))))
- `esum-bounded-implies-finite` — forall([f in fun(dom(f), rr-pos-star)], forsome([m in rr], forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn and s subset dom(f) implies finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, f, s) <= m)) implies esum(f) in rr)
- `esum-finite-iff-bounded` — forall([f in fun(dom(f), rr-pos-star)], esum(f) in rr iff forsome([m in rr], forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn and s subset dom(f) implies finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, f, s) <= m)))
- `esum-finite-implies-bounded` — forall([f in fun(dom(f), rr-pos-star)], esum(f) in rr implies forsome([m in rr], forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn and s subset dom(f) implies finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, f, s) <= m)))
- `esum-in` — forall([f in fun(dom(f), rr-pos-star)], esum(f) in rr-pos-star)
- `esum-least` — forall([f in fun(dom(f), rr-pos-star), b], b in rr-pos-star and forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn and s subset dom(f) implies finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, f, s) <= b) implies esum(f) <= b)
- `esum-partial-sum-type` — forall([f, s], f in fun(dom(f), rr-pos-star) implies s in set and card(s) in nn and s subset dom(f) implies finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, f, s) in rr-pos-star)
- `esum-upper` — forall([f in fun(dom(f), rr-pos-star), s], s in set and card(s) in nn and s subset dom(f) implies finsum(rr-pos-star-add-monoid, f, s) <= esum(f))
- `esup-empty` — esup(empty-set) = 0
- `esup-exists` — forall([s_], s_ subset rr-pos-star implies forsome([bb_ in rr-pos-star], forall([x in s_], x <= bb_) and forall([b], b in rr-pos-star and forall([x in s_], x <= b) implies bb_ <= b)))
- `esup-in` — forall([s], s subset rr-pos-star implies esup(s) in rr-pos-star)
- `esup-least` — forall([s], s subset rr-pos-star implies forall([b], b in rr-pos-star and forall([x in s], x <= b) implies esup(s) <= b))
- `esup-prop` — forall([s_], s_ subset rr-pos-star implies esup(s_) in rr-pos-star and forall([x in s_], x <= esup(s_)) and forall([b], b in rr-pos-star and forall([x in s_], x <= b) implies esup(s_) <= b))
- `esup-unique` — forall([s_, b1_, b2_], (b1_ in rr-pos-star and forall([x in s_], x <= b1_) and forall([b], b in rr-pos-star and forall([x in s_], x <= b) implies b1_ <= b)) and b2_ in rr-pos-star and forall([x in s_], x <= b2_) and forall([b], b in rr-pos-star and forall([x in s_], x <= b) implies b2_ <= b) implies b1_ = b2_)
- `esup-upper` — forall([s], s subset rr-pos-star implies forall([x in s], x <= esup(s)))
- `etail-subset-rr-pos-star` — forall([f], forall([cpsk_ in nn], f(cpsk_) in rr) implies forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpsk_)) implies forall([cpsn_], etail(f, cpsn_) subset rr-pos-star))
- `etimes-in-fun` — etimes in fun(cartesian(rr-pos-star, rr-pos-star), rr-pos-star)
- `etimes-pos-inf-left` — forall([y in rr-pos-star], not(y = 0) implies etimes(pos-inf, y) = pos-inf)
- `etimes-pos-inf-right` — forall([x in rr-pos-star], not(x = 0) implies etimes(x, pos-inf) = pos-inf)
- `etimes-pos-inf-zero` — etimes(pos-inf, 0) = 0
- `etimes-real-defined` — forall([x, y], x in rr implies 0 <= x implies y in rr implies 0 <= y implies etimes(x, y) = bintimes(x, y))
- `etimes-zero-pos-inf` — etimes(0, pos-inf) = 0
- `euclidean-division` — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], not(b = zero(s)) implies forsome([q in carr(s), r in carr(s)], a = (add(s))((mul(s))(q, b), r) and (r = zero(s) or succ((gauge(s))(r)) <= (gauge(s))(b)))))
- `euclidean-gauges-unfold` — forall([s], euclidean-gauges(s) == {dg in fun(carr(s), nn): has-div-remainder(s, dg)})
- `euclidean-ideal-has-generator` — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) implies forall([i], is-ideal(s, i) implies forsome([b in i], forall([a in i], a in principal-ideal(s, b)))))
- `euclidean-ring-has-gauge` — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) implies forsome([dg in fun(carr(s), nn)], has-div-remainder(s, dg)))
- `euclidean-ring-is-integral-domain` — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) implies is-integral-domain(s))
- `extend-const-ccint-fixes` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, icz_ in ccint(a, b), f], (extend-const(f, a, b))(icz_) == f(icz_))
- `extend-const-continuous-at` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a <= b implies forall([f], is-continuous-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([ict_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, extend-const(f, a, b), ict_))))
- `extend-const-deriv-bwd` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a <= b implies forall([f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr), icx_ in ooint(a, b), icl_], is-diff-at(extend-const(f, a, b), icx_, icl_) implies has-deriv-at(f, icx_, icl_)))
- `extend-const-deriv-fwd` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a <= b implies forall([f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr), icx_ in ooint(a, b), icl_], has-deriv-at(f, icx_, icl_) implies is-diff-at(extend-const(f, a, b), icx_, icl_)))
- `extend-const-deriv-iff` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a <= b implies forall([f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr), icx_ in ooint(a, b), icl_], has-deriv-at(f, icx_, icl_) iff is-diff-at(extend-const(f, a, b), icx_, icl_)))
- `extend-const-fixes` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, icz_ in rr], a <= icz_ implies icz_ <= b implies forall([f], (extend-const(f, a, b))(icz_) == f(icz_)))
- `extend-const-in-fun` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a <= b implies forall([f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], extend-const(f, a, b) in fun(rr, rr)))
- `extend-const-value` — forall([f, a, b, icz_ in rr], (extend-const(f, a, b))(icz_) == f(clamp(a, b, icz_)))
- `extends-on-trans` — forall([s, t, g2, g1, f], s subset t implies extends-on(t, g2, g1) implies extends-on(s, g1, f) implies extends-on(s, g2, f))
- `extreme-value-max` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forsome([c in ccint(a, b)], forall([x in ccint(a, b)], f(x) <= f(c))))
- `extreme-value-min` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forsome([c in ccint(a, b)], forall([x in ccint(a, b)], f(c) <= f(x))))
- `factorial-in-nn` — forall([n in nn], factorial(n) in nn)
- `factorial-mono` — forall([cexm_ in nn, cexk_ in nn], factorial(cexk_) <= factorial(cexk_ + cexm_))
- `factorial-real-pos` — forall([n in nn], factorial(n) in rr and 0 < factorial(n))
- `falling-in-nn` — forall([n in nn, m in nn], falling(n, m) in nn)
- `falling-in-nn-ind` — forall([m in nn, n in nn], falling(n, m) in nn and (n - m in nn or falling(n, m) = 0))
- `family-of-subsets-is-set` — forall([rcy_, rcc_], rcy_ in set implies forall([rcu_ in rcc_], rcu_ subset rcy_) implies rcc_ in set)
- `fibrewise-finite` — forall([p_, g_], g_ in set and card(g_) in nn implies forall([t_], t_ in set and forall([y_ in t_], p_(y_) in g_) and forall([c_ in g_], card({x in t_: p_(x) = c_}) in nn) implies card(t_) in nn))
- `field-additive-ag-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-field(a, b, f) implies is-hom-abelian-group(field-additive-ag(a), field-additive-ag(b), f))
- `field-as-euclidean-ring-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-field(a, b, f) implies is-hom-euclidean-ring(field-as-euclidean-ring(a), field-as-euclidean-ring(b), f))
- `field-as-integral-domain-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-field(a, b, f) implies is-hom-integral-domain(field-as-integral-domain(a), field-as-integral-domain(b), f))
- `field-id-add` — forall([f_], add(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == add(f_))
- `field-id-carr` — forall([f_], carr(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == carr(f_))
- `field-id-mul` — forall([f_], mul(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == mul(f_))
- `field-id-neg` — forall([f_], neg(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == neg(f_))
- `field-id-one` — forall([f_], one(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == one(f_))
- `field-id-zero` — forall([f_], zero(field-as-integral-domain(f_)) == zero(f_))
- `field-is-field-ring` — forall([s_], is-field(s_) implies is-field-ring(field-as-integral-domain(s_)))
- `fin-enum-is-bijection` — forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies fin-enum(s) in bijection(ord-segment(card(s)), s))
- `fin-subset-monoid-is-comm-monoid` — forall([a_ in set], is-comm-monoid(fin-subset-monoid(a_)))
- `fin-subsets-has-empty` — forall([a_], empty-set in fin-subsets(a_))
- `fin-subsets-is-set` — forall([a_ in set], fin-subsets(a_) in set)
- `fin-subsets-membership` — forall([a_, s_], s_ in fin-subsets(a_) iff s_ in set and forall([z in s_], z in a_) and card(s_) in nn)
- `fin-subsets-union-closed` — forall([a_, s_, u_], s_ in fin-subsets(a_) implies u_ in fin-subsets(a_) implies union(s_, u_) in fin-subsets(a_))
- `finite-ball-subcover-r-net` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([r], r in rr and 0 <= r and not(0 = r) implies forsome([f], f subset ball-cover(s, r) and card(f) in nn and is-open-cover(s, f)) implies forsome([n], card(n) in nn and is-r-net(s, n, pts(s), r))))
- `finite-implies-countable` — forall([rqd_ in set], card(rqd_) in nn implies is-countable(rqd_))
- `finite-set-induction` — forall([c_], empty-set in c_ and forall([s_], s_ in set and card(s_) in nn and s_ in c_ implies forall([x_], x_ in set and not(x_ in s_) implies union(s_, pair(x_, x_)) in c_)) implies forall([s_], s_ in set and card(s_) in nn implies s_ in c_))
- `finsum-abs-triangle` — forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn implies forall([a in fun(s, rr)], abs(finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), a, s)) <= finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, abs(a(i))), s)))
- `finsum-act-collect-gen` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([x in vec(md), s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([c in fun(s, carr(scal(md)))], (act(md))(finsum(ring-additive-ag(scal(md)), c, s), x) = finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(z, s, (act(md))(c(z), x)), s))))
- `finsum-act-distrib-gen` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([r in carr(scal(md)), s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(module-vector-ag(md)))], (act(md))(r, finsum(module-vector-ag(md), f, s)) = finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(z, s, (act(md))(r, f(z))), s))))
- `finsum-add` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(m)), h in fun(s, carr(m))], finsum(m, vnb-lambda(z, s, (opr(m))(f(z), h(z))), s) = (opr(m))(finsum(m, f, s), finsum(m, h, s)))))
- `finsum-add-ag` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag)), h in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, s, (opr(ag))(f(z), h(z))), s) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f, s), finsum(ag, h, s)))))
- `finsum-all-id` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag))], forall([z in s], f(z) = iden(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f, s) = iden(ag))))
- `finsum-all-id-ptwise` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([f_], forall([z_ in s_], f_(z_) = iden(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f_, s_) = iden(ag))))
- `finsum-cm-congruence-q` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f, g], forall([z in s], f(z) = g(z)) implies finsum(m, f, s) == finsum(m, g, s))))
- `finsum-cm-insert-ptwise` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([k_ in set], not(k_ in s_) implies forall([f_], forall([z_ in union(s_, pair(k_, k_))], f_(z_) in carr(m)) implies finsum(m, f_, union(s_, pair(k_, k_))) = (opr(m))(finsum(m, f_, s_), f_(k_))))))
- `finsum-cm-singleton-ptwise` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([x_ in set, f_], f_(x_) in carr(m) implies finsum(m, f_, pair(x_, x_)) = f_(x_)))
- `finsum-cm-union-disjoint` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([t_ in set], card(t_) in nn implies forall([z_ in t_], not(z_ in s_)) implies forall([f_], forall([z_ in union(s_, t_)], f_(z_) in carr(m)) implies finsum(m, f_, union(s_, t_)) = (opr(m))(finsum(m, f_, s_), finsum(m, f_, t_))))))
- `finsum-comm-monoid-permutation-invariance` — forall([n in nn, m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([g in fun(nn, carr(m)), h in fun(nn, carr(m)), phi in bijection(ord-segment(n), ord-segment(n))], forall([i in ord-segment(n)], h(i) = g(phi(i))) implies sum-ag(m, g, n) = sum-ag(m, h, n)))
- `finsum-comm-monoid-type` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(m))], finsum(m, f, s) in carr(m))))
- `finsum-comm-monoid-type-ptwise` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f], forall([z in s], f(z) in carr(m)) implies finsum(m, f, s) in carr(m))))
- `finsum-comm-monoid-well-defined` — forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(m)), enm in bijection(ord-segment(card(s)), s)], finsum(m, f, s) = sum-ag(m, enum-fam(m, f, enm, card(s)), card(s)))))
- `finsum-congruence` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f, g], forall([z in s], f(z) = g(z)) implies forall([z in s], f(z) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f, s) = finsum(ag, g, s))))
- `finsum-congruence-guarded` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag)), g], forall([z in s], f(z) = g(z)) implies finsum(ag, f, s) = finsum(ag, g, s))))
- `finsum-congruence-q` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f, g], forall([z in s], f(z) = g(z)) implies finsum(ag, f, s) == finsum(ag, g, s))))
- `finsum-embed` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([s2 in set], card(s2) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s2, carr(ag))], s subset s2 implies forall([z], z in s2 and not(z in s) implies f(z) = iden(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f, s2) = finsum(ag, f, s)))))
- `finsum-empty` — forall([ag, f], finsum(ag, f, empty-set) == iden(ag))
- `finsum-fiber` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t in set], card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in fun(s, t), f in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, s) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(t_, t, finsum(ag, f, {s_ in s: phi(s_) = t_})), t)))))
- `finsum-fiber-slice` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([k in set], card(k) in nn implies forall([ph, tp, ff, al, be], al in fun(k, s) implies forall([k_ in k], ph(al(k_)) = tp) implies forall([w_ in s], ph(w_) = tp implies be(w_) in k) implies forall([k_ in k], be(al(k_)) = k_) implies forall([w_ in s], ph(w_) = tp implies al(be(w_)) = w_) implies forall([z_ in s], ff(z_) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, ff, {s_ in s: ph(s_) = tp}) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, k, ff(al(z))), k)))))
- `finsum-fiber-value` — forall([ag, ff, ph, s, t, pt in t], (vnb-lambda(t_, t, finsum(ag, ff, {s_ in s: ph(s_) = t_})))(pt) == finsum(ag, ff, {s_ in s: ph(s_) = pt}))
- `finsum-fubini` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies forall([y], y in set and card(y) in nn implies forall([f in fun(cartesian(x, y), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(i, x, finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(j, y, f([i, j])), y)), x) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(j, y, finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(i, x, f([i, j])), x)), y)))))
- `finsum-fubini-c` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x in set], card(x) in nn implies forall([y in set], card(y) in nn implies forall([f in fun(cartesian(x, y), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(i, x, finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(j, y, f([i, j])), y)), x) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(j, y, finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(i, x, f([i, j])), x)), y)))))
- `finsum-in-subset` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([sm], iden(ag) in sm implies forall([x_ in sm, y_ in sm], (opr(ag))(x_, y_) in sm) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag))], forall([z in s], f(z) in sm) implies finsum(ag, f, s) in sm))))
- `finsum-insert` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies forall([k], k in set and not(k in x) implies forall([f in fun(union(x, pair(k, k)), carr(m))], finsum(m, f, union(x, pair(k, k))) = (opr(m))(finsum(m, f, x), f(k))))))
- `finsum-insert-ag` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x in set], card(x) in nn implies forall([k in set], not(k in x) implies forall([f in fun(union(x, pair(k, k)), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, union(x, pair(k, k))) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f, x), f(k))))))
- `finsum-insert-from-enum` — forall([m, f, x, k, enm], card(x) in nn implies card(union(x, pair(k, k))) = succ(card(x)) implies enm(card(x)) == k implies forall([i in ord-segment(card(x))], enm(i) == (fin-enum(x))(i)) implies finsum(m, f, union(x, pair(k, k))) = sum-ag(m, enum-fam(m, f, enm, card(union(x, pair(k, k)))), card(union(x, pair(k, k)))) implies finsum(m, f, union(x, pair(k, k))) = (opr(m))(finsum(m, f, x), f(k)))
- `finsum-insert-ptwise` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([k_ in set], not(k_ in s_) implies forall([f_], forall([z_ in union(s_, pair(k_, k_))], f_(z_) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f_, union(s_, pair(k_, k_))) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f_, s_), f_(k_))))))
- `finsum-interval-peel` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(interval(1, succ(n)), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, interval(1, succ(n))) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f, interval(1, n)), f(succ(n)))))
- `finsum-interval-shift` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(interval(1, succ(n)), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, interval(1, succ(n))) = (opr(ag))(f(1), finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), f(succ(z))), interval(1, n)))))
- `finsum-le-termwise` — forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn implies forall([a in fun(s, rr), b in fun(s, rr)], forall([i in s], a(i) <= b(i)) implies finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), a, s) <= finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), b, s)))
- `finsum-ord-peel` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([n in nn, f in fun(ord-segment(succ(n)), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, ord-segment(succ(n))) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f, ord-segment(n)), f(n))))
- `finsum-reindex` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in bijection(t, s), f in fun(s, carr(m))], finsum(m, f, s) = finsum(m, vnb-lambda(z, t, f(phi(z))), t)))))
- `finsum-reindex-ag` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in bijection(t, s), f in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, s) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, t, f(phi(z))), t)))))
- `finsum-reindex-inverse` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in fun(t, s), psi in fun(s, t)], forall([u_ in t], psi(phi(u_)) = u_) implies forall([v_ in s], phi(psi(v_)) = v_) implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, s) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, t, f(phi(z))), t))))))
- `finsum-reindex-inverse-ptwise` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([t], t in set and card(t) in nn implies forall([phi in fun(t, s), psi in fun(s, t)], forall([u_ in t], psi(phi(u_)) = u_) implies forall([v_ in s], phi(psi(v_)) = v_) implies forall([ff], forall([z_ in s], ff(z_) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, ff, s) = finsum(ag, vnb-lambda(z, t, ff(phi(z))), t))))))
- `finsum-ring-distrib-left` — forall([rng], is-commutative-ring(rng) implies forall([r in carr(rng), s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(rng))], (mul(rng))(r, finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rng), f, s)) = finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rng), vnb-lambda(z, s, (mul(rng))(r, f(z))), s))))
- `finsum-ring-distrib-left-gen` — forall([rng], is-ring(rng) implies forall([r in carr(rng), s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(rng))], (mul(rng))(r, finsum(ring-additive-ag(rng), f, s)) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(rng), vnb-lambda(z, s, (mul(rng))(r, f(z))), s))))
- `finsum-ring-distrib-right` — forall([rng], is-commutative-ring(rng) implies forall([r in carr(rng), s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(rng))], (mul(rng))(finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rng), f, s), r) = finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rng), vnb-lambda(z, s, (mul(rng))(f(z), r)), s))))
- `finsum-ring-distrib-right-gen` — forall([rng], is-ring(rng) implies forall([r in carr(rng), s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(rng))], (mul(rng))(finsum(ring-additive-ag(rng), f, s), r) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(rng), vnb-lambda(z, s, (mul(rng))(f(z), r)), s))))
- `finsum-ring-scalar-zz` — forall([rng], is-commutative-ring(rng) implies forall([r in carr(rng), c in zz, a in carr(rng)], (mul(rng))(r, zz-act(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rng), c, a)) = zz-act(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rng), c, (mul(rng))(r, a))))
- `finsum-rr-in-rr` — forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([f_ in fun(s_, rr)], finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), f_, s_) in rr))
- `finsum-rr-nonneg` — forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([f_ in fun(s_, rr)], forall([z_ in s_], 0 <= f_(z_)) implies 0 <= finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), f_, s_)))
- `finsum-single-support` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag)), i0 in s], forall([j in s], not(j = i0) implies f(j) = iden(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f, s) = f(i0))))
- `finsum-singleton` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x in set, f in fun(pair(x, x), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, pair(x, x)) = f(x)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `finsum-singleton-ptwise` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([x_ in set, f_], f_(x_) in carr(ag) implies finsum(ag, f_, pair(x_, x_)) = f_(x_)))
- `finsum-sq-nonneg` — forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn implies forall([a in fun(s, rr)], 0 <= finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, a(i) * a(i)), s)))
- `finsum-two-support` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag)), i0 in s, i1 in s], not(i0 = i1) implies forall([j in s], not(j = i0) implies not(j = i1) implies f(j) = iden(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f, s) = (opr(ag))(f(i0), f(i1)))))
- `finsum-type` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f, s) in carr(ag))))
- `finsum-type-ptwise` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f], forall([z in s], f(z) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f, s) in carr(ag))))
- `finsum-union-disjoint` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([t_ in set], card(t_) in nn implies forall([z_ in t_], not(z_ in s_)) implies forall([f_], forall([z_ in union(s_, t_)], f_(z_) in carr(ag)) implies finsum(ag, f_, union(s_, t_)) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f_, s_), finsum(ag, f_, t_))))))
- `finsum-union-disjoint-fun` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([s_ in set], card(s_) in nn implies forall([t_ in set], card(t_) in nn implies intersection(s_, t_) = empty-set implies forall([f_ in fun(union(s_, t_), carr(ag))], finsum(ag, f_, union(s_, t_)) = (opr(ag))(finsum(ag, f_, s_), finsum(ag, f_, t_))))))
- `finsum-well-defined` — forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(ag)), enm in bijection(ord-segment(card(s)), s)], finsum(ag, f, s) = sum-ag(ag, enum-fam(ag, f, enm, card(s)), card(s)))))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `finsupp-in-set` — forall([a_, m_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies finsupp(a_, m_) in set)
- `finsupp-membership` — forall([a_, m_, f_], f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) iff f_ in fun(carr(m_), carr(a_)) and card(supp(a_, m_, f_)) in nn)
- `finsupp-unfold` — forall([a_, m_], finsupp(a_, m_) == {f_ in fun(carr(m_), carr(a_)): card(supp(a_, m_, f_)) in nn})
- `fip-implies-compact` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([c], has-fip(s, c) implies forsome([p], p in intersection-of(c))) implies is-compact(s))
- `four-power-halving` — forall([gbn_ in nn], 4 ^ gbn_ * (recip(2 ^ gbn_) * recip(2 ^ gbn_)) = 1)
- `free-length-le-generators` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n, m, u, v], n in nn implies m in nn implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies v in mat(m, 1, vec(md)) implies generates(md, n, u) implies rel-free(md, m, v) implies m <= n))
- `free-transport` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, v, qm], v in mat(m, 1, vec(md)) implies is-invertible-mat(scal(md), m, qm) implies rel-free(md, m, v) implies rel-free(md, m, matact(md, qm, v))))
- `fsm-carr` — forall([a_], carr(fin-subset-monoid(a_)) == fin-subsets(a_))
- `fsm-iden` — forall([a_], iden(fin-subset-monoid(a_)) == empty-set)
- `fsm-opr` — forall([a_], opr(fin-subset-monoid(a_)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(fin-subsets(a_), fin-subsets(a_)), union(x_, y_)))
- `fun-append-constant-piece` — forall([rsbh_ in fun(rr, rr), rsbc_ in rr, rsbd_ in rr, rsbk_ in rr, rsbv_ in rr], rsbc_ < rsbd_ implies forsome([rsbg_ in fun(rr, rr)], forall([rsbt_ in rr], rsbt_ <= rsbc_ implies rsbg_(rsbt_) = rsbh_(rsbt_)) and forall([rsbt_ in rr], rsbc_ < rsbt_ implies rsbt_ < rsbd_ implies rsbg_(rsbt_) = rsbk_) and rsbg_(rsbd_) = rsbv_))
- `fun-apply-type-c` — forall([f, a_, b_, x_], f in fun(a_, b_) implies x_ in a_ implies f(x_) in b_)
- `fun-codomain-superset` — forall([f, a, b, c], f in fun(a, b) implies b subset c implies f in fun(a, c))
- `fun-domain-in-set` — forall([a, b, f in fun(a, b)], a in set)
- `fun-into-seg-zero-absurd` — forall([a, f, x], f in fun(a, ord-segment(0)) implies x in a implies falsity)
- `fun-range-membership` — forall([x, f, a], f in fun(x) implies a in x implies f(a) in ran(f))
- `funcomp-succ-type` — forall([x, q in nn, f in fun(interval(1, succ(q)), x)], vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, q), f(succ(z))) in fun(interval(1, q), x))
- `g-of-remainder-dfun` — forall([m, f, g, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies n in nn implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= n implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))) implies g((vadd(m))(f(x), (vneg(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n)))) = (compose(g, f))(x) - taylor-poly(compose(g, f), a, n, x))
- `gauge-is-degree` — forall([s], is-euclidean-ring(s) implies gauge(s) in fun(carr(s), nn) and has-div-remainder(s, gauge(s)))
- `gauges-in-fun` — forall([s, dg in euclidean-gauges(s)], dg in fun(carr(s), nn))
- `gauges-mem-build` — forall([s, dg in fun(carr(s), nn)], has-div-remainder(s, dg) implies dg in euclidean-gauges(s))
- `gauges-spec` — forall([s, dg in euclidean-gauges(s)], has-div-remainder(s, dg))
- `generalized-mvt` — forall([f, g, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and g in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x) and is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, x, l)) and forsome([m], is-diff-at(g, x, m))) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < b and forsome([l, m], is-diff-at(f, theta, l) and is-diff-at(g, theta, m) and l * (g(b) - g(a)) = m * (f(b) - f(a)))))
- `generalized-mvt-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([f, g], f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies g in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies is-continuous-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies is-continuous-on(g, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([x in ooint(a, b)], forsome([l], has-deriv-at(f, x, l))) implies forall([x in ooint(a, b)], forsome([m], has-deriv-at(g, x, m))) implies forsome([theta in ooint(a, b), l, m], has-deriv-at(f, theta, l) and has-deriv-at(g, theta, m) and l * (g(b) - g(a)) = m * (f(b) - f(a)))))
- `generates-coeff-matrix` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, m, u, v], u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies v in mat(m, 1, vec(md)) implies (n = 0 implies m = 0) implies generates(md, n, u) implies forsome([cm in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md)))], v = matact(md, cm, u))))
- `generates-implies-spans-vec` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], generates(md, n, u) implies spans(md, n, u, vec(md))))
- `generates-transport` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u, pm], u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies is-invertible-mat(scal(md), n, pm) implies generates(md, n, u) implies generates(md, n, matact(md, pm, u))))
- `geometric-partial-sum` — forall([r], r in rr and not(r = 1) implies forall([k in nn], series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(n, nn, r ^ n), k) = (1 - r ^ k) * recip(1 - r)))
- `geometric-partial-sum-mul` — forall([k in nn, r in rr], (1 - r) * series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(n, nn, r ^ n), k) = 1 - r ^ k)
- `geometric-series-converges` — forall([r in rr], 0 <= r implies r < 1 implies series-converges(vnb-lambda(n, nn, r ^ n)))
- `geometric-series-converges-to` — forall([r], r in rr and abs(r) < 1 implies series-converges-to(vnb-lambda(n, nn, r ^ n), recip(1 - r)))
- `gmvt-aux-cont` — forall([f, g, a, b, x], a in rr implies b in rr implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, x) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(z) * (g(b) - g(a)) - g(z) * (f(b) - f(a))), x))
- `gmvt-aux-diff` — forall([f, g, a, b, x, l, m], a in rr implies b in rr implies is-diff-at(f, x, l) implies is-diff-at(g, x, m) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(z) * (g(b) - g(a)) - g(z) * (f(b) - f(a))), x, l * (g(b) - g(a)) - m * (f(b) - f(a))))
- `gmvt-with-known-derivative` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([f, g, itv_ in fun(ooint(a, b), rr)], f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies is-continuous-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([x in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(f, x, itv_(x))) implies g in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies is-continuous-on(g, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([x in ooint(a, b)], forsome([m], has-deriv-at(g, x, m))) implies forsome([xi in ooint(a, b), m], has-deriv-at(g, xi, m) and itv_(xi) * (g(b) - g(a)) = m * (f(b) - f(a)))))
- `gof-in-fun` — forall([m, f, g], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies compose(g, f) in fun(rr, rr))
- `gof-nth-deriv-dfun` — forall([m, f, g, n, t], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies n in nn implies t in rr implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= n implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))) implies is-diff-at-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, n), t, (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(t)) implies (nth-deriv(compose(g, f), succ(n)))(t) = g((nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(t)))
- `gof-taylor-diff-dfun` — forall([m, f, g, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies n in nn implies taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= n implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))) implies taylor-differentiable(compose(g, f), a, x, n))
- `good-step` — forall([m, s, f, t, x], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) and good-sub(m, s, f, t) and x in vec(m) and not(x in t) implies good-sub(m, s, f, span-add-one(m, t, x)))
- `good-sub-in-power` — forall([m, s, f, t], good-sub(m, s, f, t) implies t in power(vec(m)))
- `good-sub-self-nvs` — forall([m, s, f], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, s) implies is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies good-sub(m, s, f, s))
- `good-sub-submodule` — forall([m, s, f, t], good-sub(m, s, f, t) implies is-submodule(m, t))
- `goursat-nest` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) subset u implies forsome([gouw_ in cc], (0 <= real-part(gouw_) and 0 <= imag-part(gouw_) and real-part(gouw_) + imag-part(gouw_) <= 1) and forall([goun_ in nn], forsome([goux_, gouy_, gouz_], goux_ in cc and gouy_ in cc and gouz_ in cc and (0 <= real-part(goux_) and 0 <= imag-part(goux_) and real-part(goux_) + imag-part(goux_) <= 1) and (0 <= real-part(gouy_) and 0 <= imag-part(gouy_) and real-part(gouy_) + imag-part(gouy_) <= 1) and (0 <= real-part(gouz_) and 0 <= imag-part(gouz_) and real-part(gouz_) + imag-part(gouz_) <= 1) and (magnitude(gouy_ - goux_) + magnitude(gouz_ - gouy_)) + magnitude(goux_ - gouz_) <= 6 * recip(2 ^ goun_) and magnitude(goux_ - gouw_) <= 12 * recip(2 ^ goun_) and magnitude(tri-int(f, goua_, goub_, gouc_)) <= 4 ^ goun_ * magnitude(tri-int(f, (real-part(goux_) * goua_ + imag-part(goux_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(goux_) - imag-part(goux_)) * gouc_, (real-part(gouy_) * goua_ + imag-part(gouy_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(gouy_) - imag-part(gouy_)) * gouc_, (real-part(gouz_) * goua_ + imag-part(gouz_) * goub_) + (1 - real-part(gouz_) - imag-part(gouz_)) * gouc_)))))))
- `goursat-triangle` — forall([u, f], holomorphic-on(u, f) implies forall([goua_ in u, goub_ in u, gouc_ in u], conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) subset u implies tri-int(f, goua_, goub_, gouc_) = 0))
- `goursat-triangle-except` — forall([u, f, goxx_], is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u) implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies goxx_ in u implies holomorphic-on(difference(u, singleton(goxx_)), restrict(f, difference(u, singleton(goxx_)))) implies forall([goxa_ in u, goxb_ in u, goxc_ in u], conv3(goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) subset u implies tri-int(f, goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) = 0))
- `goursat-vertex` — forall([u, f, goxx_], is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u) implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies goxx_ in u implies holomorphic-on(difference(u, singleton(goxx_)), restrict(f, difference(u, singleton(goxx_)))) implies forall([goxb_ in u, goxc_ in u], conv3(goxx_, goxb_, goxc_) subset u implies tri-int(f, goxx_, goxb_, goxc_) = 0))
- `greedy-chain-is-chain` — forall([phi, grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies is-chain(grd, porel, greedy-chain(phi, grd, porel)))
- `group-assoc` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `group-cancel-left` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))(c, a) = (opr(s))(c, b) implies a = b))
- `group-carrier-closed-opr` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, b) in carr(s)))
- `group-identity-in` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies iden(s) in carr(s))
- `group-inv-in` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (inv(s))(a) in carr(s)))
- `group-left-id` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(iden(s), a) = a))
- `group-left-inv` — forall([s], is-group(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))((inv(s))(a), a) = iden(s)))
- `hahn-banach` — forall([m, s, f], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(m)) and is-submodule(m, s) and is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies forsome([g_], is-linear-functional-on(m, vec(m), g_) and extends-on(s, g_, f) and forall([w_ in vec(m)], abs(g_(w_)) <= dual-norm-on(m, s, f) * (vnrm(m))(w_))))
- `hahn-banach-extend-one` — forall([m, s, f, v], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-submodule(m, s) and is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) and v in vec(m) and not(v in s) implies forsome([g_], is-linear-functional-on(m, span-add-one(m, s, v), g_) and extends-on(s, g_, f) and forall([w_ in span-add-one(m, s, v)], abs(g_(w_)) <= dual-norm-on(m, s, f) * (vnrm(m))(w_))))
- `half-magnitude` — forall([goux_ in cc], magnitude(1/2 * goux_) = 1/2 * magnitude(goux_))
- `has-deriv-at-affine` — forall([hdr_], pos-rr(hdr_) implies forall([f, hdc_ in rr, hdm_ in rr, hdx_ in rr], restrict(f, ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)) in fun(ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_), rr) implies forall([hbx_ in ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)], f(hbx_) == hdc_ + hdm_ * hbx_) implies has-deriv-at(f, hdx_, hdm_)))
- `has-deriv-at-affine-chain` — forall([crr_], pos-rr(crr_) implies forall([g, h, crp_ in rr, crq_ in rr, crx_ in rr, crm_], restrict(h, ooint(crx_ - crr_, crx_ + crr_)) in fun(ooint(crx_ - crr_, crx_ + crr_), rr) implies forall([cry_ in ooint(crx_ - crr_, crx_ + crr_)], h(cry_) == g(crp_ + crq_ * cry_)) implies has-deriv-at(g, crp_ + crq_ * crx_, crm_) implies has-deriv-at(h, crx_, crm_ * crq_)))
- `has-deriv-at-chain` — forall([crr_], pos-rr(crr_) implies forall([f, g, h, crx_, crl_, crm_], restrict(h, ooint(crx_ - crr_, crx_ + crr_)) in fun(ooint(crx_ - crr_, crx_ + crr_), rr) implies forall([cry_ in ooint(crx_ - crr_, crx_ + crr_)], h(cry_) == g(f(cry_))) implies has-deriv-at(f, crx_, crl_) implies has-deriv-at(g, f(crx_), crm_) implies has-deriv-at(h, crx_, crm_ * crl_)))
- `has-deriv-at-const` — forall([hdr_], pos-rr(hdr_) implies forall([f, hdc_ in rr, hdx_ in rr], restrict(f, ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)) in fun(ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_), rr) implies forall([hbx_ in ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)], f(hbx_) == hdc_) implies has-deriv-at(f, hdx_, 0)))
- `has-deriv-at-difference` — forall([hdr_], pos-rr(hdr_) implies forall([f, g, h, hdx_, hdl_, hdm_], restrict(h, ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)) in fun(ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_), rr) implies restrict(g, ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)) in fun(ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_), rr) implies forall([hbx_ in ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)], h(hbx_) == f(hbx_) - g(hbx_)) implies has-deriv-at(f, hdx_, hdl_) implies has-deriv-at(g, hdx_, hdm_) implies has-deriv-at(h, hdx_, hdl_ - hdm_)))
- `has-deriv-at-identity` — forall([hdr_], pos-rr(hdr_) implies forall([f, hdx_ in rr], restrict(f, ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)) in fun(ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_), rr) implies forall([hbx_ in ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)], f(hbx_) == hbx_) implies has-deriv-at(f, hdx_, 1)))
- `has-deriv-at-iff-diff-at` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), a, icl_], has-deriv-at(f, a, icl_) iff is-diff-at(f, a, icl_))
- `has-deriv-at-iff-one-sided` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) iff has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) and has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_))
- `has-deriv-at-iff-within-ooint` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) iff forsome([osv_], pos-rr(osv_) and has-deriv-at-within(f, ooint(osx_ - osv_, osx_ + osv_), osx_, osl_)))
- `has-deriv-at-implies-left` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_))
- `has-deriv-at-implies-right` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_))
- `has-deriv-at-in-rr` — forall([f, a, icl_], has-deriv-at(f, a, icl_) implies icl_ in rr)
- `has-deriv-at-intro` — forall([f, a, icl_, ice_], pos-rr(ice_) implies is-diff-on(rr-normed-field, ooint(a - ice_, a + ice_), restrict(f, ooint(a - ice_, a + ice_)), a, icl_) implies has-deriv-at(f, a, icl_))
- `has-deriv-at-local` — forall([icr_], pos-rr(icr_) implies forall([f, g, icx_, icl_], restrict(f, ooint(icx_ - icr_, icx_ + icr_)) in fun(ooint(icx_ - icr_, icx_ + icr_), rr) implies forall([hbx_ in ooint(icx_ - icr_, icx_ + icr_)], f(hbx_) == g(hbx_)) implies has-deriv-at(g, icx_, icl_) implies has-deriv-at(f, icx_, icl_)))
- `has-deriv-at-maps-into` — forall([f, crx_, crl_], has-deriv-at(f, crx_, crl_) implies forall([crd_], pos-rr(crd_) implies forall([crb_], pos-rr(crb_) implies forsome([crw_], pos-rr(crw_) and crw_ <= crb_ and forall([cry_ in ooint(crx_ - crw_, crx_ + crw_)], f(cry_) in ooint(f(crx_) - crd_, f(crx_) + crd_))))))
- `has-deriv-at-of-one-sided` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies has-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_))
- `has-deriv-at-of-within-ooint` — forall([osx_, osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forall([f, osl_], has-deriv-at-within(f, ooint(osx_ - osr_, osx_ + osr_), osx_, osl_) implies has-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_)))
- `has-deriv-at-product` — forall([icr_], pos-rr(icr_) implies forall([f, g, h, icx_, icl_, icm_], restrict(h, ooint(icx_ - icr_, icx_ + icr_)) in fun(ooint(icx_ - icr_, icx_ + icr_), rr) implies forall([hbx_ in ooint(icx_ - icr_, icx_ + icr_)], h(hbx_) == f(hbx_) * g(hbx_)) implies has-deriv-at(f, icx_, icl_) implies has-deriv-at(g, icx_, icm_) implies has-deriv-at(h, icx_, icl_ * g(icx_) + icm_ * f(icx_))))
- `has-deriv-at-pt-in-rr` — forall([f, a, icl_], has-deriv-at(f, a, icl_) implies a in rr)
- `has-deriv-at-real-mul` — forall([hdr_], pos-rr(hdr_) implies forall([g, h, hdc_ in rr, hdx_, hdm_], restrict(h, ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)) in fun(ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_), rr) implies forall([hbx_ in ooint(hdx_ - hdr_, hdx_ + hdr_)], h(hbx_) == hdc_ * g(hbx_)) implies has-deriv-at(g, hdx_, hdm_) implies has-deriv-at(h, hdx_, hdc_ * hdm_)))
- `has-deriv-at-shrink` — forall([f, a, icl_], has-deriv-at(f, a, icl_) implies forall([icr_], pos-rr(icr_) implies forsome([icw_], pos-rr(icw_) and icw_ <= icr_ and is-diff-on(rr-normed-field, ooint(a - icw_, a + icw_), restrict(f, ooint(a - icw_, a + icw_)), a, icl_))))
- `has-deriv-at-sum` — forall([icr_], pos-rr(icr_) implies forall([f, g, h, icx_, icl_, icm_], restrict(h, ooint(icx_ - icr_, icx_ + icr_)) in fun(ooint(icx_ - icr_, icx_ + icr_), rr) implies forall([hbx_ in ooint(icx_ - icr_, icx_ + icr_)], h(hbx_) == f(hbx_) + g(hbx_)) implies has-deriv-at(f, icx_, icl_) implies has-deriv-at(g, icx_, icm_) implies has-deriv-at(h, icx_, icl_ + icm_)))
- `has-deriv-at-to-within-ooint` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies forall([osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forsome([osv_], pos-rr(osv_) and osv_ <= osr_ and has-deriv-at-within(f, ooint(osx_ - osv_, osx_ + osv_), osx_, osl_))))
- `has-deriv-at-unique` — forall([f, a, icl_, icm_], has-deriv-at(f, a, icl_) implies has-deriv-at(f, a, icm_) implies icl_ = icm_)
- `has-deriv-at-value-in-rr` — forall([f, hdx_, hdl_], has-deriv-at(f, hdx_, hdl_) implies f(hdx_) in rr)
- `has-left-deriv-at-in-rr` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies osl_ in rr)
- `has-left-deriv-at-intro` — forall([f, osx_, osl_, osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies has-deriv-at-within(f, ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_), osx_, osl_) implies has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_))
- `has-left-deriv-at-local` — forall([osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forall([f, g, osx_, osl_], restrict(f, ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_)) in fun(ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_), rr) implies forall([osy_ in ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_)], f(osy_) == g(osy_)) implies has-left-deriv-at(g, osx_, osl_) implies has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_)))
- `has-left-deriv-at-pt-in-rr` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies osx_ in rr)
- `has-left-deriv-at-shrink` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies forall([osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forsome([osv_], pos-rr(osv_) and osv_ <= osr_ and has-deriv-at-within(f, ocint(osx_ - osv_, osx_), osx_, osl_))))
- `has-left-deriv-at-sum` — forall([osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forall([f, g, h, osx_, osl_, osm_], restrict(h, ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_)) in fun(ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_), rr) implies forall([osy_ in ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_)], h(osy_) == f(osy_) + g(osy_)) implies has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies has-left-deriv-at(g, osx_, osm_) implies has-left-deriv-at(h, osx_, osl_ + osm_)))
- `has-left-deriv-at-unique` — forall([f, osx_, osl_, osm_], has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osm_) implies osl_ = osm_)
- `has-right-deriv-at-in-rr` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies osl_ in rr)
- `has-right-deriv-at-intro` — forall([f, osx_, osl_, osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies has-deriv-at-within(f, coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_), osx_, osl_) implies has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_))
- `has-right-deriv-at-local` — forall([osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forall([f, g, osx_, osl_], restrict(f, coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_)) in fun(coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_), rr) implies forall([osy_ in coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_)], f(osy_) == g(osy_)) implies has-right-deriv-at(g, osx_, osl_) implies has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_)))
- `has-right-deriv-at-pt-in-rr` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies osx_ in rr)
- `has-right-deriv-at-shrink` — forall([f, osx_, osl_], has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies forall([osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forsome([osv_], pos-rr(osv_) and osv_ <= osr_ and has-deriv-at-within(f, coint(osx_, osx_ + osv_), osx_, osl_))))
- `has-right-deriv-at-sum` — forall([osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forall([f, g, h, osx_, osl_, osm_], restrict(h, coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_)) in fun(coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_), rr) implies forall([osy_ in coint(osx_, osx_ + osr_)], h(osy_) == f(osy_) + g(osy_)) implies has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies has-right-deriv-at(g, osx_, osm_) implies has-right-deriv-at(h, osx_, osl_ + osm_)))
- `has-right-deriv-at-unique` — forall([f, osx_, osl_, osm_], has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osm_) implies osl_ = osm_)
- `hb-extend-construct` — forall([m, s, f, v, a_], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-submodule(m, s) and is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) and v in vec(m) and not(v in s) and a_ in rr implies forsome([g_], is-linear-functional-on(m, span-add-one(m, s, v), g_) and extends-on(s, g_, f) and forall([y_ in s, r_ in rr], g_((vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, v))) = f(y_) + r_ * a_)))
- `hb-gap` — forall([m, s, f, v], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-submodule(m, s) and is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) and v in vec(m) and not(v in s) implies forsome([a_ in rr], forall([y_ in s, r_ in rr], abs(f(y_) + r_ * a_) <= dual-norm-on(m, s, f) * (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, v))))))
- `hb-gap-bound` — forall([m, s, f, v, c], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, s) implies is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies v in vec(m) implies c in rr implies forall([x_ in s], abs(f(x_)) <= c * (vnrm(m))(x_)) implies 0 <= c implies forsome([a_ in rr], forall([y_ in s, r_ in rr], abs(f(y_) + r_ * a_) <= c * (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, v))))))
- `hb-good-has-maximal` — forall([m, s, f], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(m)) implies is-submodule(m, s) implies is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies forsome([t], good-sub(m, s, f, t) and forall([u], good-sub(m, s, f, u) and t subset u implies t = u)))
- `hbg-core` — forall([m, s, f, v, c, a], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, s) implies is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies v in vec(m) implies c in rr implies a in rr implies forall([x_ in s], abs(f(x_)) <= c * (vnrm(m))(x_)) implies forall([u_ in s], -f(u_) - c * (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(u_, v)) <= a) implies forall([u_ in s], a <= c * (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(u_, v)) - f(u_)) implies forall([y_ in s, r_ in rr], abs(f(y_) + r_ * a) <= c * (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, v)))))
- `hbg-diff-norm` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([w_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(w_, (act(m))(-1, z_))) <= (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(w_, v_)) + (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(z_, v_))))
- `hbg-linfun-app-real` — forall([m, s, f, x_], is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies x_ in s implies f(x_) in rr)
- `hbg-neg-one-cancel` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([y_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(y_, v_), (act(m))(-1, v_)) = y_))
- `hbg-scale-back` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([y_ in vec(m), r_ in rr], not(r_ = 0) implies (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(recip(r_), y_)) = y_))
- `hbg-scale-decomp` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([y_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m), r_ in rr], not(r_ = 0) implies (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, v_)) = (act(m))(r_, (vadd(m))((act(m))(recip(r_), y_), v_))))
- `hbg-scale-neg` — forall([bd in rr, tt in rr, fu in rr, aa in rr, rho in rr], rho <= 0 implies -fu - bd * tt <= aa implies aa <= bd * tt - fu implies abs(rho * fu + rho * aa) <= bd * (-rho * tt))
- `hbg-scale-pos` — forall([bd in rr, tt in rr, fu in rr, aa in rr, rho in rr], 0 <= rho implies -fu - bd * tt <= aa implies aa <= bd * tt - fu implies abs(rho * fu + rho * aa) <= bd * (rho * tt))
- `hbg-sep` — forall([m, s, f, v, c], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, s) implies is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies v in vec(m) implies c in rr implies forall([x_ in s], abs(f(x_)) <= c * (vnrm(m))(x_)) implies 0 <= c implies forall([z_ in s, w_ in s], -f(z_) - c * (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(z_, v)) <= c * (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(w_, v)) - f(w_)))
- `hbg-shift` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([w_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(w_, (act(m))(-1, z_)) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(w_, v_), (act(m))(-1, (vadd(m))(z_, v_)))))
- `hbx-add-decomp` — forall([m, v, y1_, c1_, y2_, c2_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies v in vec(m) implies y1_ in vec(m) implies c1_ in rr implies y2_ in vec(m) implies c2_ in rr implies (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(y1_, (act(m))(c1_, v)), (vadd(m))(y2_, (act(m))(c2_, v))) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(y1_, y2_), (act(m))(c1_ + c2_, v)))
- `hbx-decomp-unique` — forall([m, s, v, p1_, c1_, p2_, c2_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, s) implies v in vec(m) implies not(v in s) implies p1_ in s implies c1_ in rr implies p2_ in s implies c2_ in rr implies (vadd(m))(p1_, (act(m))(c1_, v)) = (vadd(m))(p2_, (act(m))(c2_, v)) implies c1_ = c2_ and p1_ = p2_)
- `hbx-f-act` — forall([m, s, f, r_, x_], is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies r_ in rr implies x_ in s implies f((act(m))(r_, x_)) = r_ * f(x_))
- `hbx-f-add` — forall([m, s, f, x_, y_], is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies x_ in s implies y_ in s implies f((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = f(x_) + f(y_))
- `hbx-s-in-vec` — forall([m, s, p_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, s) implies p_ in s implies p_ in vec(m))
- `hbx-span-decomp` — forall([m, s, v, w_ in span-add-one(m, s, v)], forsome([y_ in s, r_ in rr], w_ = (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, v))))
- `hbx-span-in-vec` — forall([m, s, v, w_ in span-add-one(m, s, v)], w_ in vec(m))
- `hbx-span-mem-eq` — forall([m, s, v, w_, p_, c_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, s) implies v in vec(m) implies p_ in s implies c_ in rr implies w_ = (vadd(m))(p_, (act(m))(c_, v)) implies w_ in span-add-one(m, s, v))
- `hbx-vadd-exchange` — forall([m, aa_, bb_, cc_, dd_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies aa_ in vec(m) implies bb_ in vec(m) implies cc_ in vec(m) implies dd_ in vec(m) implies (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(aa_, bb_), (vadd(m))(cc_, dd_)) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(aa_, cc_), (vadd(m))(bb_, dd_)))
- `hbx-value` — forall([m, s, f, v, a_, u_, p_, c_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, s) implies is-linear-functional-on(m, s, f) implies v in vec(m) implies not(v in s) implies a_ in rr implies p_ in s implies c_ in rr implies u_ = (vadd(m))(p_, (act(m))(c_, v)) implies iota(tv_, tv_ in rr and forsome([pv_ in s, cv_ in rr], u_ = (vadd(m))(pv_, (act(m))(cv_, v)) and tv_ = f(pv_) + cv_ * a_)) = f(p_) + c_ * a_)
- `heine-borel-baby` — forall([a, b, f_], a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies forall([v_ in f_], is-open(rr-ms, v_)) implies ccint(a, b) subset big-union(u_, f_, u_) implies forsome([g_], g_ subset f_ and g_ in set and card(g_) in nn and ccint(a, b) subset big-union(u_, g_, u_)))
- `heine-borel-ccint` — forall([av_ in rr, bv_ in rr], is-compact(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(av_, bv_))))
- `holomorphic-chain` — forall([dm, g, x, u, f, l, d], dm subset rr implies g in fun(dm, cc) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, dm), cc-ms, g, x) implies forall([msy_ in dm], g(msy_) in u) implies is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, u, f, g(x), l) implies d in cc implies has-deriv-at(vnb-lambda(pat_, dm, real-part(g(pat_))), x, real-part(d)) implies has-deriv-at(vnb-lambda(pat_, dm, imag-part(g(pat_))), x, imag-part(d)) implies has-deriv-at(vnb-lambda(pat_, dm, real-part(f(g(pat_)))), x, real-part(l * d)) and has-deriv-at(vnb-lambda(pat_, dm, imag-part(f(g(pat_)))), x, imag-part(l * d)))
- `holomorphic-chain-within` — forall([dm, w, g, x, u, f, l, d], dm subset rr implies w subset dm implies g in fun(dm, cc) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, dm), cc-ms, g, x) implies forall([msy_ in dm], g(msy_) in u) implies is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, u, f, g(x), l) implies d in cc implies has-deriv-at-within(vnb-lambda(pat_, dm, real-part(g(pat_))), w, x, real-part(d)) implies has-deriv-at-within(vnb-lambda(pat_, dm, imag-part(g(pat_))), w, x, imag-part(d)) implies has-deriv-at-within(vnb-lambda(pat_, dm, real-part(f(g(pat_)))), w, x, real-part(l * d)) and has-deriv-at-within(vnb-lambda(pat_, dm, imag-part(f(g(pat_)))), w, x, imag-part(l * d)))
- `holomorphic-compose` — forall([algu_, algv_, algf_, algg_], holomorphic-on(algu_, algf_) implies holomorphic-on(algv_, algg_) implies forall([d2y_ in algu_], algf_(d2y_) in algv_) implies holomorphic-on(algu_, vnb-lambda(d2x_, algu_, algg_(algf_(d2x_)))))
- `holomorphic-const` — forall([u], is-open(cc-ms, u) implies forall([hbc_ in cc], holomorphic-on(u, vnb-lambda(hbx_, u, hbc_))))

- `holomorphic-on-iff-cc-ms` — forall([u, f], holomorphic-on(u, f) iff is-open(cc-ms, u) and f in fun(u, cc) and forall([a in u], forsome([l], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, u, f, a, l))))
- `holomorphic-on-in-fun` — forall([u, f], holomorphic-on(u, f) implies f in fun(u, cc))

- `holomorphic-product` — forall([u, f, g, sm], holomorphic-on(u, f) implies holomorphic-on(u, g) implies sm in fun(u, cc) implies forall([hbx_ in u], sm(hbx_) == f(hbx_) * g(hbx_)) implies holomorphic-on(u, sm))
- `holomorphic-quotient` — forall([cru_, crf_, crg_, crq_], holomorphic-on(cru_, crf_) implies holomorphic-on(cru_, crg_) implies forall([cry_ in cru_], not(crg_(cry_) = 0)) implies crq_ in fun(cru_, cc) implies forall([cry_ in cru_], crq_(cry_) == (/)(crf_(cry_), crg_(cry_))) implies holomorphic-on(cru_, crq_))
- `holomorphic-recip` — holomorphic-on(difference(cc, singleton(0)), vnb-lambda(crz_, difference(cc, singleton(0)), recip(crz_)))
- `holomorphic-recip-of` — forall([cru_, crf_, crq_], holomorphic-on(cru_, crf_) implies forall([cry_ in cru_], not(crf_(cry_) = 0)) implies crq_ in fun(cru_, cc) implies forall([cry_ in cru_], crq_(cry_) == recip(crf_(cry_))) implies holomorphic-on(cru_, crq_))
- `holomorphic-sum` — forall([u, f, g, sm], holomorphic-on(u, f) implies holomorphic-on(u, g) implies sm in fun(u, cc) implies forall([hbx_ in u], sm(hbx_) == f(hbx_) + g(hbx_)) implies holomorphic-on(u, sm))

- `hom-abelian-group-compose` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], is-hom-abelian-group(a, b, f) implies is-hom-abelian-group(b, c, g) implies is-hom-abelian-group(a, c, compose(g, f)))

- `hom-abelian-group-post-type` — forall([a, b, c, h], is-abelian-group(a) implies h in hom-abelian-group(b, c) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-abelian-group(a, b), compose(h, hmpf_)) in fun(hom-abelian-group(a, b), hom-abelian-group(a, c)))

- `hom-c-metric-space-pre-type` — forall([a, b, c, k], is-c-metric-space(b) implies k in hom-c-metric-space(c, a) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-c-metric-space(a, b), compose(hmpf_, k)) in fun(hom-c-metric-space(a, b), hom-c-metric-space(c, b)))

- `hom-pid-post-type` — forall([a, b, c, h], is-pid(a) implies h in hom-pid(b, c) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-pid(a, b), compose(h, hmpf_)) in fun(hom-pid(a, b), hom-pid(a, c)))
- `hom-pid-pre-type` — forall([a, b, c, k], is-pid(b) implies k in hom-pid(c, a) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-pid(a, b), compose(hmpf_, k)) in fun(hom-pid(a, b), hom-pid(c, b)))

- `hom-pseudometric-space-pre-type` — forall([a, b, c, k], is-pseudometric-space(b) implies k in hom-pseudometric-space(c, a) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-pseudometric-space(a, b), compose(hmpf_, k)) in fun(hom-pseudometric-space(a, b), hom-pseudometric-space(c, b)))
- `hom-ring-compose` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], is-hom-ring(a, b, f) implies is-hom-ring(b, c, g) implies is-hom-ring(a, c, compose(g, f)))
- `hom-ring-id` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies is-hom-ring(a, a, id-fun(carr(a))))

- `hom-ring-post-type` — forall([a, b, c, h], is-ring(a) implies h in hom-ring(b, c) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-ring(a, b), compose(h, hmpf_)) in fun(hom-ring(a, b), hom-ring(a, c)))
- `hom-ring-pre-type` — forall([a, b, c, k], is-ring(b) implies k in hom-ring(c, a) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-ring(a, b), compose(hmpf_, k)) in fun(hom-ring(a, b), hom-ring(c, b)))
- `hom-ringoid-compose` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], is-hom-ringoid(a, b, f) implies is-hom-ringoid(b, c, g) implies is-hom-ringoid(a, c, compose(g, f)))

- `hom-ringoid-in-set` — forall([a, b], is-ringoid(a) implies is-ringoid(b) implies hom-ringoid(a, b) in set)

- `hom-ringoid-pre-type` — forall([a, b, c, k], is-ringoid(b) implies k in hom-ringoid(c, a) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-ringoid(a, b), compose(hmpf_, k)) in fun(hom-ringoid(a, b), hom-ringoid(c, b)))
- `hom-semigroup-compose` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], is-hom-semigroup(a, b, f) implies is-hom-semigroup(b, c, g) implies is-hom-semigroup(a, c, compose(g, f)))

- `hom-semigroup-pre-type` — forall([a, b, c, k], is-semigroup(b) implies k in hom-semigroup(c, a) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-semigroup(a, b), compose(hmpf_, k)) in fun(hom-semigroup(a, b), hom-semigroup(c, b)))
- `hom-setoid-compose` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], is-hom-setoid(a, b, f) implies is-hom-setoid(b, c, g) implies is-hom-setoid(a, c, compose(g, f)))
- `hom-setoid-id` — forall([a], is-setoid(a) implies is-hom-setoid(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))
- `hom-setoid-in-set` — forall([a, b], is-setoid(a) implies is-setoid(b) implies hom-setoid(a, b) in set)

- `hom-top-space-compose` — forall([s, t, w, f, g], is-hom-top-space(s, t, f) implies is-hom-top-space(t, w, g) implies is-hom-top-space(s, w, compose(g, f)))

- `hom-top-space-post-type` — forall([a, b, c, h], is-top-space(a) implies h in hom-top-space(b, c) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-top-space(a, b), compose(h, hmpf_)) in fun(hom-top-space(a, b), hom-top-space(a, c)))

- `hom-uniformly-continuous-compose` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], is-uniformly-continuous-arrow(a, b, f) implies is-uniformly-continuous-arrow(b, c, g) implies is-uniformly-continuous-arrow(a, c, compose(g, f)))
- `hom-uniformly-continuous-id` — forall([a], is-metric-space(a) implies is-uniformly-continuous-arrow(a, a, id-fun(pts(a))))
- `hom-uniformly-continuous-in-set` — forall([a, b], is-metric-space(a) implies is-metric-space(b) implies hom-uniformly-continuous(a, b) in set)
- `hom-uniformly-continuous-member-iff` — forall([a, b, f], f in hom-uniformly-continuous(a, b) iff f in fun(pts(a), pts(b)) and is-uniformly-continuous-arrow(a, b, f))
- `hom-uniformly-continuous-post-type` — forall([a, b, c, h], is-metric-space(a) implies h in hom-uniformly-continuous(b, c) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-uniformly-continuous(a, b), compose(h, hmpf_)) in fun(hom-uniformly-continuous(a, b), hom-uniformly-continuous(a, c)))
- `hom-uniformly-continuous-pre-type` — forall([a, b, c, k], is-metric-space(b) implies k in hom-uniformly-continuous(c, a) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-uniformly-continuous(a, b), compose(hmpf_, k)) in fun(hom-uniformly-continuous(a, b), hom-uniformly-continuous(c, b)))
- `hom-uniformly-continuous-subset-hom-continuous` — forall([a, b], hom-uniformly-continuous(a, b) subset hom-continuous(a, b))
- `hom-vector-space-compose` — forall([a, b, c, f, g], is-hom-vector-space(a, b, f) implies is-hom-vector-space(b, c, g) implies is-hom-vector-space(a, c, compose(g, f)))
