- `hom-vector-space-in-set` — forall([a, b], is-vector-space(a) implies is-vector-space(b) implies hom-vector-space(a, b) in set)
- `hom-vector-space-member-iff` — forall([a, b, f], f in hom-vector-space(a, b) iff f in fun(vec(a), vec(b)) and is-hom-vector-space(a, b, f))
- `hom-vector-space-post-type` — forall([a, b, c, h], is-vector-space(a) implies h in hom-vector-space(b, c) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-vector-space(a, b), compose(h, hmpf_)) in fun(hom-vector-space(a, b), hom-vector-space(a, c)))
- `hom-vector-space-pre-type` — forall([a, b, c, k], is-vector-space(b) implies k in hom-vector-space(c, a) implies vnb-lambda(hmpf_, hom-vector-space(a, b), compose(hmpf_, k)) in fun(hom-vector-space(a, b), hom-vector-space(c, b)))
- `id-fun-apply` — forall([a, x in a], (id-fun(a))(x) = x)
- `id-fun-type` — forall([a in set], id-fun(a) in fun(a, a))
- `ideal-elt-in-carrier` — forall([s, i, x], is-ideal(s, i) implies x in i implies x in carr(s))
- `ident-lam-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(x, rr, x) in fun(rr, rr)
- `identity-continuous-at` — forall([a in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, x), a))
- `identmat-entries-defined` — forall([a, n], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies forall([i_ in interval(1, n), j_ in interval(1, n)], (vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, n), interval(1, n)), if(i = j, one(a), zero(a))))(i_, j_) in set))
- `identmat-entry-diag` — forall([a, n, i], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies i in interval(1, n) implies entry(identmat(a, n), i, i) = one(a))
- `identmat-entry-off` — forall([a, n, i, j], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(i = j) implies entry(identmat(a, n), i, j) = zero(a))
- `identmat-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n in nn], is-invertible-mat(a, n, identmat(a, n))))
- `identmat-left-identity` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], matmul(a, identmat(a, m), p) = p))
- `identmat-minor` — forall([r, n], is-ring(r) implies n in nn implies minor(identmat(r, succ(n)), 1, 1, n) = identmat(r, n))
- `identmat-right-identity` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], matmul(a, p, identmat(a, n)) = p))
- `identmat-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n in nn], identmat(a, n) in mat(n, n, carr(a))))
- `imag-part-in-rr` — forall([z in cc], imag-part(z) in rr)
- `image-of-preimage-onto` — forall([s], pts(s) in set implies forall([fqf_, wqu_], wqu_ subset image(fqf_, pts(s)) implies image(fqf_, preimage(s, fqf_, wqu_)) = wqu_))
- `image-restrict` — forall([aqa_ in set, fqf_], image(restrict(fqf_, aqa_), aqa_) = image(fqf_, aqa_))
- `image-subset-codomain` — forall([dm, cod, phi in fun(dm, cod), w in image(phi, dm)], w in cod)
- `in-transport-eq` — forall([goux_, gouy_, gous_], goux_ in gous_ implies goux_ = gouy_ implies gouy_ in gous_)
- `inf-subset-nn-unbounded` — forall([t in inf-subsets(nn), u in nn], forsome([y in nn], y in t and u < y))
- `inf-subsets-is-set` — forall([a in set], inf-subsets(a) in set)
- `inf-subsets-unfold` — forall([a_], inf-subsets(a_) == {s in power(a_): not(card(s) in nn)})
- `injection-from-empty` — forall([c in set], card(injection(empty-set, c)) = succ(0))
- `injection-image-is-bijection` — forall([dm_ in set, cod_, ph_ in injection(dm_, cod_)], vnb-lambda(z_, dm_, ph_(z_)) in bijection(dm_, image(ph_, dm_)))
- `injection-is-injective-star` — forall([x, y, f in injection(x, y)], injective-star(f))
- `insert-difference-cancel` — forall([x_, s_, k_ in set], not(k_ in x_) implies difference(union(x_, pair(k_, k_)), union(s_, pair(k_, k_))) = difference(x_, s_))
- `insert-difference-outside` — forall([x_, s_, k_ in set], not(k_ in s_) implies difference(union(x_, pair(k_, k_)), s_) = union(difference(x_, s_), pair(k_, k_)))
- `insert-remove` — forall([s_, k_ in set], not(k_ in s_) implies difference(union(s_, pair(k_, k_)), pair(k_, k_)) = s_)
- `integral-domain-cancel-zero` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, b) = zero(s) implies not(b = zero(s)) implies a = zero(s)))
- `integral-domain-is-commutative-ring` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) implies is-commutative-ring(s))
- `integral-domain-nontrivial` — forall([s], is-integral-domain(s) implies not(one(s) = zero(s)))
- `inter-of-opens-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([u, w], is-open(s, u) and is-open(s, w) implies is-open(s, intersection(u, w))))
- `interior-in-carrier` — forall([s, a], interior(s, a) subset pts(s))
- `interior-in-set` — forall([s, a], interior(s, a) subset a)
- `interior-is-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], is-open(s, interior(s, a))))
- `interior-is-set` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], interior(s, a) in set))
- `interior-max-deriv-zero` — forall([f, a, b, theta, l], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and theta in rr and a < theta and theta < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], f(x) <= f(theta)) implies is-diff-at(f, theta, l) implies l = 0)
- `interior-mem-intro` — forall([s, a, u, x], is-open(s, u) implies u subset a implies x in u implies x in interior(s, a))
- `interior-membership` — forall([s, a, x], x in interior(s, a) iff x in pts(s) and forsome([u], is-open(s, u) and x in u and u subset a))
- `interior-min-deriv-zero` — forall([f, a, b, theta, l], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and theta in rr and a < theta and theta < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], f(theta) <= f(x)) implies is-diff-at(f, theta, l) implies l = 0)
- `interior-sep-unfold` — forall([s, a], interior(s, a) == {x in pts(s): forsome([u], is-open(s, u) and x in u and u subset a)})
- `intersection-complement-in-empty` — forall([a_, b_], intersection(a_, complement-in(b_, a_)) = empty-set)
- `intersection-empty-transfer` — forall([a_ in set, b_, c_], intersection(a_, b_) = empty-set implies forall([w_ in c_], w_ in b_) implies intersection(a_, c_) = empty-set)
- `intersection-of-antitone` — forall([c_, iod_], c_ subset iod_ implies forsome([iou_], iou_ in c_) implies intersection-of(iod_) subset intersection-of(c_))
- `intersection-of-empty-family` — forall([c_], not(forsome([iou_], iou_ in c_)) implies intersection-of(c_) = empty-set)
- `intersection-of-is-set` — forall([c_], intersection-of(c_) in set)
- `intersection-of-membership` — forall([c_, iox_], iox_ in intersection-of(c_) iff forsome([iou_], iou_ in c_) and forall([iou_ in c_], iox_ in iou_))
- `intersection-of-subset-member` — forall([c_, ioa_ in c_], intersection-of(c_) subset ioa_)
- `intersection-of-unfold` — forall([c_], intersection-of(c_) == {iov_ in big-union(iow_, c_, iow_): forall([iou_ in c_], iov_ in iou_)})
- `interval-1-0-empty` — interval(1, 0) = empty-set
- `interval-1-1` — interval(1, 1) = pair(1, 1)
- `interval-card` — forall([n in nn], card(interval(1, n)) = n)
- `interval-card-in-nn` — forall([a, b in nn], card(interval(a, b)) in nn)
- `interval-elt-in-nn` — forall([a, b, i in interval(a, b)], i in nn)
- `interval-hi` — forall([a, b, i in interval(a, b)], i <= b)
- `interval-in-set` — forall([a, b], interval(a, b) in set)
- `interval-lo` — forall([a, b, i in interval(a, b)], a <= i)
- `interval-local-max-deriv-zero` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, f, theta in ooint(a, b), l], is-local-max-at(f, ccint(a, b), theta) implies has-deriv-at(f, theta, l) implies l = 0)
- `interval-local-max-left-deriv-nonneg` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, f, osx_, osl_], is-local-max-at(f, ccint(a, b), osx_) implies has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies 0 <= osl_)
- `interval-local-max-right-deriv-nonpos` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, f, osx_, osl_], is-local-max-at(f, ccint(a, b), osx_) implies has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies osl_ <= 0)
- `interval-local-min-deriv-zero` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, f, theta in ooint(a, b), l], is-local-min-at(f, ccint(a, b), theta) implies has-deriv-at(f, theta, l) implies l = 0)
- `interval-mem-intro` — forall([a, b, i in nn], a <= i implies i <= b implies i in interval(a, b))
- `interval-membership` — forall([a, b, i], i in interval(a, b) iff i in nn and a <= i and i <= b)
- `interval-succ-insert` — forall([n in nn], interval(1, succ(n)) = union(interval(1, n), pair(succ(n), succ(n))))
- `interval-unfold` — forall([a, b], {i in nn: a <= i and i <= b} == interval(a, b))
- `interval-widen` — forall([b in nn, c in nn, a, i in interval(a, b)], b <= c implies i in interval(a, c))
- `inverse-bij-in-fun` — forall([x, y, phi in bijection(x, y)], inverse-bij(phi, x, y) in fun(y, x))
- `inverse-bij-is-bijection` — forall([x, y, phi in bijection(x, y)], inverse-bij(phi, x, y) in bijection(y, x))
- `inverse-bij-left` — forall([dm, cod, phi in bijection(dm, cod), x in dm], (inverse-bij(phi, dm, cod))(phi(x)) = x)
- `inverse-bij-right` — forall([dm, cod, phi in bijection(dm, cod), y in cod], phi((inverse-bij(phi, dm, cod))(y)) = y)
- `inverse-is-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, pm, qm], pm in mat(n, n, carr(a)) implies qm in mat(n, n, carr(a)) implies matmul(a, pm, qm) = identmat(a, n) implies matmul(a, qm, pm) = identmat(a, n) implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, qm)))
- `invertible-mat-is-mat` — forall([a, n, u], is-invertible-mat(a, n, u) implies u in mat(n, n, carr(a)))
- `ip-norm-unfold` — forall([v, x], ip-norm(v, x) == sqrt((ip(v))(x, x)))
- `ip-normed-ag-is-normed-ag` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies is-normed-ag(ip-normed-ag(v)))
- `is-convex-unfold` — forall([u], is-convex(u) implies forall([goux_ in u, gouy_ in u, gout_ in ccint(0, 1)], goux_ + gout_ * (gouy_ - goux_) in u))
- `is-path-continuous` — forall([pgam, a, b], is-path(pgam, a, b) implies forall([pax_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(a, b)), cc-ms, pgam, pax_)))
- `is-path-endpoints` — forall([pgam, a, b], is-path(pgam, a, b) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b)
- `is-path-in-fun` — forall([pgam, a, b], is-path(pgam, a, b) implies pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc))
- `is-path-of-coords` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([pgam, pgr_, pgi_], pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies pgr_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies pgi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], real-part(pgam(pay_)) = pgr_(pay_)) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], imag-part(pgam(pay_)) = pgi_(pay_)) implies is-continuous-on(pgr_, ccint(a, b)) implies is-continuous-on(pgi_, ccint(a, b)) implies is-path(pgam, a, b)))
- `is-road-dgam-in-fun` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies dgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc))
- `is-road-im-antiderivative` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies is-primitive(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(pgam(pat_))), vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b))
- `is-road-im-regulated` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b))
- `is-road-is-path` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies is-path(pgam, a, b))
- `is-road-re-antiderivative` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies is-primitive(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(pgam(pat_))), vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b))
- `is-road-re-regulated` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(dgam(pat_))), a, b))
- `is-subsequence-iff-typed-subsqn` — forall([s, y, f], is-subsequence(s, y, f) iff f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and subsqn(y, f))
- `is-vector-space-unfold` — forall([m], is-vector-space(m) iff is-module(m) and is-field-ring(scal(m)))
- `ivt` — forall([f, a, b, c], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies c in rr implies a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies f(a) <= c and c <= f(b) implies forsome([p_ in rr], p_ in ccint(a, b) and f(p_) = c))
- `juxta-apply-left` — forall([p, q, a in rr, b in rr, c in rr, t in ccint(a, b)], b <= c implies (juxta(p, q, a, b, c))(t) == p(t))
- `juxta-apply-right` — forall([p, q, a in rr, b in rr, c in rr, t in ccint(b, c)], a <= b implies b < t implies (juxta(p, q, a, b, c))(t) == q(t))
- `juxta-apply-right-closed` — forall([p, q, a in rr, b in rr, c in rr], a <= b implies p(b) = q(b) implies forall([t in ccint(b, c)], (juxta(p, q, a, b, c))(t) == q(t)))
- `juxta-in-fun` — forall([p, q, a in rr, b in rr, c in rr, cod], a <= b implies b <= c implies p in fun(ccint(a, b), cod) implies q in fun(ccint(b, c), cod) implies juxta(p, q, a, b, c) in fun(ccint(a, c), cod))
- `juxta-is-road` — forall([pgam1, dgam1, pgam2, dgam2, a, b, c], is-road(pgam1, dgam1, a, b) implies is-road(pgam2, dgam2, b, c) implies pgam1(b) = pgam2(b) implies is-road(juxta(pgam1, pgam2, a, b, c), juxta(dgam1, dgam2, a, b, c), a, c))
- `juxta-trace` — forall([p, q, a in rr, b in rr, c in rr], a <= b implies b <= c implies p in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies q in fun(ccint(b, c), cc) implies p(b) = q(b) implies trace(juxta(p, q, a, b, c), a, c) = union(trace(p, a, b), trace(q, b, c)))
- `juxta-trace-subset` — forall([p, q, a in rr, b in rr, c in rr], a <= b implies b <= c implies p in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies q in fun(ccint(b, c), cc) implies trace(juxta(p, q, a, b, c), a, c) subset union(trace(p, a, b), trace(q, b, c)))
- `juxta-unfold` — forall([jxp_, jxq_, jxa_, jxb_, jxc_], juxta(jxp_, jxq_, jxa_, jxb_, jxc_) == vnb-lambda(jxt_, ccint(jxa_, jxc_), if(jxt_ <= jxb_, jxp_(jxt_), jxq_(jxt_))))
- `lam-slot-add-apply` — forall([dm_, a_, b_], a_ in dm_ implies b_ in dm_ implies (vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(dm_, dm_), x_ + y_))(a_, b_) == a_ + b_)
- `lam-slot-mul-apply` — forall([dm_, a_, b_], a_ in dm_ implies b_ in dm_ implies (vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(dm_, dm_), x_ * y_))(a_, b_) == a_ * b_)
- `lam-slot-neg-apply` — forall([dm_, a_ in dm_], (vnb-lambda(x_, dm_, -x_))(a_) == -a_)
- `lambda-compose-value` — forall([ff, ph, dm, pt in dm], (vnb-lambda(z, dm, ff(ph(z))))(pt) == ff(ph(pt)))
- `lambda-eta-value` — forall([ff_, dm_, pt_ in dm_], (vnb-lambda(z_, dm_, ff_(z_)))(pt_) == ff_(pt_))
- `lastcoeff-set-is-ideal` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-commutative-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([p in nn, u in mat(succ(p), 1, vec(md)), sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies is-ideal(scal(md), lastcoeff-set(md, p, u, sm))))
- `lastcoeff-zero-in-span` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([p in nn, u in mat(succ(p), 1, vec(md)), c in mat(1, succ(p), carr(scal(md)))], entry(c, 1, succ(p)) = zero(scal(md)) implies lincomb(md, succ(p), c, u) in span(md, p, block(u, p, 1))))
- `le-bound-mono` — forall([p, a, b, n], p in rr implies a in rr implies b in rr implies n in rr implies 0 <= n implies p <= a * n implies a <= b implies p <= b * n)
- `leadcoef-nonzero` — forall([a_, p_], is-ring(a_) implies p_ in carr(poly(a_)) implies not(p_ = zero(poly(a_))) implies not(leadcoef(a_, p_) = zero(a_)))
- `leadcoef-nonzero-ptwise` — forall([a_, p_ in carr(poly(a_))], forsome([k_ in nn], not(p_(k_) = zero(a_))) implies not(leadcoef(a_, p_) = zero(a_)))
- `leadcoef-unfold` — forall([a_, p_], leadcoef(a_, p_) == p_(deg(a_, p_)))
- `lebesgue-number` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies seq-compact(s) implies forall([cov], is-open-cover(s, cov) implies forsome([del], pos-rr(del) and forall([p in pts(s)], forsome([u in cov], ball(s, p, del) subset u)))))
- `left-limit-within-ccint-unique` — forall([f, a in rr, b in rr, rqx_ in rr, rql_, rqm_], a < rqx_ implies rqx_ <= b implies is-left-limit-within(f, ccint(a, b), rqx_, rql_) implies is-left-limit-within(f, ccint(a, b), rqx_, rqm_) implies rql_ = rqm_)
- `left-limit-within-le` — forall([f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_, rqm_], is-left-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_) implies is-left-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, rqm_) implies forall([rqr_], pos-rr(rqr_) implies forsome([rqt_ in rqw_], rqx_ - rqr_ < rqt_ and rqt_ < rqx_)) implies rql_ - rqm_ <= 0)
- `left-limit-within-local` — forall([g, jlv_, f, jlw_, jlx_, jll_, jlr_], is-left-limit-within(g, jlv_, jlx_, jll_) implies jlw_ subset rr implies f in fun(jlw_, rr) implies pos-rr(jlr_) implies forall([jlt_ in jlw_], jlx_ - jlr_ < jlt_ implies jlt_ < jlx_ implies jlt_ in jlv_) implies forall([jlt_ in jlw_], jlx_ - jlr_ < jlt_ implies jlt_ < jlx_ implies f(jlt_) == g(jlt_)) implies is-left-limit-within(f, jlw_, jlx_, jll_))
- `left-limit-within-magnitude` — forall([f, g, lih_, liw_, lix_, lil_, lik_], is-left-limit-within(f, liw_, lix_, lil_) implies is-left-limit-within(g, liw_, lix_, lik_) implies lih_ in fun(liw_, rr) implies forall([liz_ in liw_], lih_(liz_) == magnitude(f(liz_) + g(liz_) * 1i)) implies is-left-limit-within(lih_, liw_, lix_, magnitude(lil_ + lik_ * 1i)))
- `left-limit-within-mul` — forall([f, g, rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_, rgm_], is-left-limit-within(f, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_) implies is-left-limit-within(g, rgw_, rgx_, rgm_) implies rgh_ in fun(rgw_, rr) implies forall([rgz_ in rgw_], rgh_(rgz_) == f(rgz_) * g(rgz_)) implies is-left-limit-within(rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_ * rgm_))
- `left-limit-within-scalar` — forall([f, rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_, rgc_ in rr], is-left-limit-within(f, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_) implies rgh_ in fun(rgw_, rr) implies forall([rgz_ in rgw_], rgh_(rgz_) == rgc_ * f(rgz_)) implies is-left-limit-within(rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgc_ * rgl_))
- `left-limit-within-sub` — forall([f, g, rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_, rgm_], is-left-limit-within(f, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_) implies is-left-limit-within(g, rgw_, rgx_, rgm_) implies rgh_ in fun(rgw_, rr) implies forall([rgz_ in rgw_], rgh_(rgz_) == f(rgz_) - g(rgz_)) implies is-left-limit-within(rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_ - rgm_))
- `left-limit-within-subset` — forall([f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_, g, rqv_], is-left-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_) implies rqv_ subset rqw_ implies g in fun(rqv_, rr) implies forall([rqz_ in rqv_], g(rqz_) = f(rqz_)) implies is-left-limit-within(g, rqv_, rqx_, rql_))
- `left-limit-within-sum` — forall([f, g, rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_, rgm_], is-left-limit-within(f, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_) implies is-left-limit-within(g, rgw_, rgx_, rgm_) implies rgh_ in fun(rgw_, rr) implies forall([rgz_ in rgw_], rgh_(rgz_) == f(rgz_) + g(rgz_)) implies is-left-limit-within(rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_ + rgm_))
- `left-limit-within-unique` — forall([f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_, rqm_], is-left-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_) implies is-left-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, rqm_) implies forall([rqr_], pos-rr(rqr_) implies forsome([rqt_ in rqw_], rqx_ - rqr_ < rqt_ and rqt_ < rqx_)) implies rql_ = rqm_)
- `lfe-coef-unique` — forall([m, v], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies v in vec(m) implies forall([a_ in rr, b_ in rr], (act(m))(a_, v) = (act(m))(b_, v) implies a_ * (vnrm(m))(v) = b_ * (vnrm(m))(v)))
- `lfe-coef-value` — forall([m, v, u_, c_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies v in vec(m) implies c_ in rr implies u_ = (act(m))(c_, v) implies iota(t_, t_ in rr and forsome([q_ in rr], u_ = (act(m))(q_, v) and t_ = q_ * (vnrm(m))(v))) = c_ * (vnrm(m))(v))
- `lfe-line-coef` — forall([m, v, w_ in line(m, v)], forsome([r_ in rr], w_ = (act(m))(r_, v)))
- `lfe-line-in-vec` — forall([m, v, w_ in line(m, v)], w_ in vec(m))
- `lfe-line-mem-eq` — forall([m, v, w_, r_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies v in vec(m) implies r_ in rr implies w_ = (act(m))(r_, v) implies w_ in line(m, v))
- `lfe-line-membership` — forall([m, v, w_], w_ in line(m, v) iff w_ in vec(m) and forsome([r_ in rr], w_ = (act(m))(r_, v)))
- `lfe-line-subset` — forall([m, v], line(m, v) subset vec(m))
- `lfn-cc-factor-identity` — forall([pa_ in cc, pb_ in cc, pc_ in cc, pe_ in cc, ph_ in cc], pa_ * (pb_ * ph_ + pe_ * (pc_ * ph_)) = ph_ * (pa_ * (pb_ + pc_ * pe_)))
- `lincomb-empty` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([c, u], lincomb(md, 0, c, u) = vzero(md)))
- `lincomb-row-add` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, c1, c2, u], c1 in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies c2 in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies lincomb(md, n, matadd(scal(md), c1, c2), u) = (vadd(md))(lincomb(md, n, c1, u), lincomb(md, n, c2, u))))
- `lincomb-row-peel` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n in nn, c in mat(1, succ(n), carr(scal(md))), u in mat(succ(n), 1, vec(md))], lincomb(md, succ(n), c, u) = (vadd(md))(lincomb(md, n, block(c, 1, n), block(u, n, 1)), (act(md))(entry(c, 1, succ(n)), entry(u, succ(n), 1)))))
- `lincomb-row-scale` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, r, c, u], r in carr(scal(md)) implies c in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies lincomb(md, n, matscale(scal(md), r, c), u) = (act(md))(r, lincomb(md, n, c, u))))
- `lincomb-snoc` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n in nn, c in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))), u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)), r in carr(scal(md)), x in vec(md)], lincomb(md, succ(n), snoc-row(c, n, r), snoc-col(u, n, x)) = (vadd(md))(lincomb(md, n, c, u), (act(md))(r, x))))
- `lincomb-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, c, u], c in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies lincomb(md, n, c, u) in vec(md)))
- `lincomb-unfold` — forall([md, n, c, u], lincomb(md, n, c, u) == finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))(entry(c, 1, j), entry(u, j, 1))), interval(1, n)))
- `lincomb-unitrow` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, i, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], i in interval(1, n) implies lincomb(md, n, unitrow(scal(md), n, i), u) = entry(u, i, 1)))
- `lincomb-zerorow` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], lincomb(md, n, zeromat(scal(md), 1, n), u) = vzero(md)))
- `line-functional-exists` — forall([m, v], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies v in vec(m) implies forsome([f_], is-bounded-linear-functional-on(m, line(m, v), f_) and dual-norm-on(m, line(m, v), f_) <= 1 and f_(v) = (vnrm(m))(v)))
- `line-has-v` — forall([m, v], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies v in vec(m) implies v in line(m, v))
- `line-int-abs-bound` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies forall([liab_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], forall([liy_ in ccint(a, b)], liab_(liy_) == magnitude(f(pgam(liy_))) * magnitude(dgam(liy_))) implies magnitude(line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b)) <= pw-int(liab_, a, b)))
- `line-int-adjacent` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies forall([licv_ in ooint(a, b)], line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b) = line-int(f, restrict(pgam, ccint(a, licv_)), restrict(dgam, ccint(a, licv_)), a, licv_) + line-int(f, restrict(pgam, ccint(licv_, b)), restrict(dgam, ccint(licv_, b)), licv_, b)))
- `line-int-affine-reparam` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies forall([lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies lrpc_ < lrpd_ implies forall([lrpr_, lrpk_], lrpr_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), cc) implies lrpk_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), cc) implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrpr_(lrpz_) == pgam(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrpk_(lrpz_) == lrpl_ * dgam(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies line-int(f, lrpr_, lrpk_, lrpc_, lrpd_) = line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b))))
- `line-int-closed-road-of-derivative` — forall([u, f, df, pgam, dgam, a, b], holomorphic-on(u, f) implies df in fun(u, cc) implies forall([hbz_ in u], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, u, f, hbz_, df(hbz_))) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies pgam(b) = pgam(a) implies line-int(df, pgam, dgam, a, b) = 0)
- `line-int-concat` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, prho, drho, a, b, c, d], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies is-road(prho, drho, c, d) implies pgam(b) = prho(c) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies trace(prho, c, d) subset u implies line-int(f, juxta(pgam, vnb-lambda(jxs_, ccint(b, b + d - c), prho(c + jxs_ - b)), a, b, b + d - c), juxta(dgam, vnb-lambda(jxs_, ccint(b, b + d - c), drho(c + jxs_ - b)), a, b, b + d - c), a, b + d - c) = line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b) + line-int(f, prho, drho, c, d))
- `line-int-exists` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies forsome([pwf_, paw_], is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part((vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), f(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_)))(pat_))), a, b) and is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part((vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), f(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_)))(pat_))), a, b)))
- `line-int-in-cc` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b) in cc)
- `line-int-integrand-in-fun` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([pgam, dgam, pf, pad_], pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies dgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies pf in fun(pad_, cc) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset pad_ implies vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), pf(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_)) in fun(ccint(a, b), cc)))
- `line-int-interior-congruence` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies forall([qgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc), qdg in fun(ccint(a, b), cc)], forall([t in ccint(a, b)], qgam(t) == pgam(t)) implies forall([t in ooint(a, b)], qdg(t) == dgam(t)) implies line-int(f, qgam, qdg, a, b) = line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b)))
- `line-int-juxta` — forall([u, f, pgam1, dgam1, pgam2, dgam2, a, b, c], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam1, dgam1, a, b) implies is-road(pgam2, dgam2, b, c) implies pgam1(b) = pgam2(b) implies trace(pgam1, a, b) subset u implies trace(pgam2, b, c) subset u implies line-int(f, juxta(pgam1, pgam2, a, b, c), juxta(dgam1, dgam2, a, b, c), a, c) = line-int(f, pgam1, dgam1, a, b) + line-int(f, pgam2, dgam2, b, c))
- `line-int-length-bound` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies forall([bnd in rr], forall([bny_ in trace(pgam, a, b)], magnitude(f(bny_)) <= bnd) implies magnitude(line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b)) <= road-length(dgam, a, b) * bnd))
- `line-int-of-derivative` — forall([u, f, df, pgam, dgam, a, b], holomorphic-on(u, f) implies df in fun(u, cc) implies forall([hbz_ in u], is-diff-on(cc-normed-field, u, f, hbz_, df(hbz_))) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies line-int(df, pgam, dgam, a, b) = f(pgam(b)) - f(pgam(a)))
- `line-int-of-one` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b, pf, pad_], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies pf in fun(pad_, cc) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset pad_ implies forall([pay_ in pad_], pf(pay_) = 1) implies line-int(pf, pgam, dgam, a, b) = pgam(b) - pgam(a))
- `line-int-opposite` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([pgam, dgam, pf, pad_], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies pf in fun(pad_, cc) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset pad_ implies forall([prgb_, prdb_], prgb_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies prdb_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prgb_(pay_) == pgam(a + b - pay_)) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prdb_(pay_) == -1 * dgam(a + b - pay_)) implies forall([pwf_, paw_], is-primitive(pwf_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part((vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), pf(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_)))(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part((vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), pf(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_)))(pat_))), a, b) implies line-int(pf, prgb_, prdb_, a, b) = -1 * line-int(pf, pgam, dgam, a, b)))))
- `line-int-opposite-road` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies forall([lirg_, lird_], lirg_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies lird_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], lirg_(pay_) == pgam(a + b - pay_)) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], lird_(pay_) == -1 * dgam(a + b - pay_)) implies line-int(f, lirg_, lird_, a, b) = -1 * line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b)))
- `line-int-scalar` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies forall([lic_ in cc, lih_ in fun(u, cc)], forall([liz_ in u], lih_(liz_) == lic_ * f(liz_)) implies line-int(lih_, pgam, dgam, a, b) = lic_ * line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b)))
- `line-int-sum` — forall([u, f, g, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies g in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), g, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies forall([lih_ in fun(u, cc)], forall([liz_ in u], lih_(liz_) == f(liz_) + g(liz_)) implies line-int(lih_, pgam, dgam, a, b) = line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b) + line-int(g, pgam, dgam, a, b)))
- `line-int-unfold` — forall([pf, pgam, dgam, a, b], line-int(pf, pgam, dgam, a, b) == cc-int(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), pf(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_)), a, b))
- `line-int-value` — forall([f, pgam, dgam, a, b, lig1_, lig2_], is-primitive(lig1_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(f(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_))), a, b) implies is-primitive(lig2_, vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(f(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_))), a, b) implies line-int(f, pgam, dgam, a, b) = lig1_(b) - lig1_(a) + (lig2_(b) - lig2_(a)) * 1i)
- `line-is-submodule` — forall([m, v], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies v in vec(m) implies is-submodule(m, line(m, v)))
- `line-unfold` — forall([m, v], line(m, v) == {y_ in vec(m): forsome([r_ in rr], y_ = (act(m))(r_, v))})
- `linfun-app-abs-real` — forall([m, t, g, w_], is-linear-functional-on(m, t, g) implies w_ in t implies abs(g(w_)) in rr)
- `linfun-is-linfun-on-vec` — forall([m, f], is-linear-functional(m, f) implies is-linear-functional-on(m, vec(m), f))
- `linfun-on-vec-is-linfun` — forall([m, f], is-linear-functional-on(m, vec(m), f) implies is-linear-functional(m, f))
- `lipschitz-arrow-is-uniformly-continuous-arrow` — forall([a, b, f], is-lipschitz-arrow(a, b, f) implies is-uniformly-continuous-arrow(a, b, f))
- `little-o-scalar` — forall([c, g, a], c in rr implies little-o-at(g, a) implies little-o-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * g(x)), a))
- `little-o-sum` — forall([g, h, a], little-o-at(g, a) implies little-o-at(h, a) implies little-o-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) + h(x)), a))
- `local-max-left-deriv-nonneg` — forall([f, osd_, osx_, osl_], is-local-max-at(f, osd_, osx_) implies has-left-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies 0 <= osl_)
- `local-max-right-deriv-nonpos` — forall([f, osd_, osx_, osl_], is-local-max-at(f, osd_, osx_) implies has-right-deriv-at(f, osx_, osl_) implies osl_ <= 0)
- `log-above-any` — forall([y_ in rr], forsome([b_ in rr], 0 < b_ and y_ <= log(b_)))
- `log-below-any` — forall([y_ in rr], forsome([a_ in rr], 0 < a_ and log(a_) <= y_))
- `log-deriv` — forall([c_ in rr], 0 < c_ implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x_, rr, log(x_)), c_, recip-star(c_)))
- `log-diff` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies 0 < u implies 0 < v implies c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), u, v) = log(v) - log(u))
- `log-in-rr` — forall([x_], log(x_) in rr)
- `log-injective-pos` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies 0 < u_ implies 0 < v_ implies log(u_) = log(v_) implies u_ = v_)
- `log-lam-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(x_, rr, log(x_)) in fun(rr, rr)
- `log-mul` — forall([x_, y_], x_ in rr implies y_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies 0 < y_ implies log(x_ * y_) = log(x_) + log(y_))
- `log-neg-below-one` — forall([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies x_ < 1 implies log(x_) < 0)
- `log-one` — log(1) = 0
- `log-pos-above-one` — forall([x_ in rr], 1 < x_ implies 0 < log(x_))
- `log-r-exp` — forall([y_ in rr], log(r-exp(y_)) = y_)
- `log-recip` — forall([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies log(recip-star(x_)) = -log(x_))
- `log-rpow-star` — forall([x_, s_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies log(rpow-star(x_, s_)) = s_ * log(x_))
- `log-strictly-increasing` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies 0 < u implies u < v implies log(u) < log(v))
- `log-surjective` — forall([y_ in rr], forsome([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ and log(x_) = y_))
- `log-two-pos` — 0 < log(2)
- `log-unbounded-above` — forall([n_ in nn], forsome([b_ in rr], 0 < b_ and n_ * log(2) <= log(b_)))
- `log-unfold` — forall([x_], log(x_) == c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), 1, x_))
- `log0-bound` — forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc)], algc_(0) = 0 implies forall([algj_ in nn], algc_(succ(algj_)) = -1 ^ algj_ * recip(succ(algj_))) implies forall([algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ < 1 implies forall([algz_ in ball(cc-ms, 1, algr_)], magnitude(cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * (algz_ - 1) ^ cpsk_))) <= recip(1 - magnitude(algz_ - 1)) - 1)))
- `log0-exp` — forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc)], algc_(0) = 0 implies forall([algj_ in nn], algc_(succ(algj_)) = -1 ^ algj_ * recip(succ(algj_))) implies forall([algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ < 1 implies forall([algz_ in ball(cc-ms, 1, algr_)], cc-exp(cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * (algz_ - 1) ^ cpsk_))) = algz_)))
- `log0-is-ln` — forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc)], algc_(0) = 0 implies forall([algj_ in nn], algc_(succ(algj_)) = -1 ^ algj_ * recip(succ(algj_))) implies forall([algt_ in rr], 0 < algt_ implies algt_ < 2 implies cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * (algt_ - 1) ^ cpsk_)) = log(algt_)))
- `log0-is-log-on-ball` — forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc)], algc_(0) = 0 implies forall([algj_ in nn], algc_(succ(algj_)) = -1 ^ algj_ * recip(succ(algj_))) implies forall([algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ * (1 + pi) <= pi implies forall([algz_ in ball(cc-ms, 1, algr_)], cc-log(algz_) = cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * (algz_ - 1) ^ cpsk_)))))
- `log0-real` — forall([algc_ in fun(nn, cc)], algc_(0) = 0 implies forall([algj_ in nn], algc_(succ(algj_)) = -1 ^ algj_ * recip(succ(algj_))) implies forall([algr_ in rr], 0 < algr_ implies algr_ < 1 implies forall([algz_ in ball(cc-ms, 1, algr_)], algz_ in rr implies cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * (algz_ - 1) ^ cpsk_)) in cc and imag-part(cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, algc_(cpsk_) * (algz_ - 1) ^ cpsk_))) = 0)))
- `makeset-card-bound` — forall([n in nn, a, l in tuples(a)], length(l) = n implies card(make-set(l)) <= n)
- `makeset-card-bound-strong` — forall([n in nn, a, l in tuples(a)], length(l) = n implies make-set(l) in set and card(make-set(l)) in nn and card(make-set(l)) <= n)
- `makeset-of-empty-tuple` — forall([l_], l_ = [] implies make-set(l_) = empty-set)
- `makeset2-membership` — forall([x_, a_, b_], x_ in {a_, b_} iff x_ in set and (x_ = a_ or x_ = b_))
- `makeset2-split` — forall([a_ in set, b_ in set], {a_, b_} = union(pair(a_, a_), pair(b_, b_)))
- `mal-dist-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, k) implies vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), (act(md))((vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, j), entry(q, j, x))))(z), entry(u, x, col))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mal-inf-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, k) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, j), entry(q, j, x))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(scal(md)))))
- `mal-outf-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies 1 <= n implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, k), (act(md))(entry(matmul(scal(md), p, q), row, j), entry(u, j, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mal-red-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, k) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))((mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, j), entry(q, j, x)), entry(u, x, col))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mal-tout-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (act(md))((mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(u, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, n))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mar-dist-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, k), (act(md))(entry(p, row, x), (vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, k), (act(md))(entry(q, x, j), entry(u, j, col))))(z))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mar-gj-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, k), (act(md))(entry(q, x, j), entry(u, j, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mar-outf-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies 1 <= k implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))(entry(p, row, j), entry(matact(md, q, u), j, col))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mar-red-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (act(md))((mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, x), entry(q, x, c)), entry(u, c, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mar-tout-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, u, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(scal(md))) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(k, l, vec(md)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(module-vector-ag(md), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (act(md))((mul(scal(md)))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(u, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mat-0-1-nonempty` — forall([x], forsome([p], p in mat(0, 1, x)))
- `mat-1-0-nonempty` — forall([x], forsome([p], p in mat(1, 0, x)))
- `mat-colcount-transfer` — forall([n, k, y, q, m, x, r], q in mat(n, k, y) implies (n = 0 implies m = 0 or k = 0) implies r in mat(m, nth(2, size(q)), x) implies r in mat(m, k, x))
- `mat-cols` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], 1 <= m implies length(nth(1, q)) = n)
- `mat-cols-in-nn` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], n in nn)
- `mat-empty-any-cols` — forall([c, x, q, k], q in mat(0, c, x) implies k in nn implies q in mat(0, k, x))
- `mat-equiv-left-mult` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, c, u], c in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies is-invertible-mat(a, m, u) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, matmul(a, u, c))))
- `mat-equiv-refl` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, c in mat(m, n, carr(a))], mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, c)))
- `mat-equiv-right-mult` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, c, v], c in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, v) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, matmul(a, c, v))))
- `mat-equiv-target-is-mat` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, c, d], c in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, d) implies d in mat(m, n, carr(a))))
- `mat-equiv-trans` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, c, d, e], c in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, d) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, d, e) implies mat-equiv(a, m, n, c, e)))
- `mat-in-matrix` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], q in matrix(x))
- `mat-intro` — forall([m, n, x, q in matrix(x)], n in nn implies 1 <= m implies length(q) = m implies length(nth(1, q)) = n implies q in mat(m, n, x))
- `mat-intro-empty` — forall([n, x, q in matrix(x)], n in nn implies length(q) = 0 implies q in mat(0, n, x))
- `mat-is-set` — forall([x in set, m, n], mat(m, n, x) in set)
- `mat-length` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], length(q) = m)
- `mat-mono` — forall([m, n, a, b], forall([w in a], w in b) implies forall([q in mat(m, n, a)], q in mat(m, n, b)))
- `mat-ring-add` — forall([a, n], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies add(mat-ring(a, n)) = vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), matadd(a, p, q)))
- `mat-ring-add-fun` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n in nn], vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), matadd(a, p, q)) in fun(cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), mat(n, n, carr(a)))))
- `mat-ring-carr` — forall([a, n], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies carr(mat-ring(a, n)) = mat(n, n, carr(a)))
- `mat-ring-is-ring` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n in nn], is-ring(mat-ring(a, n))))
- `mat-ring-mul` — forall([a, n], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies mul(mat-ring(a, n)) = vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), matmul(a, p, q)))
- `mat-ring-mul-fun` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n in nn], vnb-lambda([p, q], cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), matmul(a, p, q)) in fun(cartesian(mat(n, n, carr(a)), mat(n, n, carr(a))), mat(n, n, carr(a)))))
- `mat-ring-neg` — forall([a, n], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies neg(mat-ring(a, n)) = vnb-lambda(p, mat(n, n, carr(a)), matneg(a, p)))
- `mat-ring-one` — forall([a, n], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies one(mat-ring(a, n)) = identmat(a, n))
- `mat-ring-zero` — forall([a, n], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies zero(mat-ring(a, n)) = zeromat(a, n, n))
- `mat-rows-in-nn` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], m in nn)
- `mat-size` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], 1 <= m implies size(q) = [m, n])
- `mat-size-cols` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], 1 <= m implies nth(2, size(q)) = n)
- `mat-size-cols-in-nn` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], nth(2, size(q)) in nn)
- `mat-size-cols-pos` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], 1 <= nth(2, size(q)) implies nth(2, size(q)) = n)
- `mat-size-rows` — forall([m, n, x, q in mat(m, n, x)], nth(1, size(q)) = m)
- `mat-tabulation-exists` — forall([m, n, x, g], m in nn implies n in nn implies 1 <= m implies forall([i_ in interval(1, m), j_ in interval(1, n)], g(i_, j_) in x) implies forsome([p in mat(m, n, x)], forall([i_ in interval(1, m), j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(p, i_, j_) = g(i_, j_))))

- `matof-exists-image` — forall([m, n, g], m in nn implies n in nn implies 1 <= m implies forall([i_ in interval(1, m), j_ in interval(1, n)], g(i_, j_) in set) implies forsome([p in mat(m, n, image(g, cartesian(interval(1, m), interval(1, n))))], forall([i_ in interval(1, m), j_ in interval(1, n)], entry(p, i_, j_) = g(i_, j_))))
- `matof-in-mat` — forall([m, n, x, g], m in nn implies n in nn implies forall([i in interval(1, m), j in interval(1, n)], g(i, j) in x) implies matof(m, n, g) in mat(m, n, x))
- `matprod-summand-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, kc, p, q, i, c], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, kc, carr(a)) implies i in interval(1, m) implies c in interval(1, kc) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(a))(entry(p, i, j), entry(q, j, c))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `matrix-entry-extensionality` — forall([m, n, x, p, q], p in mat(m, n, x) implies q in mat(m, n, x) implies forall([i in interval(1, m), j in interval(1, n)], entry(p, i, j) = entry(q, i, j)) implies p = q)

- `metric-chain-bound` — forall([n_ in nn, s, f, rad, m], is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and rad in fun(nn, rr) and forall([k in nn], (dist(s))(f(k), f(succ(k))) <= rad(k)) and m in nn and m <= n_ implies (dist(s))(f(m), f(n_)) <= series-partial-sum(rad, n_) - series-partial-sum(rad, m))
- `metric-chain-triangle` — forall([s, f], is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) implies forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], m <= n_ implies (dist(s))(f(m), f(n_)) <= series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, (dist(s))(f(k), f(succ(k)))), n_) - series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, (dist(s))(f(k), f(succ(k)))), m)))

- `min-degree-entry` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], forsome([i0, j0], i0 in interval(1, m) and j0 in interval(1, n) and not(entry(p, i0, j0) = zero(a))) implies forsome([is, js], is in interval(1, m) and js in interval(1, n) and not(entry(p, is, js) = zero(a)) and forall([i, j], i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, i, j) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, is, js)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(p, i, j))))))

- `minor-entry` — forall([r, s, p, q, n, u_, v_], is-ring(r) implies n in nn implies s in mat(succ(n), succ(n), carr(r)) implies u_ in interval(1, n) implies v_ in interval(1, n) implies entry(minor(s, p, q, n), u_, v_) = entry(s, if(u_ < p, u_, succ(u_)), if(v_ < q, v_, succ(v_))))
- `minor-type` — forall([r, s, p, q, n], is-ring(r) implies p in nn implies q in nn implies n in nn implies s in mat(succ(n), succ(n), carr(r)) implies minor(s, p, q, n) in mat(n, n, carr(r)))
- `module-act-neg-one` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([x in vec(md)], (act(md))((neg(scal(md)))(one(scal(md))), x) = (vneg(md))(x)))
