- `monalg-add-assoc` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, h_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies h_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-add(a_, m_, monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, g_), h_) = monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, monalg-add(a_, m_, g_, h_)))
- `monalg-add-comm` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, g_) = monalg-add(a_, m_, g_, f_))
- `monalg-add-fun` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, g_) in finsupp(a_, m_))
- `monalg-add-neg-left` — forall([a_, m_, f_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-add(a_, m_, monalg-neg(a_, m_, f_), f_) = monalg-zero(a_, m_))
- `monalg-add-neg-right` — forall([a_, m_, f_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, monalg-neg(a_, m_, f_)) = monalg-zero(a_, m_))
- `monalg-add-op` — forall([a_, m_], add(monalg(a_, m_)) == vnb-lambda([f, g], cartesian(finsupp(a_, m_), finsupp(a_, m_)), monalg-add(a_, m_, f, g)))
- `monalg-add-zero-left` — forall([a_, m_, f_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-add(a_, m_, monalg-zero(a_, m_), f_) = f_)
- `monalg-add-zero-right` — forall([a_, m_, f_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, monalg-zero(a_, m_)) = f_)
- `monalg-carr` — forall([a_, m_], carr(monalg(a_, m_)) == finsupp(a_, m_))
- `monalg-comm` — forall([a, m], is-commutative-ring(a) implies is-comm-monoid(m) implies is-commutative-ring(monalg(a, m)))
- `monalg-distrib-left` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, h_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies h_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, monalg-add(a_, m_, g_, h_)) = monalg-add(a_, m_, monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, g_), monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, h_)))
- `monalg-distrib-left-at` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, h_, x_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies h_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies x_ in carr(m_) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, monalg-add(a_, m_, g_, h_))): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))(f_(nth(1, p)), (monalg-add(a_, m_, g_, h_))(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, monalg-add(a_, m_, g_, h_))): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}) = (add(a_))(finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))(f_(nth(1, p)), g_(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))(f_(nth(1, p)), h_(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_})))
- `monalg-distrib-right` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, h_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies h_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-mul(a_, m_, monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, g_), h_) = monalg-add(a_, m_, monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, h_), monalg-mul(a_, m_, g_, h_)))
- `monalg-distrib-right-at` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, h_, x_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies h_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies x_ in carr(m_) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, g_)), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))((monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, g_))(nth(1, p)), h_(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, monalg-add(a_, m_, f_, g_)), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}) = (add(a_))(finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))(f_(nth(1, p)), h_(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, g_), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))(g_(nth(1, p)), h_(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, g_), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_})))
- `monalg-ext` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_], f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies forall([x_ in carr(m_)], f_(x_) = g_(x_)) implies f_ = g_)
- `monalg-index-swap-bijection` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, x_], is-comm-monoid(m_) implies vnb-lambda(q_, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, [nth(2, q_), nth(1, q_)]) in bijection({p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, g_), supp(a_, m_, f_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}))
- `monalg-index-swap-fun` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, x_], is-comm-monoid(m_) implies vnb-lambda(q_, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, [nth(2, q_), nth(1, q_)]) in fun({p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, g_), supp(a_, m_, f_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}))
- `monalg-index-swap-inverse` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, x_], is-comm-monoid(m_) implies forall([u_ in {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}], (vnb-lambda(q_, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, g_), supp(a_, m_, f_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, [nth(2, q_), nth(1, q_)]))((vnb-lambda(q_, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, [nth(2, q_), nth(1, q_)]))(u_)) = u_))
- `monalg-is-ring` — forall([a, m], is-ring(a) implies is-monoid(m) implies is-ring(monalg(a, m)))
- `monalg-mul-apply` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, x_ in carr(m_)], (monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, g_))(x_) == finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))(f_(nth(1, p)), g_(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}))
- `monalg-mul-assoc` — forall([a, m, f, g, h], is-ring(a) implies is-monoid(m) implies f in finsupp(a, m) implies g in finsupp(a, m) implies h in finsupp(a, m) implies monalg-mul(a, m, monalg-mul(a, m, f, g), h) = monalg-mul(a, m, f, monalg-mul(a, m, g, h)))
- `monalg-mul-assoc-at` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, h_, x_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies h_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies x_ in carr(m_) implies (monalg-mul(a_, m_, monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, g_), h_))(x_) = (monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, monalg-mul(a_, m_, g_, h_)))(x_))
- `monalg-mul-assoc-left-core` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, h_, x_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies h_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies x_ in carr(m_) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(image(vnb-lambda(q, cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)), (opr(m_))(nth(1, q), nth(2, q))), cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_))), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))((monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, g_))(nth(1, p)), h_(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(image(vnb-lambda(q, cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)), (opr(m_))(nth(1, q), nth(2, q))), cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_))), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(w, {w in cartesian(cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))((opr(m_))(nth(1, nth(1, w)), nth(2, nth(1, w))), nth(2, w)) = x_}, (mul(a_))((mul(a_))(f_(nth(1, nth(1, w))), g_(nth(2, nth(1, w)))), h_(nth(2, w)))), {w in cartesian(cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))((opr(m_))(nth(1, nth(1, w)), nth(2, nth(1, w))), nth(2, w)) = x_}))
- `monalg-mul-assoc-right-core` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, h_, x_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies h_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies x_ in carr(m_) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), image(vnb-lambda(q, cartesian(supp(a_, m_, g_), supp(a_, m_, h_)), (opr(m_))(nth(1, q), nth(2, q))), cartesian(supp(a_, m_, g_), supp(a_, m_, h_)))): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))(f_(nth(1, p)), (monalg-mul(a_, m_, g_, h_))(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), image(vnb-lambda(q, cartesian(supp(a_, m_, g_), supp(a_, m_, h_)), (opr(m_))(nth(1, q), nth(2, q))), cartesian(supp(a_, m_, g_), supp(a_, m_, h_)))): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(w, {w in cartesian(cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))((opr(m_))(nth(1, nth(1, w)), nth(2, nth(1, w))), nth(2, w)) = x_}, (mul(a_))(f_(nth(1, nth(1, w))), (mul(a_))(g_(nth(2, nth(1, w))), h_(nth(2, w))))), {w in cartesian(cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)), supp(a_, m_, h_)): (opr(m_))((opr(m_))(nth(1, nth(1, w)), nth(2, nth(1, w))), nth(2, w)) = x_}))
- `monalg-mul-comm` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_], is-commutative-ring(a_) implies is-comm-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, g_) = monalg-mul(a_, m_, g_, f_))
- `monalg-mul-comm-at` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, x_], is-commutative-ring(a_) implies is-comm-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies x_ in carr(m_) implies (monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, g_))(x_) = (monalg-mul(a_, m_, g_, f_))(x_))
- `monalg-mul-fun` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, g_) in finsupp(a_, m_))
- `monalg-mul-op` — forall([a_, m_], mul(monalg(a_, m_)) == vnb-lambda([f, g], cartesian(finsupp(a_, m_), finsupp(a_, m_)), monalg-mul(a_, m_, f, g)))
- `monalg-mul-supp-in-image` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, z_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies z_ in supp(a_, m_, monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, g_)) implies z_ in image(vnb-lambda(q, cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_)), (opr(m_))(nth(1, q), nth(2, q))), cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, g_))))
- `monalg-mul-value-in-carr` — forall([a_, m_, f_, g_, x_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies g_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies x_ in carr(m_) implies (monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, g_))(x_) in carr(a_))
- `monalg-neg-apply` — forall([a_, m_, f_, x_ in carr(m_)], (monalg-neg(a_, m_, f_))(x_) == (neg(a_))(f_(x_)))
- `monalg-neg-fun` — forall([a_, m_, f_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-neg(a_, m_, f_) in finsupp(a_, m_))
- `monalg-neg-op` — forall([a_, m_], neg(monalg(a_, m_)) == vnb-lambda(f, finsupp(a_, m_), monalg-neg(a_, m_, f)))
- `monalg-one-apply` — forall([a_, m_, x_ in carr(m_)], (monalg-one(a_, m_))(x_) == if(x_ = iden(m_), one(a_), zero(a_)))
- `monalg-one-in` — forall([a_, m_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies monalg-one(a_, m_) in finsupp(a_, m_))
- `monalg-one-left` — forall([a_, m_, f_, x_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies x_ in carr(m_) implies (monalg-mul(a_, m_, monalg-one(a_, m_), f_))(x_) = f_(x_))
- `monalg-one-left-at` — forall([a_, m_, f_, x_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies x_ in carr(m_) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, monalg-one(a_, m_)), supp(a_, m_, f_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))((monalg-one(a_, m_))(nth(1, p)), f_(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, monalg-one(a_, m_)), supp(a_, m_, f_)): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}) = f_(x_))
- `monalg-one-left-fn` — forall([a_, m_, f_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-mul(a_, m_, monalg-one(a_, m_), f_) = f_)
- `monalg-one-right-at` — forall([a_, m_, f_, x_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies x_ in carr(m_) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(a_), vnb-lambda(p, {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, monalg-one(a_, m_))): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}, (mul(a_))(f_(nth(1, p)), (monalg-one(a_, m_))(nth(2, p)))), {p in cartesian(supp(a_, m_, f_), supp(a_, m_, monalg-one(a_, m_))): (opr(m_))(nth(1, p), nth(2, p)) = x_}) = f_(x_))
- `monalg-one-right-fn` — forall([a_, m_, f_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies f_ in finsupp(a_, m_) implies monalg-mul(a_, m_, f_, monalg-one(a_, m_)) = f_)
- `monalg-one-slot` — forall([a_, m_], one(monalg(a_, m_)) == monalg-one(a_, m_))
- `monalg-one-supp-subset` — forall([a_, m_, z_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies z_ in supp(a_, m_, monalg-one(a_, m_)) implies z_ in pair(iden(m_), iden(m_)))
- `monalg-zero-apply` — forall([a_, m_, x_ in carr(m_)], (monalg-zero(a_, m_))(x_) == zero(a_))
- `monalg-zero-fun` — forall([a_, m_], carr(m_) in set implies zero(a_) in carr(a_) implies monalg-zero(a_, m_) in fun(carr(m_), carr(a_)))
- `monalg-zero-in` — forall([a_, m_], is-ring(a_) implies is-monoid(m_) implies monalg-zero(a_, m_) in finsupp(a_, m_))
- `monalg-zero-slot` — forall([a_, m_], zero(monalg(a_, m_)) == monalg-zero(a_, m_))
- `monoid-assoc` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `monoid-carrier-closed-opr` — forall([m, a, b], is-monoid(m) and a in carr(m) and b in carr(m) implies (opr(m))(a, b) in carr(m))
- `monoid-carrier-is-set` — forall([m_], is-monoid(m_) implies carr(m_) in set)
- `monoid-identity-in` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies iden(m) in carr(m))
- `monoid-left-id` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(iden(s), a) = a))
- `monoid-right-id` — forall([s], is-monoid(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a, iden(s)) = a))
- `monomial-at` — forall([a_, c_, n_ in nn], (monomial(a_, c_, n_))(n_) = c_)
- `monomial-deg` — forall([a_, c_, n_], is-ring(a_) implies c_ in carr(a_) implies not(c_ = zero(a_)) implies n_ in nn implies deg(a_, monomial(a_, c_, n_)) = n_)
- `monomial-in-poly` — forall([a_, c_, n_], is-ring(a_) implies c_ in carr(a_) implies n_ in nn implies monomial(a_, c_, n_) in carr(poly(a_)))
- `monomial-leadcoef` — forall([a_, c_, n_], is-ring(a_) implies c_ in carr(a_) implies not(c_ = zero(a_)) implies n_ in nn implies leadcoef(a_, monomial(a_, c_, n_)) = c_)
- `monomial-off` — forall([a_, c_, n_, k_ in nn], not(k_ = n_) implies (monomial(a_, c_, n_))(k_) = zero(a_))
- `monomial-support` — forall([a_, c_, n_, k_], is-ring(a_) implies c_ in carr(a_) implies not(c_ = zero(a_)) implies n_ in nn implies k_ in supp(a_, nn-add-monoid, monomial(a_, c_, n_)) iff k_ in nn and k_ = n_)
- `monotone-ccint-inverse-exists` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies is-strictly-increasing-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([y in ccint(f(a), f(b))], forsome([p_ in ccint(a, b)], f(p_) = y and forall([q_ in ccint(a, b)], f(q_) = y implies p_ = q_))))
- `monotone-ccint-inverse-order` — forall([f, a, b], is-strictly-increasing-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([u_ in ccint(a, b), v_ in ccint(a, b)], f(u_) < f(v_) implies u_ < v_))
- `monotone-convergence-rr` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= f(succ(k))) and forsome([bnd in rr], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= bnd)) implies converges(rr-ms, f))
- `monotone-convergent-bounded-above` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= f(succ(k))) implies converges(rr-ms, f) implies forsome([bnd in rr], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= bnd)))
- `mpow-add` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m), j in nn, k in nn], mpow(m, x, j + k) = (opr(m))(mpow(m, x, j), mpow(m, x, k))))
- `mpow-add-ind` — forall([j in nn, m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m), k in nn], mpow(m, x, j + k) = (opr(m))(mpow(m, x, j), mpow(m, x, k))))
- `mpow-comm-monoid-type-ind` — forall([n in nn, m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m)], mpow(m, x, n) in carr(m)))
- `mpow-one` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m)], mpow(m, x, 1) = x))
- `mpow-type` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m), n in nn], mpow(m, x, n) in carr(m)))
- `mpow-type-ind` — forall([n in nn, m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m)], mpow(m, x, n) in carr(m)))
- `mra-combined-summand-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, c1, c2, u], c1 in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies c2 in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), (opr(module-vector-ag(md)))((vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))(entry(c1, 1, j), entry(u, j, 1))))(z), (vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))(entry(c2, 1, j), entry(u, j, 1))))(z))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `mrs-scaled-summand-type` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, r, c, u], r in carr(scal(md)) implies c in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md))) implies u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), (act(md))(r, (vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (act(md))(entry(c, 1, j), entry(u, j, 1))))(z))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(module-vector-ag(md)))))
- `ms-agree-ball` — forall([msa_, msb_], is-metric-space(msa_) implies is-metric-space(msb_) implies pts(msb_) == pts(msa_) implies forall([dfu_ in pts(msa_), dfv_ in pts(msa_)], (dist(msb_))(dfu_, dfv_) == (dist(msa_))(dfu_, dfv_)) implies forall([dfc_ in pts(msa_), dfr_], ball(msb_, dfc_, dfr_) = ball(msa_, dfc_, dfr_)))
- `ms-agree-continuous-at` — forall([msa_, msb_, mta_, mtb_, dfg_, dfp_], is-metric-space(msb_) implies is-metric-space(mtb_) implies pts(msb_) == pts(msa_) implies pts(mtb_) == pts(mta_) implies forall([dfu_ in pts(msa_), dfv_ in pts(msa_)], (dist(msb_))(dfu_, dfv_) == (dist(msa_))(dfu_, dfv_)) implies forall([dfu_ in pts(mta_), dfv_ in pts(mta_)], (dist(mtb_))(dfu_, dfv_) == (dist(mta_))(dfu_, dfv_)) implies is-continuous-at(msa_, mta_, dfg_, dfp_) implies is-continuous-at(msb_, mtb_, dfg_, dfp_))
- `ms-agree-dist-sym` — forall([msa_, msb_], pts(msb_) == pts(msa_) implies forall([dfu_ in pts(msa_), dfv_ in pts(msa_)], (dist(msb_))(dfu_, dfv_) == (dist(msa_))(dfu_, dfv_)) implies forall([dfu_ in pts(msb_), dfv_ in pts(msb_)], (dist(msa_))(dfu_, dfv_) == (dist(msb_))(dfu_, dfv_)))
- `ms-agree-open` — forall([msa_, msb_], is-metric-space(msb_) implies pts(msb_) == pts(msa_) implies forall([dfu_ in pts(msa_), dfv_ in pts(msa_)], (dist(msb_))(dfu_, dfv_) == (dist(msa_))(dfu_, dfv_)) implies forall([dfw_], is-open(msa_, dfw_) implies is-open(msb_, dfw_)))
- `ms-agree-pts-sym` — forall([msa_, msb_], pts(msb_) == pts(msa_) implies pts(msa_) == pts(msb_))
- `ms-compose-continuous-at` — forall([mss_, mst_, msu_, msf_, msg_, msa_], is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, msf_, msa_) implies is-continuous-at(mst_, msu_, msg_, msf_(msa_)) implies is-continuous-at(mss_, msu_, vnb-lambda(msz_, pts(mss_), msg_(msf_(msz_))), msa_))
- `ms-compose-continuous-on` — forall([mss_, d2d_], pts(mss_) == d2d_ implies forall([mst_, msu_, msf_, msg_, msa_], is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, msf_, msa_) implies is-continuous-at(mst_, msu_, msg_, msf_(msa_)) implies is-continuous-at(mss_, msu_, vnb-lambda(msz_, d2d_, msg_(msf_(msz_))), msa_)))
- `ms-const-continuous-at` — forall([mss_], is-metric-space(mss_) implies forall([mst_], is-metric-space(mst_) implies forall([msc_ in pts(mst_), msa_ in pts(mss_)], is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, vnb-lambda(msz_, pts(mss_), msc_), msa_))))
- `ms-const-continuous-on` — forall([mss_, d2d_], pts(mss_) == d2d_ implies forall([mst_], is-metric-space(mss_) implies is-metric-space(mst_) implies forall([msc_ in pts(mst_), msa_ in pts(mss_)], is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, vnb-lambda(msz_, d2d_, msc_), msa_))))
- `ms-const-lam-in-fun` — forall([mss_], is-metric-space(mss_) implies forall([mst_, msc_ in pts(mst_)], vnb-lambda(msz_, pts(mss_), msc_) in fun(pts(mss_), pts(mst_))))
- `ms-cont-agree-off-pt` — forall([mss_, mst_, msf_, msg_, msp_], is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, msf_, msp_) implies is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, msg_, msp_) implies forall([msr_], pos-rr(msr_) implies forsome([msb_ in pts(mss_)], not(msb_ = msp_) and (dist(mss_))(msp_, msb_) <= msr_)) implies forall([msy_ in pts(mss_)], not(msy_ = msp_) implies msf_(msy_) = msg_(msy_)) implies msf_(msp_) = msg_(msp_))
- `ms-cont-transfer-on` — forall([mss_, mst_, d2d_, d2c_], pts(mss_) == d2d_ implies pts(mst_) == d2c_ implies forall([msg_, msf_, msa_], is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, msg_, msa_) implies msf_ in fun(d2d_, d2c_) implies forall([msy_ in d2d_], msf_(msy_) = msg_(msy_)) implies is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, msf_, msa_)))
- `ms-cont-transfer-ptwise-eq` — forall([mss_, mst_, msg_, msf_, msa_], is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, msg_, msa_) implies msf_ in fun(pts(mss_), pts(mst_)) implies forall([msz_ in pts(mss_)], msf_(msz_) = msg_(msz_)) implies is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, msf_, msa_))
- `ms-corestrict-continuous` — forall([mss_, mst_], is-metric-space(mst_) implies forall([d2s_], d2s_ subset pts(mst_) implies forall([msf_, msa_], is-continuous-at(mss_, mst_, msf_, msa_) implies forall([msy_ in pts(mss_)], msf_(msy_) in d2s_) implies is-continuous-at(mss_, subspace-ms(mst_, d2s_), msf_, msa_))))
- `ms-eq-symm` — forall([x_, y_], x_ = y_ iff y_ = x_)
- `ms-ident-lam-in-fun` — forall([mss_], is-metric-space(mss_) implies vnb-lambda(msz_, pts(mss_), msz_) in fun(pts(mss_), pts(mss_)))
- `ms-identity-continuous-at` — forall([mss_], is-metric-space(mss_) implies forall([msa_ in pts(mss_)], is-continuous-at(mss_, mss_, vnb-lambda(msz_, pts(mss_), msz_), msa_)))
- `ms-identity-continuous-on` — forall([mss_, d2d_], pts(mss_) == d2d_ implies is-metric-space(mss_) implies forall([msa_ in pts(mss_)], is-continuous-at(mss_, mss_, vnb-lambda(msz_, d2d_, msz_), msa_)))
- `ms-index-2` — forall([i_ in nn], 1 <= i_ implies i_ <= 2 implies i_ = 1 or i_ = 2)
- `ms-nth-2` — forall([a_, b_, i_ in nn], 1 <= i_ implies i_ <= 2 implies nth(i_, [a_, b_]) = a_ or nth(i_, [a_, b_]) = b_)
- `ms-pts-is-set` — forall([mss_], is-metric-space(mss_) implies pts(mss_) in set)
- `mvag-carr` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies carr(module-vector-ag(md)) = vec(md))
- `mvag-id` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies iden(module-vector-ag(md)) = vzero(md))
- `mvag-op` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies opr(module-vector-ag(md)) = vadd(md))
- `mvi-abs-off-countable` — forall([zh_ in fun(rr, rr), zm_ in rr, zu_ in rr, zv_ in rr, zc_], zu_ < zv_ implies forall([zx_ in ccint(zu_, zv_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, zh_, zx_)) implies is-countable(zc_) implies zc_ subset rr implies forall([zt_ in ooint(zu_, zv_)], not(zt_ in zc_) implies forsome([zl_], is-diff-at(zh_, zt_, zl_) and abs(zl_) <= zm_)) implies abs(zh_(zv_) - zh_(zu_)) <= zm_ * (zv_ - zu_))
- `mvi-abs-off-countable-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, zm_ in rr], a < b implies forall([zf_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(zf_, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([zd_], is-countable(zd_) implies zd_ subset ccint(a, b) implies forall([zt_ in ooint(a, b)], not(zt_ in zd_) implies forsome([zl_], has-deriv-at(zf_, zt_, zl_) and abs(zl_) <= zm_)) implies abs(zf_(b) - zf_(a)) <= zm_ * (b - a))))
- `mvi-off-countable` — forall([zh_ in fun(rr, rr), zm_ in rr, zu_ in rr, zv_ in rr, zc_], zu_ < zv_ implies forall([zx_ in ccint(zu_, zv_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, zh_, zx_)) implies is-countable(zc_) implies zc_ subset rr implies forall([zt_ in ooint(zu_, zv_)], not(zt_ in zc_) implies forsome([zl_], is-diff-at(zh_, zt_, zl_) and zl_ <= zm_)) implies zh_(zv_) - zh_(zu_) <= zm_ * (zv_ - zu_))
- `mvt` — forall([f, a, b], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, x, l))) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < b and forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, theta, l) and l * (b - a) = f(b) - f(a))))
- `mvt-abs-bound` — forall([f, a, b, m_], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies m_ in rr implies a < b implies forall([x_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x_)) implies forall([x_], x_ in rr and a < x_ and x_ < b implies forsome([l_ in rr], is-diff-at(f, x_, l_) and abs(l_) <= m_)) implies abs(f(b) - f(a)) <= m_ * (b - a))
- `mvt-abs-bound-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, m in rr], a < b implies forall([f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([x in ooint(a, b)], forsome([l], has-deriv-at(f, x, l) and abs(l) <= m)) implies abs(f(b) - f(a)) <= m * (b - a)))
- `mvt-aux-cont` — forall([f, a, b, x], a in rr implies b in rr implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(z) * (b - a) - z * (f(b) - f(a))), x))
- `mvt-aux-diff` — forall([f, a, b, x, l], a in rr implies b in rr implies is-diff-at(f, x, l) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(z) * (b - a) - z * (f(b) - f(a))), x, l * (b - a) - (f(b) - f(a))))
- `mvt-lower-bound` — forall([f, a, b, m], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and m in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, x, l) and m <= l)) implies m * (b - a) <= f(b) - f(a))
- `mvt-lower-bound-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, m in rr], a < b implies forall([f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([x in ooint(a, b)], forsome([l], has-deriv-at(f, x, l) and m <= l)) implies m * (b - a) <= f(b) - f(a)))
- `mvt-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([x in ooint(a, b)], forsome([l], has-deriv-at(f, x, l))) implies forsome([theta in ooint(a, b), l], has-deriv-at(f, theta, l) and l = (f(b) - f(a)) * recip(b - a))))
- `mvt-upper-bound` — forall([f, a, b, m], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and m in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(f, x, l) and l <= m)) implies f(b) - f(a) <= m * (b - a))
- `mvt-upper-bound-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, m in rr], a < b implies forall([f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([x in ooint(a, b)], forsome([l], has-deriv-at(f, x, l) and l <= m)) implies f(b) - f(a) <= m * (b - a)))
- `nag-metric-carrier` — forall([nag], pts(nag-metric-space(nag)) == carr(nag))
- `nag-metric-distance` — forall([nag], is-normed-ag(nag) implies forall([u in carr(nag), v in carr(nag)], (dist(nag-metric-space(nag)))(u, v) = (nrm(nag))((opr(nag))(u, (inv(nag))(v)))))
- `nag-metric-space-is-metric-space` — forall([nag], is-normed-ag(nag) implies is-metric-space(nag-metric-space(nag)))
- `neg-continuous-at` — forall([f, x], f in fun(rr, rr) implies x in rr implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, x) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z_, rr, -f(z_)), x))
- `neg-deriv-off-countable-nonincreasing` — forall([zh_ in fun(rr, rr), zu_ in rr, zv_ in rr, zc_], zu_ < zv_ implies forall([zx_ in ccint(zu_, zv_)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, zh_, zx_)) implies is-countable(zc_) implies zc_ subset rr implies forall([zt_ in ooint(zu_, zv_)], not(zt_ in zc_) implies forsome([zl_], is-diff-at(zh_, zt_, zl_) and zl_ < 0)) implies zh_(zv_) <= zh_(zu_))
- `neg-fun-in-fun` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr)], vnb-lambda(z_, rr, -f(z_)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `neg-inf-in-rr-star` — neg-inf in rr-star
- `neg-inf-not-in-rr-pos-star` — not(neg-inf in rr-pos-star)
- `neq-sym` — forall([a, b], not(a = b) implies not(b = a))
- `nested-closed-balls-point` — forall([s], is-complete(s) implies forall([ctr_ in fun(nn, pts(s)), rds_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n in nn], 0 <= rds_(n)) implies forall([n in nn], closed-ball(s, ctr_(succ(n)), rds_(succ(n))) subset closed-ball(s, ctr_(n), rds_(n))) implies forall([n in nn], rds_(succ(n)) <= recip(n + 1)) implies forsome([lv_ in pts(s)], forall([n in nn], lv_ in closed-ball(s, ctr_(n), rds_(n))))))
- `nested-family-monotone` — forall([m in nn, w, gg in fun(nn, w)], forall([n in nn], gg(succ(n)) subset gg(n)) implies forall([n in nn], n <= m implies gg(m) subset gg(n)))
- `nf-add-in-carr` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in carr(k) implies v_ in carr(k) implies (add(k))(u_, v_) in carr(k)))
- `nf-cc-agree-dist` — forall([dfu_ in pts(cc-ms), dfv_ in pts(cc-ms)], (dist(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field)))(dfu_, dfv_) == (dist(cc-ms))(dfu_, dfv_))
- `nf-cc-agree-pts` — pts(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field)) == pts(cc-ms)
- `nf-cc-is-metric-space` — is-metric-space(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field))
- `nf-lam-comp-z-in-fun` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u in set, d2s_, d2f_, d2g_ in fun(d2s_, carr(k))], forall([d2y_ in u], d2f_(d2y_) in d2s_) implies vnb-lambda(msz_, u, d2g_(d2f_(msz_))) in fun(u, carr(k))))
- `nf-lam-const-in-fun` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u in set, d2c_ in carr(k)], vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, d2c_) in fun(u, carr(k))))
- `nf-lam-const-z-in-fun` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u in set, d2c_ in carr(k)], vnb-lambda(msz_, u, d2c_) in fun(u, carr(k))))
- `nf-lam-id-in-fun` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u in set], u subset carr(k) implies vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, d2x_) in fun(u, carr(k))))
- `nf-lam-prod-in-fun` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u in set, d2f_ in fun(u, carr(k)), d2g_ in fun(u, carr(k))], vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, (mul(k))(d2f_(d2x_), d2g_(d2x_))) in fun(u, carr(k))))
- `nf-lam-prod-v-in-fun` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u in set, d2f_ in fun(u, carr(k)), d2g_ in fun(u, carr(k))], vnb-lambda(d2v_, u, (mul(k))(d2f_(d2v_), d2g_(d2v_))) in fun(u, carr(k))))
- `nf-lam-sum-in-fun` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u in set, d2f_ in fun(u, carr(k)), d2g_ in fun(u, carr(k))], vnb-lambda(d2x_, u, (add(k))(d2f_(d2x_), d2g_(d2x_))) in fun(u, carr(k))))
- `nf-metric-space-is-metric-space` — forall([nf], is-normed-field(nf) implies is-metric-space(nf-metric-space(nf)))
- `nf-mul-cancel` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([d2u_, d2v_, d2w_], d2u_ in carr(k) implies d2v_ in carr(k) implies d2w_ in carr(k) implies not(d2w_ = zero(k)) implies (mul(k))(d2u_, d2w_) = (mul(k))(d2v_, d2w_) implies d2u_ = d2v_))
- `nf-mul-in-carr` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in carr(k) implies v_ in carr(k) implies (mul(k))(u_, v_) in carr(k)))
- `nf-mul-sub-split` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([nfu_, nfv_, nfp_, nfq_], nfu_ in carr(k) implies nfv_ in carr(k) implies nfp_ in carr(k) implies nfq_ in carr(k) implies (add(k))((mul(k))(nfu_, nfv_), (neg(k))((mul(k))(nfp_, nfq_))) = (add(k))((mul(k))(nfu_, (add(k))(nfv_, (neg(k))(nfq_))), (mul(k))(nfq_, (add(k))(nfu_, (neg(k))(nfp_))))))
- `nf-neg-continuous-at` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([mss_, msg_, msa_], is-continuous-at(mss_, nf-metric-space(k), msg_, msa_) implies is-continuous-at(mss_, nf-metric-space(k), vnb-lambda(msz_, pts(mss_), (neg(k))(msg_(msz_))), msa_)))
- `nf-neg-in-carr` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u_ in carr(k)], (neg(k))(u_) in carr(k)))
- `nf-neg-lam-in-fun` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([mss_], is-metric-space(mss_) implies forall([msg_ in fun(pts(mss_), pts(nf-metric-space(k)))], vnb-lambda(msz_, pts(mss_), (neg(k))(msg_(msz_))) in fun(pts(mss_), pts(nf-metric-space(k))))))
- `nf-neg-sub-split` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([nfu_, nfv_], nfu_ in carr(k) implies nfv_ in carr(k) implies (add(k))((neg(k))(nfu_), (neg(k))((neg(k))(nfv_))) = (neg(k))((add(k))(nfu_, (neg(k))(nfv_)))))
- `nf-norm-in-rr` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u_ in carr(k)], (fnrm(k))(u_) in rr))
- `nf-norm-le-add` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in carr(k) implies v_ in carr(k) implies (fnrm(k))(u_) <= (fnrm(k))((add(k))(u_, (neg(k))(v_))) + (fnrm(k))(v_)))
- `nf-norm-mult` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in carr(k) implies v_ in carr(k) implies (fnrm(k))((mul(k))(u_, v_)) = (fnrm(k))(u_) * (fnrm(k))(v_)))
- `nf-norm-neg` — forall([nf], is-normed-field(nf) implies forall([a_ in carr(nf)], (fnrm(nf))((neg(nf))(a_)) = (fnrm(nf))(a_)))
- `nf-norm-neg-diff` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([nfu_, nfv_], nfu_ in carr(k) implies nfv_ in carr(k) implies (fnrm(k))((add(k))((neg(k))(nfu_), (neg(k))((neg(k))(nfv_)))) = (fnrm(k))((add(k))(nfu_, (neg(k))(nfv_)))))
- `nf-norm-nonneg` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([u_ in carr(k)], 0 <= (fnrm(k))(u_)))
- `nf-norm-of-zero` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies (fnrm(k))(zero(k)) = 0)
- `nf-norm-prod-bound` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([nfu_, nfv_, nfp_, nfq_, nfm_, nfk_, nft_, nfe_], nfu_ in carr(k) implies nfv_ in carr(k) implies nfp_ in carr(k) implies nfq_ in carr(k) implies nfm_ in rr implies nfk_ in rr implies nft_ in rr implies nfe_ in rr implies (fnrm(k))(nfu_) <= nfm_ implies (fnrm(k))(nfq_) <= nfk_ implies (fnrm(k))((add(k))(nfu_, (neg(k))(nfp_))) <= nft_ implies (fnrm(k))((add(k))(nfv_, (neg(k))(nfq_))) <= nft_ implies (nfm_ + nfk_) * nft_ <= nfe_ implies (fnrm(k))((add(k))((mul(k))(nfu_, nfv_), (neg(k))((mul(k))(nfp_, nfq_)))) <= nfe_))
- `nf-norm-sub-sym` — forall([k], is-normed-field(k) implies forall([nfu_, nfv_], nfu_ in carr(k) implies nfv_ in carr(k) implies (fnrm(k))((add(k))(nfu_, (neg(k))(nfv_))) = (fnrm(k))((add(k))(nfv_, (neg(k))(nfu_)))))

- `normed-field-as-commutative-ring-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-field(a, b, f) implies is-hom-commutative-ring(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(a), normed-field-as-commutative-ring(b), f))
- `normed-field-as-integral-domain-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-field(a, b, f) implies is-hom-integral-domain(normed-field-as-integral-domain(a), normed-field-as-integral-domain(b), f))
- `normed-field-ring-view-add` — forall([f_], add(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == add(f_))
- `normed-field-ring-view-carr` — forall([f_], carr(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == carr(f_))
- `normed-field-ring-view-mul` — forall([f_], mul(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == mul(f_))
- `normed-field-ring-view-neg` — forall([f_], neg(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == neg(f_))
- `normed-field-ring-view-one` — forall([f_], one(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == one(f_))
- `normed-field-ring-view-zero` — forall([f_], zero(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(f_)) == zero(f_))
- `normed-vector-space-as-module-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-vector-space(a, b, f) implies is-hom-module(normed-vector-space-as-module(a), normed-vector-space-as-module(b), f))
- `normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-normed-vector-space(a, b, f) implies is-hom-normed-ag(normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag(a), normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag(b), f))
- `not-continuous-witness` — forall([s, t, f, a, eps, dl], not(forall([b_ in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b_) <= dl implies (dist(t))(f(a), f(b_)) <= eps)) implies forsome([b_ in pts(s)], (dist(s))(a, b_) <= dl and not((dist(t))(f(a), f(b_)) <= eps)))
- `nowhere-dense-ball-point` — forall([s, a], is-nowhere-dense(s, a) implies forall([c in pts(s), r], pos-rr(r) implies forsome([x in ball(s, c, r)], not(x in closure(s, a)))))
- `nowhere-dense-closed-ball` — forall([s, a], is-nowhere-dense(s, a) implies forall([c in pts(s), r], pos-rr(r) implies forall([bnd], pos-rr(bnd) implies forsome([x in pts(s), q], pos-rr(q) and q <= bnd and closed-ball(s, x, q) subset ball(s, c, r) and forall([z in closed-ball(s, x, q)], not(z in closure(s, a)))))))
- `nt-unit-factor` — forall([y_, k_], y_ in rr implies k_ in nn implies y_ * (1 - 0) ^ succ(k_) = y_)
- `nth-deriv-monomial` — forall([n in nn, tck_ in nn], tck_ <= n implies nth-deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ n), tck_) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, (factorial(n) * recip(factorial(nn-minus(n, tck_)))) * x ^ nn-minus(n, tck_)))
- `nth-deriv-monomial-beyond` — forall([tcj_ in nn, n in nn], nth-deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ n), succ(n + tcj_)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, 0))
- `nth-deriv-monomial-core` — forall([tck_ in nn, tcm_ in nn], nth-deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ (tcm_ + tck_)), tck_) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, (factorial(tcm_ + tck_) * recip(factorial(tcm_))) * x ^ tcm_))
- `nth-deriv-monomial-gap` — forall([tck_ in nn, tcm_ in nn, tcp_], tcp_ = tcm_ + tck_ implies nth-deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ tcp_), tck_) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, (factorial(tcp_) * recip(factorial(tcm_))) * x ^ tcm_))
- `nth-deriv-monomial-vanish` — forall([n in nn, tck_ in nn], n < tck_ implies nth-deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ n), tck_) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, 0))
- `nth-deriv-on-holomorphic-ball` — forall([cf in fun(nn, cc), cpdr_ in rr], 0 < cpdr_ implies cpdr_ < cps-radius(cf) implies forall([ndlk_ in nn], holomorphic-on(ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), nth-deriv-on(cc-normed-field, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), vnb-lambda(cpdz_, ball(cc-ms, 0, cpdr_), cc-series-limit(vnb-lambda(cpsk_, nn, cf(cpsk_) * cpdz_ ^ cpsk_))), ndlk_))))
- `nth-deriv-on-in-fun` — forall([ndlkf_], is-normed-field(ndlkf_) implies forall([d2e_], pos-rr(d2e_) implies forsome([d2h_ in carr(ndlkf_)], not(d2h_ = zero(ndlkf_)) and (fnrm(ndlkf_))(d2h_) < d2e_)) implies forall([ndlu_, ndlf_ in fun(ndlu_, carr(ndlkf_)), ndlj_ in nn], forall([ndli_ in nn], ndli_ < ndlj_ implies forall([ndlz_ in ndlu_], forsome([ndll_], is-diff-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, nth-deriv-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, ndlf_, ndli_), ndlz_, ndll_)))) implies nth-deriv-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, ndlf_, ndlj_) in fun(ndlu_, carr(ndlkf_))))
- `nth-deriv-on-of-is-diff-on` — forall([ndlkf_], is-normed-field(ndlkf_) implies forall([d2e_], pos-rr(d2e_) implies forsome([d2h_ in carr(ndlkf_)], not(d2h_ = zero(ndlkf_)) and (fnrm(ndlkf_))(d2h_) < d2e_)) implies forall([ndlu_, ndlf_, ndlj_ in nn, ndlg_], forall([ndlz_ in ndlu_], is-diff-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, nth-deriv-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, ndlf_, ndlj_), ndlz_, ndlg_(ndlz_))) implies forall([ndlw_ in ndlu_], (nth-deriv-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, ndlf_, succ(ndlj_)))(ndlw_) = ndlg_(ndlw_))))
- `nth-deriv-on-succ-apply` — forall([ndlkf_, ndlu_, ndlf_, ndlj_ in nn, ndlz_ in ndlu_], (nth-deriv-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, ndlf_, succ(ndlj_)))(ndlz_) == deriv-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, nth-deriv-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, ndlf_, ndlj_), ndlz_))
- `nth-deriv-on-succ-eq` — forall([ndlkf_], is-normed-field(ndlkf_) implies forall([d2e_], pos-rr(d2e_) implies forsome([d2h_ in carr(ndlkf_)], not(d2h_ = zero(ndlkf_)) and (fnrm(ndlkf_))(d2h_) < d2e_)) implies forall([ndlu_, ndlf_, ndlj_ in nn, ndlg_ in fun(ndlu_, carr(ndlkf_))], forall([ndlz_ in ndlu_], is-diff-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, nth-deriv-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, ndlf_, ndlj_), ndlz_, ndlg_(ndlz_))) implies nth-deriv-on(ndlkf_, ndlu_, ndlf_, succ(ndlj_)) = ndlg_))
- `nth-deriv-one` — forall([f], nth-deriv(f, 1) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv(f, x)))
- `nth-deriv-succ-inner` — forall([tck_ in nn, f], nth-deriv(f, succ(tck_)) == nth-deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, deriv(f, x)), tck_))
- `nth-deriv-v-in-vec` — forall([m, f, k, t], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies k in nn implies t in rr implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= k implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))) implies (nth-deriv-v(m, f, k))(t) in vec(m))
- `nth1-pair` — forall([a, b], nth(1, [a, b]) == a)
- `nth2-pair` — forall([a, b], nth(2, [a, b]) == b)
- `null-rr-seq-exists` — forsome([rad], null-rr-seq(rad))
- `nvs-act-add-scalars` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, s_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(s_, x_)) = (act(m))(r_ + s_, x_)))
- `nvs-act-collect` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, s_ in rr, x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, x_)), (act(m))(s_, x_)) = (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_ + s_, x_))))
- `nvs-act-distrib-scalar` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))((add(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(s_, x_))))
- `nvs-act-distrib-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, (vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(r_, y_))))
- `nvs-act-distrib-vec-rr` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, (vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(m))((act(m))(r_, x_), (act(m))(r_, y_))))
- `nvs-act-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, x_) in vec(m)))
- `nvs-act-mul-compat` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), s_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))((mul(scal(m)))(r_, s_), x_) = (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(s_, x_))))
- `nvs-act-one` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(1, x_)) = (vadd(m))(y_, x_)))
- `nvs-act-scale-assoc` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, s_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, (act(m))(s_, x_)) = (act(m))(r_ * s_, x_)))
- `nvs-act-type` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(r_, x_) in vec(m)))
- `nvs-act-unital` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(one(scal(m)), x_) = x_))
- `nvs-act-vneg` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (vneg(m))((act(m))(r_, x_)) = (act(m))(-1 * r_, x_)))
- `nvs-act-zero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(0, x_)) = y_))
- `nvs-dist-shift` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (dist(nvs-metric-space(m)))(u_, (vadd(m))(u_, w_)) = (vnrm(m))(w_)))
- `nvs-metric-distance` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m)], (dist(nvs-metric-space(m)))(u_, v_) = (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(v_)))))
- `nvs-metric-distance-q` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m)], (dist(nvs-metric-space(m)))(u_, v_) == (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(v_)))))
- `nvs-metric-is-ms` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-metric-space(nvs-metric-space(m)))
- `nvs-module-view-act` — forall([m_], act(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == act(m_))
- `nvs-module-view-scal` — forall([m_], scal(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == scal(m_))
- `nvs-module-view-vadd` — forall([m_], vadd(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == vadd(m_))
- `nvs-module-view-vec` — forall([m_], vec(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == vec(m_))
- `nvs-module-view-vneg` — forall([m_], vneg(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == vneg(m_))
- `nvs-module-view-vzero` — forall([m_], vzero(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_)) == vzero(m_))
- `nvs-ms-pts` — forall([m], pts(nvs-metric-space(m)) == vec(m))
- `nvs-neg-one-act` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(-1, x_) = (vneg(m))(x_)))
- `nvs-neg-one-act-sub` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(0 - 1, u_) = (vneg(m))(u_)))
- `nvs-norm-one-term-bound` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([v1 in vec(m), v2 in vec(m), c1 in rr, bd in rr], 0 <= bd implies abs(c1) <= bd implies (vnrm(m))((act(m))(c1, v1)) <= bd * (1 + ((vnrm(m))(v2) + (vnrm(m))(v1)))))
- `nvs-norm-two-term-bound` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([v1 in vec(m), v2 in vec(m), c1 in rr, c2 in rr, bd in rr], 0 <= bd implies abs(c1) <= bd implies abs(c2) <= bd implies (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))((act(m))(c1, v1), (act(m))(c2, v2))) <= bd * (1 + ((vnrm(m))(v2) + (vnrm(m))(v1)))))
- `nvs-one-act` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(1, x_) = x_))
- `nvs-scal-add` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies add(scal(m)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_))
- `nvs-scal-carr` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies carr(scal(m)) == rr)
- `nvs-scal-mul` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies mul(scal(m)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_))
- `nvs-scal-neg` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies neg(scal(m)) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_))
- `nvs-scal-one` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies one(scal(m)) == 1)
- `nvs-scal-zero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies zero(scal(m)) == 0)
- `nvs-taylor-remainder-bound` — forall([dm, cm, f, a, h, n], is-normed-vector-space(dm) implies is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(dm)) implies is-normed-vector-space(cm) implies is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(cm)) implies f in fun(vec(dm), vec(cm)) implies a in vec(dm) implies h in vec(dm) implies n in nn implies taylor-differentiable-v(cm, vnb-lambda(t, rr, f((vadd(dm))(a, (act(dm))(t, h)))), 0, 1, n) implies forsome([theta], 0 < theta and theta < 1 and factorial(succ(n)) * (vnrm(cm))((vadd(cm))(f((vadd(dm))(a, h)), (vneg(cm))(taylor-poly-v(cm, vnb-lambda(t, rr, f((vadd(dm))(a, (act(dm))(t, h)))), 0, 1, n)))) <= (vnrm(cm))((nth-deriv-v(cm, vnb-lambda(t, rr, f((vadd(dm))(a, (act(dm))(t, h)))), succ(n)))(theta))))
- `nvs-vadd-assoc` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(u_, v_), w_) = (vadd(m))(u_, (vadd(m))(v_, w_))))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, z_) = (vadd(m))(x_, w_) implies z_ = w_))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel-right` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(z_, x_) = (vadd(m))(w_, x_) implies z_ = w_))
- `nvs-vadd-cancel-zero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), z_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, z_) = x_ implies z_ = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-vadd-comm` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), w_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(u_, w_) = (vadd(m))(w_, u_)))
- `nvs-vadd-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(x_, y_) in vec(m)))
- `nvs-vadd-shuffle` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([a_ in vec(m), b_ in vec(m), c_ in vec(m), d_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(a_, c_), (vadd(m))(b_, d_)) = (vadd(m))((vadd(m))(a_, b_), (vadd(m))(c_, d_))))
- `nvs-vneg-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (vneg(m))(x_) in vec(m)))
- `nvs-vneg-left` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))((vneg(m))(u_), u_) = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-vneg-right` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(u_)) = vzero(m)))
- `nvs-vneg-vadd` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vneg(m))((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) = (vadd(m))((vneg(m))(x_), (vneg(m))(y_))))
- `nvs-vnrm-homog` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in rr, x_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((act(m))(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * (vnrm(m))(x_)))
- `nvs-vnrm-homog-c` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([r_ in carr(scal(m)), x_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((act(m))(r_, x_)) = abs(r_) * (vnrm(m))(x_)))
- `nvs-vnrm-triangle` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m), y_ in vec(m)], (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(x_, y_)) <= (vnrm(m))(x_) + (vnrm(m))(y_)))
- `nvs-vnrm-vzero` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies (vnrm(m))(vzero(m)) = 0)
- `nvs-vzero-in-vec` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies vzero(m) in vec(m))
- `nvs-vzero-left` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(vzero(m), u_) = u_))
- `nvs-vzero-right` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], (vadd(m))(u_, vzero(m)) = u_))
- `nvs-zero-act` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([x_ in vec(m)], (act(m))(0, x_) = vzero(m)))
- `ocint-elt-in-rr` — forall([a, b, osy_ in ocint(a, b)], osy_ in rr)
- `ocint-membership` — forall([a, b, osy_], osy_ in ocint(a, b) iff osy_ in rr and a < osy_ and osy_ <= b)
- `ocint-of-ooint` — forall([osx_, osr_], osx_ in rr implies pos-rr(osr_) implies forall([osy_ in ooint(osx_ - osr_, osx_ + osr_)], osy_ <= osx_ implies osy_ in ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_)))
- `ocint-right-endpoint` — forall([osx_, osr_], osx_ in rr implies pos-rr(osr_) implies osx_ in ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_))
- `ocint-right-limit-point` — forall([osx_, osd_], osx_ in rr implies pos-rr(osd_) implies forall([osr_], pos-rr(osr_) implies forsome([osb_ in ocint(osx_ - osd_, osx_)], not(osb_ = osx_) and abs(osx_ - osb_) <= osr_)))
- `ocint-shrink` — forall([osx_, osr_, osv_], osx_ in rr implies pos-rr(osr_) implies pos-rr(osv_) implies osv_ <= osr_ implies ocint(osx_ - osv_, osx_) subset ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_))
- `ocint-subset-ooint` — forall([osx_, osr_], osx_ in rr implies pos-rr(osr_) implies ocint(osx_ - osr_, osx_) subset ooint(osx_ - osr_, osx_ + osr_))
- `ocint-subset-rr` — forall([a, b], ocint(a, b) subset rr)
- `one-in-interval` — forall([n in nn], 1 in interval(1, succ(n)))
- `one-in-interval-1` — 1 in interval(1, 1)
- `ooint-center` — forall([icy_, ice_], icy_ in rr implies pos-rr(ice_) implies icy_ in ooint(icy_ - ice_, icy_ + ice_))
- `ooint-center-nested` — forall([x in rr, lfv_, lfr_ in rr], pos-rr(lfv_) implies lfv_ <= lfr_ implies ooint(x - lfv_, x + lfv_) subset ooint(x - lfr_, x + lfr_))
- `ooint-elt-in-rr` — forall([a, b, icy_ in ooint(a, b)], icy_ in rr)
- `ooint-inner-radius` — forall([pbu_ in rr, pbv_ in rr, pbt_ in ooint(pbu_, pbv_)], forsome([pbr_], pos-rr(pbr_) and ooint(pbt_ - pbr_, pbt_ + pbr_) subset ccint(pbu_, pbv_)))
- `ooint-interior-radius` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, itx_ in ooint(a, b)], forsome([itr_], pos-rr(itr_) and ooint(itx_ - itr_, itx_ + itr_) subset ooint(a, b)))
- `ooint-membership` — forall([a, b, icy_], icy_ in ooint(a, b) iff icy_ in rr and a < icy_ and icy_ < b)
- `ooint-open` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies is-open(rr-ms, ooint(a, b)))
- `ooint-open-nf` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies is-open(nf-metric-space(rr-normed-field), ooint(a, b)))
- `ooint-reflect-in` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pay_ in ooint(a, b)], a + b - pay_ in ooint(a, b))
- `ooint-subset-ccint` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies ooint(a, b) subset ccint(a, b))
- `ooint-subset-rr` — forall([a, b], ooint(a, b) subset rr)
- `open-cover-covers-point` — forall([s, f], is-open-cover(s, f) implies forall([p in pts(s)], forsome([u in f], p in u)))
- `open-in-power` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([ou_], is-open(s, ou_) implies ou_ in power(pts(s))))
- `open-preimage-implies-continuous` — forall([s, t, f], is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) implies forall([v], is-open(t, v) implies is-open(s, preimage(s, f, v))) implies is-continuous(s, t, f))
- `opposite-is-road` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pgam, dgam, prgb_, prdb_], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies prgb_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies prdb_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prgb_(pay_) == pgam(a + b - pay_)) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prdb_(pay_) == -1 * dgam(a + b - pay_)) implies is-road(prgb_, prdb_, a, b))
- `opr-rearrange-laws` — forall([s], iden(s) in carr(s) implies forall([a_ in carr(s), b_ in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a_, b_) in carr(s)) implies forall([a_ in carr(s), b_ in carr(s), c_ in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a_, b_), c_) = (opr(s))(a_, (opr(s))(b_, c_))) implies forall([a_ in carr(s), b_ in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a_, b_) = (opr(s))(b_, a_)) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, c), b)))
- `ord-lt-succ-cases` — forall([a_, b_], a_ in ord implies b_ in ord implies ord-lt(b_, succ_ord(a_)) implies ord-lt(b_, a_) or b_ = a_)
- `ord-lt-succ-iff-le` — forall([al in ord, cz in ord], ord-lt(cz, succ_ord(al)) iff ord-le(cz, al))
- `ord-no-injection-into-set` — forall([grd, phi], grd in set implies forall([alpha in ord], phi(alpha) in grd) implies forall([a1_ in ord, a2_ in ord], phi(a1_) = phi(a2_) implies a1_ = a2_) implies falsity)
- `ord-segment-insert` — forall([n in nn], ord-segment(succ(n)) = union(ord-segment(n), pair(n, n)))
- `ord-segment-nn-subset` — forall([m in nn, k in ord-segment(m)], k in nn)
- `ord-segment-nn-succ` — forall([n, k], n in nn implies k in ord-segment(succ(n)) iff k in ord-segment(n) or k = n)
- `ord-segment-pigeonhole-guarded` — forall([al in nn, be in nn], ord-lt(be, al) implies forall([f_], not(f_ in injection(ord-segment(al), ord-segment(be)))))
- `ord-segment-self` — forall([n in nn], not(n in ord-segment(n)))
- `ord-segment-trans` — forall([m in nn, k, i], i in ord-segment(k) and k in ord-segment(m) implies i in ord-segment(m))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `ord-segment-zero-no-members` — forall([k], not(k in ord-segment(0)))
- `ord-succ-lt-limit` — forall([lam, b_], limit-ord(lam) implies b_ in ord implies ord-lt(b_, lam) implies ord-lt(succ_ord(b_), lam))
- `ord-well-ordered` — forall([cl], forall([x in cl], x in ord) and forsome([w], w in cl) implies forsome([m in cl], forall([k in cl], ord-le(m, k))))
- `pair-in-cartesian` — forall([x, y, u, v], u in x implies v in y implies [u, v] in cartesian(x, y))
- `pair-tuple-is-set` — forall([x, y, u, v], u in x implies v in y implies [u, v] in set)
- `partition-locates-point` — forall([n in nn, p, a, b, x], is-partition(p, n, a, b) implies x in ccint(a, b) implies forsome([i_ in interval(0, n)], i_ < n and p(i_) <= x and x <= p(succ(i_))))
- `path-trace-compact` — forall([pgam, av_, bv_], is-path(pgam, av_, bv_) implies is-compact(subspace-ms(cc-ms, trace(pgam, av_, bv_))))
- `permutations-zero` — card(permutations(0)) = succ(0)
- `pi-pos` — pi in rr and 0 < pi
- `pi-spec` — pi in rr and 0 < pi and forall([cekz_ in cc], cc-exp(cekz_) = 1 iff forsome([cekk_ in zz], cekz_ = cekk_ * ((2 * pi) * 1i)))
- `pigeonhole-infinite` — forall([s], s in set and not(card(s) in nn) implies forall([f], f in set and card(f) in nn implies forall([pmap_ in fun(s, f)], forsome([c in f], not(card({x in s: pmap_(x) = c}) in nn)))))
- `pigeonhole-segments` — forall([n_ in nn, f_], not(f_ in injection(ord-segment(succ(n_)), ord-segment(n_))))
- `pigeonhole-segments-gen` — forall([m_, n_], m_ in nn implies n_ in nn implies n_ < m_ implies forall([f_], not(f_ in injection(ord-segment(m_), ord-segment(n_)))))
- `pigeonhole-step` — forall([n_ in nn], forall([f_], not(f_ in injection(ord-segment(succ(n_)), ord-segment(n_)))) implies forall([g_], not(g_ in injection(ord-segment(succ(succ(n_))), ord-segment(succ(n_))))))
- `pivot-clears-col` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, j], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(1 = j) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and entry(q, 1, j) = zero(a) and forall([ii, c], ii in interval(1, m) implies c in interval(1, n) implies not(c = j) implies entry(q, ii, c) = entry(p, ii, c)))))
- `pivot-clears-row` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, i], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies i in interval(1, m) implies not(i = 1) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forall([c], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, c) implies forall([ii, jj], ii in interval(1, m) implies jj in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(c, ii, jj) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(c, ii, jj)))) implies forsome([q], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, q) and entry(q, 1, 1) = entry(p, 1, 1) and entry(q, i, 1) = zero(a) and forall([rw, c], rw in interval(1, m) implies c in interval(1, n) implies not(rw = i) implies entry(q, rw, c) = entry(p, rw, c)))))
- `pivot-col-reduce` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, j], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(1 = j) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forsome([q in carr(a), r in carr(a)], entry(matmul(a, p, elem-g(a, n, (neg(a))(q), 1, j)), 1, j) = r and (r = zero(a) or succ((gauge(a))(r)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1))))))
- `pivot-row-reduce` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p, i], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies i in interval(1, m) implies not(i = 1) implies not(entry(p, 1, 1) = zero(a)) implies forsome([q in carr(a), r in carr(a)], entry(matmul(a, elem-g(a, m, (neg(a))(q), i, 1), p), i, 1) = r and (r = zero(a) or succ((gauge(a))(r)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(p, 1, 1))))))
- `place-min-pivot` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, p in mat(m, n, carr(a))], 1 in interval(1, m) implies 1 in interval(1, n) implies forsome([i0, j0], i0 in interval(1, m) and j0 in interval(1, n) and not(entry(p, i0, j0) = zero(a))) implies forsome([b], mat-equiv(a, m, n, p, b) and not(entry(b, 1, 1) = zero(a)) and forall([i, j], i in interval(1, m) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(entry(p, i, j) = zero(a)) implies (gauge(a))(entry(b, 1, 1)) <= (gauge(a))(entry(p, i, j))))))
- `po-reflexive` — forall([grd, porel, x_], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies x_ in grd implies [x_, x_] in porel)
- `po-transitive` — forall([grd, porel, x_, y_, z_], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies x_ in grd implies y_ in grd implies z_ in grd implies [x_, y_] in porel implies [y_, z_] in porel implies [x_, z_] in porel)
- `poly-antiderivative` — forall([n in nn, cf in fun(nn, rr), pt in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, (recip(succ(k)) * cf(k)) * x ^ succ(k)), succ(n))), pt, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * pt ^ k), succ(n))))
- `poly-carrier` — forall([a_], carr(poly(a_)) == finsupp(a_, nn-add-monoid))
- `poly-degree-zero-value` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr), tcv_ in rr], series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * tcv_ ^ k), succ(0)) = cf(0))
- `poly-deriv-coef-exists` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr)], forsome([tcd_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([tcj_ in nn], tcd_(tcj_) = succ(tcj_) * cf(succ(tcj_)))))
- `poly-deriv-fn` — forall([n in nn, cf in fun(nn, rr), tcd_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([tcj_ in nn], tcd_(tcj_) = succ(tcj_) * cf(succ(tcj_))) implies vnb-lambda(tcx_, rr, deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k), succ(n))), tcx_)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, tcd_(k) * x ^ k), n)))
- `poly-deriv-fn-zero` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr)], vnb-lambda(tcx_, rr, deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k), 0)), tcx_)) == vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k), 0)))
- `poly-dseq-lam-in-fun` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr), pt in rr], vnb-lambda(j, nn, (succ(j) * cf(succ(j))) * pt ^ j) in fun(nn, rr))
- `poly-fn-antiderivable` — forall([n_ in nn, cf, ph_, a, b], cf in fun(nn, rr) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies ph_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([x_ in rr], ph_(x_) == series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, cf(k_) * x_ ^ k_), n_)) implies is-antiderivable(ph_, a, b))
- `poly-is-antiderivable` — forall([n in nn, cf in fun(nn, rr), a, b], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x_, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, cf(k_) * x_ ^ k_), succ(n))), a, b))
- `poly-is-ring` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies is-ring(poly(a)))
- `poly-lam-in-fun` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr), n in nn], vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k), n)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `poly-membership` — forall([a_, f_], f_ in carr(poly(a_)) iff f_ in sqn(carr(a_)) and card(supp(a_, nn-add-monoid, f_)) in nn)
- `poly-nth-deriv-at-zero` — forall([tck_ in nn, n in nn, cf in fun(nn, rr)], tck_ <= n implies (nth-deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k), succ(n))), tck_))(0) = factorial(tck_) * cf(tck_))
- `poly-nth-deriv-in-fun` — forall([tck_ in nn, n in nn, cf in fun(nn, rr)], nth-deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k), n)), tck_) in fun(rr, rr))
- `poly-step-identity` — forall([c, u, v], c in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies (c * u) * v = u * (c * v))
- `poly-tail-zero` — forall([a_, f_], f_ in carr(poly(a_)) iff f_ in sqn(carr(a_)) and forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies f_(k_) = zero(a_))))
- `poly-tail-zero-bwd` — forall([a_, f_ in sqn(carr(a_))], forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies f_(k_) = zero(a_))) implies f_ in carr(poly(a_)))
- `poly-tail-zero-fwd` — forall([a_, f_ in carr(poly(a_))], forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies f_(k_) = zero(a_))))
- `poly-term-lam-in-fun` — forall([cf in fun(nn, rr), x in rr], vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k) in fun(nn, rr))
- `poly-value-at-zero` — forall([n in nn, cf in fun(nn, rr)], series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * 0 ^ k), succ(n)) = cf(0))
- `poly-zero-apply` — forall([a_, k_], is-ring(a_) implies k_ in nn implies (zero(poly(a_)))(k_) = zero(a_))
- `poly-zero-fun` — forall([a_], is-ring(a_) implies zero(poly(a_)) in fun(nn, carr(a_)))
- `poly-zero-iff` — forall([a_, p_], is-ring(a_) implies p_ in carr(poly(a_)) implies p_ = zero(poly(a_)) iff forall([k_ in nn], p_(k_) = zero(a_)))
- `poly-zero-unfold` — forall([a_], zero(poly(a_)) == monalg-zero(a_, nn-add-monoid))
- `pos-inf-in-rr-pos-star` — pos-inf in rr-pos-star
- `pos-inf-in-rr-star` — pos-inf in rr-star
- `pos-inf-neq-neg-inf` — not(pos-inf = neg-inf)
- `pos-inf-not-in-rr` — not(pos-inf in rr)
- `post-compose` — forall([a, b, c, u, s, s2, f, h, k], a in set implies b in set implies f in fun(a, b) implies h in fun(b, c) implies k in fun(c, u) implies f in s implies compose(h, f) in s2 implies (vnb-lambda(hmpf_, s, compose(compose(k, h), hmpf_)))(f) = (vnb-lambda(hmpf_, s2, compose(k, hmpf_)))((vnb-lambda(hmpf_, s, compose(h, hmpf_)))(f)))
- `post-id` — forall([a, b, s, f], a in set implies b in set implies f in s implies f in fun(a, b) implies (vnb-lambda(hmpf_, s, compose(id-fun(b), hmpf_)))(f) = f)
- `pow-lam-in-fun` — forall([n in nn], vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ n) in fun(rr, rr))
- `power-antiderivable` — forall([m_, a, b], m_ in nn implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, x ^ m_), a, b))
- `power-big-union-closed` — forall([a_, fam_], a_ in set implies fam_ subset power(a_) implies big-union(u_, fam_, u_) in power(a_))
- `power-closed-at` — forall([n in nn, x in rr], x ^ n in rr)
- `power-complement-closed` — forall([a_, b_], a_ in set implies complement-in(a_, b_) in power(a_))
- `power-empty-in` — forall([a_], empty-set in power(a_))
- `power-fun-union-closed` — forall([a_, f_ in fun(nn, power(a_))], big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_)) in power(a_))
- `power-insert-cover` — forall([x in set, k], k in set and not(k in x) implies power(union(x, pair(k, k))) = union(power(x), image(vnb-lambda(s, power(x), union(s, pair(k, k))), power(x))))
- `power-insert-disjoint` — forall([x in set, k], k in set and not(k in x) implies intersection(power(x), image(vnb-lambda(s, power(x), union(s, pair(k, k))), power(x))) = empty-set)
- `power-insert-injective` — forall([x in set, k_ in set], not(k_ in x) implies vnb-lambda(s, power(x), union(s, pair(k_, k_))) in injection(power(x), power(union(x, pair(k_, k_)))))
- `power-inter-closed` — forall([a_, u_, v_], u_ in power(a_) implies v_ in power(a_) implies intersection(u_, v_) in power(a_))
- `power-mem-in` — forall([a_, b_, z_], b_ in power(a_) implies z_ in b_ implies z_ in a_)
- `power-mem-intro` — forall([a_, b_ in set], forall([z_ in b_], z_ in a_) implies b_ in power(a_))
- `power-mem-sethood` — forall([a_, b_ in power(a_)], b_ in set)
- `power-of-empty` — power(empty-set) = pair(empty-set, empty-set)
- `power-real-closed` — forall([x in rr, n in nn], x ^ n in rr)
- `power-small-threshold` — forall([r in rr], 0 <= r and r < 1 implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k in nn], cap <= k implies r ^ k <= eps))))
- `power-sub-succ-continuous` — forall([b in rr, itk_ in nn, ity_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, (b - z) ^ succ(itk_)), ity_))
- `power-sub-succ-has-deriv` — forall([b in rr, itk_ in nn, itx_ in rr], has-deriv-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, (b - z) ^ succ(itk_)), itx_, (succ(itk_) * (b - itx_) ^ itk_) * -1))
- `power-tends-to-zero` — forall([r], r in rr and abs(r) < 1 implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(n, nn, r ^ n), 0))
- `power-two-pos` — forall([k in nn], 0 < 2 ^ k)
- `power-whole-in` — forall([a_ in set], a_ in power(a_))
- `power-zero-base` — forall([n in nn], 0 ^ succ(n) = 0)
- `pre-compose` — forall([a, b, c, u, s, s2, f, h, k], a in set implies b in set implies k in fun(a, b) implies h in fun(b, c) implies f in fun(c, u) implies f in s implies compose(f, h) in s2 implies (vnb-lambda(hmpf_, s, compose(hmpf_, compose(h, k))))(f) = (vnb-lambda(hmpf_, s2, compose(hmpf_, k)))((vnb-lambda(hmpf_, s, compose(hmpf_, h)))(f)))
- `pre-id` — forall([a, b, s, f], a in set implies f in s implies f in fun(a, b) implies (vnb-lambda(hmpf_, s, compose(hmpf_, id-fun(a))))(f) = f)
- `pred-in-interval` — forall([p in nn, i in interval(1, succ(p))], not(i = 1) implies nn-minus(i, 1) in interval(1, p))
- `pred-in-nn` — forall([n_ in nn], not(n_ = 0) implies pred(n_) in nn)
- `pred-of-succ` — forall([m_ in nn], pred(succ(m_)) = m_)
- `pred-property` — forall([n_ in nn], not(n_ = 0) implies pred(n_) in nn and succ(pred(n_)) = n_)
- `pred-succ` — forall([n_ in nn], not(n_ = 0) implies succ(pred(n_)) = n_)
- `preimage-complement` — forall([s, t, f, u], is-metric-space(s) and is-metric-space(t) and f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) implies preimage(s, f, complement-in(pts(t), u)) = complement-in(pts(s), preimage(s, f, u)))
- `preimage-in-power` — forall([s], pts(s) in set implies forall([fqf_, vqu_], preimage(s, fqf_, vqu_) in power(pts(s))))
- `preimage-subspace-open` — forall([s, t, fqf_], is-continuous(s, t, fqf_) implies forall([bqb_], bqb_ subset pts(t) implies forall([zqp_ in pts(s)], fqf_(zqp_) in bqb_) implies forall([wqu_], is-open(subspace-ms(t, bqb_), wqu_) implies is-open(s, preimage(s, fqf_, wqu_)))))
- `primitive-continuous` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies is-continuous-on(pwf_, ccint(a, b)))
- `primitive-endpoints` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b)
- `primitive-exceptional-set` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies forsome([rqs_], is-countable(rqs_) and rqs_ subset ccint(a, b) and forall([rqt_ in ooint(a, b)], not(rqt_ in rqs_) implies has-deriv-at(pwf_, rqt_, pphi_(rqt_)))))
- `primitive-family-deriv-at` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([gfam in fun(nn, fun(ccint(a, b), rr)), ffam in fun(nn, fun(ccint(a, b), rr)), pufc_ in fun(rr, rr), put_ in ooint(a, b), pulv_ in rr], forall([puk_ in nn], is-primitive(gfam(puk_), ffam(puk_), a, b)) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([pun_ in nn], forall([puk_ in nn, pul_ in nn], pun_ <= puk_ implies pun_ <= pul_ implies forall([pux_ in ccint(a, b)], abs((ffam(puk_))(pux_) - (ffam(pul_))(pux_)) <= eps)))) implies forall([pux_ in ccint(a, b)], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(puk_, nn, (gfam(puk_))(pux_)), pufc_(pux_))) implies forall([puk_ in nn], has-deriv-at(gfam(puk_), put_, (ffam(puk_))(put_))) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(puk_, nn, (ffam(puk_))(put_)), pulv_) implies is-diff-at(pufc_, put_, pulv_)))
- `primitive-family-limit` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([gfam in fun(nn, fun(ccint(a, b), rr)), ffam in fun(nn, fun(ccint(a, b), rr))], forall([puk_ in nn], is-primitive(gfam(puk_), ffam(puk_), a, b)) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([pun_ in nn], forall([puk_ in nn, pul_ in nn], pun_ <= puk_ implies pun_ <= pul_ implies forall([pux_ in ccint(a, b)], abs((ffam(puk_))(pux_) - (ffam(pul_))(pux_)) <= eps)))) implies forall([puk_ in nn], (gfam(puk_))(a) = 0) implies forsome([pufc_ in fun(rr, rr)], forall([pux_ in ccint(a, b)], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(puk_, nn, (gfam(puk_))(pux_)), pufc_(pux_))) and forall([put_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, pufc_, put_)))))
- `primitive-family-normalised-choice` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, rhpff_], forall([rhpk_ in nn], forsome([rhpg_], is-primitive(rhpg_, rhpff_(rhpk_), a, b))) implies forsome([rhpgf_ in fun(nn, fun(ccint(a, b), rr))], forall([rhpk_ in nn], is-primitive(rhpgf_(rhpk_), rhpff_(rhpk_), a, b) and (rhpgf_(rhpk_))(a) = 0)))
- `primitive-glue` — forall([a in rr, c in rr, b in rr], a < c implies c < b implies forall([g1, f1, g2, f2], is-primitive(g1, f1, a, c) implies is-primitive(g2, f2, c, b) implies g1(c) = g2(c) implies forall([g in fun(ccint(a, b), rr), f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], forall([t in ccint(a, c)], g(t) == g1(t)) implies forall([t in ccint(c, b)], g(t) == g2(t)) implies forall([t in ooint(a, c)], f(t) == f1(t)) implies forall([t in ooint(c, b)], f(t) == f2(t)) implies is-primitive(g, f, a, b))))
- `primitive-in-fun` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies pwf_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr))
- `primitive-integrand-in-fun` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr))
- `primitive-integrand-interior-congruence` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, ppsi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies ppsi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([pay_ in ooint(a, b)], pphi_(pay_) == ppsi_(pay_)) implies is-primitive(pwf_, ppsi_, a, b))
- `primitive-of-constant-piece` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u < v implies forall([f in fun(rr, rr), cv in rr, k in rr], forall([t_], t_ in rr and u < t_ and t_ < v implies f(t_) = cv) implies is-primitive(vnb-lambda(pgs_, ccint(u, v), k + cv * (pgs_ - u)), restrict(f, ccint(u, v)), u, v)))
- `primitive-pair-lipschitz` — forall([a, b, pug1_, puf1_, pug2_, puf2_, pum_ in rr], is-primitive(pug1_, puf1_, a, b) implies is-primitive(pug2_, puf2_, a, b) implies forall([pux_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(puf1_(pux_) - puf2_(pux_)) <= pum_) implies forall([pux_ in ccint(a, b), puy_ in ccint(a, b)], pux_ < puy_ implies abs(pug1_(puy_) - pug2_(puy_) - (pug1_(pux_) - pug2_(pux_))) <= pum_ * (puy_ - pux_)))
- `primitive-pair-lipschitz-abs` — forall([a, b, pug1_, puf1_, pug2_, puf2_, pum_ in rr], is-primitive(pug1_, puf1_, a, b) implies is-primitive(pug2_, puf2_, a, b) implies forall([pux_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(puf1_(pux_) - puf2_(pux_)) <= pum_) implies forall([pux_ in ccint(a, b), puy_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(pug1_(puy_) - pug2_(puy_) - (pug1_(pux_) - pug2_(pux_))) <= pum_ * abs(puy_ - pux_)))
- `primitive-shift-const` — forall([rhpg_, rhpf_, a, b, rhpc_ in rr], is-primitive(rhpg_, rhpf_, a, b) implies is-primitive(vnb-lambda(rhpy_, ccint(a, b), rhpg_(rhpy_) - rhpc_), rhpf_, a, b))
- `primitive-uniform-limit` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([gfam in fun(nn, fun(ccint(a, b), rr)), ffam in fun(nn, fun(ccint(a, b), rr)), f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], forall([puk_ in nn], is-primitive(gfam(puk_), ffam(puk_), a, b)) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([pun_ in nn], forall([puk_ in nn], pun_ <= puk_ implies forall([pux_ in ccint(a, b)], abs((ffam(puk_))(pux_) - f(pux_)) <= eps)))) implies forall([puk_ in nn], (gfam(puk_))(a) = 0) implies forsome([pug_], is-primitive(pug_, f, a, b) and forall([pux_ in ccint(a, b)], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(puk_, nn, (gfam(puk_))(pux_)), pug_(pux_))))))
- `principal-ideal-in-ideal` — forall([s, i, b], is-ideal(s, i) implies b in i implies forall([x in principal-ideal(s, b)], x in i))
- `prod-lam-in-fun` — forall([g, h], g in fun(rr, rr) implies h in fun(rr, rr) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) * h(x)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `prod-of-sums-expansion` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies forall([a in fun(x, carr(r)), b in fun(x, carr(r))], prod-ring(r, vnb-lambda(k, x, (add(r))(a(k), b(k))), x) = finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(s, power(x), (mul(r))(prod-ring(r, a, s), prod-ring(r, b, difference(x, s)))), power(x)))))
- `prod-of-sums-ptwise` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([w in set], card(w) in nn implies forall([a_], forall([z_ in w], a_(z_) in carr(r)) implies forall([b_], forall([z_ in w], b_(z_) in carr(r)) implies prod-ring(r, vnb-lambda(kv_, w, (add(r))(a_(kv_), b_(kv_))), w) = finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(sv_, power(w), (mul(r))(prod-ring(r, a_, sv_), prod-ring(r, b_, difference(w, sv_)))), power(w))))))
- `prod-of-sums-summand-type` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([w in set], card(w) in nn implies forall([a_], forall([z_ in w], a_(z_) in carr(r)) implies forall([b_], forall([z_ in w], b_(z_) in carr(r)) implies forall([s_ in power(w)], (mul(r))(prod-ring(r, a_, s_), prod-ring(r, b_, difference(w, s_))) in carr(r))))))
- `prod-ring-congruence-q` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in set], card(x) in nn implies forall([f, g], forall([z_ in x], f(z_) = g(z_)) implies prod-ring(r, f, x) == prod-ring(r, g, x))))
- `prod-ring-empty` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([f], prod-ring(r, f, empty-set) = one(r)))
- `prod-ring-insert` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies forall([k], k in set and not(k in x) implies forall([f in fun(union(x, pair(k, k)), carr(r))], prod-ring(r, f, union(x, pair(k, k))) = (mul(r))(prod-ring(r, f, x), f(k))))))
- `prod-ring-insert-ptwise` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in set], card(x) in nn implies forall([k_ in set], not(k_ in x) implies forall([f], forall([z_ in union(x, pair(k_, k_))], f(z_) in carr(r)) implies prod-ring(r, f, union(x, pair(k_, k_))) = (mul(r))(prod-ring(r, f, x), f(k_))))))
- `prod-ring-singleton` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in set, f in fun(pair(x, x), carr(r))], prod-ring(r, f, pair(x, x)) = f(x)))
- `prod-ring-type` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x], x in set and card(x) in nn implies forall([f in fun(x, carr(r))], prod-ring(r, f, x) in carr(r))))
- `prod-ring-type-ptwise` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in set], card(x) in nn implies forall([f], forall([z_ in x], f(z_) in carr(r)) implies prod-ring(r, f, x) in carr(r))))
- `prod-ring-type-subset` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([w in set], card(w) in nn implies forall([f], forall([z_ in w], f(z_) in carr(r)) implies forall([s_ in power(w)], prod-ring(r, f, s_) in carr(r)))))
- `prod-set-disjoint-union-defined` — forall([cm], is-comm-monoid(cm) implies forall([s1, s2, f], s1 in set and s2 in set and intersection(s1, s2) = empty-set and f in fun(union(s1, s2), carr(cm)) and card(s1) in nn and card(s2) in nn implies prod-set(cm, union(s1, s2), f) = (opr(cm))(prod-set(cm, s1, f), prod-set(cm, s2, f))))
- `prod-set-empty` — forall([cm, f], prod-set(cm, empty-set, f) == iden(cm))
- `prod-set-singleton-defined` — forall([cm], is-comm-monoid(cm) implies forall([x in set, f], f(x) in carr(cm) implies prod-set(cm, pair(x, x), f) = f(x)))
- `prod-set-type-defined` — forall([cm, x, s, f], is-comm-monoid(cm) and x in set and s subset x and f in fun(x, carr(cm)) and card(s) in nn implies prod-set(cm, s, f) in carr(cm))
- `product-carrier-coord` — forall([ms, x_ in product-carrier(ms), n_ in nn], x_(n_) in pts(ms(n_)))
- `product-carrier-unfold` — forall([ms], product-carrier(ms) == {x in fun(nn, big-union(n, nn, pts(ms(n)))): forall([n in nn], x(n) in pts(ms(n)))})
- `product-continuous-at` — forall([g, h, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) * h(x)), a))
- `product-convergence-coordinatewise` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms)), lv in product-carrier(ms)], converges-to(product-metric-w(ms, w), seq, lv) iff forall([n_ in nn], converges-to(ms(n_), vnb-lambda(k, nn, (seq(k))(n_)), lv(n_))))))
- `product-convergence-coordinatewise-bwd` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms)), lv in product-carrier(ms)], forall([n_ in nn], converges-to(ms(n_), vnb-lambda(k, nn, (seq(k))(n_)), lv(n_))) implies converges-to(product-metric-w(ms, w), seq, lv))))
- `product-convergence-coordinatewise-fwd` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms)), lv in product-carrier(ms)], converges-to(product-metric-w(ms, w), seq, lv) implies forall([n_ in nn], converges-to(ms(n_), vnb-lambda(k, nn, (seq(k))(n_)), lv(n_))))))
- `product-coord-from-weighted-null` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms)), lv in product-carrier(ms), n_ in nn], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, w(n_) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n_))))((seq(j_))(n_), lv(n_))), 0) implies converges-to(ms(n_), vnb-lambda(k, nn, (seq(k))(n_)), lv(n_)))))
- `product-coord-weighted-null` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([seq in fun(nn, product-carrier(ms)), lv in product-carrier(ms), n_ in nn], converges-to(ms(n_), vnb-lambda(k, nn, (seq(k))(n_)), lv(n_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, w(n_) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n_))))((seq(j_))(n_), lv(n_))), 0))))
- `product-dist-in-rr` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms)], (dist(product-metric-w(ms, w)))(x, y) in rr)))
- `product-identity-continuous` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([w2], summable-weight(w2) implies is-continuous(product-metric-w(ms, w), product-metric-w(ms, w2), vnb-lambda(x, pts(product-metric-w(ms, w)), x)))))
- `product-is-metric-space` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies is-metric-space(product-metric-w(ms, w))))
- `product-metric-carrier` — forall([ms, w], pts(product-metric-w(ms, w)) == product-carrier(ms))
- `product-metric-default-summable` — summable-weight(vnb-lambda(n, nn, (/)(1, 2 ^ (n + 1))))
- `product-metric-dist-converges` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms)], series-converges-to(vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n))), (dist(product-metric-w(ms, w)))(x, y)))))
