- `product-metric-distance` — forall([ms, w, x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms)], (dist(product-metric-w(ms, w)))(x, y) == series-limit(vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n)))))
- `product-of-invertibles-is-invertible` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, u1, u2], is-invertible-mat(a, n, u1) implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, u2) implies is-invertible-mat(a, n, matmul(a, u1, u2))))
- `product-projection-continuous` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([n in nn], is-continuous(product-metric-w(ms, w), ms(n), product-proj(ms, n)))))
- `product-sum-seq-in-fun` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms), z in product-carrier(ms)], vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n)) + w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(y(n), z(n))) in fun(nn, rr))))
- `product-sum-seq-split` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms), z in product-carrier(ms), i_ in nn], (vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n)) + w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(y(n), z(n))))(i_) = (vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n))))(i_) + (vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(y(n), z(n))))(i_))))
- `product-term-nonneg` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms), n_ in nn], 0 <= (vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n))))(n_))))
- `product-term-self-zero` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), n_ in nn], (vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), x(n))))(n_) = 0)))
- `product-term-seq-in-fun` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms)], vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n))) in fun(nn, rr))))
- `product-term-sym` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms), n_ in nn], (vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n))))(n_) <= (vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(y(n), x(n))))(n_))))
- `product-term-triangle` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms), z in product-carrier(ms), n_ in nn], (vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), z(n))))(n_) <= (vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n)) + w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(y(n), z(n))))(n_))))
- `product-weighted-summable` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([x in product-carrier(ms), y in product-carrier(ms)], series-converges(vnb-lambda(n, nn, w(n) * (dist(bdd-metric(ms(n))))(x(n), y(n)))))))
- `product-weights-equivalent` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([w], summable-weight(w) implies forall([w2], summable-weight(w2) implies is-continuous(product-metric-w(ms, w), product-metric-w(ms, w2), vnb-lambda(x, pts(product-metric-w(ms, w)), x)) and is-continuous(product-metric-w(ms, w2), product-metric-w(ms, w), vnb-lambda(x, pts(product-metric-w(ms, w2)), x)))))
- `proj-in-fun` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies proj(s) in fun(pts(s), quotient(s)))
- `proj-unfold` — forall([s], proj(s) == vnb-lambda(a_, pts(s), class(s, a_)))
- `proper-subset-witness` — forall([a, b], a subset b implies not(a = b) implies forsome([x_ in b], not(x_ in a)))
- `ps-abs-term` — forall([coef in fun(nn, rr), x in rr, n in nn], abs(coef(n) * x ^ n) = abs(coef(n)) * abs(x) ^ n)
- `ps-absolute-implies-convergent` — forall([coef in fun(nn, rr), x], x in rr and ps-absolutely-converges-at(coef, x) implies ps-converges-at(coef, x))
- `ps-converges-as-series` — forall([coef in fun(nn, rr), x in rr], ps-converges-at(coef, x) iff series-converges(vnb-lambda(n, nn, coef(n) * x ^ n)))
- `ps-partial-sum-as-series` — forall([coef in fun(nn, rr), x in rr, k in nn], ps-partial-sum(coef, x, k) = series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(n, nn, coef(n) * x ^ n), k))
- `pseudometric-pos` — forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) implies forall([u in crr, v in crr], 0 <= dst(u, v)))
- `pseudometric-self-zero` — forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) implies forall([u in crr], dst(u, u) = 0))
- `pseudometric-sym` — forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) implies forall([u in crr, v in crr], dst(u, v) = dst(v, u)))
- `pseudometric-triangle` — forall([dst, crr], is-pseudometric(dst, crr) implies forall([u in crr, v in crr, w in crr], dst(u, w) <= dst(u, v) + dst(v, w)))
- `ptwise-cauchy-compact-equicont-unif` — forall([s, fam], is-compact(s) implies fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies is-equicontinuous(s, rr-ms, fam) implies is-ptwise-cauchy(s, fam) implies is-unif-cauchy(s, fam))
- `pw-affine-antiderivative` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([pac_ in rr, pam_ in rr, pwf_, pphi_], pwf_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pwf_(pay_) == pac_ + pam_ * pay_) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pphi_(pay_) == pam_) implies is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b)))
- `pw-antiderivative-differ-by-constant` — forall([pwf_, paw_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, pphi_, a, b) implies forall([pbs_ in ccint(a, b)], pwf_(pbs_) = paw_(pbs_) + pwf_(a) - paw_(a)))
- `pw-antiderivative-differ-by-some-constant` — forall([pwf_, paw_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, pphi_, a, b) implies forsome([pbc_ in rr], forall([pbs_ in ccint(a, b)], pwf_(pbs_) = paw_(pbs_) + pbc_)))
- `pw-antiderivative-endpoint-difference` — forall([pwf_, paw_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, pphi_, a, b) implies pwf_(b) - pwf_(a) = paw_(b) - paw_(a))
- `pw-antiderivative-integrand-congruence` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, ppsi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies ppsi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pphi_(pay_) == ppsi_(pay_)) implies is-primitive(pwf_, ppsi_, a, b))
- `pw-antiderivative-linear` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pcc_ in rr, pdd_ in rr, pwf_, paw_, pphi_, ppsi_], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, ppsi_, a, b) implies forall([pauh_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr), pchi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pauh_(pay_) == pcc_ * pwf_(pay_) + pdd_ * paw_(pay_)) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pchi_(pay_) == pcc_ * pphi_(pay_) + pdd_ * ppsi_(pay_)) implies is-primitive(pauh_, pchi_, a, b) and pw-int(pchi_, a, b) = pcc_ * pw-int(pphi_, a, b) + pdd_ * pw-int(ppsi_, a, b)))
- `pw-antiderivative-one-piece` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([pwf_, pphi_], pwf_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies is-continuous-on(pwf_, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([pat_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(pwf_, pat_, pphi_(pat_))) implies is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b)))
- `pw-antiderivative-real-mul` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pwf_, pphi_, pcc_ in rr], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies forall([pauh_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr), pchi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pauh_(pay_) == pcc_ * pwf_(pay_)) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pchi_(pay_) == pcc_ * pphi_(pay_)) implies is-primitive(pauh_, pchi_, a, b) and pw-int(pchi_, a, b) = pcc_ * pw-int(pphi_, a, b)))
- `pw-antiderivative-reflect` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pwf_, pphi_], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies forall([prg_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr), prk_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prg_(pay_) == pwf_(a + b - pay_)) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prk_(pay_) == -1 * pphi_(a + b - pay_)) implies is-primitive(prg_, prk_, a, b)))
- `pw-antiderivative-restrict` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pcv_ in rr, pdv_ in rr, pwf_, pphi_], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies a <= pcv_ implies pcv_ < pdv_ implies pdv_ <= b implies is-primitive(restrict(pwf_, ccint(pcv_, pdv_)), restrict(pphi_, ccint(pcv_, pdv_)), pcv_, pdv_))
- `pw-antiderivative-sum` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pwf_, paw_, pphi_, ppsi_], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, ppsi_, a, b) implies forall([pauh_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr), pchi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pauh_(pay_) == pwf_(pay_) + paw_(pay_)) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pchi_(pay_) == pphi_(pay_) + ppsi_(pay_)) implies is-primitive(pauh_, pchi_, a, b) and pw-int(pchi_, a, b) = pw-int(pphi_, a, b) + pw-int(ppsi_, a, b)))
- `pw-antiderivative-value-in-rr` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pwf_(pay_) in rr))
- `pw-continuous-endpoints` — forall([pphi_, a, b], is-pw-continuous-on(pphi_, a, b) implies a in rr and b in rr and a < b)
- `pw-continuous-in-fun` — forall([pphi_, a, b], is-pw-continuous-on(pphi_, a, b) implies pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr))
- `pw-continuous-one-piece` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(pphi_, ccint(a, b)) implies is-pw-continuous-on(pphi_, a, b)))
- `pw-continuous-reflect` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pphi_, prc_ in rr, prq_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-pw-continuous-on(pphi_, a, b) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prq_(pay_) == prc_ * pphi_(a + b - pay_)) implies is-pw-continuous-on(prq_, a, b))
- `pw-int-abs-bound` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, paw_, pabs_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, pabs_, a, b) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pabs_(pay_) == abs(pphi_(pay_))) implies abs(pw-int(pphi_, a, b)) <= pw-int(pabs_, a, b))
- `pw-int-adjacent` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pcv_ in ooint(a, b), pwf_, pphi_], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies pw-int(pphi_, a, b) = pw-int(restrict(pphi_, ccint(a, pcv_)), a, pcv_) + pw-int(restrict(pphi_, ccint(pcv_, b)), pcv_, b))
- `pw-int-affine-subst` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies lrpc_ < lrpd_ implies forall([pwf_, pphi_], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies forall([lrph_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), rr), lrps_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), rr)], forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrph_(lrpz_) == pwf_(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrps_(lrpz_) == lrpl_ * pphi_(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies is-primitive(lrph_, lrps_, lrpc_, lrpd_) and pw-int(lrps_, lrpc_, lrpd_) = pw-int(pphi_, a, b))))
- `pw-int-congruence` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, ppsi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies ppsi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pphi_(pay_) == ppsi_(pay_)) implies pw-int(pphi_, a, b) = pw-int(ppsi_, a, b))
- `pw-int-const` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([pac_ in rr, pphi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pphi_(pay_) == pac_) implies pw-int(pphi_, a, b) = pac_ * (b - a)))
- `pw-int-in-rr` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies pw-int(pphi_, a, b) in rr)
- `pw-int-interior-congruence` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, ppsi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies ppsi_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([pay_ in ooint(a, b)], pphi_(pay_) == ppsi_(pay_)) implies pw-int(pphi_, a, b) = pw-int(ppsi_, a, b))
- `pw-int-monotone` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, paw_, ppsi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies is-primitive(paw_, ppsi_, a, b) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pphi_(pay_) <= ppsi_(pay_)) implies pw-int(pphi_, a, b) <= pw-int(ppsi_, a, b))
- `pw-int-nonneg` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], 0 <= pphi_(pay_)) implies 0 <= pw-int(pphi_, a, b))
- `pw-int-reflect` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pwf_, pphi_], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies forall([prh_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr), prs_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prh_(pay_) == -1 * pwf_(a + b - pay_)) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prs_(pay_) == pphi_(a + b - pay_)) implies is-primitive(prh_, prs_, a, b) and pw-int(prs_, a, b) = pw-int(pphi_, a, b)))
- `pw-int-value` — forall([pwf_, pphi_, a, b], is-primitive(pwf_, pphi_, a, b) implies pw-int(pphi_, a, b) = pwf_(b) - pwf_(a))
- `pw-lt-ne` — forall([pcu_ in rr, pcv_ in rr], pcu_ < pcv_ implies not(pcu_ = pcv_) and not(pcv_ = pcu_))
- `pw-nondecreasing-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([h in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(h, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([pas_ in set], card(pas_) in nn implies forall([pat_ in ooint(a, b)], not(pat_ in pas_) implies forsome([pal_], has-deriv-at(h, pat_, pal_) and 0 <= pal_)) implies h(a) <= h(b))))
- `pw-not-in-insert` — forall([pcy_ in set, pcw_, pct_], not(pct_ in pcw_) implies not(pct_ = pcy_) implies not(pct_ in union(pcw_, pair(pcy_, pcy_))))
- `pw-restrict-continuous-on` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([f, pwf_], f in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([pay_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, pay_)) implies pwf_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], pwf_(pay_) == f(pay_)) implies is-continuous-on(pwf_, ccint(a, b))))
- `pw-step-identity` — forall([c, u, v], c in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies (c + 1) * (u * v) = 1 * (u * v) + u * (c * v))
- `pw-zero-deriv-off-finite-set` — forall([pas_ in set], card(pas_) in nn implies forall([pah_ in fun(rr, rr)], forall([pax_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, pah_, pax_)) implies forall([pau_ in rr, pav_ in rr], pau_ < pav_ implies forall([pat_ in ooint(pau_, pav_)], not(pat_ in pas_) implies is-diff-at(pah_, pat_, 0)) implies pah_(pau_) = pah_(pav_))))
- `pw-zero-deriv-total` — forall([pah_ in fun(rr, rr)], forall([pax_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, pah_, pax_)) implies forall([pau_ in rr, pav_ in rr], pau_ < pav_ implies forall([pat_ in ooint(pau_, pav_)], is-diff-at(pah_, pat_, 0)) implies pah_(pau_) = pah_(pav_)))
- `qq-add-cancel` — forall([a in qq, b in qq, c in qq], a + c = b + c implies a = b)
- `qq-field-is-field` — is-field(qq-field)
- `qq-field-mul-inv-type` — vnb-lambda(x_, difference(qq, singleton(0)), recip(x_)) in fun(difference(qq, singleton(0)), difference(qq, singleton(0)))
- `qq-is-ring` — is-ring(qq-ring)
- `qq-line-is-module` — is-module(qq-line)
- `qq-line-is-vector-space` — is-vector-space(qq-line)
- `qq-nonneg-is-fraction` — forall([q], q in qq and 0 <= q implies forsome([a in nn, b in nn], not(b = 0) and q * b = a))
- `qq-recip-nonzero` — forall([a_ in qq], not(a_ = 0) implies not(recip(a_) = 0))
- `quarter-step` — forall([gbj_ in rr, gba_ in rr, gbb_ in rr, gbc_ in rr, gbd_ in rr], 0 <= gba_ implies 0 <= gbb_ implies 0 <= gbc_ implies 0 <= gbd_ implies gbj_ <= ((gba_ + gbb_) + gbc_) + gbd_ implies gbj_ <= 4 * gba_ or gbj_ <= 4 * gbb_ or gbj_ <= 4 * gbc_ or gbj_ <= 4 * gbd_)
- `quotient-is-set` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies quotient(s) in set)
- `quotient-rep` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([x in quotient(s)], forsome([a in pts(s)], x = class(s, a))))
- `quotient-unfold` — forall([s], quotient(s) == image(proj(s), pts(s)))
- `quotient-universal` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([z, f], f in fun(pts(s), z) and respects(s, f) implies forsome([g in fun(quotient(s), z)], forall([a in pts(s)], g(class(s, a)) = f(a)) and forall([g_], g_ in fun(quotient(s), z) and forall([a in pts(s)], g_(class(s, a)) = f(a)) implies g_ = g))))
- `r-exp-add` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies r-exp(u_ + v_) = r-exp(u_) * r-exp(v_))
- `r-exp-char` — forall([y_, x_], y_ in rr implies x_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies log(x_) = y_ implies r-exp(y_) = x_)
- `r-exp-continuous-at` — forall([t_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(y_, rr, r-exp(y_)), t_))
- `r-exp-deriv` — forall([t_ in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(y_, rr, r-exp(y_)), t_, r-exp(t_)))
- `r-exp-diff-at-log` — forall([c_, w_], c_ in rr implies w_ in rr implies 0 < c_ implies w_ = log(c_) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(y_, rr, r-exp(y_)), w_, c_))
- `r-exp-in-rr` — forall([y_ in rr], r-exp(y_) in rr)
- `r-exp-lam-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(y_, rr, r-exp(y_)) in fun(rr, rr)
- `r-exp-log` — forall([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies r-exp(log(x_)) = x_)
- `r-exp-log-not-global` — not(forall([x_ in rr], r-exp(log(x_)) = x_))
- `r-exp-mono` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies u_ <= v_ implies r-exp(u_) <= r-exp(v_))
- `r-exp-neg` — forall([y_ in rr], r-exp(-y_) = recip-star(r-exp(y_)))
- `r-exp-pos` — forall([y_ in rr], 0 < r-exp(y_))
- `r-exp-prop` — forall([y_ in rr], r-exp(y_) in rr and 0 < r-exp(y_) and log(r-exp(y_)) = y_)
- `r-exp-strictly-increasing` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies u_ < v_ implies r-exp(u_) < r-exp(v_))
- `r-exp-zero` — r-exp(0) = 1
- `r12d-cross-est` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([bigf, rf, rad, mm], bigf in fun(nn, quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))) implies rf in fun(nn, cseq(m)) implies rad in fun(nn, rr) implies mm in fun(nn, nn) implies forall([p_ in nn], bigf(p_) = class(cauchy-setoid(m), rf(p_))) implies forall([p_ in nn, i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies mm(p_) <= i_ implies mm(p_) <= j_ implies (dist(m))((rf(p_))(i_), (rf(p_))(j_)) <= rad(p_)) implies forall([p_, q_, c], p_ in nn implies q_ in nn implies c in rr implies pos-rr(c) implies mm(p_) <= q_ implies (dist(m))((rf(q_))(mm(q_)), (rf(p_))(q_)) <= (rad(q_) + rad(p_)) + ((dist(completion(m)))(bigf(q_), bigf(p_)) + c))))
- `r12d-diag-at` — forall([rf, mm, p_ in nn], (vnb-lambda(pv_, nn, (rf(pv_))(mm(pv_))))(p_) == (rf(p_))(mm(p_)))
- `r12d-diag-cauchy` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([bigf, rf, rad, mm], bigf in fun(nn, quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))) implies rf in fun(nn, cseq(m)) implies null-rr-seq(rad) implies mm in fun(nn, nn) implies forall([p_ in nn], bigf(p_) = class(cauchy-setoid(m), rf(p_))) implies forall([p_ in nn, i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies mm(p_) <= i_ implies mm(p_) <= j_ implies (dist(m))((rf(p_))(i_), (rf(p_))(j_)) <= rad(p_)) implies is-cauchy-seq(completion(m), bigf) implies is-cauchy-seq(m, vnb-lambda(pv_, nn, (rf(pv_))(mm(pv_))))))
- `r12d-diag-est` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([bigf, rf, rad, mm], bigf in fun(nn, quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))) implies rf in fun(nn, cseq(m)) implies rad in fun(nn, rr) implies mm in fun(nn, nn) implies forall([p_ in nn], bigf(p_) = class(cauchy-setoid(m), rf(p_))) implies forall([p_ in nn, i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies mm(p_) <= i_ implies mm(p_) <= j_ implies (dist(m))((rf(p_))(i_), (rf(p_))(j_)) <= rad(p_)) implies forall([p_, q_, c], p_ in nn implies q_ in nn implies c in rr implies pos-rr(c) implies (dist(m))((rf(p_))(mm(p_)), (rf(q_))(mm(q_))) <= (rad(p_) + rad(q_)) + ((dist(completion(m)))(bigf(p_), bigf(q_)) + c))))
- `r12d-diag-in-cseq` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([bigf, rf, rad, mm], bigf in fun(nn, quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))) implies rf in fun(nn, cseq(m)) implies null-rr-seq(rad) implies mm in fun(nn, nn) implies forall([p_ in nn], bigf(p_) = class(cauchy-setoid(m), rf(p_))) implies forall([p_ in nn, i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies mm(p_) <= i_ implies mm(p_) <= j_ implies (dist(m))((rf(p_))(i_), (rf(p_))(j_)) <= rad(p_)) implies is-cauchy-seq(completion(m), bigf) implies vnb-lambda(pv_, nn, (rf(pv_))(mm(pv_))) in cseq(m)))
- `r12d-diag-in-fun` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([rf, mm], rf in fun(nn, cseq(m)) implies mm in fun(nn, nn) implies vnb-lambda(pv_, nn, (rf(pv_))(mm(pv_))) in fun(nn, pts(m))))
- `r12d-diag-limit` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([bigf, rf, rad, mm], bigf in fun(nn, quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))) implies rf in fun(nn, cseq(m)) implies null-rr-seq(rad) implies mm in fun(nn, nn) implies forall([p_ in nn], bigf(p_) = class(cauchy-setoid(m), rf(p_))) implies forall([p_ in nn, i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies mm(p_) <= i_ implies mm(p_) <= j_ implies (dist(m))((rf(p_))(i_), (rf(p_))(j_)) <= rad(p_)) implies is-cauchy-seq(completion(m), bigf) implies converges-to(completion(m), bigf, class(cauchy-setoid(m), vnb-lambda(pv_, nn, (rf(pv_))(mm(pv_)))))))
- `r12d-key` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([bigf, rf], bigf in fun(nn, quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))) implies rf in fun(nn, cseq(m)) implies forall([p_ in nn], bigf(p_) = class(cauchy-setoid(m), rf(p_))) implies forall([p_, q_, b_, c], p_ in nn implies q_ in nn implies b_ in nn implies c in rr implies pos-rr(c) implies forsome([n_ in nn], b_ <= n_ and (dist(m))((rf(p_))(n_), (rf(q_))(n_)) <= (dist(completion(m)))(bigf(p_), bigf(q_)) + c))))
- `r12d-rad-tail` — forall([rad], null-rr-seq(rad) implies forall([ev], pos-rr(ev) implies forsome([nr_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], nr_ <= k_ implies rad(k_) <= ev))))
- `r12d-rapid-indices` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([rf, rad], rf in fun(nn, cseq(m)) implies rad in fun(nn, rr) implies forall([p_ in nn], pos-rr(rad(p_))) implies forsome([mm in fun(nn, nn)], forall([p_ in nn, i_, j_], i_ in nn implies j_ in nn implies mm(p_) <= i_ implies mm(p_) <= j_ implies (dist(m))((rf(p_))(i_), (rf(p_))(j_)) <= rad(p_)))))
- `r12d-repfam` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([bigf in fun(nn, quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)))], forsome([rf in fun(nn, cseq(m))], forall([p_ in nn], bigf(p_) = class(cauchy-setoid(m), rf(p_))))))
- `r6b-add-respects2` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies respects2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (add(r))(a, b)))))
- `r6b-radd-interchange` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, c, d], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies c in carr(r) implies d in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), (add(r))(c, d)) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, c), (add(r))(b, d))))
- `r6b-radd-right-id` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, zero(r)) = a))
- `r6b-radd-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, b) in carr(r)))
- `r6b-rdiff-add` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, u, v], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies u in carr(r) implies v in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, b), (neg(r))((add(r))(u, v))) = (add(r))((add(r))(a, (neg(r))(u)), (add(r))(b, (neg(r))(v)))))
- `r6b-related-in` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)) in idl(r) implies related(ringoid-setoid(r), a, b)))
- `r6b-related-out` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies related(ringoid-setoid(r), a, b) implies (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)) in idl(r)))
- `r6b-rinv-unique` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(r))(a, b) = zero(r) implies b = (neg(r))(a)))
- `r6b-rneg-add` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (neg(r))((add(r))(a, b)) = (add(r))((neg(r))(a), (neg(r))(b))))
- `r6b-rneg-neg` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (neg(r))((neg(r))(a)) = a))
- `r6b-rneg-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (neg(r))(a) in carr(r)))
- `r6b-rq-add-lam-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (add(r))(a, b))) in fun(cartesian(pts(ringoid-setoid(r)), pts(ringoid-setoid(r))), quotient(ringoid-setoid(r))))
- `r6b-setoid-rel` — forall([r], rel(ringoid-setoid(r)) == ringoid-rel(r))
- `r7n-mul-respects2` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies respects2(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (mul(r))(a, b)))))
- `r7n-neg-respects` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies respects(ringoid-setoid(r), vnb-lambda(a, carr(r), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (neg(r))(a)))))
- `r7n-radd-idem-zero` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies a = zero(r)))
- `r7n-rdiff-mul` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b, u, v], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies u in carr(r) implies v in carr(r) implies (add(r))((mul(r))(a, b), (neg(r))((mul(r))(u, v))) = (add(r))((mul(r))((add(r))(a, (neg(r))(u)), b), (mul(r))(u, (add(r))(b, (neg(r))(v))))))
- `r7n-rmul-neg-right` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (mul(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)) = (neg(r))((mul(r))(a, b))))
- `r7n-rmul-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (mul(r))(a, b) in carr(r)))
- `r7n-rmul-zero-right` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (mul(r))(a, zero(r)) = zero(r)))
- `r7n-rneg-mul-left` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (mul(r))((neg(r))(a), b) = (neg(r))((mul(r))(a, b))))
- `r7n-rone-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies one(r) in carr(r))
- `r7n-rq-mul-lam-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies vnb-lambda([a, b], cartesian(carr(r), carr(r)), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (mul(r))(a, b))) in fun(cartesian(pts(ringoid-setoid(r)), pts(ringoid-setoid(r))), quotient(ringoid-setoid(r))))
- `r7n-rq-neg-lam-type` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies vnb-lambda(a, carr(r), class(ringoid-setoid(r), (neg(r))(a))) in fun(pts(ringoid-setoid(r)), quotient(ringoid-setoid(r))))
- `r7n-rzero-mul-left` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([b in carr(r)], (mul(r))(zero(r), b) = zero(r)))
- `r7q-cseq-cauchy` — forall([m, f_ in cseq(m)], is-cauchy-seq(m, f_))
- `r7q-dhat-in-rr` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([x_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)), y_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))], (dist(completion(m)))(x_, y_) in rr))
- `r7q-dhat-iota` — forall([m, x_, y_], x_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)) implies y_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)) implies (dist(completion(m)))(x_, y_) == iota(dval, forsome([f, g], f in cseq(m) and g in cseq(m) and x_ = class(cauchy-setoid(m), f) and y_ = class(cauchy-setoid(m), g) and converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f, g), dval))))
- `r7q-dhat-nonneg` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([x_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)), y_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))], 0 <= (dist(completion(m)))(x_, y_)))
- `r7q-dhat-self-zero` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([x_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))], (dist(completion(m)))(x_, x_) = 0))
- `r7q-dhat-sym` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([x_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)), y_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))], (dist(completion(m)))(x_, y_) = (dist(completion(m)))(y_, x_)))
- `r7q-dhat-triangle` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([x_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)), y_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)), z_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))], (dist(completion(m)))(x_, z_) <= (dist(completion(m)))(x_, y_) + (dist(completion(m)))(y_, z_)))
- `r7q-dhat-zero-eq` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([x_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)), y_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m))], (dist(completion(m)))(x_, y_) = 0 implies x_ = y_))
- `r7q-dist-seq-cauchy` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([f_ in cseq(m), g_ in cseq(m)], is-cauchy-seq(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, g_))))
- `r7q-dist-seq-converges` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([f_ in cseq(m), g_ in cseq(m)], converges(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, g_))))
- `r7q-dist-seq-sym` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)), g_ in fun(nn, pts(m)), lv], converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, g_), lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, g_, f_), lv)))
- `r7q-dist-value` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([f_ in cseq(m), g_ in cseq(m), lv], converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, g_), lv) implies (dist(completion(m)))(class(cauchy-setoid(m), f_), class(cauchy-setoid(m), g_)) = lv))
- `r7q-iota-dhat` — forall([m, x_, y_], x_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)) implies y_ in quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)) implies iota(dval, forsome([f, g], f in cseq(m) and g in cseq(m) and x_ = class(cauchy-setoid(m), f) and y_ = class(cauchy-setoid(m), g) and converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f, g), dval))) == (dist(completion(m)))(x_, y_))
- `r7q-limit-indep` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)), g_ in fun(nn, pts(m)), p_ in fun(nn, pts(m)), q_ in fun(nn, pts(m))], converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, p_), 0) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, g_, q_), 0) implies forall([lv], converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, g_), lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, p_, q_), lv))))
- `r7q-null-transfer` — forall([t], is-metric-space(t) implies forall([f_ in fun(nn, pts(t)), h_ in fun(nn, pts(t)), lv in pts(t)], converges-to(t, f_, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(t, f_, h_), 0) implies converges-to(t, h_, lv)))
- `r7q-quad-null` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)), g_ in fun(nn, pts(m)), p_ in fun(nn, pts(m)), q_ in fun(nn, pts(m))], converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, p_), 0) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, g_, q_), 0) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, g_), dist-seq(m, p_, q_)), 0)))
- `r7q-rr-pts` — rr == pts(rr-ms)
- `r8e-blf-compose-continuous` — forall([m, g, h, t], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies h in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, nvs-metric-space(m), h, t) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, compose(g, h), t))
- `r8e-blf-diff-commute` — forall([m, g, h, t, bl], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies is-diff-at-v(m, h, t, bl) implies is-diff-at(compose(g, h), t, g(bl)))
- `r8e-blf-sub` — forall([m, g], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies forall([u_ in vec(m), v_ in vec(m)], g((vadd(m))(u_, (vneg(m))(v_))) = g(u_) - g(v_)))
- `r8e-blf-vneg` — forall([m, g], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies forall([u_ in vec(m)], g((vneg(m))(u_)) = 0 - g(u_)))
- `r8e-coef-comm` — forall([p_ in rr, q_ in rr, v_ in rr], (p_ * q_) * v_ = (v_ * p_) * q_)
- `r8e-eps-over` — forall([c_ in rr], 0 <= c_ implies forall([e_], pos-rr(e_) implies forsome([d_], pos-rr(d_) and forall([z_ in rr], z_ <= d_ implies c_ * z_ <= e_))))
- `r8e-g-of-poly-ind` — forall([n in nn, m, f, g, a, x], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= n implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))) implies g(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n)) = taylor-poly(compose(g, f), a, n, x))
- `r8e-gof-nth-deriv-fn` — forall([k in nn, m, f, g], is-bounded-linear-functional(m, g) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= k implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))) implies nth-deriv(compose(g, f), k) = compose(g, nth-deriv-v(m, f, k)))
- `r8e-nth-deriv-v-diff` — forall([m, f, j_, x_], j_ in nn implies x_ in rr implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(j_)) in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies is-diff-at-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_), x_, (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(j_)))(x_)))
- `r8e-td-v-diff-at` — forall([m, f, a, x, n, t], taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) implies n in nn implies a < t implies t < x implies is-diff-at-v(m, nth-deriv-v(m, f, n), t, (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(t)))
- `r8e-tp-unfold` — forall([ff, aa, nd, xx], taylor-poly(ff, aa, nd, xx) == series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, ((nth-deriv(ff, k))(aa) * (xx - aa) ^ k) * recip(factorial(k))), succ(nd)))
- `raag-carr` — forall([a_], carr(ring-additive-ag(a_)) == carr(a_))
- `raag-iden` — forall([a_], iden(ring-additive-ag(a_)) == zero(a_))
- `raag-opr` — forall([a_], opr(ring-additive-ag(a_)) == add(a_))
- `ran-subset-codomain` — forall([x, y, f in fun(x, y)], ran(f) subset y)
- `range-membership` — forall([f, a in dom(f)], f(a) in ran(f))
- `ras-carr` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies carr(ring-additive-ag(a)) = carr(a))
- `ras-id` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies iden(ring-additive-ag(a)) = zero(a))
- `ras-inv` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies inv(ring-additive-ag(a)) = neg(a))
- `ras-op` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies opr(ring-additive-ag(a)) = add(a))
- `ratio-test` — forall([cpsth_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 < cpsth_(cpsk_)) implies (elimsup(vnb-lambda(cpsj_, nn, cpsth_(succ(cpsj_)) * recip(cpsth_(cpsj_)))) < 1 implies series-converges(cpsth_)) and (1 < eliminf(vnb-lambda(cpsj_, nn, cpsth_(succ(cpsj_)) * recip(cpsth_(cpsj_)))) implies not(series-converges(cpsth_))))
- `ratio-test-converges` — forall([coef in fun(nn, rr), l], l in rr and 0 <= l and forall([n in nn], not(coef(n) = 0)) and ps-ratio-limit(coef, l) implies forall([x], x in rr and abs(x) * l < 1 implies ps-converges-at(coef, x)))
- `ratio-test-converges-limsup` — forall([cpsth_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 < cpsth_(cpsk_)) implies elimsup(vnb-lambda(cpsj_, nn, cpsth_(succ(cpsj_)) * recip(cpsth_(cpsj_)))) < 1 implies series-converges(cpsth_))
- `ratio-test-diverges-liminf` — forall([cpsth_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 < cpsth_(cpsk_)) implies 1 < eliminf(vnb-lambda(cpsj_, nn, cpsth_(succ(cpsj_)) * recip(cpsth_(cpsj_)))) implies not(series-converges(cpsth_)))
- `real-part-in-rr` — forall([z in cc], real-part(z) in rr)
- `recip-continuous-at` — forall([h, p], h in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([x in rr], not(h(x) = 0)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, p) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, recip(h(x))), p))
- `recip-factorial-in-rr` — forall([n in nn], recip(factorial(n)) in rr)
- `recip-lam-in-fun` — forall([h in fun(rr, rr)], forall([x in rr], not(h(x) = 0)) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, recip(h(x))) in fun(rr, rr))
- `recip-power-two-halves` — forall([k in nn], recip(2 ^ k) = 2 * recip(2 * 2 ^ k))
- `recip-star-antiderivable-pos` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies 0 < a implies a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), a, b))
- `recip-star-continuous-at` — forall([c in rr], not(c = 0) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), c))
- `recip-star-continuous-on-ccint` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, x_ in rr], 0 < a implies x_ in ccint(a, b) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), x_))
- `recip-star-in-rr` — forall([u_ in rr], recip-star(u_) in rr)
- `recip-star-int-additive` — forall([u, v, w], u in rr implies v in rr implies w in rr implies 0 < u implies 0 < v implies 0 < w implies c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), u, w) = c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), u, v) + c-int-or(vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)), v, w))
- `recip-star-inverse` — forall([u_ in rr], not(u_ = 0) implies u_ * recip-star(u_) = 1)
- `recip-star-lam-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(z_, rr, recip-star(z_)) in fun(rr, rr)
- `recip-star-nn-nonneg` — forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= recip-star(cpsk_))
- `recip-star-nn-pos` — forall([cpsk_ in nn], 1 <= cpsk_ implies 0 < recip-star(cpsk_))
- `recip-star-scale` — forall([lam, u], lam in rr implies u in rr implies not(lam = 0) implies recip-star(lam * u) * lam = recip-star(u))
- `recip-star-value` — forall([u_ in rr], not(u_ = 0) implies recip-star(u_) = recip(u_))
- `recip-star-zero` — recip-star(0) = 0
- `recip-succ-cancel` — forall([m in nn, c in rr, v in rr], c * v = (succ(m) * (recip(succ(m)) * c)) * v)
- `recip-succ-small` — forall([d], pos-rr(d) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([m_ in nn], n_ <= m_ implies recip(m_ + 1) <= d)))
- `reflect-continuous-at-sub` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, f, prc_ in rr, prq_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prq_(pay_) == prc_ * f(a + b - pay_)) implies forall([prt_ in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(a, b)), rr-ms, f, a + b - prt_) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, ccint(a, b)), rr-ms, prq_, prt_)))
- `reflect-continuous-on` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, f, prc_ in rr, prq_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(f, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prq_(pay_) == prc_ * f(a + b - pay_)) implies is-continuous-on(prq_, ccint(a, b)))
- `reflect-image-countable` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([prd_], is-countable(prd_) implies prd_ subset ccint(a, b) implies is-countable(image(vnb-lambda(prx_, ccint(a, b), a + b - prx_), prd_)) and image(vnb-lambda(prx_, ccint(a, b), a + b - prx_), prd_) subset ccint(a, b)))
- `reflect-image-finite` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([prd_ in set], card(prd_) in nn implies prd_ subset ccint(a, b) implies image(vnb-lambda(prx_, ccint(a, b), a + b - prx_), prd_) in set and card(image(vnb-lambda(prx_, ccint(a, b), a + b - prx_), prd_)) in nn and image(vnb-lambda(prx_, ccint(a, b), a + b - prx_), prd_) subset ccint(a, b)))
- `reflect-not-in-image` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies forall([prd_], prd_ subset ccint(a, b) implies forall([prt_ in ccint(a, b)], not(prt_ in image(vnb-lambda(prx_, ccint(a, b), a + b - prx_), prd_)) implies not(a + b - prt_ in prd_))))
- `regulated-on-congruence` — forall([f, g, a, b], is-regulated-on(g, a, b) implies f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([rgz_ in ccint(a, b)], f(rgz_) == g(rgz_)) implies is-regulated-on(f, a, b))
- `regulated-on-glue` — forall([a in rr, c in rr, b in rr], a < c implies c < b implies forall([f1, f2, f], is-regulated-on(f1, a, c) implies is-regulated-on(f2, c, b) implies f in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([t in ccint(a, c)], t < c implies f(t) == f1(t)) implies forall([t in ccint(c, b)], c < t implies f(t) == f2(t)) implies is-regulated-on(f, a, b)))
- `regulated-on-has-primitive` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, f], is-regulated-on(f, a, b) implies forsome([g], is-primitive(g, f, a, b)))
- `regulated-on-is-step-limit` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, f], is-regulated-on(f, a, b) implies forsome([fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr))], forall([k in nn], is-step-fn(fam(k), a, b)) and is-unif-limit-on(fam, extend-const(f, a, b), a, b)))
- `regulated-on-left-window` — forall([rswa_, rswb_, rswf_, rswg_, rswe_, rswt_], is-regulated-on(rswf_, rswa_, rswb_) implies rswg_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([rswz_ in ccint(rswa_, rswb_)], rswg_(rswz_) = rswf_(rswz_)) implies pos-rr(rswe_) implies rswt_ in ccint(rswa_, rswb_) implies forsome([rsld_ in rr], 0 < rsld_ and forsome([rsll_ in rr], forall([rsls_ in rr], rswa_ <= rsls_ implies rswt_ - rsld_ < rsls_ implies rsls_ < rswt_ implies abs(rswg_(rsls_) - rsll_) <= rswe_))))
- `regulated-on-magnitude` — forall([f, g, lih_, a, b], is-regulated-on(f, a, b) implies is-regulated-on(g, a, b) implies lih_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([liz_ in ccint(a, b)], lih_(liz_) == magnitude(f(liz_) + g(liz_) * 1i)) implies is-regulated-on(lih_, a, b))
- `regulated-on-mul` — forall([f, g, rgh_, a, b], is-regulated-on(f, a, b) implies is-regulated-on(g, a, b) implies rgh_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([rgz_ in ccint(a, b)], rgh_(rgz_) == f(rgz_) * g(rgz_)) implies is-regulated-on(rgh_, a, b))
- `regulated-on-reflect` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, f, prc_ in rr, prq_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-regulated-on(f, a, b) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prq_(pay_) == prc_ * f(a + b - pay_)) implies is-regulated-on(prq_, a, b))
- `regulated-on-restrict` — forall([f, a, b, rqc_, rqd_, g], is-regulated-on(f, a, b) implies rqc_ in rr implies rqd_ in rr implies a <= rqc_ implies rqc_ < rqd_ implies rqd_ <= b implies g in fun(ccint(rqc_, rqd_), rr) implies forall([rqz_ in ccint(rqc_, rqd_)], g(rqz_) = f(rqz_)) implies is-regulated-on(g, rqc_, rqd_))
- `regulated-on-right-window` — forall([rswa_, rswb_, rswf_, rswg_, rswe_, rswt_], is-regulated-on(rswf_, rswa_, rswb_) implies rswg_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([rswz_ in ccint(rswa_, rswb_)], rswg_(rswz_) = rswf_(rswz_)) implies pos-rr(rswe_) implies rswt_ in ccint(rswa_, rswb_) implies forsome([rsrd_ in rr], 0 < rsrd_ and forsome([rsrl_ in rr], forall([rsrs_ in rr], rswt_ < rsrs_ implies rsrs_ < rswt_ + rsrd_ implies rsrs_ <= rswb_ implies abs(rswg_(rsrs_) - rsrl_) <= rswe_))))
- `regulated-on-scalar` — forall([f, rgh_, a, b, rgc_ in rr], is-regulated-on(f, a, b) implies rgh_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([rgz_ in ccint(a, b)], rgh_(rgz_) == rgc_ * f(rgz_)) implies is-regulated-on(rgh_, a, b))
- `regulated-on-step-approx` — forall([rswa_, rswb_, rswf_], is-regulated-on(rswf_, rswa_, rswb_) implies forall([rswe_], pos-rr(rswe_) implies forsome([rsth_ in fun(rr, rr)], is-step-fn(rsth_, rswa_, rswb_) and forall([rsqz_ in ccint(rswa_, rswb_)], abs((extend-const(rswf_, rswa_, rswb_))(rsqz_) - rsth_(rsqz_)) <= rswe_))))
- `regulated-on-step-approx-creep` — forall([rswa_, rswb_, rswf_, rswg_, rswe_], is-regulated-on(rswf_, rswa_, rswb_) implies rswg_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([rswz_ in ccint(rswa_, rswb_)], rswg_(rswz_) = rswf_(rswz_)) implies pos-rr(rswe_) implies forsome([rsqn_ in nn, rsqp_ in fun(nn, rr), rsqh_ in fun(rr, rr)], rsqp_(0) = rswa_ and rsqp_(rsqn_) = rswb_ and forall([rsi_, rsj_], rsi_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rsj_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rsi_ < rsj_ implies rsqp_(rsi_) < rsqp_(rsj_)) and forall([rspi_], rspi_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rspi_ < rsqn_ implies forsome([rspc_ in rr], forall([rspt_], rspt_ in rr and rsqp_(rspi_) < rspt_ and rspt_ < rsqp_(succ(rspi_)) implies rsqh_(rspt_) = rspc_))) and forall([rsqz_ in ccint(rswa_, rswb_)], abs(rswg_(rsqz_) - rsqh_(rsqz_)) <= rswe_)))
- `regulated-on-sub` — forall([f, g, rgh_, a, b], is-regulated-on(f, a, b) implies is-regulated-on(g, a, b) implies rgh_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([rgz_ in ccint(a, b)], rgh_(rgz_) == f(rgz_) - g(rgz_)) implies is-regulated-on(rgh_, a, b))
- `regulated-on-sum` — forall([f, g, rgh_, a, b], is-regulated-on(f, a, b) implies is-regulated-on(g, a, b) implies rgh_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr) implies forall([rgz_ in ccint(a, b)], rgh_(rgz_) == f(rgz_) + g(rgz_)) implies is-regulated-on(rgh_, a, b))
- `remove-restore` — forall([a_ in set, x_ in a_], union(difference(a_, pair(x_, x_)), pair(x_, x_)) = a_)
- `respects-unfold` — forall([s, f], respects(s, f) iff forall([a_ in pts(s), b_ in pts(s)], related(s, a_, b_) implies f(a_) = f(b_)))
- `respects2-unfold` — forall([s, f], respects2(s, f) iff forall([a_, b_, c_, d_], a_ in pts(s) implies b_ in pts(s) implies c_ in pts(s) implies d_ in pts(s) implies related(s, a_, c_) and related(s, b_, d_) implies f(a_, b_) = f(c_, d_)))
- `restrict-apply` — forall([f, a, u_ in a], (restrict(f, a))(u_) == f(u_))
- `restrict-continuous-at` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([tt_, f, pa_ in a], is-continuous-at(s, tt_, f, pa_) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(s, a), tt_, restrict(f, a), pa_))))
- `restrict-fun-domain-subset` — forall([f, osd_, osc_, osw_, osb_], f in fun(osd_, osc_) implies restrict(f, osw_) in fun(osw_, osb_) implies osw_ subset osd_)
- `restrict-in-fun` — forall([f, dm_, cd_, a], f in fun(dm_, cd_) implies a subset dm_ implies restrict(f, a) in fun(a, cd_))
- `restrict-in-fun-of-restrict` — forall([f, u, icc_, icv_], restrict(f, u) in fun(u, icc_) implies icv_ subset u implies restrict(f, icv_) in fun(icv_, icc_))
- `right-limit-within-ccint-unique` — forall([f, a in rr, b in rr, rqx_ in rr, rql_, rqm_], a <= rqx_ implies rqx_ < b implies is-right-limit-within(f, ccint(a, b), rqx_, rql_) implies is-right-limit-within(f, ccint(a, b), rqx_, rqm_) implies rql_ = rqm_)
- `right-limit-within-le` — forall([f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_, rqm_], is-right-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_) implies is-right-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, rqm_) implies forall([rqr_], pos-rr(rqr_) implies forsome([rqt_ in rqw_], rqx_ < rqt_ and rqt_ < rqx_ + rqr_)) implies rql_ - rqm_ <= 0)
- `right-limit-within-local` — forall([g, jlv_, f, jlw_, jlx_, jll_, jlr_], is-right-limit-within(g, jlv_, jlx_, jll_) implies jlw_ subset rr implies f in fun(jlw_, rr) implies pos-rr(jlr_) implies forall([jlt_ in jlw_], jlx_ < jlt_ implies jlt_ < jlx_ + jlr_ implies jlt_ in jlv_) implies forall([jlt_ in jlw_], jlx_ < jlt_ implies jlt_ < jlx_ + jlr_ implies f(jlt_) == g(jlt_)) implies is-right-limit-within(f, jlw_, jlx_, jll_))
- `right-limit-within-magnitude` — forall([f, g, lih_, liw_, lix_, lil_, lik_], is-right-limit-within(f, liw_, lix_, lil_) implies is-right-limit-within(g, liw_, lix_, lik_) implies lih_ in fun(liw_, rr) implies forall([liz_ in liw_], lih_(liz_) == magnitude(f(liz_) + g(liz_) * 1i)) implies is-right-limit-within(lih_, liw_, lix_, magnitude(lil_ + lik_ * 1i)))
- `right-limit-within-mul` — forall([f, g, rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_, rgm_], is-right-limit-within(f, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_) implies is-right-limit-within(g, rgw_, rgx_, rgm_) implies rgh_ in fun(rgw_, rr) implies forall([rgz_ in rgw_], rgh_(rgz_) == f(rgz_) * g(rgz_)) implies is-right-limit-within(rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_ * rgm_))
- `right-limit-within-scalar` — forall([f, rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_, rgc_ in rr], is-right-limit-within(f, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_) implies rgh_ in fun(rgw_, rr) implies forall([rgz_ in rgw_], rgh_(rgz_) == rgc_ * f(rgz_)) implies is-right-limit-within(rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgc_ * rgl_))
- `right-limit-within-sub` — forall([f, g, rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_, rgm_], is-right-limit-within(f, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_) implies is-right-limit-within(g, rgw_, rgx_, rgm_) implies rgh_ in fun(rgw_, rr) implies forall([rgz_ in rgw_], rgh_(rgz_) == f(rgz_) - g(rgz_)) implies is-right-limit-within(rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_ - rgm_))
- `right-limit-within-subset` — forall([f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_, g, rqv_], is-right-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_) implies rqv_ subset rqw_ implies g in fun(rqv_, rr) implies forall([rqz_ in rqv_], g(rqz_) = f(rqz_)) implies is-right-limit-within(g, rqv_, rqx_, rql_))
- `right-limit-within-sum` — forall([f, g, rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_, rgm_], is-right-limit-within(f, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_) implies is-right-limit-within(g, rgw_, rgx_, rgm_) implies rgh_ in fun(rgw_, rr) implies forall([rgz_ in rgw_], rgh_(rgz_) == f(rgz_) + g(rgz_)) implies is-right-limit-within(rgh_, rgw_, rgx_, rgl_ + rgm_))
- `right-limit-within-unique` — forall([f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_, rqm_], is-right-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, rql_) implies is-right-limit-within(f, rqw_, rqx_, rqm_) implies forall([rqr_], pos-rr(rqr_) implies forsome([rqt_ in rqw_], rqx_ < rqt_ and rqt_ < rqx_ + rqr_)) implies rql_ = rqm_)
- `ring-add-closed` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (add(s))(a, b) in carr(s)))
- `ring-add-inverse-unique` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (add(s))(a, b) = zero(s) implies b = (neg(s))(a)))
- `ring-add-right-id` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (add(s))(a, zero(s)) = a))
- `ring-add-right-inv` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (add(s))(a, (neg(s))(a)) = zero(s)))
- `ring-additive-ag-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ring(a, b, f) implies is-hom-abelian-group(ring-additive-ag(a), ring-additive-ag(b), f))
- `ring-carr-in-set` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies carr(r) in set)
- `ring-carrier-closed-mul` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (mul(r))(a, b) in carr(r)))
- `ring-mpow-one` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r)], mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), x, 1) = x))
- `ring-mul-interchange` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r), d in carr(r)], (mul(r))((mul(r))(a, b), (mul(r))(c, d)) = (mul(r))((mul(r))(a, c), (mul(r))(b, d))))
- `ring-mul-zero-left` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (mul(s))(zero(s), a) = zero(s)))
- `ring-mul-zero-right` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (mul(s))(a, zero(s)) = zero(s)))
- `ring-multiplicative-monoid-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ring(a, b, f) implies is-hom-monoid(ring-multiplicative-monoid(a), ring-multiplicative-monoid(b), f))
- `ring-neg-in-carr` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (neg(s))(a) in carr(s)))
- `ring-neg-mul-left` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (mul(s))((neg(s))(a), b) = (neg(s))((mul(s))(a, b))))
- `ring-neg-neg` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies forall([r in carr(s)], (neg(s))((neg(s))(r)) = r))
- `ring-neg-zero` — forall([a_], is-ring(a_) implies (neg(a_))(zero(a_)) = zero(a_))
- `ring-one-in` — forall([s], is-ring(s) implies one(s) in carr(s))
- `ring-power-add` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), j in nn, k in nn], ring-power(r, x, j + k) = (mul(r))(ring-power(r, x, j), ring-power(r, x, k))))
- `ring-power-add-ind` — forall([k in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), j in nn], ring-power(r, x, j + k) = (mul(r))(ring-power(r, x, j), ring-power(r, x, k))))
- `ring-power-mult` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r), n in nn], ring-power(r, (mul(r))(x, y), n) = (mul(r))(ring-power(r, x, n), ring-power(r, y, n))))
- `ring-power-mult-ind` — forall([n in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), y in carr(r)], ring-power(r, (mul(r))(x, y), n) = (mul(r))(ring-power(r, x, n), ring-power(r, y, n))))
- `ring-power-of-one` — forall([n_ in nn, r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies ring-power(r, one(r), n_) = one(r))
- `ring-power-one` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r)], ring-power(r, x, 1) = x))
- `ring-power-succ` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), n in nn], ring-power(r, x, succ(n)) = (mul(r))(ring-power(r, x, n), x)))
- `ring-power-succ-left` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), n in nn], ring-power(r, x, succ(n)) = (mul(r))(x, ring-power(r, x, n))))
- `ring-power-type` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies forall([x in carr(r), n in nn], ring-power(r, x, n) in carr(r)))
- `ring-power-zero` — forall([r, x], ring-power(r, x, 0) == one(r))
- `ring-scalar-zz-nn` — forall([k in nn, rng], is-commutative-ring(rng) implies forall([r in carr(rng), a in carr(rng)], (mul(rng))(r, zz-act(ring-additive-ag(rng), k, a)) = zz-act(ring-additive-ag(rng), k, (mul(rng))(r, a))))
- `ringoid-as-ring-functorial` — forall([a, b, f], is-hom-ringoid(a, b, f) implies is-hom-ring(ringoid-as-ring(a), ringoid-as-ring(b), f))
- `ringoid-diff-telescope` — forall([r, a, b, c], is-ringoid(r) implies a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies c in carr(r) implies (add(r))((add(r))(a, (neg(r))(b)), (add(r))(b, (neg(r))(c))) = (add(r))(a, (neg(r))(c)))
- `ringoid-neg-diff` — forall([r, a, b], is-ringoid(r) implies a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (neg(r))((add(r))(a, (neg(r))(b))) = (add(r))(b, (neg(r))(a)))
- `ringoid-quotient-is-ring` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies is-ring(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)))
- `ringoid-rel-is-equivalence` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies is-equivalence(ringoid-rel(r), carr(r)))
- `ringoid-setoid-is-setoid` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies is-setoid(ringoid-setoid(r)))
- `rko2-cauchy-setoid-length` — forall([m], length(cauchy-setoid(m)) = 2)
- `rko2-cauchy-setoid-rel` — forall([m], rel(cauchy-setoid(m)) == crel(m))
- `rko2-class-eq-null` — forall([m, p_, q_], is-metric-space(m) implies p_ in cseq(m) implies q_ in cseq(m) implies class(cauchy-setoid(m), p_) = class(cauchy-setoid(m), q_) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, p_, q_), 0))
- `rko2-completion-dist` — forall([m], dist(completion(m)) == completion-dist(m))
- `rko2-const-equiv-converges` — forall([m, u_, f_], is-metric-space(m) implies u_ in pts(m) implies f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, embed-seq(m, u_), f_), 0) implies converges-to(m, f_, u_))
- `rko2-const-seq-converges` — forall([m, u_], is-metric-space(m) implies u_ in pts(m) implies converges-to(m, embed-seq(m, u_), u_))
- `rko2-crel-elim` — forall([m, p_, q_], [p_, q_] in crel(m) implies cseq-equiv(m, p_, q_))
- `rko2-crel-intro` — forall([m, p_, q_], p_ in cseq(m) implies q_ in cseq(m) implies cseq-equiv(m, p_, q_) implies [p_, q_] in crel(m))
- `rko2-crel-is-set` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies crel(m) in set)
- `rko2-crel-unfold` — forall([m], crel(m) == {p_ in cartesian(cseq(m), cseq(m)): cseq-equiv(m, nth(1, p_), nth(2, p_))})
- `rko2-cseq-in-fun` — forall([m, f_ in cseq(m)], f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)))
- `rko2-cseq-unfold` — forall([m], cseq(m) == {f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)): is-cauchy-seq(m, f_)})
- `rko2-dist-converges` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([f_, g_, a_, b_], f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies g_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies a_ in pts(m) implies b_ in pts(m) implies converges-to(m, f_, a_) implies converges-to(m, g_, b_) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, g_), (dist(m))(a_, b_))))
- `rko2-dist-seq-at` — forall([m, f_, g_, n_ in nn], (dist-seq(m, f_, g_))(n_) == (dist(m))(f_(n_), g_(n_)))
- `rko2-dist-seq-type` — forall([m, f_, g_], is-metric-space(m) implies f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies g_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies dist-seq(m, f_, g_) in fun(nn, rr))
- `rko2-dist-seq-unfold` — forall([m, f_, g_], dist-seq(m, f_, g_) == vnb-lambda(n_, nn, (dist(m))(f_(n_), g_(n_))))
- `rko2-null-refl` — forall([m, f_], is-metric-space(m) implies f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, f_), 0))
- `rko2-null-sym` — forall([m, f_, g_], is-metric-space(m) implies f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies g_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, g_), 0) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, g_, f_), 0))
- `rko2-null-trans` — forall([m, f_, g_, h_], is-metric-space(m) implies f_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies g_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies h_ in fun(nn, pts(m)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, g_), 0) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, g_, h_), 0) implies converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f_, h_), 0))
- `rko2-quad` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies forall([a_, b_, c_, e_], a_ in pts(m) implies b_ in pts(m) implies c_ in pts(m) implies e_ in pts(m) implies abs((dist(m))(a_, b_) - (dist(m))(c_, e_)) <= (dist(m))(a_, c_) + (dist(m))(b_, e_)))
- `rko2-refl` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a_ in pts(s)], related(s, a_, a_)))
- `rko2-related-unfold` — forall([s, a_, b_], related(s, a_, b_) iff [a_, b_] in rel(s))
- `rko2-sym` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a_, b_], a_ in pts(s) implies b_ in pts(s) implies related(s, a_, b_) implies related(s, b_, a_)))
- `rko2-trans` — forall([s], is-setoid(s) implies forall([a_, b_, c_], a_ in pts(s) implies b_ in pts(s) implies c_ in pts(s) implies related(s, a_, b_) implies related(s, b_, c_) implies related(s, a_, c_)))
- `rkt-cauchy-setoid-pts` — forall([m], pts(cauchy-setoid(m)) == cseq(m))
- `rkt-class-is-set` — forall([m, w_], is-metric-space(m) implies class(cauchy-setoid(m), w_) in set)
- `rkt-completion-pts` — forall([m], pts(completion(m)) == quotient(cauchy-setoid(m)))
- `rkt-cseq-is-set` — forall([m], is-metric-space(m) implies cseq(m) in set)
- `rkt-embed-seq-cauchy` — forall([m, u], is-metric-space(m) implies u in pts(m) implies is-cauchy-seq(m, embed-seq(m, u)))
- `rkt-embed-seq-in-cseq` — forall([m, u], is-metric-space(m) implies u in pts(m) implies embed-seq(m, u) in cseq(m))
- `rkt-embed-seq-in-fun` — forall([m, u], is-metric-space(m) implies u in pts(m) implies embed-seq(m, u) in fun(nn, pts(m)))
- `rkt-vtaylor-poly-in-vec-ind` — forall([n in nn, m, f, a, x], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= n implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))) implies taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n) in vec(m))
- `rmm-carr` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies carr(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r)) = carr(r))
- `rmm-iden` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies iden(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r)) = one(r))
- `rmm-op` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies opr(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r)) = mul(r))
- `road-affine-reparam` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies lrpc_ < lrpd_ implies forall([pgam, dgam, lrpr_, lrpk_], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies lrpr_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), cc) implies lrpk_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), cc) implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrpr_(lrpz_) == pgam(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrpk_(lrpz_) == lrpl_ * dgam(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies is-road(lrpr_, lrpk_, lrpc_, lrpd_)))
- `road-integrand-regulated` — forall([u, f, pgam, dgam, a, b], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset u implies is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), real-part(f(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_))), a, b) and is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(pat_, ccint(a, b), imag-part(f(pgam(pat_)) * dgam(pat_))), a, b))
- `road-length-affine-reparam` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies lrpc_ < lrpd_ implies forall([pgam, dgam, lrpk_], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies lrpk_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), cc) implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrpk_(lrpz_) == lrpl_ * dgam(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies road-length(lrpk_, lrpc_, lrpd_) = road-length(dgam, a, b)))
- `road-length-in-rr` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies road-length(dgam, a, b) in rr)
- `road-length-juxta` — forall([pgam1, dgam1, pgam2, dgam2, a, b, c], is-road(pgam1, dgam1, a, b) implies is-road(pgam2, dgam2, b, c) implies pgam1(b) = pgam2(b) implies road-length(juxta(dgam1, dgam2, a, b, c), a, c) = road-length(dgam1, a, b) + road-length(dgam2, b, c))
- `road-length-nonneg` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies 0 <= road-length(dgam, a, b))
- `road-length-primitive` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies forsome([rlg_], is-primitive(rlg_, vnb-lambda(rlt_, ccint(a, b), magnitude(dgam(rlt_))), a, b)))
- `road-length-regulated` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies is-regulated-on(vnb-lambda(rlt_, ccint(a, b), magnitude(dgam(rlt_))), a, b))
- `road-length-segment` — forall([p in cc, q in cc], road-length(seg-deriv(p, q), 0, 1) = magnitude(q - p))
- `road-length-tri` — forall([a in cc, b in cc, c in cc], road-length(tri-road-deriv(a, b, c), 0, 3) = (magnitude(b - a) + magnitude(c - b)) + magnitude(a - c))
- `road-length-unfold` — forall([rld_, rla_, rlb_], road-length(rld_, rla_, rlb_) == pw-int(vnb-lambda(rlt_, ccint(rla_, rlb_), magnitude(rld_(rlt_))), rla_, rlb_))
- `road-restrict` — forall([pgam, dgam, a, b, licv_ in rr, lidv_ in rr], is-road(pgam, dgam, a, b) implies a <= licv_ implies licv_ < lidv_ implies lidv_ <= b implies is-road(restrict(pgam, ccint(licv_, lidv_)), restrict(dgam, ccint(licv_, lidv_)), licv_, lidv_))
- `rolle` — forall([h, a, b], h in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies forall([x in ccint(a, b)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, x)) implies forall([x], x in rr and a < x and x < b implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(h, x, l))) implies h(a) = h(b) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < b and is-diff-at(h, theta, 0)))
- `rolle-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([h in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(h, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([x in ooint(a, b)], forsome([l], has-deriv-at(h, x, l))) implies h(a) = h(b) implies forsome([theta in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(h, theta, 0))))
- `root-test` — forall([cpsth_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= cpsth_(cpsk_)) implies (elimsup(vnb-lambda(cpsj_, nn, rpow-star(cpsth_(cpsj_), recip-star(cpsj_)))) < 1 implies series-converges(cpsth_)) and (1 < elimsup(vnb-lambda(cpsj_, nn, rpow-star(cpsth_(cpsj_), recip-star(cpsj_)))) implies not(series-converges(cpsth_))))
- `root-test-converges` — forall([cpsth_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= cpsth_(cpsk_)) implies elimsup(vnb-lambda(cpsj_, nn, rpow-star(cpsth_(cpsj_), recip-star(cpsj_)))) < 1 implies series-converges(cpsth_))
- `root-test-diverges` — forall([cpsth_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= cpsth_(cpsk_)) implies 1 < elimsup(vnb-lambda(cpsj_, nn, rpow-star(cpsth_(cpsj_), recip-star(cpsj_)))) implies not(series-converges(cpsth_)))
- `row-tabulation` — forall([n in nn, a, g, c_], forall([j_], j_ in nn and 1 <= j_ and j_ <= n implies g(c_, j_) in a) implies forsome([l in tuples(a)], length(l) = n and forall([j_], j_ in nn and 1 <= j_ and j_ <= n implies nth(j_, l) = g(c_, j_))))
- `row-tabulation-offset` — forall([n in nn, a, g, c_, k in nn], forall([j_], j_ in nn and 1 <= j_ and j_ <= n implies g(c_, k + j_) in a) implies forsome([l in tuples(a)], length(l) = n and forall([j_], j_ in nn and 1 <= j_ and j_ <= n implies nth(j_, l) = g(c_, k + j_))))
- `rpow-star-add` — forall([x_, s_, t_], x_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies t_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, s_ + t_) = rpow-star(x_, s_) * rpow-star(x_, t_))
- `rpow-star-in-rr` — forall([x_, s_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, s_) in rr)
- `rpow-star-mono-base` — forall([x_, y_, s_], x_ in rr implies y_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies x_ < y_ implies 0 < s_ implies rpow-star(x_, s_) < rpow-star(y_, s_))
- `rpow-star-mono-exp` — forall([x_, s_, t_], x_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies t_ in rr implies 1 < x_ implies s_ < t_ implies rpow-star(x_, s_) < rpow-star(x_, t_))
- `rpow-star-mul-base` — forall([x_, y_, s_], x_ in rr implies y_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies 0 < y_ implies rpow-star(x_ * y_, s_) = rpow-star(x_, s_) * rpow-star(y_, s_))
- `rpow-star-nat` — forall([cpsk_ in nn, cpsx_ in rr], 0 < cpsx_ implies rpow-star(cpsx_, cpsk_) = cpsx_ ^ cpsk_)
- `rpow-star-neg` — forall([x_, s_], x_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, -s_) = recip-star(rpow-star(x_, s_)))
- `rpow-star-nonneg-in-rr` — forall([cpsx_ in rr], 0 <= cpsx_ implies forall([cpss_ in rr], 0 <= cpss_ implies rpow-star(cpsx_, cpss_) in rr))
- `rpow-star-nonneg-nonneg` — forall([cpsx_ in rr], 0 <= cpsx_ implies forall([cpss_ in rr], 0 <= cpss_ implies 0 <= rpow-star(cpsx_, cpss_)))
- `rpow-star-one` — forall([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, 1) = x_)
- `rpow-star-pos` — forall([x_, s_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies 0 < rpow-star(x_, s_))
- `rpow-star-pow` — forall([x_, s_, t_], x_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies t_ in rr implies 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(rpow-star(x_, s_), t_) = rpow-star(x_, s_ * t_))
- `rpow-star-root-power` — forall([cpsk_ in nn], 1 <= cpsk_ implies forall([cpsx_ in rr], 0 < cpsx_ implies rpow-star(cpsx_, recip-star(cpsk_)) ^ cpsk_ = cpsx_))
- `rpow-star-unfold` — forall([x_, s_], rpow-star(x_, s_) == if(0 < x_, r-exp(s_ * log(x_)), if(0 < s_, 0, 1)))
- `rpow-star-value` — forall([x_, s_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, s_) = r-exp(s_ * log(x_)))
- `rpow-star-zero` — forall([x_ in rr], 0 < x_ implies rpow-star(x_, 0) = 1)
- `rpow-star-zero-base` — forall([s_ in rr], 0 < s_ implies rpow-star(0, s_) = 0)
- `rpow-star-zero-zero` — rpow-star(0, 0) = 1
- `rq-add-computes` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (add(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)))(class(ringoid-setoid(r), a), class(ringoid-setoid(r), b)) = class(ringoid-setoid(r), (add(r))(a, b))))
- `rq-mul-computes` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a, b], a in carr(r) implies b in carr(r) implies (mul(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)))(class(ringoid-setoid(r), a), class(ringoid-setoid(r), b)) = class(ringoid-setoid(r), (mul(r))(a, b))))
- `rq-neg-computes` — forall([r], is-ringoid(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (neg(ringoid-quotient-ring(r)))(class(ringoid-setoid(r), a)) = class(ringoid-setoid(r), (neg(r))(a))))
- `rr-abs-bound` — forall([x in rr, c in rr], abs(x) <= c iff -c <= x and x <= c)
- `rr-abs-cases` — forall([x in rr], 0 <= x and abs(x) = x or not(0 <= x) and abs(x) = -x)
- `rr-abs-closed` — forall([a in rr], abs(a) in rr)
- `rr-abs-le-of-parts` — forall([rqx_ in rr, rqc_ in rr], -rqc_ <= rqx_ implies rqx_ <= rqc_ implies abs(rqx_) <= rqc_)
- `rr-abs-le-parts` — forall([rqx_ in rr, rqc_ in rr], abs(rqx_) <= rqc_ implies -rqc_ <= rqx_ and rqx_ <= rqc_)
- `rr-abs-lt-of-parts` — forall([rqx_ in rr, rqc_ in rr], -rqc_ < rqx_ implies rqx_ < rqc_ implies abs(rqx_) < rqc_)
- `rr-abs-lt-parts` — forall([rqx_ in rr, rqc_ in rr], abs(rqx_) < rqc_ implies -rqc_ < rqx_ and rqx_ < rqc_)
- `rr-abs-mult` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies abs(a * b) = abs(a) * abs(b))
- `rr-abs-neg` — forall([a in rr], abs(-a) = abs(a))
- `rr-abs-nonneg` — forall([a in rr], 0 <= abs(a))
- `rr-abs-of-neg` — forall([x in rr], x < 0 implies abs(x) = -x)
- `rr-abs-of-nonneg` — forall([x in rr], 0 <= x implies abs(x) = x)
- `rr-abs-of-nonpos` — forall([x in rr], x <= 0 implies abs(x) = -x)
- `rr-abs-power` — forall([k in nn, r in rr], abs(r ^ k) = abs(r) ^ k)
- `rr-abs-prod-bound` — forall([u, v, p, q, m, k, t, e], u in rr implies v in rr implies p in rr implies q in rr implies m in rr implies k in rr implies t in rr implies e in rr implies ((abs(u) <= m and abs(q) <= k) and abs(u - p) <= t and abs(v - q) <= t) and (m + k) * t <= e implies abs(u * v - p * q) <= e)
- `rr-abs-reverse-triangle` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], abs(abs(x) - abs(y)) <= abs(x - y))
- `rr-abs-sub-sym` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies abs(u - v) = abs(v - u))
- `rr-abs-sum-bound` — forall([u, v, p, q, h_, e], u in rr implies v in rr implies p in rr implies q in rr implies h_ in rr implies e in rr implies (abs(u - p) <= h_ and abs(v - q) <= h_) and h_ + h_ = e implies abs(u + v - (p + q)) <= e)
- `rr-abs-triangle` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies abs(a + b) <= abs(a) + abs(b))
- `rr-abs-triangle-c` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies abs(a + b) <= abs(a) + abs(b))
- `rr-abs-unit-scale` — forall([s_ in rr, t_ in rr], 0 < s_ implies s_ < 1 implies abs(s_ * t_) <= abs(t_))
- `rr-abs-zero` — forall([a in rr], abs(a) = 0 iff a = 0)
- `rr-abs-zero-value` — abs(0) = 0
- `rr-add-in-rr` — forall([a_, b_], a_ in rr implies b_ in rr implies a_ + b_ in rr)
- `rr-add-nonneg` — forall([x, y], x in rr implies y in rr implies 0 <= x and 0 <= y implies 0 <= x + y)
- `rr-additive-ag-iden` — iden(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field)) == 0
- `rr-additive-ag-opr` — opr(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_)
- `rr-amgm-2` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], 4 * (a * b) <= (a + b) * (a + b))
- `rr-archimedean-multiple` — forall([ev_], pos-rr(ev_) implies forall([xv_ in rr], forsome([nv_ in nn], xv_ <= nv_ * ev_)))
- `rr-bernoulli` — forall([x in rr, n in nn], -1 <= x implies 1 + n * x <= (1 + x) ^ n)
- `rr-bernoulli-ind` — forall([n in nn, x in rr], -1 <= x implies 1 + n * x <= (1 + x) ^ n)
- `rr-bolzano-weierstrass` — forall([hqv_ in fun(nn, rr), bdv_ in rr], forall([iqv_ in nn], abs(hqv_(iqv_)) <= bdv_) implies forsome([phv_], strictly-mono-nn(phv_) and forsome([ptv_ in rr], converges-to(rr-ms, subseq(hqv_, phv_), ptv_))))
- `rr-bounded-equivalent` — is-continuous(rr-ms, rr-bounded-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, x)) and is-continuous(rr-bounded-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, x))
- `rr-bounded-ms-bounded` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], (dist(rr-bounded-ms))(x, y) < 1)
- `rr-bounded-ms-is-metric-space` — is-metric-space(rr-bounded-ms)
- `rr-cancel-mul-left` — forall([c in rr, u in rr, v in rr], not(c = 0) implies c * u = c * v implies u = v)
- `rr-cancel-mul-right` — forall([u, v, c], u in rr implies v in rr implies c in rr implies not(c = 0) implies u * c = v * c implies u = v)
- `rr-cauchy-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([m_ in nn, p_ in nn], n_ <= m_ implies n_ <= p_ implies abs(f(m_) - f(p_)) <= eps))) implies converges(rr-ms, f))
- `rr-cauchy-schwarz-2` — forall([a1 in rr, a2 in rr, b1 in rr, b2 in rr], (a1 * b1 + a2 * b2) * (a1 * b1 + a2 * b2) <= (a1 * a1 + a2 * a2) * (b1 * b1 + b2 * b2))
- `rr-complete` — is-complete(rr-ms)
- `rr-const-converges-to` — forall([c in rr], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(n, nn, c), c))
- `rr-converges-ptwise-eq` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_)) implies converges(rr-ms, f) implies converges(rr-ms, h))
- `rr-converges-to-abs` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr], forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies abs(f(k_) - lv) <= eps))) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv))
- `rr-diff-split-mid` — forall([x_ in rr, y_ in rr, z_ in rr], x_ - z_ = x_ - y_ + y_ - z_)
- `rr-diff-zero-eq` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], 0 = u - v implies u = v)
- `rr-double-nonneg` — forall([x in rr], 0 <= x + x implies 0 <= x)
- `rr-factor-shift` — forall([d_ in rr, u_ in rr, r_ in rr], (d_ * r_) * (u_ - 0) = d_ * (u_ * r_))
- `rr-half-ball-nonzero` — forall([c in rr, m in rr, y_ in rr], 0 < m implies m + m = abs(c) implies abs(y_ - c) <= m implies not(y_ = 0))
- `rr-halving-identity` — forall([b in rr], 1 - 2 * b + b = 1 - b)
- `rr-interval-avoids-sequence` — forall([zse_ in fun(nn, rr), zp_ in rr, zq_ in rr], zp_ < zq_ implies forsome([zy_ in rr], zp_ < zy_ and zy_ < zq_ and forall([n in nn], not(zse_(n) = zy_))))
- `rr-interval-seq-has-convergent-subseq` — forall([lov_ in rr, hiv_ in rr, hqv_ in fun(nn, ccint(lov_, hiv_))], forsome([phv_], strictly-mono-nn(phv_) and forsome([ptv_ in rr], converges-to(rr-ms, subseq(hqv_, phv_), ptv_))))
- `rr-is-metric-space` — is-metric-space(rr-ms)
- `rr-is-normed-field` — is-normed-field(rr-normed-field)
- `rr-le-abs` — forall([x in rr], x <= abs(x))
- `rr-le-add` — forall([x, y, u, v], x in rr implies y in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies x <= y and u <= v implies x + u <= y + v)
- `rr-le-all-pos-nonpos` — forall([x in rr], forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies x <= eps) implies x <= 0)
- `rr-le-cases` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u <= v implies u < v or u = v)
- `rr-le-diff-nonpos` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u <= v implies u - v <= 0)
- `rr-le-from-diff-nonneg` — forall([x, y], x in rr implies y in rr implies 0 <= y - x implies x <= y)
- `rr-le-lt-trans` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x <= y and y < z implies x < z)
- `rr-le-max-left` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], x <= max(x, y))
- `rr-le-max-right` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], y <= max(x, y))
- `rr-le-min` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], z <= x implies z <= y implies z <= min(x, y))
- `rr-le-ne-lt` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u <= v and not(u = v) implies u < v)
- `rr-le-neg` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u <= v implies -v <= -u)
- `rr-le-of-le-add-all-pos` — forall([a_ in rr, b_ in rr], forall([d_], pos-rr(d_) implies a_ <= b_ + d_) implies a_ <= b_)
- `rr-le-of-sq-le` — forall([t_, s_], t_ in rr implies s_ in rr implies 0 <= s_ implies t_ * t_ <= s_ * s_ implies t_ <= s_)
- `rr-le-scale-nonneg` — forall([c, x, y], c in rr implies x in rr implies y in rr implies 0 <= c and x <= y implies c * x <= c * y)
- `rr-le-scale-nonneg-right` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, c in rr], 0 <= c implies x <= y implies x * c <= y * c)
- `rr-le-total` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u <= v or v <= u)
- `rr-le-trans` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x <= y and y <= z implies x <= z)
- `rr-le-trans-c` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x <= y implies y <= z implies x <= z)
- `rr-limit-abs-le` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, bd in rr], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies forall([n_ in nn], abs(f(n_)) <= bd) implies abs(lv) <= bd)
- `rr-limit-add` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, mv in rr], forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_) + g(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, mv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, lv + mv))
- `rr-limit-in-ccint` — forall([lo_ in rr, hi_ in rr, q_ in fun(nn, ccint(lo_, hi_)), pt_ in rr], converges-to(rr-ms, q_, pt_) implies pt_ in ccint(lo_, hi_))
- `rr-limit-le` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, mv in rr], forall([n_ in nn], f(n_) <= g(n_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, mv) implies lv <= mv)
- `rr-limit-le-of-tail` — forall([f, lv, c, nt_], f in fun(nn, rr) implies lv in rr implies c in rr implies nt_ in nn implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies forall([k_ in nn], nt_ <= k_ implies f(k_) <= c) implies lv <= c)
- `rr-limit-ptwise-eq` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr], forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, lv))
- `rr-limit-scale` — forall([c in rr, f in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr], forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_) * c) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, lv * c))
- `rr-limit-sub` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, mv in rr], forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_) - g(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, mv) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, lv - mv))
- `rr-limit-tail-abs-le` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, v_ in rr, c in rr, n_ in nn], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies forall([k in nn], n_ <= k implies abs(f(k) - v_) <= c) implies abs(lv - v_) <= c)
- `rr-limit-tail-le` — forall([f, lv, c], f in fun(nn, rr) implies lv in rr implies c in rr implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies pos-rr(c) implies forsome([nt_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], nt_ <= k_ implies f(k_) <= lv + c)))
- `rr-limit-unique` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), lm1 in rr, lm2 in rr], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lm1) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, lm2) implies lm1 = lm2)
- `rr-lt-add` — forall([x, y, u, v], x in rr implies y in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies x < y and u <= v implies x + u < y + v)
- `rr-lt-asymm` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies not(b < a))
- `rr-lt-diff-neg` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u < v implies u - v < 0)
- `rr-lt-diff-pos` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u < v implies 0 < v - u)
- `rr-lt-from-diff-pos` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], 0 < y - x implies x < y)
- `rr-lt-implies-le` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], x < y implies x <= y)
- `rr-lt-irrefl` — forall([a in rr], not(a < a))
- `rr-lt-le-trans` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x < y and y <= z implies x < z)
- `rr-lt-min` — forall([osc_, osy_, osb_], osc_ in rr implies osy_ in rr implies osb_ in rr implies osc_ < osy_ implies osc_ < osb_ implies osc_ < min(osy_, osb_))
- `rr-lt-not-le` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies not(b <= a))
- `rr-lt-of-pos-rr` — forall([t], pos-rr(t) implies 0 < t)
- `rr-lt-scale-pos` — forall([c, x, y], c in rr implies x in rr implies y in rr implies 0 < c and x < y implies c * x < c * y)
- `rr-lt-trans` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x < y and y < z implies x < z)
- `rr-lt-trichotomy` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u < v or u = v or v < u)
- `rr-magnitude-is-abs` — forall([a in rr], magnitude(a) = abs(a))
- `rr-max-cases` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], max(x, y) = x or max(x, y) = y)
- `rr-max-closed` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], max(x, y) in rr)
- `rr-max-le` — forall([x in rr, y in rr, z in rr], x <= z implies y <= z implies max(x, y) <= z)
- `rr-max-left-le` — forall([osy_, osb_], osy_ in rr implies osb_ in rr implies osb_ <= osy_ implies max(osy_, osb_) = osy_)
- `rr-max-lipschitz` — forall([u in rr, v in rr, c in rr], abs(max(u, c) - max(v, c)) <= abs(u - v))
- `rr-max-lt` — forall([osy_, osb_, osc_], osy_ in rr implies osb_ in rr implies osc_ in rr implies osy_ < osc_ implies osb_ < osc_ implies max(osy_, osb_) < osc_)
- `rr-max-retract-close` — forall([osy_, osc_], osy_ in rr implies osc_ in rr implies abs(osc_ - max(osy_, osc_)) <= abs(osc_ - osy_))
- `rr-max-right-le` — forall([osy_, osb_], osy_ in rr implies osb_ in rr implies osy_ <= osb_ implies max(osy_, osb_) = osb_)
- `rr-midpoint-between` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], u < v implies forsome([w in rr], u < w and w < v))
- `rr-min-cases` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], min(x, y) = x or min(x, y) = y)
- `rr-min-closed` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], min(x, y) in rr)
- `rr-min-le-left` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], min(x, y) <= x)
- `rr-min-le-right` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], min(x, y) <= y)
- `rr-min-left-le` — forall([osy_, osb_], osy_ in rr implies osb_ in rr implies osy_ <= osb_ implies min(osy_, osb_) = osy_)
- `rr-min-lipschitz` — forall([u in rr, v in rr, c in rr], abs(min(u, c) - min(v, c)) <= abs(u - v))
- `rr-min-one-mono` — forall([a_ in rr, b_ in rr], a_ <= b_ implies min(1, a_) <= min(1, b_))
- `rr-min-one-subadditive` — forall([x in rr, y in rr], 0 <= x implies 0 <= y implies min(1, x + y) <= min(1, x) + min(1, y))
- `rr-min-pos` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies 0 < u implies 0 < v implies forsome([w_ in rr], 0 < w_ and w_ <= u and w_ <= v))
- `rr-min-retract-close` — forall([osy_, osc_], osy_ in rr implies osc_ in rr implies abs(osc_ - min(osy_, osc_)) <= abs(osc_ - osy_))
- `rr-min-right-le` — forall([osy_, osb_], osy_ in rr implies osb_ in rr implies osb_ <= osy_ implies min(osy_, osb_) = osb_)
- `rr-mono-le-limit` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([k in nn], f(k) <= f(succ(k))) implies forall([lm_], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lm_) implies forall([m in nn], f(m) <= lm_)))
- `rr-ms-dist` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies (dist(rr-ms))(u_, v_) == abs(u_ - v_))
- `rr-mul-in-rr` — forall([a_, b_], a_ in rr implies b_ in rr implies a_ * b_ in rr)
- `rr-mul-le-cancel-pos` — forall([pula_ in rr, pulc_ in rr, pulm_ in rr], 0 < pulc_ implies pula_ * pulc_ <= pulm_ * pulc_ implies pula_ <= pulm_)
- `rr-mul-le-right` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, c in rr], a <= b implies 0 <= c implies a * c <= b * c)
- `rr-mul-neg-neg-pos` — forall([osy_, osb_], osy_ in rr implies osb_ in rr implies osy_ < 0 implies osb_ < 0 implies 0 < osy_ * osb_)
- `rr-mul-one-mid` — forall([u in rr], (u * 1) * 1 = u)
- `rr-mul-pos` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], 0 < a implies 0 < b implies 0 < a * b)
- `rr-mul-recip-cancel` — forall([a_ in rr, n_ in rr], not(n_ = 0) implies a_ = (a_ * n_) * recip(n_))
- `rr-mul-rotate` — forall([s_ in rr, u_ in rr, r_ in rr], (u_ * s_) * r_ = s_ * (u_ * r_))
- `rr-mul-zero` — forall([a in rr], a * 0 = 0)
- `rr-mul-zero-mid` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], (u * 0) * v = 0)
- `rr-neg-abs-le` — forall([x in rr], -abs(x) <= x)
- `rr-neg-eq-zero` — forall([u in rr], -u = 0 implies u = 0)
- `rr-nested-intervals` — forall([zlo_ in fun(nn, rr), zhi_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([k in nn], zlo_(k) <= zlo_(succ(k))) implies forall([k in nn], zhi_(succ(k)) <= zhi_(k)) implies forall([k in nn], zlo_(k) <= zhi_(k)) implies forsome([zy_ in rr], forall([n in nn], zlo_(n) <= zy_ and zy_ <= zhi_(n))))
- `rr-nf-add` — add(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_)
- `rr-nf-add-apply` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies (add(rr-normed-field))(u_, v_) == u_ + v_)
- `rr-nf-carr` — carr(rr-normed-field) == rr
- `rr-nf-fnrm` — fnrm(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, abs(x_))
- `rr-nf-fnrm-apply` — forall([u_ in rr], (fnrm(rr-normed-field))(u_) == abs(u_))
- `rr-nf-fun-class` — fun(rr, carr(rr-normed-field)) == fun(rr, rr)
- `rr-nf-mul` — mul(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_)
- `rr-nf-mul-apply` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies (mul(rr-normed-field))(u_, v_) == u_ * v_)
- `rr-nf-neg` — neg(rr-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_)
- `rr-nf-neg-apply` — forall([u_ in rr], (neg(rr-normed-field))(u_) == -u_)
