- `rr-nf-small-elements` — forall([d2e_], pos-rr(d2e_) implies forsome([d2h_ in carr(rr-normed-field)], not(d2h_ = zero(rr-normed-field)) and (fnrm(rr-normed-field))(d2h_) < d2e_))
- `rr-nf-sub` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in rr implies v_ in rr implies (add(rr-normed-field))(u_, (neg(rr-normed-field))(v_)) == u_ - v_)
- `rr-nf-zero` — zero(rr-normed-field) == 0
- `rr-no-zero-divisors` — forall([a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies a * b = 0 implies a = 0 or b = 0)
- `rr-nonneg-cancel-pos` — forall([c, d], c in rr implies d in rr implies 0 < c implies 0 <= c * d implies 0 <= d)
- `rr-nonneg-has-zero` — iden(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field)) in {t_ in rr: 0 <= t_}
- `rr-nonneg-opr-closed` — forall([x_ in {t_ in rr: 0 <= t_}, y_ in {t_ in rr: 0 <= t_}], (opr(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field)))(x_, y_) in {t_ in rr: 0 <= t_})
- `rr-not-le-lt` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], not(a <= b) implies b < a)
- `rr-null-scale` — forall([c in rr, f in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], 0 <= c implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, 0) implies forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = c * f(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, 0))
- `rr-null-squeeze` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], converges-to(rr-ms, f, 0) implies forall([j_ in nn], 0 <= h(j_) and h(j_) <= f(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, 0))
- `rr-null-sum` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], converges-to(rr-ms, f, 0) implies converges-to(rr-ms, g, 0) implies forall([j_ in nn], h(j_) = f(j_) + g(j_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, h, 0))
- `rr-nvs-is-normed-vector-space` — is-normed-vector-space(rr-nvs)
- `rr-one-plus-nonneg-pos` — forall([d in rr], 0 <= d implies 0 < 1 + d)
- `rr-open-in-nf` — is-open(nf-metric-space(rr-normed-field), rr)
- `rr-pos-halvable` — forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([d], pos-rr(d) and d + d = eps))
- `rr-pos-ne-zero` — forall([c in rr], 0 < c implies not(c = 0))
- `rr-pos-rr-in-rr` — forall([t], pos-rr(t) implies t in rr)
- `rr-pos-rr-of-lt` — forall([x in rr], 0 < x implies pos-rr(x))
- `rr-pos-shrink` — forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([d], pos-rr(d) and d < eps))
- `rr-pos-star-below-pos-inf` — forall([x in rr-pos-star], x <= pos-inf)
- `rr-pos-star-is-comm-monoid` — is-comm-monoid(rr-pos-star-add-monoid)
- `rr-pos-star-is-set` — rr-pos-star in set
- `rr-pos-star-le-antisymm` — forall([x, y], x in rr-pos-star and y in rr-pos-star implies x <= y and y <= x implies x = y)
- `rr-pos-star-le-real-in-rr` — forall([x, y], x in rr-pos-star and y in rr implies x <= y implies x in rr)
- `rr-pos-star-le-refl` — forall([x in rr-pos-star], x <= x)
- `rr-pos-star-le-trans` — forall([x, y, z], x in rr-pos-star and y in rr-pos-star and z in rr-pos-star implies x <= y and y <= z implies x <= z)
- `rr-pos-star-nonneg` — forall([x in rr-pos-star], 0 <= x)
- `rr-pos-star-subset-rr-star` — rr-pos-star subset rr-star
- `rr-power-le-one` — forall([k in nn, r in rr], 0 <= r and r <= 1 implies r ^ k <= 1)
- `rr-power-mono-base` — forall([cpsk_ in nn, cpsx_ in rr, cpsy_ in rr], 0 <= cpsx_ implies cpsx_ <= cpsy_ implies cpsx_ ^ cpsk_ <= cpsy_ ^ cpsk_)
- `rr-power-mul-base` — forall([cpsk_ in nn, cpsx_ in rr, cpsy_ in rr], (cpsx_ * cpsy_) ^ cpsk_ = cpsx_ ^ cpsk_ * cpsy_ ^ cpsk_)
- `rr-power-nonneg` — forall([k in nn, r in rr], 0 <= r implies 0 <= r ^ k)
- `rr-power-one` — forall([k_ in nn], 1 ^ k_ = 1)
- `rr-power-pos` — forall([d in rr, n in nn], 0 < d implies 0 < d ^ n)
- `rr-power-power` — forall([cpsk_ in nn, cpsm_ in nn, cpsx_ in rr], (cpsx_ ^ cpsk_) ^ cpsm_ = (cpsx_ ^ cpsm_) ^ cpsk_)
- `rr-power-shrink-bound` — forall([k in nn, r in rr], 0 <= r and r <= 1 implies (k * (1 - r)) * r ^ k <= 1)
- `rr-prod-le-prod` — forall([a, b, u, v], a in rr implies b in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies (0 <= a and a <= b) and 0 <= u and u <= v implies a * u <= b * v)
- `rr-prod-nonpos-neg` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], v < 0 and u * v <= 0 implies 0 <= u)
- `rr-prod-nonpos-pos` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], 0 < v and u * v <= 0 implies u <= 0)
- `rr-qm-am-2` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], (a + b) * (a + b) <= 2 * (a * a + b * b))
- `rr-recip-antitone` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], 0 < u implies u <= v implies recip(v) <= recip(u))
- `rr-recip-cancel-right` — forall([a_ in rr, n_ in rr], not(n_ = 0) implies a_ = (a_ * recip(n_)) * n_)
- `rr-recip-factorial` — forall([n in nn], factorial(n) * recip(factorial(n)) = 1)
- `rr-recip-one` — recip(1) = 1
- `rr-recip-pos` — forall([a in rr], 0 < a implies 0 < recip(a))
- `rr-recip-recip-pos` — forall([c_ in rr], 0 < c_ implies recip(recip(c_)) = c_)
- `rr-recip-solve` — forall([c, u, v], c in rr implies u in rr implies v in rr implies not(c = 0) implies u = c * v implies v = recip(c) * u)
- `rr-recip-succ-converges-to-zero` — converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, recip(k + 1)), 0)
- `rr-scalar-ring-add` — add(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ + y_)
- `rr-scalar-ring-carr` — carr(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == rr
- `rr-scalar-ring-mul` — mul(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr, rr), x_ * y_)
- `rr-scalar-ring-neg` — neg(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == vnb-lambda(x_, rr, -x_)
- `rr-scalar-ring-one` — one(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == 1
- `rr-scalar-ring-zero` — zero(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field)) == 0
- `rr-scale-eps` — forall([c in rr, eps in rr], 0 <= c implies pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([d], pos-rr(d) and forall([t in rr], 0 <= t implies t <= d implies c * t <= eps)))
- `rr-scaled-inverse-unique` — forall([a in rr, u in rr, v in rr], a * u = 1 implies (2 * a) * v = 1 implies u = 2 * v)
- `rr-sq-add-le` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], (u + v) * (u + v) <= (u * u + u * u) + (v * v + v * v))
- `rr-sq-nonneg` — forall([x in rr], 0 <= x * x)
- `rr-sub-in-rr` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies u - v in rr)
- `rr-sub-ne-zero` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], not(u = v) implies not(u - v = 0))
- `rr-subset-nf-pts` — rr subset pts(nf-metric-space(rr-normed-field))
- `rr-subset-rr-star` — rr subset rr-star
- `rr-sup-approx` — forall([s_, d_], s_ subset rr implies forsome([x_], x_ in s_) implies rr-bounded-above(s_) implies d_ in rr implies 0 < d_ implies forsome([w_ in s_], sup(s_) - d_ < w_))
- `rr-upper-of-two` — forall([u, v], u in rr implies v in rr implies forsome([w_ in rr], u <= w_ and v <= w_))
- `rr-upper-of-two-below` — forall([u, v, m], u in rr implies v in rr implies m in rr implies u < m implies v < m implies forsome([w_ in rr], u <= w_ and v <= w_ and w_ < m))
- `rr-young-2` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a * b <= (/)(a * a + b * b, 2))
- `rr-zero-lt-one` — 0 < 1
- `rr-zero-lt-two` — 0 < 2
- `rr-zero-mul` — forall([v in rr], 0 * v = 0)
- `rr-zero-plus` — forall([t_ in rr], 0 + t_ = t_)
- `scale-continuous-at` — forall([c in rr, f, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * f(x)), a))
- `scale-lam-in-fun` — forall([c, f], c in rr implies f in fun(rr, rr) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, c * f(x)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `seg-curve-apply` — forall([m, f, a, eta, t_ in rr], (seg-curve(m, f, a, eta))(t_) == f((vadd(m))(a, (act(m))(t_, eta))))
- `seg-curve-diff-at` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies forall([f in fun(vec(m), rr), b_ in vec(m), eta in vec(m), s_ in rr, k_], is-dir-diff-at(m, f, (vadd(m))(b_, (act(m))(s_, eta)), eta, k_) implies is-diff-at(seg-curve(m, f, b_, eta), s_, k_)))
- `seg-curve-in-fun` — forall([m, f, a, eta], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(vec(m), rr) implies a in vec(m) implies eta in vec(m) implies seg-curve(m, f, a, eta) in fun(rr, rr))
- `seg-deriv-bridge` — forall([goua_, goub_, gouw_], goub_ - goua_ = gouw_ implies seg-deriv(goua_, goub_) == vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), gouw_))
- `seg-image-insert` — forall([n_ in nn, b_, g_ in bijection(ord-segment(succ(n_)), b_)], b_ = union(image(g_, ord-segment(n_)), pair(g_(n_), g_(n_))))
- `seg-image-omits-top` — forall([n_ in nn, b_, g_ in bijection(ord-segment(succ(n_)), b_)], not(g_(n_) in image(g_, ord-segment(n_))))
- `seg-image-subset` — forall([n_ in nn, b_, g_ in bijection(ord-segment(succ(n_)), b_), w_ in image(g_, ord-segment(n_))], w_ in b_)
- `seg-int-abs-bound` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc], trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1) subset u implies forall([goum_ in rr], forall([gouz_ in trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1)], magnitude(f(gouz_)) <= goum_) implies magnitude(seg-int(f, goua_, goub_)) <= magnitude(goub_ - goua_) * goum_)))
- `seg-int-bridge` — forall([f, goua_, goub_, goup_, gouw_], goua_ = goup_ implies goub_ - goua_ = gouw_ implies seg-int(f, goua_, goub_) == line-int(f, vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), goup_ + psx_ * gouw_), vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), gouw_), 0, 1))
- `seg-int-congruence` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goxw_, goxg_ in fun(goxw_, cc), goxa_ in cc, goxb_ in cc], trace(seg-path(goxa_, goxb_), 0, 1) subset u implies trace(seg-path(goxa_, goxb_), 0, 1) subset goxw_ implies forall([goxz_ in trace(seg-path(goxa_, goxb_), 0, 1)], goxg_(goxz_) == f(goxz_)) implies seg-int(goxg_, goxa_, goxb_) = seg-int(f, goxa_, goxb_)))
- `seg-int-const` — forall([u], is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u) implies forall([goup_ in cc, gouw_ in cc, goug_ in fun(u, cc)], forall([gouz_ in u], goug_(gouz_) == goup_ + 0 * (gouz_ - gouw_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc], trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1) subset u implies seg-int(goug_, goua_, goub_) = goup_ * (goub_ - goua_))))
- `seg-int-degenerate` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([gouz_ in u], seg-int(f, gouz_, gouz_) = 0))
- `seg-int-in-cc` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc], trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1) subset u implies seg-int(f, goua_, goub_) in cc))
- `seg-int-reverse-at` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc], trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1) subset u implies seg-int(f, goub_, goua_) = -1 * seg-int(f, goua_, goub_)))
- `seg-int-split-at` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc], trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1) subset u implies forall([gouc_ in trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1)], seg-int(f, goua_, goub_) = seg-int(f, goua_, gouc_) + seg-int(f, gouc_, goub_))))
- `seg-int-sum` — forall([u, f, g], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies g in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), g, rgy_)) implies forall([gouh_ in fun(u, cc)], forall([gouz_ in u], gouh_(gouz_) == f(gouz_) + g(gouz_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc], trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1) subset u implies seg-int(gouh_, goua_, goub_) = seg-int(f, goua_, goub_) + seg-int(g, goua_, goub_))))
- `seg-int-unfold` — forall([f, goua_, goub_], seg-int(f, goua_, goub_) == line-int(f, seg-path(goua_, goub_), seg-deriv(goua_, goub_), 0, 1))
- `seg-mem-lt` — forall([n_ in nn, j_ in nn], j_ in ord-segment(n_) iff j_ < n_)
- `seg-mem-succ-le` — forall([n_ in nn, j_ in nn], j_ in ord-segment(succ(n_)) iff j_ <= n_)
- `seg-path-apply` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gout_ in ccint(0, 1)], (seg-path(goua_, goub_))(gout_) = goua_ + gout_ * (goub_ - goua_))
- `seg-path-bridge` — forall([goua_, goub_, goup_, gouw_], goua_ = goup_ implies goub_ - goua_ = gouw_ implies seg-path(goua_, goub_) == vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), goup_ + psx_ * gouw_))
- `seg-path-is-road` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc], is-road(seg-path(goua_, goub_), seg-deriv(goua_, goub_), 0, 1))
- `seg-perm-restrict-is-bijection` — forall([n in nn, phi in bijection(ord-segment(succ(n)), ord-segment(succ(n))), k], phi(n) = k implies vnb-lambda(i_, ord-segment(n), if(phi(i_) = n, k, phi(i_))) in bijection(ord-segment(n), ord-segment(n)))
- `seg-restrict-is-bijection` — forall([n_ in nn, b_, g_ in bijection(ord-segment(succ(n_)), b_)], vnb-lambda(i_, ord-segment(n_), g_(i_)) in bijection(ord-segment(n_), image(g_, ord-segment(n_))))
- `seg-shift-is-road` — forall([p in cc, q in cc, k in rr, m in rr], m = k + 1 implies is-road(vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(k, m), (seg-path(p, q))(trs_ - k)), vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(k, m), (seg-deriv(p, q))(trs_ - k)), k, m))
- `seg-shift-length` — forall([p in cc, q in cc, k in rr, m in rr], m = k + 1 implies road-length(vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(k, m), (seg-deriv(p, q))(trs_ - k)), k, m) = magnitude(q - p))
- `seg-shift-line-int` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([p in cc, q in cc, k in rr, m in rr], m = k + 1 implies trace(seg-path(p, q), 0, 1) subset u implies line-int(f, vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(k, m), (seg-path(p, q))(trs_ - k)), vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(k, m), (seg-deriv(p, q))(trs_ - k)), k, m) = line-int(f, seg-path(p, q), seg-deriv(p, q), 0, 1)))
- `seg-shift-trace` — forall([p in cc, q in cc, k in rr, m in rr], m = k + 1 implies trace(vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(k, m), (seg-path(p, q))(trs_ - k)), k, m) = trace(seg-path(p, q), 0, 1))
- `seg-trace-bridge` — forall([goua_, goub_, goup_, gouw_], goua_ = goup_ implies goub_ - goua_ = gouw_ implies trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1) == trace(vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), goup_ + psx_ * gouw_), 0, 1))
- `seg-trace-elim` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouz_ in trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1)], forsome([gout_ in ccint(0, 1)], gouz_ = goua_ + gout_ * (goub_ - goua_)))
- `seg-trace-in-conv3` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1) subset conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) and trace(seg-path(goub_, gouc_), 0, 1) subset conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) and trace(seg-path(gouc_, goua_), 0, 1) subset conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_))
- `seg-trace-in-convex` — forall([u], is-convex(u) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc], goua_ in u implies goub_ in u implies trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1) subset u))
- `seg-trace-intro` — forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gout_ in ccint(0, 1)], goua_ + gout_ * (goub_ - goua_) in trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1))
- `seg0-empty` — empty-set = ord-segment(0)
- `segment-int-abs-bound` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([pz0_ in cc, pzw_ in cc], trace(vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pz0_ + psx_ * pzw_), 0, 1) subset u implies forall([lrpn_ in rr], forall([lrpy_ in trace(vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pz0_ + psx_ * pzw_), 0, 1)], magnitude(f(lrpy_)) <= lrpn_) implies magnitude(line-int(f, vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pz0_ + psx_ * pzw_), vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pzw_), 0, 1)) <= magnitude(pzw_) * lrpn_)))
- `segment-int-reverse` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([pz0_ in cc, pzw_ in cc], trace(vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pz0_ + psx_ * pzw_), 0, 1) subset u implies line-int(f, vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), (pz0_ + pzw_) + psx_ * (-1 * pzw_)), vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), -1 * pzw_), 0, 1) = -1 * line-int(f, vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pz0_ + psx_ * pzw_), vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pzw_), 0, 1)))
- `segment-int-split` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([pz0_ in cc, pzw_ in cc, lrpt_ in ooint(0, 1)], trace(vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pz0_ + psx_ * pzw_), 0, 1) subset u implies line-int(f, vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pz0_ + psx_ * pzw_), vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pzw_), 0, 1) = line-int(f, vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pz0_ + psx_ * (lrpt_ * pzw_)), vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), lrpt_ * pzw_), 0, 1) + line-int(f, vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), (pz0_ + lrpt_ * pzw_) + psx_ * ((1 - lrpt_) * pzw_)), vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), (1 - lrpt_) * pzw_), 0, 1)))
- `segment-is-road` — forall([pz0_, pzw_], pz0_ in cc implies pzw_ in cc implies is-road(vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pz0_ + psx_ * pzw_), vnb-lambda(psx_, ccint(0, 1), pzw_), 0, 1))
- `seq-compact-countable-product` — forall([ms], is-ms-sequence(ms) implies forall([n in nn], seq-compact(ms(n))) implies seq-compact(product-metric(ms)))
- `seq-compact-implies-compact` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies seq-compact(s) implies is-compact(s))
- `seq-compact-implies-totally-bounded` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies seq-compact(s) implies totally-bounded(s))
- `seq-limit-converges-to` — forall([f], converges(rr-ms, f) implies converges-to(rr-ms, f, seq-limit(f)))
- `seq-limit-in-rr` — forall([f], seq-limit(f) in rr)
- `seq-limit-value` — forall([f, lv], converges-to(rr-ms, f, lv) implies seq-limit(f) = lv)
- `sequential-implies-continuous-at` — forall([s, t, f, a], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(t) implies f in fun(pts(s), pts(t)) implies a in pts(s) implies forall([sq in sqn(pts(s))], converges-to(s, sq, a) implies converges-to(t, compose(f, sq), f(a))) implies is-continuous-at(s, t, f, a))
- `series-abs-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([j in nn], g(j) = abs(f(j))) implies series-converges(g) implies series-converges(f))
- `series-abs-triangle` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([j in nn], g(j) = abs(f(j))) implies abs(series-partial-sum(f, k)) <= series-partial-sum(g, k))
- `series-abs-triangle-block` — forall([d in nn, m in nn, f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([j in nn], g(j) = abs(f(j))) implies abs(series-partial-sum(f, m + d) - series-partial-sum(f, m)) <= series-partial-sum(g, m + d) - series-partial-sum(g, m))
- `series-abs-triangle-le` — forall([k in nn, m in nn, f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([j in nn], g(j) = abs(f(j))) implies m <= k implies abs(series-partial-sum(f, k) - series-partial-sum(f, m)) <= series-partial-sum(g, k) - series-partial-sum(g, m))
- `series-block-le` — forall([k in nn, n_ in nn, f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= f(i_) and f(i_) <= g(i_)) implies n_ <= k implies series-partial-sum(f, k) - series-partial-sum(f, n_) <= series-partial-sum(g, k) - series-partial-sum(g, n_))
- `series-bounded-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpsk_)) implies forall([cpsb_ in rr], forall([cpsk_ in nn], series-partial-sum(f, cpsk_) <= cpsb_) implies series-converges(f)))
- `series-cauchy-criterion` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], series-converges(f) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([bnd in nn], forall([m in nn, n_ in nn], bnd <= m and m <= n_ implies abs(series-partial-sum(f, n_) - series-partial-sum(f, m)) <= eps))))
- `series-converges-sum` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= f(i_)) implies forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= g(i_)) implies forall([i_ in nn], h(i_) = f(i_) + g(i_)) implies series-converges(f) implies series-converges(g) implies series-converges(h))
- `series-converges-to-add` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, mv in rr], forall([i_ in nn], h(i_) = f(i_) + g(i_)) implies series-converges-to(f, lv) implies series-converges-to(g, mv) implies series-converges-to(h, lv + mv))
- `series-converges-to-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr], series-converges-to(f, lv) implies series-converges(f))
- `series-dominated-partial-bound` — forall([cpdk_ in nn, f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpdj_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpdj_)) implies forall([g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpdj_ in nn], 0 <= g(cpdj_)) implies forall([cpdn_ in nn], forall([cpdj_ in nn], cpdn_ <= cpdj_ implies f(cpdj_) <= g(cpdj_)) implies series-partial-sum(f, cpdk_) <= series-partial-sum(f, cpdn_) + series-partial-sum(g, cpdk_))))
- `series-eventually-dominated-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpdj_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpdj_)) implies forall([g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpdj_ in nn], 0 <= g(cpdj_)) implies forall([cpdn_ in nn], forall([cpdj_ in nn], cpdn_ <= cpdj_ implies f(cpdj_) <= g(cpdj_)) implies series-converges(g) implies series-converges(f))))
- `series-eventually-geometric-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpsk_)) implies forall([cpsq_ in rr], 0 <= cpsq_ implies cpsq_ < 1 implies forall([cpsc_ in rr], 0 <= cpsc_ implies forall([cpsn_ in nn], forall([cpsk_ in nn], cpsn_ <= cpsk_ implies f(cpsk_) <= cpsc_ * cpsq_ ^ cpsk_) implies series-converges(f)))))
- `series-frequently-large-diverges` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), cpsc_ in rr], 0 < cpsc_ implies forall([cpsn_ in nn], forsome([cpsk_ in nn], cpsn_ <= cpsk_ and cpsc_ <= f(cpsk_))) implies not(series-converges(f)))
- `series-head-null` — forall([n_ in nn, u], forall([j_ in nn], u(j_) in fun(nn, rr)) implies forall([i_ in nn], converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (u(j_))(i_)), 0)) implies forall([hd in fun(nn, rr)], forall([j_ in nn], hd(j_) = series-partial-sum(u(j_), n_)) implies converges-to(rr-ms, hd, 0)))
- `series-limit-add` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], h(i_) = f(i_) + g(i_)) implies series-converges(f) implies series-converges(g) implies series-limit(h) = series-limit(f) + series-limit(g))
- `series-limit-converges-to` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], series-converges(f) implies series-converges-to(f, series-limit(f)))
- `series-limit-in-rr` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], series-converges(f) implies series-limit(f) in rr)
- `series-limit-iota` — forall([f], iota(l, series-converges-to(f, l)) == series-limit(f))
- `series-limit-le` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), lv in rr, mv in rr], forall([n_ in nn], f(n_) <= g(n_)) implies series-converges-to(f, lv) implies series-converges-to(g, mv) implies lv <= mv)
- `series-nonneg-zero-sum` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n_ in nn], 0 <= f(n_)) implies series-converges-to(f, 0) implies forall([n_ in nn], f(n_) = 0))
- `series-partial-sum-abs-le` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, rr)], abs(series-partial-sum(f, k)) <= series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, abs(f(n_))), k))
- `series-partial-sum-add` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr), h in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], h(i_) = f(i_) + g(i_)) implies series-partial-sum(h, k) = series-partial-sum(f, k) + series-partial-sum(g, k))
- `series-partial-sum-add-ptwise` — forall([n_ in nn, f_, g_, h_], forall([k_ in nn], f_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], g_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = f_(k_) + g_(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(h_, n_) = series-partial-sum(f_, n_) + series-partial-sum(g_, n_))
- `series-partial-sum-agree-below` — forall([n in nn, tcu_, tcg_], forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < n implies tcg_(tck_) in rr) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < n implies tcu_(tck_) = tcg_(tck_)) implies series-partial-sum(tcu_, n) = series-partial-sum(tcg_, n))
- `series-partial-sum-antiderivable` — forall([n_ in nn, s_, ph_, a, b], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies ph_ in fun(rr, rr) implies forall([j_ in nn], is-antiderivable(s_(j_), a, b)) implies forall([x_ in rr], ph_(x_) == series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(j_, nn, (s_(j_))(x_)), n_)) implies is-antiderivable(ph_, a, b))
- `series-partial-sum-bounded` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= f(i_)) implies series-converges(f) implies forsome([bnd in rr], forall([kx in nn], series-partial-sum(f, kx) <= bnd)))
- `series-partial-sum-in-rr` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, rr)], series-partial-sum(f, k) in rr)
- `series-partial-sum-in-rr-below` — forall([itn_ in nn, itf_], forall([itk_ in nn], itk_ < itn_ implies itf_(itk_) in rr) implies series-partial-sum(itf_, itn_) in rr)
- `series-partial-sum-in-rr-ptwise` — forall([n_ in nn, f_], forall([k_ in nn], f_(k_) in rr) implies series-partial-sum(f_, n_) in rr)
- `series-partial-sum-is-ring-sum` — forall([n_ in nn, f_], series-partial-sum(f_, n_) == sum(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(rr-normed-field), f_, n_))
- `series-partial-sum-le-termwise` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n_ in nn], f(n_) <= g(n_)) implies forall([k in nn], series-partial-sum(f, k) <= series-partial-sum(g, k)))
- `series-partial-sum-le-termwise-ptwise` — forall([n_ in nn, f_, g_], forall([k_ in nn], f_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], g_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], f_(k_) <= g_(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(f_, n_) <= series-partial-sum(g_, n_))
- `series-partial-sum-mono` — forall([k in nn, m in nn, f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= f(i_)) implies m <= k implies series-partial-sum(f, m) <= series-partial-sum(f, k))
- `series-partial-sum-monotone-nonneg` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n_ in nn], 0 <= f(n_)) implies forall([k in nn], series-partial-sum(f, k) <= series-partial-sum(f, succ(k))))
- `series-partial-sum-nonneg` — forall([k in nn, f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([i_ in nn], 0 <= f(i_)) implies 0 <= series-partial-sum(f, k))
- `series-partial-sum-scale-ptwise` — forall([n_ in nn, c_, f_, h_], c_ in rr implies forall([k_ in nn], f_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], h_(k_) = c_ * f_(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(h_, n_) = c_ * series-partial-sum(f_, n_))
- `series-partial-sum-seq-apply` — forall([f, j in nn], (vnb-lambda(k, nn, series-partial-sum(f, k)))(j) == series-partial-sum(f, j))
- `series-partial-sum-seq-in-fun` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], vnb-lambda(k, nn, series-partial-sum(f, k)) in fun(nn, rr))
- `series-partial-sum-shift` — forall([n in nn, tcu_ in fun(nn, rr)], series-partial-sum(tcu_, succ(n)) = tcu_(0) + series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(tci_, nn, tcu_(succ(tci_))), n))
- `series-partial-sum-succ` — forall([f, k in nn], series-partial-sum(f, k) in rr implies f(k) in rr implies series-partial-sum(f, succ(k)) == series-partial-sum(f, k) + f(k))
- `series-partial-sum-unfold` — forall([f, k], series-partial-sum(f, k) == sum-ag(normed-field-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), f, k))
- `series-partial-sum-weighted-expansion` — forall([n_ in nn, x_, y_, g_, w_, p_, q_, s_], x_ in rr implies y_ in rr implies forall([k_ in zz], g_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in zz], w_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], q_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], s_(k_) in rr) implies forall([k_ in nn], p_(k_) = w_(k_) * (x_ * g_(k_ - 1) + y_ * g_(k_))) implies forall([k_ in nn], q_(k_) = w_(succ(k_)) * g_(k_)) implies forall([k_ in nn], s_(k_) = w_(k_) * g_(k_)) implies series-partial-sum(p_, succ(n_)) = x_ * (w_(0) * g_(0 - 1) + series-partial-sum(q_, n_)) + y_ * (series-partial-sum(s_, n_) + w_(n_) * g_(n_)))
- `series-partial-sum-zero` — forall([f], series-partial-sum(f, 0) == 0)
- `series-partial-sum-zero-seq` — forall([k_ in nn], series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, 0), k_) = 0)
- `series-scale-converges` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpsk_)) implies forall([cpsc_ in rr], 0 <= cpsc_ implies forall([h in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpsk_ in nn], h(cpsk_) = cpsc_ * f(cpsk_)) implies series-converges(f) implies series-converges(h))))
- `series-shift-converges-iff` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpdj_ in nn], 0 <= f(cpdj_)) implies forall([g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpdj_ in nn], g(cpdj_) = f(succ(cpdj_))) implies series-converges(f) iff series-converges(g)))
- `series-shift-partial-sum` — forall([cpdk_ in nn, f in fun(nn, rr), g in fun(nn, rr)], forall([cpdj_ in nn], g(cpdj_) = f(succ(cpdj_))) implies series-partial-sum(f, succ(cpdk_)) = f(0) + series-partial-sum(g, cpdk_))
- `series-tail-small` — forall([g in fun(nn, rr)], series-converges(g) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([bnd in nn], forall([k in nn], bnd <= k implies series-partial-sum(g, k) - series-partial-sum(g, bnd) <= eps))))
- `series-term-le-sum` — forall([f in fun(nn, rr)], forall([n_ in nn], 0 <= f(n_)) implies forall([lm_], series-converges-to(f, lm_) implies forall([n_ in nn], f(n_) <= lm_)))
- `shift-continuous-at` — forall([k in rr, f, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, f, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) + k), a))
- `shift-lam-in-fun` — forall([k in rr, f in fun(rr, rr)], vnb-lambda(x, rr, f(x) + k) in fun(rr, rr))
- `sigma-algebra-ambient-set` — forall([omega, ca], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies omega in set)
- `sigma-algebra-complement-closed` — forall([omega, ca, a_], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies a_ in ca implies complement-in(omega, a_) in ca)
- `sigma-algebra-difference-closed` — forall([omega, ca, a_, b_], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies a_ in ca implies b_ in ca implies complement-in(a_, b_) in ca)
- `sigma-algebra-empty-in` — forall([omega, ca], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies empty-set in ca)
- `sigma-algebra-inter-closed-2` — forall([omega, ca, a_, b_], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies a_ in ca implies b_ in ca implies intersection(a_, b_) in ca)
- `sigma-algebra-union-closed` — forall([omega, ca, f_], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies f_ in fun(nn, ca) implies big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_)) in ca)
- `sigma-algebra-union-closed-2` — forall([omega, ca, a_, b_], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies a_ in ca implies b_ in ca implies union(a_, b_) in ca)
- `sigma-algebra-whole-in` — forall([omega, ca], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies omega in ca)
- `sigma-generated-in-every` — forall([omega, ce, a_, cb_], a_ in sigma-generated(omega, ce) implies is-sigma-algebra(omega, cb_) implies ce subset cb_ implies a_ in cb_)
- `sigma-generated-in-power` — forall([omega, ce, a_ in sigma-generated(omega, ce)], a_ in power(omega))
- `sigma-generated-intro` — forall([omega, ce, a_ in power(omega)], forall([cb_], is-sigma-algebra(omega, cb_) and ce subset cb_ implies a_ in cb_) implies a_ in sigma-generated(omega, ce))
- `sigma-generated-is-sigma-algebra` — forall([omega, ce], omega in set implies ce subset power(omega) implies is-sigma-algebra(omega, sigma-generated(omega, ce)))
- `singleton-membership` — forall([y_, x_], x_ in singleton(y_) iff x_ in set and x_ = y_)
- `singleton-unfold` — forall([y_], singleton(y_) == {y_})
- `size-nonempty` — forall([m], not(length(m) = 0) implies [length(m), length(nth(1, m))] == size(m))
- `size-unfold` — forall([m], [length(m), if(length(m) = 0, 0, length(nth(1, m)))] == size(m))
- `small-dyadic` — forall([goum_ in rr], 0 <= goum_ implies forall([gour_], pos-rr(gour_) implies forsome([goun_ in nn], goum_ * recip(2 ^ goun_) < gour_)))
- `smith-diagonalization` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([k in nn, n, p], n in nn implies p in mat(k, n, carr(a)) implies forsome([d], mat-equiv(a, k, n, p, d) and is-diagonal(a, k, n, d))))
- `smith-normal-form` — forall([a], is-euclidean-ring(a) implies forall([k in nn, n, p], n in nn implies p in mat(k, n, carr(a)) implies forsome([d, kk in nn], mat-equiv(a, k, n, p, d) and smith-staircase(a, k, n, d, kk))))
- `snoc-col-last` — forall([w, n, v, x], n in nn implies w in mat(n, 1, x) implies v in x implies entry(snoc-col(w, n, v), succ(n), 1) = v)
- `snoc-col-type` — forall([x, n, w, v], n in nn implies w in mat(n, 1, x) implies v in x implies snoc-col(w, n, v) in mat(succ(n), 1, x))
- `snoc-row-last` — forall([c, n, r, x], n in nn implies c in mat(1, n, x) implies r in x implies entry(snoc-row(c, n, r), 1, succ(n)) = r)
- `snoc-row-type` — forall([x, n, c, r], n in nn implies c in mat(1, n, x) implies r in x implies snoc-row(c, n, r) in mat(1, succ(n), x))
- `span-add-one-has-v-nvs` — forall([m, t, v], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, t) implies v in vec(m) implies v in span-add-one(m, t, v))
- `span-add-one-membership` — forall([m, s, v, w_], w_ in span-add-one(m, s, v) iff w_ in vec(m) and forsome([y_ in s, r_ in rr], w_ = (vadd(m))(y_, (act(m))(r_, v))))
- `span-add-one-submodule` — forall([m, t, v], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, t) implies v in vec(m) implies is-submodule(m, span-add-one(m, t, v)))
- `span-add-one-superset-nvs` — forall([m, t, v], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies is-submodule(m, t) implies v in vec(m) implies t subset span-add-one(m, t, v))
- `span-add-one-unfold` — forall([m, s, v], span-add-one(m, s, v) == {y_ in vec(m): forsome([x_ in s, r_ in rr], y_ = (vadd(m))(x_, (act(m))(r_, v)))})
- `span-is-submodule` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], is-submodule(md, span(md, n, u))))
- `spans-fg-base` — forall([md], is-module(md) and is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([u in mat(0, 1, vec(md)), bm], is-submodule(md, bm) implies spans(md, 0, u, bm) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies sm subset bm implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= 0) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm))))))
- `spans-fg-step` — forall([md], is-module(md) and is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn], forall([u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)), bm], is-submodule(md, bm) implies spans(md, n, u, bm) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies sm subset bm implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= n) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm))))) implies forall([u in mat(succ(n), 1, vec(md)), bm], is-submodule(md, bm) implies spans(md, succ(n), u, bm) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies sm subset bm implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= succ(n)) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm)))))))
- `spans-span` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, n, u, span(md, n, u))))
- `spans-submodule-fg` — forall([md], is-module(md) and is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)), bm], is-submodule(md, bm) implies spans(md, n, u, bm) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies sm subset bm implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= n) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm))))))
- `spans-transport` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([n, u, pm, sm], u in mat(n, 1, vec(md)) implies is-invertible-mat(scal(md), n, pm) implies is-submodule(md, sm) implies spans(md, n, u, sm) implies spans(md, n, matact(md, pm, u), sm)))
- `sq-continuous-at` — forall([t in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z_, rr, z_ * z_), t))
- `sq-fun-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(z_, rr, z_ * z_) in fun(rr, rr)
- `sqrt-char` — forall([a, x_], a in rr and 0 <= a implies x_ in rr and 0 <= x_ implies x_ * x_ = a implies sqrt(a) = x_)
- `sqrt-exists` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies forsome([x_ in rr], 0 <= x_ and x_ * x_ = a))
- `sqrt-mono` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies forall([b], b in rr and a <= b implies sqrt(a) <= sqrt(b)))
- `sqrt-mul` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies forall([b], b in rr and 0 <= b implies sqrt(a * b) = sqrt(a) * sqrt(b)))
- `sqrt-nonneg` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies sqrt(a) in rr and 0 <= sqrt(a))
- `sqrt-of-sq` — forall([a in rr], sqrt(a * a) = abs(a))
- `sqrt-prop` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies sqrt(a) in rr and 0 <= sqrt(a) and sqrt(a) * sqrt(a) = a)
- `sqrt-sq` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies sqrt(a) * sqrt(a) = a)
- `sqrt-unique` — forall([x_, y_], x_ in rr and y_ in rr implies 0 <= x_ and 0 <= y_ implies x_ * x_ = y_ * y_ implies x_ = y_)
- `sqrt-zero` — sqrt(0) = 0
- `sqrt2-irrational` — forall([p in nn, q in nn], not(q = 0) implies not(p * p = 2 * (q * q)))
- `sqrt3-irrational` — forall([p in nn, q in nn], not(q = 0) implies not(p * p = 3 * (q * q)))
- `std-dist` — forall([goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc], 0 <= real-part(goux_) implies 0 <= imag-part(goux_) implies real-part(goux_) + imag-part(goux_) <= 1 implies 0 <= real-part(gouy_) implies 0 <= imag-part(gouy_) implies real-part(gouy_) + imag-part(gouy_) <= 1 implies magnitude(goux_ - gouy_) <= 2)
- `std-limit` — forall([gous_ in fun(nn, cc), gouw_ in cc], forall([goun_ in nn], 0 <= real-part(gous_(goun_)) and 0 <= imag-part(gous_(goun_)) and real-part(gous_(goun_)) + imag-part(gous_(goun_)) <= 1) implies converges-to(cc-ms, gous_, gouw_) implies 0 <= real-part(gouw_) and 0 <= imag-part(gouw_) and real-part(gouw_) + imag-part(gouw_) <= 1)
- `std-mid` — forall([goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc], 0 <= real-part(goux_) implies 0 <= imag-part(goux_) implies real-part(goux_) + imag-part(goux_) <= 1 implies 0 <= real-part(gouy_) implies 0 <= imag-part(gouy_) implies real-part(gouy_) + imag-part(gouy_) <= 1 implies 0 <= real-part(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) and 0 <= imag-part(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) and real-part(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) + imag-part(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) <= 1)
- `step-approx-append` — forall([rsca_, rscx_, rscy_, rscf_, rsce_, rsck_], rsca_ in rr implies rscx_ in rr implies rscy_ in rr implies rscf_ in fun(rr, rr) implies rsce_ in rr implies rsck_ in rr implies 0 <= rsce_ implies forsome([rsqn_ in nn, rsqp_ in fun(nn, rr), rsqh_ in fun(rr, rr)], rsqp_(0) = rsca_ and rsqp_(rsqn_) = rscx_ and forall([rsi_, rsj_], rsi_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rsj_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rsi_ < rsj_ implies rsqp_(rsi_) < rsqp_(rsj_)) and forall([rspi_], rspi_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rspi_ < rsqn_ implies forsome([rspc_ in rr], forall([rspt_], rspt_ in rr and rsqp_(rspi_) < rspt_ and rspt_ < rsqp_(succ(rspi_)) implies rsqh_(rspt_) = rspc_))) and forall([rsqz_ in ccint(rsca_, rscx_)], abs(rscf_(rsqz_) - rsqh_(rsqz_)) <= rsce_)) implies rscx_ < rscy_ implies forall([rscs_ in rr], rscx_ < rscs_ implies rscs_ < rscy_ implies abs(rscf_(rscs_) - rsck_) <= rsce_) implies forsome([rsqn_ in nn, rsqp_ in fun(nn, rr), rsqh_ in fun(rr, rr)], rsqp_(0) = rsca_ and rsqp_(rsqn_) = rscy_ and forall([rsi_, rsj_], rsi_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rsj_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rsi_ < rsj_ implies rsqp_(rsi_) < rsqp_(rsj_)) and forall([rspi_], rspi_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rspi_ < rsqn_ implies forsome([rspc_ in rr], forall([rspt_], rspt_ in rr and rsqp_(rspi_) < rspt_ and rspt_ < rsqp_(succ(rspi_)) implies rsqh_(rspt_) = rspc_))) and forall([rsqz_ in ccint(rsca_, rscy_)], abs(rscf_(rsqz_) - rsqh_(rsqz_)) <= rsce_)))
- `step-approx-q-step-fn` — forall([rsta_, rstb_, rstf_, rste_], rsta_ in rr implies rstb_ in rr implies rsta_ < rstb_ implies forsome([rsqn_ in nn, rsqp_ in fun(nn, rr), rsqh_ in fun(rr, rr)], rsqp_(0) = rsta_ and rsqp_(rsqn_) = rstb_ and forall([rsi_, rsj_], rsi_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rsj_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rsi_ < rsj_ implies rsqp_(rsi_) < rsqp_(rsj_)) and forall([rspi_], rspi_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rspi_ < rsqn_ implies forsome([rspc_ in rr], forall([rspt_], rspt_ in rr and rsqp_(rspi_) < rspt_ and rspt_ < rsqp_(succ(rspi_)) implies rsqh_(rspt_) = rspc_))) and forall([rsqz_ in ccint(rsta_, rstb_)], abs(rstf_(rsqz_) - rsqh_(rsqz_)) <= rste_)) implies forsome([rsth_ in fun(rr, rr)], is-step-fn(rsth_, rsta_, rstb_) and forall([rsqz_ in ccint(rsta_, rstb_)], abs(rstf_(rsqz_) - rsth_(rsqz_)) <= rste_)))
- `step-approx-start` — forall([rsca_, rscf_, rsce_], rsca_ in rr implies rscf_ in fun(rr, rr) implies rsce_ in rr implies 0 <= rsce_ implies forsome([rsqn_ in nn, rsqp_ in fun(nn, rr), rsqh_ in fun(rr, rr)], rsqp_(0) = rsca_ and rsqp_(rsqn_) = rsca_ and forall([rsi_, rsj_], rsi_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rsj_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rsi_ < rsj_ implies rsqp_(rsi_) < rsqp_(rsj_)) and forall([rspi_], rspi_ in interval(0, rsqn_) and rspi_ < rsqn_ implies forsome([rspc_ in rr], forall([rspt_], rspt_ in rr and rsqp_(rspi_) < rspt_ and rspt_ < rsqp_(succ(rspi_)) implies rsqh_(rspt_) = rspc_))) and forall([rsqz_ in ccint(rsca_, rsca_)], abs(rscf_(rsqz_) - rsqh_(rsqz_)) <= rsce_)))
- `step-fn-has-primitive` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, f], is-step-fn(f, a, b) implies forsome([g], is-primitive(g, restrict(f, ccint(a, b)), a, b)))
- `step-fn-has-primitive-ind` — forall([n in nn, p, a, b, f in fun(rr, rr)], is-partition(p, n, a, b) implies forall([i_], i_ in interval(0, n) and i_ < n implies forsome([c_ in rr], forall([t_], t_ in rr and p(i_) < t_ and t_ < p(succ(i_)) implies f(t_) = c_))) implies forsome([g], is-primitive(g, restrict(f, ccint(a, b)), a, b)))
- `step-pieces-append` — forall([rsp_, rsn_, rsh_, rsq_, rsg_, rsc_, rsd_, rsk_], rsp_ in fun(nn, rr) implies rsn_ in nn implies rsh_ in fun(rr, rr) implies rsq_ in fun(nn, rr) implies rsg_ in fun(rr, rr) implies rsc_ in rr implies rsd_ in rr implies rsk_ in rr implies forall([rsi_, rsj_], rsi_ in interval(0, rsn_) and rsj_ in interval(0, rsn_) and rsi_ < rsj_ implies rsp_(rsi_) < rsp_(rsj_)) implies forall([rspi_], rspi_ in interval(0, rsn_) and rspi_ < rsn_ implies forsome([rspc_ in rr], forall([rspt_], rspt_ in rr and rsp_(rspi_) < rspt_ and rspt_ < rsp_(succ(rspi_)) implies rsh_(rspt_) = rspc_))) implies rsp_(rsn_) = rsc_ implies forall([rsj_ in nn], rsj_ <= rsn_ implies rsq_(rsj_) = rsp_(rsj_)) implies rsq_(succ(rsn_)) = rsd_ implies forall([rst_ in rr], rst_ <= rsc_ implies rsg_(rst_) = rsh_(rst_)) implies forall([rst_ in rr], rsc_ < rst_ implies rst_ < rsd_ implies rsg_(rst_) = rsk_) implies forall([rspi_], rspi_ in interval(0, succ(rsn_)) and rspi_ < succ(rsn_) implies forsome([rspc_ in rr], forall([rspt_], rspt_ in rr and rsq_(rspi_) < rspt_ and rspt_ < rsq_(succ(rspi_)) implies rsg_(rspt_) = rspc_))))
- `strict-increase-injective` — forall([f, s], is-strictly-increasing-on(f, s) implies forall([u in s, v in s], f(u) = f(v) implies u = v))
- `strict-seq-append-point` — forall([rsap_, rsan_, rsad_], rsap_ in fun(nn, rr) implies rsan_ in nn implies rsad_ in rr implies forall([rsi_, rsj_], rsi_ in interval(0, rsan_) and rsj_ in interval(0, rsan_) and rsi_ < rsj_ implies rsap_(rsi_) < rsap_(rsj_)) implies rsap_(rsan_) < rsad_ implies forsome([rsaq_ in fun(nn, rr)], rsaq_(succ(rsan_)) = rsad_ and forall([rsak_ in nn], rsak_ <= rsan_ implies rsaq_(rsak_) = rsap_(rsak_)) and forall([rsi_, rsj_], rsi_ in interval(0, succ(rsan_)) and rsj_ in interval(0, succ(rsan_)) and rsi_ < rsj_ implies rsaq_(rsi_) < rsaq_(rsj_))))
- `strictly-below-irreflexive` — forall([porel, x_], not(is-strictly-below(porel, x_, x_)))
- `strictly-below-not-equal` — forall([porel, x_, y_], x_ = y_ implies not(is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_)))
- `strictly-below-transitive` — forall([grd, porel, x_, y_, z_], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies x_ in grd implies y_ in grd implies z_ in grd implies is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_) implies is-strictly-below(porel, y_, z_) implies is-strictly-below(porel, x_, z_))
- `strictly-mono-ge-id` — forall([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) implies forall([k in nn], k <= phi(k)))
- `strictly-mono-ge-id-ind` — forall([k_ in nn, phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) implies k_ <= phi(k_))
- `strictly-mono-image-infinite` — forall([psi], strictly-mono-nn(psi) implies forall([a], a subset nn implies forall([k_ in nn], psi(k_) in a) implies {m_ in a: forsome([j_ in nn], m_ = psi(j_))} in inf-subsets(nn)))
- `strictly-mono-le-reflect` — forall([psi], strictly-mono-nn(psi) implies forall([i_ in nn, j_ in nn], psi(i_) <= psi(j_) implies i_ <= j_))
- `sub-continuous-at` — forall([g, h, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) - h(x)), a))
- `sub-lam-in-fun` — forall([g, h], g in fun(rr, rr) implies h in fun(rr, rr) implies vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) - h(x)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `sub-rr-agree-dist` — forall([dfu_ in pts(rr-ms), dfv_ in pts(rr-ms)], (dist(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(rr-normed-field), rr)))(dfu_, dfv_) == (dist(rr-ms))(dfu_, dfv_))
- `sub-rr-agree-pts` — pts(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(rr-normed-field), rr)) == pts(rr-ms)
- `sub-rr-is-metric-space` — is-metric-space(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(rr-normed-field), rr))
- `subclass-of-set-is-set` — forall([a, b in set], a subset b implies a in set)
- `submat-type` — forall([a, p, q, s], p in nn implies q in nn implies s in mat(succ(p), succ(q), carr(a)) implies submat(s, p, q) in mat(p, q, carr(a)))
- `submodule-fg` — forall([md], is-module(md) and is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], generates(md, n, u) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= n) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm))))))
- `submodule-finsum-closed` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(module-vector-ag(md)))], forall([z in s], f(z) in sm) implies finsum(module-vector-ag(md), f, s) in sm))))
- `submodule-intersection` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies forall([s1], is-submodule(md, s1) implies forall([s2], is-submodule(md, s2) implies is-submodule(md, intersection(s1, s2)))))
- `submodule-nvs-module-view` — forall([m_, t_], is-submodule(m_, t_) implies is-submodule(normed-vector-space-as-module(m_), t_))
- `subseq-apply` — forall([f, phi, k_ in nn], (subseq(f, phi))(k_) == f(phi(k_)))
- `subseq-is-fun` — forall([s, f, phi], f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and strictly-mono-nn(phi) implies subseq(f, phi) in fun(nn, pts(s)))
- `subseq-of-convergent` — forall([s, f, phi, l], converges-to(s, f, l) implies strictly-mono-nn(phi) implies converges-to(s, subseq(f, phi), l))
- `subsequence-capture` — forall([s in inf-subsets(nn)], forsome([f in fun(nn, s)], forall([m in nn, n in nn], m < n implies f(m) < f(n))))
- `subsequence-principle` — forall([s, f, l], is-metric-space(s) implies f in fun(nn, pts(s)) implies l in pts(s) implies forall([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) implies forsome([psi], strictly-mono-nn(psi) and converges-to(s, subseq(subseq(f, phi), psi), l))) implies converges-to(s, f, l))
- `subset-mem-fwd` — forall([a, b, x], a subset b implies x in a implies x in b)
- `subset-of-empty-is-empty` — forall([w_ in set], forall([z_ in w_], z_ in empty-set) implies w_ = empty-set)
- `subset-refl` — forall([a_], a_ subset a_)
- `subset-trans` — forall([a, b, c], a subset b implies b subset c implies a subset c)
- `subspace-ball` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([c in a, r], ball(subspace-ms(s, a), c, r) = intersection(ball(s, c, r), a))))
- `subspace-ball-membership` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([c in a, r, y], y in ball(subspace-ms(s, a), c, r) iff y in ball(s, c, r) and y in a)))
- `subspace-complete` — forall([s], is-complete(s) implies forall([a], is-closed(s, a) implies is-complete(subspace-ms(s, a))))
- `subspace-converges-to` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([g in fun(nn, a), l in a], converges-to(subspace-ms(s, a), g, l) iff converges-to(s, g, l))))
- `subspace-dist` — forall([s, a, u_, v_], u_ in a implies v_ in a implies (dist(subspace-ms(s, a)))(u_, v_) == (dist(s))(u_, v_))
- `subspace-dist-slot` — forall([s, a], dist(subspace-ms(s, a)) == vnb-lambda([sbu_, sbv_], cartesian(a, a), (dist(s))(sbu_, sbv_)))
- `subspace-is-cauchy-seq` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, a)], is-cauchy-seq(subspace-ms(s, a), f) iff is-cauchy-seq(s, f))))
- `subspace-is-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies is-metric-space(subspace-ms(s, a))))
- `subspace-open-is-trace` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([vs_], is-open(subspace-ms(s, a), vs_) implies forsome([us_], is-open(s, us_) and vs_ = intersection(us_, a)))))
- `subspace-open-trace` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a], a subset pts(s) implies forall([u], is-open(s, u) implies is-open(subspace-ms(s, a), intersection(u, a)))))
- `subspace-pts` — forall([s, a], pts(subspace-ms(s, a)) == a)
- `subspace-restrict-continuous-at` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([icu_], icu_ subset pts(s) implies forall([icv_], icv_ subset icu_ implies forall([ict_], is-metric-space(ict_) implies forall([f, icz_ in icv_], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(s, icu_), ict_, f, icz_) implies is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(s, icv_), ict_, restrict(f, icv_), icz_))))))
- `subspace-whole` — forall([s], pts(subspace-ms(s, pts(s))) == pts(s) and forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(subspace-ms(s, pts(s))))(u_, v_) == (dist(s))(u_, v_)))
- `subsqn-preserves-sqn` — forall([a, f, y], f in sqn(a) implies subsqn(y, f) implies y in sqn(a))
- `succ-in-interval` — forall([q in nn, z in interval(1, q)], succ(z) in interval(1, succ(q)))
- `succ-nn-minus-1` — forall([i in nn], 1 <= i implies succ(nn-minus(i, 1)) = i)
- `succ-nn-ord` — forall([n in nn], succ(n) = succ_ord(n))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `succ-not-one` — forall([q, z in interval(1, q)], not(succ(z) = 1))
- `sum-ag-act-collect-ind` — forall([n in nn, md], is-module(md) implies forall([x in vec(md), gc_, gv_], forall([i_ in ord-segment(n)], gc_(i_) in carr(scal(md))) implies forall([i_ in ord-segment(n)], gv_(i_) == (act(md))(gc_(i_), x)) implies sum-ag(module-vector-ag(md), gv_, n) = (act(md))(sum-ag(ring-additive-ag(scal(md)), gc_, n), x)))
- `sum-ag-act-ind` — forall([n in nn, md], is-module(md) implies forall([r in carr(scal(md)), ga_, gb_], forall([i_ in ord-segment(n)], ga_(i_) in carr(module-vector-ag(md))) implies forall([i_ in ord-segment(n)], gb_(i_) == (act(md))(r, ga_(i_))) implies sum-ag(module-vector-ag(md), gb_, n) = (act(md))(r, sum-ag(module-vector-ag(md), ga_, n))))

- `sum-ag-fubini-ind` — forall([m in nn, ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([n in nn, ff_, gg_, rs_, cs_], forall([i_ in ord-segment(m), j_ in ord-segment(n)], (ff_(i_))(j_) in carr(ag)) implies forall([i_ in ord-segment(m), j_ in ord-segment(n)], (gg_(j_))(i_) == (ff_(i_))(j_)) implies forall([i_ in ord-segment(m)], rs_(i_) == sum-ag(ag, ff_(i_), n)) implies forall([j_ in ord-segment(n)], cs_(j_) == sum-ag(ag, gg_(j_), m)) implies sum-ag(ag, rs_, m) = sum-ag(ag, cs_, n)))
- `sum-ag-in-subset-ind` — forall([n in nn, ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([sm], iden(ag) in sm implies forall([x_ in sm, y_ in sm], (opr(ag))(x_, y_) in sm) implies forall([g_], forall([i_ in ord-segment(n)], g_(i_) in sm) implies sum-ag(ag, g_, n) in sm)))
- `sum-ag-permutation-invariance` — forall([n in nn, ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([g in fun(nn, carr(ag)), h in fun(nn, carr(ag)), phi in bijection(ord-segment(n), ord-segment(n))], forall([i in ord-segment(n)], h(i) = g(phi(i))) implies sum-ag(ag, g, n) = sum-ag(ag, h, n)))  _[warrant: informal]_

- `sum-ag-replace-entry-laws` — forall([n in nn, s], iden(s) in carr(s) implies forall([a_ in carr(s), b_ in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a_, b_) in carr(s)) implies forall([a_ in carr(s), b_ in carr(s), c_ in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a_, b_), c_) = (opr(s))(a_, (opr(s))(b_, c_))) implies forall([a_ in carr(s), b_ in carr(s)], (opr(s))(a_, b_) = (opr(s))(b_, a_)) implies forall([g in fun(nn, carr(s)), gp in fun(nn, carr(s)), k in ord-segment(n)], forall([i_ in ord-segment(n)], not(i_ = k) implies g(i_) == gp(i_)) implies (opr(s))(sum-ag(s, g, n), gp(k)) = (opr(s))(sum-ag(s, gp, n), g(k))))

- `sum-continuous-at` — forall([g, h, a], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, h, a) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(x, rr, g(x) + h(x)), a))

- `taylor-deriv-real` — forall([f, a, x, n, t], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies a < t implies t < x implies n in nn implies (nth-deriv(f, succ(n)))(t) in rr)

- `taylor-factor-fn-value` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, tcf_, tcm_ in nn], forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= succ(tcm_) implies tcf_(tck_) in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies is-continuous-on(tcf_(tck_), ccint(a, b))) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies forall([tcx_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(tcf_(tck_), tcx_, (tcf_(succ(tck_)))(tcx_)))) implies forall([tct_ in rr], a < tct_ implies tct_ <= b implies forsome([tcxi_ in ooint(a, tct_)], (vnb-lambda(tcs_, rr, (((extend-const(tcf_(0), a, b))(tcs_) - taylor-sum(tcf_, succ(tcm_), tcs_, a)) * factorial(succ(tcm_))) * recip-star((tcs_ - a) ^ succ(tcm_))))(tct_) = (tcf_(succ(tcm_)))(tcxi_))))
- `taylor-factor-glue-continuous` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, tcf_, tcm_ in nn], a < b implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= succ(tcm_) implies tcf_(tck_) in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies is-continuous-on(tcf_(tck_), ccint(a, b))) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies forall([tcx_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(tcf_(tck_), tcx_, (tcf_(succ(tck_)))(tcx_)))) implies is-continuous-on(tcf_(succ(tcm_)), ccint(a, b)) implies forall([tcp_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(tcs_, rr, if(tcs_ <= a, (tcf_(succ(tcm_)))(a), (((extend-const(tcf_(0), a, b))(tcs_) - taylor-sum(tcf_, succ(tcm_), tcs_, a)) * factorial(succ(tcm_))) * recip-star((tcs_ - a) ^ succ(tcm_)))), tcp_)))
- `taylor-factor-value` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, tcf_, tcm_ in nn], forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= succ(tcm_) implies tcf_(tck_) in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies is-continuous-on(tcf_(tck_), ccint(a, b))) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies forall([tcx_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(tcf_(tck_), tcx_, (tcf_(succ(tck_)))(tcx_)))) implies forall([tct_ in rr], a < tct_ implies tct_ <= b implies forsome([tcxi_ in ooint(a, tct_)], (taylor-rem(tcf_, succ(tcm_), a, tct_) * factorial(succ(tcm_))) * recip((tct_ - a) ^ succ(tcm_)) = (tcf_(succ(tcm_)))(tcxi_))))
- `taylor-family-continuous` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies forall([itk_ in nn], itk_ < itn_ implies is-continuous-on(itd_(itk_), ccint(a, b))))
- `taylor-family-deriv` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies forall([itk_ in nn], itk_ < itn_ implies forall([itx_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(itd_(itk_), itx_, (itd_(succ(itk_)))(itx_)))))
- `taylor-family-endpoints-lt` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies a < b)
- `taylor-family-in-fun` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies forall([itk_ in nn], itk_ < itn_ implies itd_(itk_) in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)))
- `taylor-family-left-in-rr` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies a in rr)
- `taylor-family-order-in-nn` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies itn_ in nn)
- `taylor-family-order-pos` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies 1 <= itn_)
- `taylor-family-right-in-rr` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies b in rr)
- `taylor-family-sum-at-endpoint` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies forall([itm_ in nn], succ(itm_) <= itn_ implies taylor-sum(itd_, succ(itm_), b, b) = (itd_(0))(b)))
- `taylor-family-top-in-fun` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies itd_(itn_) in fun(ooint(a, b), rr))
- `taylor-formula-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, itd_, itm_ in nn], is-taylor-family(itd_, succ(itm_), a, b) implies forall([itg_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(itg_, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([itx_ in ooint(a, b)], forsome([itl_], has-deriv-at(itg_, itx_, itl_))) implies forsome([itxi_ in ooint(a, b), itmu_], has-deriv-at(itg_, itxi_, itmu_) and (itmu_ * taylor-rem(itd_, succ(itm_), a, b)) * factorial(itm_) = ((itd_(succ(itm_)))(itxi_) * (b - itxi_) ^ itm_) * (itg_(b) - itg_(a)) and itmu_ * taylor-rem(itd_, succ(itm_), a, b) = (((itd_(succ(itm_)))(itxi_) * recip(factorial(itm_))) * (b - itxi_) ^ itm_) * (itg_(b) - itg_(a)))))
- `taylor-formula-on-interval-from-aux` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, itd_, itm_ in nn], is-taylor-family(itd_, succ(itm_), a, b) implies is-continuous-on(taylor-aux(itd_, succ(itm_), a, b), ccint(a, b)) implies forall([itx_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(taylor-aux(itd_, succ(itm_), a, b), itx_, ((itd_(succ(itm_)))(itx_) * (b - itx_) ^ itm_) * recip(factorial(itm_)))) implies taylor-sum(itd_, succ(itm_), b, b) = (itd_(0))(b) implies forall([itg_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(itg_, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([itx_ in ooint(a, b)], forsome([itl_], has-deriv-at(itg_, itx_, itl_))) implies forsome([itxi_ in ooint(a, b), itmu_], has-deriv-at(itg_, itxi_, itmu_) and (itmu_ * taylor-rem(itd_, succ(itm_), a, b)) * factorial(itm_) = ((itd_(succ(itm_)))(itxi_) * (b - itxi_) ^ itm_) * (itg_(b) - itg_(a)) and itmu_ * taylor-rem(itd_, succ(itm_), a, b) = (((itd_(succ(itm_)))(itxi_) * recip(factorial(itm_))) * (b - itxi_) ^ itm_) * (itg_(b) - itg_(a)))))
- `taylor-g-at-a` — forall([f, a, x, n], f in fun(rr, rr) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies n in nn implies forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies nth-deriv(f, k) in fun(rr, rr)) implies (vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)))(a) = f(x) - taylor-poly(f, a, n, x))
- `taylor-g-at-x` — forall([f, x, n in nn], f in fun(rr, rr) implies x in rr implies forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies nth-deriv(f, k) in fun(rr, rr)) implies (vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)))(x) = 0)
- `taylor-g-cont` — forall([f, a, x, n, t], n in nn implies x in rr implies taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies t in ccint(a, x) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)), t))
- `taylor-g-diff` — forall([f, a, x, n, t], n in nn implies x in rr implies taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies a < t and t < x implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)), t, 0 - (recip(factorial(n)) * (nth-deriv(f, succ(n)))(t)) * (x - t) ^ n))
- `taylor-g-in-fun` — forall([n in nn, f in fun(rr, rr), x in rr], forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies nth-deriv(f, k) in fun(rr, rr)) implies vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `taylor-gmvt-cont` — forall([f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies x in rr implies n in nn implies forall([t in ccint(a, x)], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)), t) and is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)), t)))
- `taylor-gmvt-diff` — forall([f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies n in nn implies x in rr implies forall([t], t in rr and a < t and t < x implies forsome([l], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, f(x) - taylor-poly(f, z, n, x)), t, l)) and forsome([m], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)), t, m))))
- `taylor-h-at-a` — forall([a, x, n], a in rr implies x in rr implies n in nn implies (vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)))(a) = (x - a) ^ succ(n))
- `taylor-h-at-x` — forall([x, n in nn], x in rr implies (vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)))(x) = 0)
- `taylor-h-cont` — forall([x, n, t], x in rr implies t in rr implies n in nn implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)), t))
- `taylor-h-diff` — forall([x, n, t], x in rr implies t in rr implies n in nn implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)), t, 0 - succ(n) * (x - t) ^ n))
- `taylor-h-in-fun` — forall([n in nn, x in rr], vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(n)) in fun(rr, rr))
- `taylor-interpolant-at-zero` — forall([n in nn, f], forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= n implies (nth-deriv(f, tck_))(0) in rr) implies forsome([cf in fun(nn, rr)], forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= n implies cf(tck_) = (nth-deriv(f, tck_))(0) * recip(factorial(tck_))) and forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= n implies (nth-deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, cf(k) * x ^ k), succ(n))), tck_))(0) = (nth-deriv(f, tck_))(0)) and forall([tcg_ in fun(nn, rr)], forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= n implies (nth-deriv(vnb-lambda(x, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, tcg_(k) * x ^ k), succ(n))), tck_))(0) = (nth-deriv(f, tck_))(0)) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= n implies tcg_(tck_) = cf(tck_)))))
- `taylor-lagrange` — forall([f, a, x, n], f in fun(rr, rr) and a in rr and x in rr and n in nn and a < x implies taylor-differentiable(f, a, x, n) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < x and factorial(succ(n)) * (f(x) - taylor-poly(f, a, n, x)) = (nth-deriv(f, succ(n)))(theta) * (x - a) ^ succ(n)))
- `taylor-lagrange-on-interval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, itd_, itm_ in nn], is-taylor-family(itd_, succ(itm_), a, b) implies forsome([itxi_ in ooint(a, b)], taylor-rem(itd_, succ(itm_), a, b) = ((itd_(succ(itm_)))(itxi_) * recip(factorial(succ(itm_)))) * (b - a) ^ succ(itm_)))
- `taylor-lagrange-subinterval` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, tcf_, tcm_ in nn], forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= succ(tcm_) implies tcf_(tck_) in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies is-continuous-on(tcf_(tck_), ccint(a, b))) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies forall([tcx_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(tcf_(tck_), tcx_, (tcf_(succ(tck_)))(tcx_)))) implies forall([tct_ in rr], a < tct_ implies tct_ <= b implies forsome([tcxi_ in ooint(a, tct_)], taylor-rem(tcf_, succ(tcm_), a, tct_) = ((tcf_(succ(tcm_)))(tcxi_) * recip(factorial(succ(tcm_)))) * (tct_ - a) ^ succ(tcm_))))
- `taylor-poly-at-center` — forall([f, x, n], x in rr implies n in nn implies forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies (nth-deriv(f, k))(x) in rr) implies taylor-poly(f, x, n, x) = f(x))
- `taylor-poly-in-rr` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), a in rr, n in nn, x in rr], forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies nth-deriv(f, k) in fun(rr, rr)) implies taylor-poly(f, a, n, x) in rr)
- `taylor-poly-in-rr-at` — forall([n in nn, f, a in rr, x in rr], forall([k], k in nn and k <= n implies (nth-deriv(f, k))(a) in rr) implies series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, ((nth-deriv(f, k))(a) * (x - a) ^ k) * recip(factorial(k))), succ(n)) in rr)
- `taylor-remainder-factor` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, tcf_, tcm_ in nn], a < b implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= succ(tcm_) implies tcf_(tck_) in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies is-continuous-on(tcf_(tck_), ccint(a, b))) implies forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies forall([tcx_ in ooint(a, b)], has-deriv-at(tcf_(tck_), tcx_, (tcf_(succ(tck_)))(tcx_)))) implies is-continuous-on(tcf_(succ(tcm_)), ccint(a, b)) implies forsome([tcs_ in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(tcs_, ccint(a, b)) and tcs_(a) = (tcf_(succ(tcm_)))(a) and forall([tct_ in ccint(a, b)], taylor-rem(tcf_, succ(tcm_), a, tct_) = (tcs_(tct_) * (tct_ - a) ^ succ(tcm_)) * recip(factorial(succ(tcm_)))) and forall([tct_ in ccint(a, b)], abs(tcs_(tct_)) <= sup(image(vnb-lambda(tcv_, ccint(a, b), abs((tcf_(succ(tcm_)))(tcv_))), ccint(a, b))))))
- `taylor-remainder-sup-bound` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, tcf_, tcm_ in nn], is-taylor-family(tcf_, succ(tcm_), a, b) implies rr-bounded-above(image(vnb-lambda(tcv_, ooint(a, b), abs((tcf_(succ(tcm_)))(tcv_))), ooint(a, b))) implies abs(taylor-rem(tcf_, succ(tcm_), a, b)) <= ((b - a) ^ succ(tcm_) * recip(factorial(succ(tcm_)))) * sup(image(vnb-lambda(tcv_, ooint(a, b), abs((tcf_(succ(tcm_)))(tcv_))), ooint(a, b))))
- `taylor-step-continuous` — forall([b in rr, itc_ in rr, itk_ in nn, itg_ in fun(rr, rr), itf_ in fun(rr, rr), ity_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, itg_, ity_) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, itf_, ity_) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(itw_, rr, itg_(itw_) + (itf_(itw_) * (b - itw_) ^ succ(itk_)) * itc_), ity_))
- `taylor-step-identity` — forall([d1 in rr, d2 in rr, p in rr, q in rr, r in rr, s in rr], (d1 * p) * (s * r) + r * (d2 * q + ((s * p) * -1) * d1) = (d2 * q) * r)
- `taylor-sum-at-base` — forall([tcj_ in nn, tcf_, a in rr], forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ <= tcj_ implies (tcf_(tck_))(a) in rr) implies series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(tk_, nn, ((tcf_(tk_))(a) * (a - a) ^ tk_) * recip(factorial(tk_))), succ(tcj_)) = (tcf_(0))(a))
- `taylor-sum-at-endpoint` — forall([itm_ in nn, itd_, b in rr], forall([itk_ in nn], itk_ <= itm_ implies (itd_(itk_))(b) in rr) implies taylor-sum(itd_, succ(itm_), b, b) = (itd_(0))(b))
- `taylor-sum-continuous` — forall([a in rr, tcf_, tcm_ in nn], forall([tck_ in nn], tck_ < succ(tcm_) implies (tcf_(tck_))(a) in rr) implies forall([tcp_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(tcs_, rr, taylor-sum(tcf_, succ(tcm_), tcs_, a)), tcp_)))
- `taylor-sum-in-rr` — forall([itd_, itn_, a, b], is-taylor-family(itd_, itn_, a, b) implies forall([itj_ in nn], itj_ <= itn_ implies forall([itz_ in ccint(a, b)], taylor-sum(itd_, itj_, b, itz_) in rr)))
- `taylor-sum-one` — forall([itd_, b in rr, itv_ in rr], (itd_(0))(itv_) in rr implies taylor-sum(itd_, succ(0), b, itv_) = (itd_(0))(itv_))
- `taylor-sum-unfold` — forall([itd_, itn_, b, itv_], taylor-sum(itd_, itn_, b, itv_) == series-partial-sum(taylor-term(itd_, b, itv_), itn_))
- `taylor-term-value` — forall([itd_, b, itv_, itk_ in nn], (taylor-term(itd_, b, itv_))(itk_) == ((itd_(itk_))(itv_) * (b - itv_) ^ itk_) * recip(factorial(itk_)))
- `taylor-v-deriv-in-vec` — forall([m, f, a, x, n, t], taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) implies a < t implies t < x implies n in nn implies (nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(t) in vec(m))
- `taylor-v-derivs-in-fun` — forall([m, f, a, x, n], taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies a < x implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= n implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))))
- `tb-block-step` — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), r], pos-rr(r) implies forall([j in inf-subsets(nn)], forsome([j_ in inf-subsets(nn)], j_ subset j and forsome([c in pts(s)], forall([i in j_], f(i) in ball(s, c, r)))))))
- `tb-has-eps-cauchy-subseq` — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and is-eps-cauchy-seq(s, eps, subseq(f, phi)))))
- `tb-rad-ball-cover` — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([rad], forall([k in nn], pos-rr(rad(k))) implies pts(s) in set and forsome([cov], forall([k in nn], is-finite-cover(cov(k), pts(s))) and forall([k in nn, u in cov(k)], forsome([c in pts(s)], u = ball(s, c, rad(k)))))))
- `tb-scale-dense-seq` — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([x0 in pts(s), r], pos-rr(r) implies forsome([g in fun(nn, pts(s))], forall([p in pts(s)], forsome([j in nn], (dist(s))(p, g(j)) < r)))))
- `tel-dist-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, k) implies vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, n), (mul(a))((vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(a))(entry(p, row, j), entry(q, j, x))))(z), entry(r, x, col))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
