- `tel-outf-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies 1 <= n implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, k), (mul(a))(entry(matmul(a, p, q), row, j), entry(r, j, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `tel-red-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, k) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, j), entry(q, j, x)), entry(r, x, col))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `tel-tout-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, n))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `ter-dist-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(z, interval(1, k), (mul(a))(entry(p, row, x), (vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, k), (mul(a))(entry(q, x, j), entry(r, j, col))))(z))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `ter-gj-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, k), (mul(a))(entry(q, x, j), entry(r, j, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(a))))
- `ter-outf-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies 1 <= k implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (mul(a))(entry(p, row, j), entry(matmul(a, q, r), j, col))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `ter-red-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col, x], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies x in interval(1, n) implies vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, x), entry(q, x, c)), entry(r, c, col))) in fun(interval(1, k), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `ter-tout-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))) in fun(interval(1, n), carr(ring-additive-ag(a)))))
- `tg-neg-scale` — forall([s in rr, p in rr], 0 - s * p = (s * p) * (0 + -1))
- `tgc-sum-cont` — forall([m in nn, f, x in rr, t in rr], forall([k], k in nn and k <= m implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, nth-deriv(f, k), t)) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, ((nth-deriv(f, k))(z) * (x - z) ^ k) * recip(factorial(k))), succ(m))), t))
- `tgc-term-cont` — forall([m in nn, f, x in rr, t in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, nth-deriv(f, succ(m)), t) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, vnb-lambda(z, rr, ((nth-deriv(f, succ(m)))(z) * (x - z) ^ succ(m)) * recip(factorial(succ(m)))), t))
- `tgd-base-sum-value` — forall([f in fun(rr, rr), x in rr, z in rr], series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, ((nth-deriv(f, k))(z) * (x - z) ^ k) * recip(factorial(k))), succ(0)) == f(z))
- `tgd-power-sub-diff` — forall([m in nn, x in rr, t in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, (x - z) ^ succ(m)), t, (succ(m) * (x - t) ^ m) * -1))
- `tgd-recip-factorial-succ` — forall([m in nn], recip(factorial(m)) = succ(m) * recip(factorial(succ(m))))
- `tgd-step-identity` — forall([s in rr, r in rr, d1 in rr, d2 in rr, p in rr, q in rr], (d2 * q + d1 * ((s * p) * -1)) * r + (d1 * q) * 0 = (r * d2) * q - ((s * r) * d1) * p)
- `tgd-sub-diff` — forall([x in rr, t in rr], is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, x - z), t, -1))
- `tgd-sum-diff` — forall([m in nn, f, x in rr, t in rr], forall([k], k in nn and k <= m implies is-diff-at(nth-deriv(f, k), t, (nth-deriv(f, succ(k)))(t))) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, series-partial-sum(vnb-lambda(k, nn, ((nth-deriv(f, k))(z) * (x - z) ^ k) * recip(factorial(k))), succ(m))), t, (recip(factorial(m)) * (nth-deriv(f, succ(m)))(t)) * (x - t) ^ m))
- `tgd-telescope-identity` — forall([u in rr, v in rr], v = u + v - u)
- `tgd-term-diff` — forall([m in nn, f, x in rr, t in rr], is-diff-at(nth-deriv(f, succ(m)), t, (nth-deriv(f, succ(succ(m))))(t)) implies is-diff-at(vnb-lambda(z, rr, ((nth-deriv(f, succ(m)))(z) * (x - z) ^ succ(m)) * recip(factorial(succ(m)))), t, (recip(factorial(succ(m))) * (nth-deriv(f, succ(succ(m))))(t)) * (x - t) ^ succ(m) - (recip(factorial(m)) * (nth-deriv(f, succ(m)))(t)) * (x - t) ^ m))
- `totally-bounded-has-cauchy-subsequence` — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s))], forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and is-cauchy-seq(s, subseq(f, phi)))))
- `trace-affine-reparam` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr, lrpl_ in rr, lrpm_ in rr], 0 < lrpl_ implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpc_ = a implies lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpd_ = b implies forall([pgam, lrpr_], pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies lrpr_ in fun(ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_), cc) implies forall([lrpz_ in ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)], lrpr_(lrpz_) == pgam(lrpm_ + lrpl_ * lrpz_)) implies trace(lrpr_, lrpc_, lrpd_) subset trace(pgam, a, b) and trace(pgam, a, b) subset trace(lrpr_, lrpc_, lrpd_)))
- `trace-is-set` — forall([pgam, a, b], a in rr implies b in rr implies trace(pgam, a, b) in set)
- `trace-opposite` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pgam, prgb_], pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies prgb_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prgb_(pay_) == pgam(a + b - pay_)) implies trace(prgb_, a, b) subset trace(pgam, a, b) and trace(pgam, a, b) subset trace(prgb_, a, b))
- `trace-opposite-subset` — forall([a in rr, b in rr, pgam, prgb_], pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies prgb_ in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([pay_ in ccint(a, b)], prgb_(pay_) == pgam(a + b - pay_)) implies trace(prgb_, a, b) subset trace(pgam, a, b))
- `trace-restrict-subset` — forall([pgam, a, b, lrpc_ in rr, lrpd_ in rr], pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies a in rr implies b in rr implies a <= lrpc_ implies lrpd_ <= b implies trace(restrict(pgam, ccint(lrpc_, lrpd_)), lrpc_, lrpd_) subset trace(pgam, a, b))
- `trace-subset-cc` — forall([pgam, a, b], pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies trace(pgam, a, b) subset cc)
- `trace-unfold` — forall([pgam, a, b], trace(pgam, a, b) == image(pgam, ccint(a, b)))
- `trace-value-in` — forall([pgam, a, b], pgam in fun(ccint(a, b), cc) implies forall([pat_ in ccint(a, b)], pgam(pat_) in trace(pgam, a, b)))
- `transpose-entry` — forall([a, m, n, x, i, j], a in mat(m, n, x) implies i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, m) implies entry(transpose(a, m, n), i, j) = entry(a, j, i))
- `transpose-identity` — forall([r, n], is-ring(r) implies n in nn implies transpose(identmat(r, n), n, n) = identmat(r, n))
- `transpose-in-mat` — forall([a, m, n, x], a in mat(m, n, x) implies transpose(a, m, n) in mat(n, m, x))
- `transpose-matmul` — forall([r, m, n, kc, p, q], is-commutative-ring(r) implies p in mat(m, n, carr(r)) implies q in mat(n, kc, carr(r)) implies (n = 0 implies m = 0 or kc = 0) implies transpose(matmul(r, p, q), m, kc) = matmul(r, transpose(q, n, kc), transpose(p, m, n)))
- `transpose-transpose` — forall([a, m, n, x], a in mat(m, n, x) implies transpose(transpose(a, m, n), n, m) = a)
- `tri-child-geom-1` — forall([goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc, gouz_ in cc], (magnitude(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) - goux_) + magnitude(cc-mid(goux_, gouz_) - cc-mid(goux_, gouy_))) + magnitude(goux_ - cc-mid(goux_, gouz_)) = 1/2 * ((magnitude(gouy_ - goux_) + magnitude(gouz_ - gouy_)) + magnitude(goux_ - gouz_)) and magnitude(goux_ - goux_) <= (magnitude(gouy_ - goux_) + magnitude(gouz_ - gouy_)) + magnitude(goux_ - gouz_))
- `tri-child-geom-2` — forall([goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc, gouz_ in cc], (magnitude(gouy_ - cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) + magnitude(cc-mid(gouy_, gouz_) - gouy_)) + magnitude(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) - cc-mid(gouy_, gouz_)) = 1/2 * ((magnitude(gouy_ - goux_) + magnitude(gouz_ - gouy_)) + magnitude(goux_ - gouz_)) and magnitude(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) - goux_) <= (magnitude(gouy_ - goux_) + magnitude(gouz_ - gouy_)) + magnitude(goux_ - gouz_))
- `tri-child-geom-3` — forall([goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc, gouz_ in cc], (magnitude(cc-mid(gouy_, gouz_) - cc-mid(goux_, gouz_)) + magnitude(gouz_ - cc-mid(gouy_, gouz_))) + magnitude(cc-mid(goux_, gouz_) - gouz_) = 1/2 * ((magnitude(gouy_ - goux_) + magnitude(gouz_ - gouy_)) + magnitude(goux_ - gouz_)) and magnitude(cc-mid(goux_, gouz_) - goux_) <= (magnitude(gouy_ - goux_) + magnitude(gouz_ - gouy_)) + magnitude(goux_ - gouz_))
- `tri-child-geom-4` — forall([goux_ in cc, gouy_ in cc, gouz_ in cc], (magnitude(cc-mid(gouy_, gouz_) - cc-mid(goux_, gouy_)) + magnitude(cc-mid(goux_, gouz_) - cc-mid(gouy_, gouz_))) + magnitude(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) - cc-mid(goux_, gouz_)) = 1/2 * ((magnitude(gouy_ - goux_) + magnitude(gouz_ - gouy_)) + magnitude(goux_ - gouz_)) and magnitude(cc-mid(goux_, gouy_) - goux_) <= (magnitude(gouy_ - goux_) + magnitude(gouz_ - gouy_)) + magnitude(goux_ - gouz_))
- `tri-int-abs-bound` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) subset u implies forall([goum_ in rr], forall([gouz_ in conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_)], magnitude(f(gouz_)) <= goum_) implies magnitude(tri-int(f, goua_, goub_, gouc_)) <= ((magnitude(goub_ - goua_) + magnitude(gouc_ - goub_)) + magnitude(goua_ - gouc_)) * goum_)))
- `tri-int-abs-bound-sides` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1) subset u implies trace(seg-path(goub_, gouc_), 0, 1) subset u implies trace(seg-path(gouc_, goua_), 0, 1) subset u implies forall([goum_ in rr], forall([gouz_ in trace(seg-path(goua_, goub_), 0, 1)], magnitude(f(gouz_)) <= goum_) implies forall([gouz_ in trace(seg-path(goub_, gouc_), 0, 1)], magnitude(f(gouz_)) <= goum_) implies forall([gouz_ in trace(seg-path(gouc_, goua_), 0, 1)], magnitude(f(gouz_)) <= goum_) implies magnitude(tri-int(f, goua_, goub_, gouc_)) <= ((magnitude(goub_ - goua_) + magnitude(gouc_ - goub_)) + magnitude(goua_ - gouc_)) * goum_)))
- `tri-int-affine-zero` — forall([u], is-open(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u) implies forall([goup_ in cc, gouq_ in cc, gouw_ in cc, goug_ in fun(u, cc)], forall([gouz_ in u], goug_(gouz_) == goup_ + gouq_ * (gouz_ - gouw_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) subset u implies tri-int(goug_, goua_, goub_, gouc_) = 0)))
- `tri-int-collinear` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goxa_ in cc, goxb_ in cc, goxc_ in cc], conv3(goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) subset u implies goxc_ in trace(seg-path(goxa_, goxb_), 0, 1) or goxa_ in trace(seg-path(goxb_, goxc_), 0, 1) or goxb_ in trace(seg-path(goxc_, goxa_), 0, 1) implies tri-int(f, goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) = 0))
- `tri-int-fan` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goxa_ in cc, goxb_ in cc, goxc_ in cc], conv3(goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) subset u implies forall([goxx_ in conv3(goxa_, goxb_, goxc_)], tri-int(f, goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) = (tri-int(f, goxa_, goxb_, goxx_) + tri-int(f, goxb_, goxc_, goxx_)) + tri-int(f, goxc_, goxa_, goxx_))))
- `tri-int-in-cc` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) subset u implies tri-int(f, goua_, goub_, gouc_) in cc))
- `tri-int-is-line-int` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([a in cc, b in cc, c in cc], trace(seg-path(a, b), 0, 1) subset u implies trace(seg-path(b, c), 0, 1) subset u implies trace(seg-path(c, a), 0, 1) subset u implies tri-int(f, a, b, c) = line-int(f, tri-road(a, b, c), tri-road-deriv(a, b, c), 0, 3)))
- `tri-int-is-line-int-conv3` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([a in cc, b in cc, c in cc], conv3(a, b, c) subset u implies tri-int(f, a, b, c) = line-int(f, tri-road(a, b, c), tri-road-deriv(a, b, c), 0, 3)))
- `tri-int-restrict` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goxw_], goxw_ subset u implies forall([goxa_ in cc, goxb_ in cc, goxc_ in cc], conv3(goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) subset goxw_ implies tri-int(restrict(f, goxw_), goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) = tri-int(f, goxa_, goxb_, goxc_))))
- `tri-int-subdivision` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) subset u implies tri-int(f, goua_, goub_, gouc_) = ((tri-int(f, goua_, cc-mid(goua_, goub_), cc-mid(goua_, gouc_)) + tri-int(f, cc-mid(goua_, goub_), goub_, cc-mid(goub_, gouc_))) + tri-int(f, cc-mid(goua_, gouc_), cc-mid(goub_, gouc_), gouc_)) + tri-int(f, cc-mid(goua_, goub_), cc-mid(goub_, gouc_), cc-mid(goua_, gouc_))))
- `tri-int-sum` — forall([u, f, g], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies g in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), g, rgy_)) implies forall([gouh_ in fun(u, cc)], forall([gouz_ in u], gouh_(gouz_) == f(gouz_) + g(gouz_)) implies forall([goua_ in cc, goub_ in cc, gouc_ in cc], conv3(goua_, goub_, gouc_) subset u implies tri-int(gouh_, goua_, goub_, gouc_) = tri-int(f, goua_, goub_, gouc_) + tri-int(g, goua_, goub_, gouc_))))
- `tri-int-unfold` — forall([f, goua_, goub_, gouc_], tri-int(f, goua_, goub_, gouc_) == (seg-int(f, goua_, goub_) + seg-int(f, goub_, gouc_)) + seg-int(f, gouc_, goua_))
- `tri-int-vertex-split` — forall([u, f], u subset cc implies f in fun(u, cc) implies forall([rgy_ in u], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), u), nf-metric-space(cc-normed-field), f, rgy_)) implies forall([goxa_ in cc, goxb_ in cc, goxc_ in cc], conv3(goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) subset u implies forall([goxt_ in ccint(0, 1)], tri-int(f, goxa_, goxb_, goxc_) = (tri-int(f, goxa_, goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxb_ - goxa_), goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxc_ - goxa_)) + tri-int(f, goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxb_ - goxa_), goxb_, goxc_)) + tri-int(f, goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxb_ - goxa_), goxc_, goxa_ + goxt_ * (goxc_ - goxa_)))))
- `tri-road-deriv-unfold` — forall([tra_, trb_, trc_], tri-road-deriv(tra_, trb_, trc_) == juxta(juxta(seg-deriv(tra_, trb_), vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(1, 2), (seg-deriv(trb_, trc_))(trs_ - 1)), 0, 1, 2), vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(2, 3), (seg-deriv(trc_, tra_))(trs_ - 2)), 0, 2, 3))
- `tri-road-is-road` — forall([a in cc, b in cc, c in cc], is-road(tri-road(a, b, c), tri-road-deriv(a, b, c), 0, 3))
- `tri-road-trace` — forall([a in cc, b in cc, c in cc], trace(tri-road(a, b, c), 0, 3) = union(union(trace(seg-path(a, b), 0, 1), trace(seg-path(b, c), 0, 1)), trace(seg-path(c, a), 0, 1)))
- `tri-road-unfold` — forall([tra_, trb_, trc_], tri-road(tra_, trb_, trc_) == juxta(juxta(seg-path(tra_, trb_), vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(1, 2), (seg-path(trb_, trc_))(trs_ - 1)), 0, 1, 2), vnb-lambda(trs_, ccint(2, 3), (seg-path(trc_, tra_))(trs_ - 2)), 0, 2, 3))
- `trinum-mono` — forall([b_ in nn, a_ in nn], a_ <= b_ implies trinum(a_) <= trinum(b_))
- `trinum-type` — forall([n_ in nn], trinum(n_) in nn)
- `triple-entry-left` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies 1 <= n implies 1 <= k implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matmul(a, matmul(a, p, q), r), row, col) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, n))), interval(1, k))))
- `triple-entry-right` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n, k, l, p, q, r, row, col], p in mat(m, n, carr(a)) implies q in mat(n, k, carr(a)) implies r in mat(k, l, carr(a)) implies 1 <= n implies 1 <= k implies row in interval(1, m) implies col in interval(1, l) implies entry(matmul(a, p, matmul(a, q, r)), row, col) = finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, n), finsum(ring-additive-ag(a), vnb-lambda(c, interval(1, k), (vnb-lambda(z, cartesian(interval(1, k), interval(1, n)), (mul(a))((mul(a))(entry(p, row, nth(2, z)), entry(q, nth(2, z), nth(1, z))), entry(r, nth(1, z), col))))([c, j])), interval(1, k))), interval(1, n))))
- `trunc-metric-bounded` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(trunc-metric(s)))(u_, v_) <= 1))
- `trunc-metric-dist` — forall([s, u_, v_], u_ in pts(s) implies v_ in pts(s) implies (dist(trunc-metric(s)))(u_, v_) == min(1, (dist(s))(u_, v_)))
- `trunc-metric-id-uniformly-continuous` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-uniformly-continuous(s, trunc-metric(s), vnb-lambda(a_, pts(s), a_)))
- `trunc-metric-id-uniformly-continuous-back` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-uniformly-continuous(trunc-metric(s), s, vnb-lambda(a_, pts(s), a_)))
- `trunc-metric-is-metric-space` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies is-metric-space(trunc-metric(s)))
- `trunc-metric@preimage` — forall([s, f, v], preimage(trunc-metric(s), f, v) == preimage(s, f, v))
- `tuple-extensionality` — forall([a, l, m], l in tuples(a) implies m in tuples(a) implies length(l) = length(m) implies forall([k_], k_ in nn and 1 <= k_ and k_ <= length(l) implies nth(k_, l) = nth(k_, m)) implies l = m)
- `tuple-tabulation` — forall([n in nn, a, f], forall([i_], i_ in nn and 1 <= i_ and i_ <= n implies f(i_) in a) implies forsome([l in tuples(a)], length(l) = n and forall([i_], i_ in nn and 1 <= i_ and i_ <= n implies nth(i_, l) = f(i_))))
- `tuple-tabulation-offset` — forall([n in nn, a, f, k in nn], forall([i_], i_ in nn and 1 <= i_ and i_ <= n implies f(k + i_) in a) implies forsome([l in tuples(a)], length(l) = n and forall([i_], i_ in nn and 1 <= i_ and i_ <= n implies nth(i_, l) = f(k + i_))))
- `tuples-induction` — forall([a, c], [] in c and forall([x, m], x in a and m in tuples(a) and m in c implies cons(x, m) in c) implies forall([l in tuples(a)], l in c))
- `tuples-mono` — forall([a, b], forall([w in a], w in b) implies forall([l in tuples(a)], l in tuples(b)))
- `unif-cauchy-cont-implies-uniform-limit` — forall([s, fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr))], forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) implies forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g) and converges-uniformly(s, fam, g)))
- `unif-cauchy-has-uniform-limit` — forall([s, fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr))], is-unif-cauchy(s, fam) implies forsome([g in fun(pts(s), rr)], converges-uniformly(s, fam, g)))
- `uniform-cauchy-limit` — forall([g in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), s_], forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn, l_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies n_ <= l_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in s_ implies abs((g(k_))(x_) - (g(l_))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies forsome([h in fun(rr, rr)], forall([x_ in rr], x_ in s_ implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (g(k_))(x_)), h(x_))) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in s_ implies abs(h(x_) - (g(k_))(x_)) <= eps))))))
- `uniform-limit-continuous` — forall([s, fam, g], fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) implies g in fun(pts(s), rr) implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous(s, rr-ms, fam(k))) implies converges-uniformly(s, fam, g) implies is-continuous(s, rr-ms, g))
- `uniform-limit-continuous-at` — forall([fam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), g in fun(rr, rr), t_ in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies forall([k in nn], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, fam(k), t_)) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cp_ in nn], forall([k in nn], cp_ <= k implies forall([x_ in rr], abs(x_ - t_) <= rho implies abs(g(x_) - (fam(k))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, g, t_))
- `uniform-limit-of-regulated` — forall([fam, g, a, b], is-unif-limit-on(fam, g, a, b) implies forall([k_ in nn], is-regulated(fam(k_), a, b)) implies is-regulated(g, a, b))
- `uniform-witness-diff-at` — forall([gfam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), wfam in fun(nn, fun(rr, rr)), fc in fun(rr, rr), lv in rr, a in rr, b in rr, t_ in rr, rho in rr], 0 < rho implies forall([y_ in rr], abs(y_ - t_) <= rho implies y_ in ccint(a, b)) implies t_ in ccint(a, b) implies forall([k_ in nn], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, wfam(k_), t_)) implies forall([k_ in nn, y_ in rr], y_ in ccint(a, b) implies (gfam(k_))(y_) - (gfam(k_))(t_) = (wfam(k_))(y_) * (y_ - t_)) implies forall([y_ in rr], y_ in ccint(a, b) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (gfam(k_))(y_)), fc(y_))) implies forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n_ in nn], forall([k_ in nn, l_ in nn], n_ <= k_ implies n_ <= l_ implies forall([x_ in rr], x_ in ccint(a, b) implies abs((wfam(k_))(x_) - (wfam(l_))(x_)) <= eps)))) implies converges-to(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k_, nn, (wfam(k_))(t_)), lv) implies is-diff-at(fc, t_, lv))
- `uniformly-continuous-arrow-is-continuous-arrow` — forall([a, b, f], is-uniformly-continuous-arrow(a, b, f) implies is-continuous-arrow(a, b, f))
- `uniformly-continuous-is-continuous` — forall([s, t, f], is-uniformly-continuous(s, t, f) implies is-continuous(s, t, f))
- `union-as-disjoint` — forall([a_, b_], union(a_, b_) = union(a_, complement-in(b_, a_)))
- `union-assoc` — forall([a_, b_, c_], union(union(a_, b_), c_) = union(a_, union(b_, c_)))
- `union-comm` — forall([a_, b_], union(a_, b_) = union(b_, a_))
- `union-complement-in` — forall([a_, b_], a_ subset b_ implies b_ = union(a_, complement-in(b_, a_)))
- `union-empty-left` — forall([a in set], union(empty-set, a) = a)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `union-empty-right` — forall([a_], union(a_, empty-set) = a_)
- `union-of-opens-open` — forall([s], is-metric-space(s) implies forall([a, g], forall([i in a], is-open(s, g(i))) implies is-open(s, big-union(i, a, g(i)))))
- `union-right-subset` — forall([a_, s_], s_ subset union(a_, s_))
- `union-singleton-absorb` — forall([s_, y_ in s_], union(pair(y_, y_), s_) = s_)
- `union-singleton-mem` — forall([y_, s_], y_ in set implies y_ in union(pair(y_, y_), s_))
- `union-singleton-subset` — forall([b_, y_, s_], y_ in b_ implies s_ subset b_ implies union(pair(y_, y_), s_) subset b_)
- `unitrow-entries-defined` — forall([a, n, i], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies forall([i_ in interval(1, 1), j_ in interval(1, n)], (vnb-lambda([rw, cl], cartesian(interval(1, 1), interval(1, n)), if(cl = i, one(a), zero(a))))(i_, j_) in set))
- `unitrow-entry-at` — forall([a, n, i], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies i in interval(1, n) implies entry(unitrow(a, n, i), 1, i) = one(a))
- `unitrow-entry-off` — forall([a, n, i, j], is-ring(a) implies n in nn implies i in interval(1, n) implies j in interval(1, n) implies not(j = i) implies entry(unitrow(a, n, i), 1, j) = zero(a))
- `unitrow-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([n, i], n in nn implies unitrow(a, n, i) in mat(1, n, carr(a))))
- `vec-is-set` — forall([m], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies vec(m) in set)
- `vector-taylor-remainder-bound` — forall([m, f, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) and is-finite-dimensional(normed-vector-space-as-module(m)) and f in fun(rr, vec(m)) and a in rr and x in rr and n in nn and a < x implies taylor-differentiable-v(m, f, a, x, n) implies forsome([theta in rr], a < theta and theta < x and factorial(succ(n)) * (vnrm(m))((vadd(m))(f(x), (vneg(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n)))) <= (vnrm(m))((nth-deriv-v(m, f, succ(n)))(theta)) * (x - a) ^ succ(n)))
- `vnrm-nonneg` — forall([m, w_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies w_ in vec(m) implies 0 <= (vnrm(m))(w_))
- `vnrm-real` — forall([m, w_], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies w_ in vec(m) implies (vnrm(m))(w_) in rr)
- `vspace-vec-is-set` — forall([m], is-vector-space(m) implies vec(m) in set)
- `vtaylor-clear` — forall([rv in rr, gv in rr, nv in rr, pw in rr, n in nn], factorial(succ(n)) * rv = gv * pw implies gv <= nv implies 0 <= pw implies factorial(succ(n)) * rv <= nv * pw)
- `vtaylor-poly-in-vec` — forall([m, f, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies n in nn implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= n implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))) implies taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n) in vec(m))
- `vtaylor-remainder-in-vec` — forall([m, f, a, x, n], is-normed-vector-space(m) implies f in fun(rr, vec(m)) implies a in rr implies x in rr implies n in nn implies forall([j_], j_ in nn and j_ <= n implies nth-deriv-v(m, f, j_) in fun(rr, vec(m))) implies (vadd(m))(f(x), (vneg(m))(taylor-poly-v(m, f, a, x, n))) in vec(m))
- `well-ordering-principle` — forall([s in set], forsome([phi], phi in bijection(ord-segment(card(s)), s)))
- `whole-module-is-submodule` — forall([md], is-module(md) implies is-submodule(md, vec(md)))
- `zen-enum-in-fun` — forall([al in ord, s in set], forall([cz], ord-lt(cz, al) implies forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, cz) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))})) implies vnb-lambda(a_, ord-segment(al), zen(s, a_)) in fun(ord-segment(al), s))
- `zen-enum-injective` — forall([al in ord, s in set], forall([cz], ord-lt(cz, al) implies forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, cz) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))})) implies forall([a1_ in ord-segment(al), a2_ in ord-segment(al)], (vnb-lambda(a_, ord-segment(al), zen(s, a_)))(a1_) = (vnb-lambda(a_, ord-segment(al), zen(s, a_)))(a2_) implies a1_ = a2_))
- `zen-enum-surjective` — forall([al in ord, s in set], not(forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, al) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))})) implies forall([w in s], forsome([z in ord-segment(al)], (vnb-lambda(a_, ord-segment(al), zen(s, a_)))(z) = w)))
- `zen-exhausts` — forall([s in set], forsome([alpha in ord], not(forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))}))))
- `zen-hits` — forall([alpha in ord, s], forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))}) implies zen(s, alpha) in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))})
- `zen-step` — forall([alpha in ord, x], zen(x, alpha) = choice({y_ in x: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, alpha) implies not(zen(x, c_) = y_))}))
- `zermelo-bijection` — forall([s in set], forsome([al in ord, phi], phi in bijection(ord-segment(al), s)))
- `zermelo-least-ordinal` — forall([s in set], forsome([al in ord], not(forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, al) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))})) and forall([cz], ord-lt(cz, al) implies forsome([x], x in {y_ in s: forall([c_], ord-lt(c_, cz) implies not(zen(s, c_) = y_))}))))
- `zero-deriv-off-countable` — forall([zs_], is-countable(zs_) implies zs_ subset rr implies forall([zh_ in fun(rr, rr)], forall([zx_ in rr], is-continuous-at(rr-ms, rr-ms, zh_, zx_)) implies forall([zu_ in rr, zv_ in rr], zu_ < zv_ implies forall([zt_ in ooint(zu_, zv_)], not(zt_ in zs_) implies is-diff-at(zh_, zt_, 0)) implies zh_(zu_) = zh_(zv_))))
- `zero-deriv-off-countable-constant` — forall([a in rr, b in rr], a < b implies forall([g in fun(ccint(a, b), rr)], is-continuous-on(g, ccint(a, b)) implies forall([zd_], is-countable(zd_) implies zd_ subset ccint(a, b) implies forall([zt_ in ooint(a, b)], not(zt_ in zd_) implies has-deriv-at(g, zt_, 0)) implies forall([zs_ in ccint(a, b), zw_ in ccint(a, b)], g(zs_) = g(zw_)))))
- `zero-in-rr-pos-star` — 0 in rr-pos-star
- `zero-is-antiderivable` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr and a < b implies is-antiderivable(vnb-lambda(x, rr, 0), a, b))
- `zero-seq-in-fun` — vnb-lambda(n_, nn, 0) in fun(nn, rr)
- `zero-series-converges` — series-converges(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, 0))
- `zeromat-entries-defined` — forall([a, m, n], is-ring(a) implies m in nn implies n in nn implies forall([i_ in interval(1, m), j_ in interval(1, n)], (vnb-lambda([i, j], cartesian(interval(1, m), interval(1, n)), zero(a)))(i_, j_) in set))
- `zeromat-type` — forall([a], is-ring(a) implies forall([m, n], m in nn implies n in nn implies zeromat(a, m, n) in mat(m, n, carr(a))))
- `zorn-chain-plus-keepset` — forall([phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies is-chain(grd, porel, kset) implies is-chain(grd, porel, union(kset, keep-set(phi, grd, porel, kset, alpha))))
- `zorn-empty-is-chain` — forall([grd, porel], grd in set implies is-chain(grd, porel, empty-set))
- `zorn-lemma` — forall([grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies forsome([w_], w_ in grd) implies forall([ch_], is-chain(grd, porel, ch_) implies forsome([u_], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch_, u_))) implies forsome([mx_], is-maximal(grd, porel, mx_)))
- `zorn-zkept-is-chain` — forall([alpha in ord, phi, grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies is-chain(grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, alpha)))
- `zorn-zkept-limit-is-chain` — forall([phi, grd, porel, lam], is-partial-order(grd, porel) implies limit-ord(lam) implies forall([beta in ord-segment(lam)], is-chain(grd, porel, zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta))) implies is-chain(grd, porel, big-union(beta2, ord-segment(lam), zkept(phi, grd, porel, beta2))))
- `zorn-zkept-monotone` — forall([b in ord, phi, grd, porel, a in ord], ord-le(a, b) implies zkept(phi, grd, porel, a) subset zkept(phi, grd, porel, b))
- `zup-injective` — forall([grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) and forsome([w_], w_ in grd) and forall([ch_], is-chain(grd, porel, ch_) implies forsome([u_], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch_, u_))) and forall([x_ in grd], forsome([y_ in grd], is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_))) implies forall([a1_, a2_], a1_ in ord implies a2_ in ord implies zup(grd, porel, a1_) = zup(grd, porel, a2_) implies a1_ = a2_))
- `zup-tower` — forall([a_ in ord, grd, porel], is-partial-order(grd, porel) and forsome([w_], w_ in grd) and forall([ch_], is-chain(grd, porel, ch_) implies forsome([u_], is-upper-bound(grd, porel, ch_, u_))) and forall([x_ in grd], forsome([y_ in grd], is-strictly-below(porel, x_, y_))) implies zup(grd, porel, a_) in grd and forall([b_], ord-lt(b_, a_) implies zup(grd, porel, b_) in grd and is-strictly-below(porel, zup(grd, porel, b_), zup(grd, porel, a_))))
- `zz-2m-neq-1` — forall([m in zz], not(2 * m = 1))
- `zz-abs-cases` — forall([a_ in zz], a_ = abs(a_) or a_ = -abs(a_))
- `zz-abs-in-nn` — forall([a_ in zz], abs(a_) in nn)
- `zz-act-add` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, j + k, a) = (opr(g))(zz-act(g, j, a), zz-act(g, k, a))))
- `zz-act-neg` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in nn, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, -k, a) = (inv(g))(mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, k))))
- `zz-act-neg-sign` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, -k, a) = (inv(g))(zz-act(g, k, a))))
- `zz-act-nonneg` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in nn, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, k, a) = mpow(abelian-group-as-monoid(g), a, k)))
- `zz-act-one` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, 1, a) = a))
- `zz-act-type` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, k, a) in carr(g)))
- `zz-act-zero` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, 0, a) = iden(g)))
- `zz-add-cancel` — forall([a in zz, b in zz, c in zz], a + c = b + c implies a = b)
- `zz-bezout` — forall([a, b], a in zz implies b in zz implies forsome([d in zz-bezout-set(a, b)], zz-divides(d, a) and zz-divides(d, b)))
- `zz-bezout-set-is-ideal` — forall([a, b], a in zz implies b in zz implies is-ideal(zz-ring, zz-bezout-set(a, b)))
- `zz-discrete` — forall([a_ in zz], 0 < a_ implies 1 <= a_)
- `zz-division` — forall([a_ in zz, b_ in zz], not(b_ = 0) implies forsome([q_ in zz, r_ in zz], a_ = q_ * b_ + r_ and (r_ = 0 or succ(abs(r_)) <= abs(b_))))
- `zz-even-iff-not-odd` — forall([x in zz], even(x) iff not(odd(x)))
- `zz-even-mul` — forall([x in zz, y in zz], even(x) implies even(x * y))
- `zz-in-rr` — forall([k_ in zz], k_ in rr)
- `zz-is-commutative-ring` — is-commutative-ring(zz-ring)
- `zz-is-euclidean-ring` — is-euclidean-ring(zz-ring)
- `zz-is-integral-domain` — is-integral-domain(zz-ring)
- `zz-is-ring` — is-ring(zz-ring)
- `zz-lt-succ-le` — forall([a_ in zz, b_ in zz], a_ < b_ implies a_ + 1 <= b_)
- `zz-mul-cancel-zero` — forall([a in zz, b in zz], a * b = 0 implies not(b = 0) implies a = 0)
- `zz-no-zero-divisors` — forall([a in zz, b in zz], a * b = 0 implies a = 0 or b = 0)
- `zz-nonneg-in-nn` — forall([a_ in zz], 0 <= a_ implies a_ in nn)
- `zz-not-even-and-odd` — forall([x in zz], not(even(x) and odd(x)))
- `zz-odd-mul-odd` — forall([x in zz, y in zz], odd(x) implies odd(y) implies odd(x * y))
- `zz-odd-sq-odd` — forall([x in zz], odd(x) implies odd(x * x))
- `zz-parity` — forall([a in zz], even(a) or odd(a))
- `zz-sub-in-zz` — forall([u_, v_], u_ in zz implies v_ in zz implies u_ - v_ in zz)
- `zz-trichotomy` — forall([a_ in zz], a_ < 0 or a_ = 0 or 0 < a_)

## Proof Support Set

Results we accept without a machine proof in VNB.  Logically treated as theorems.

- `absolute-summable-implies-summable` — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) and is-complete(nag-metric-space(grp)) implies forall([f], f in fun(dom(f), carr(grp)) and is-absolutely-summable(grp, f) implies is-summable(grp, f)))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `amgm-2-sqrt` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 <= a implies forall([b], b in rr and 0 <= b implies sqrt(a * b) <= (/)(a + b, 2)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bernoulli-rpow` — forall([x], x in rr and 0 < 1 + x implies forall([b], b in qq and 1 <= b implies 1 + b * x <= rpow(1 + x, b)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `bounded-metrizable-iff-gauge-countable` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies forsome([md], is-bounded-metric-space(md) and metric-top(md) == s) iff is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `caratheodory-measurable-sigma-algebra` — forall([omega, mu], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) implies is-sigma-algebra(omega, caratheodory-sets(omega, mu)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `caratheodory-measure` — forall([omega, mu], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) implies is-measure(omega, caratheodory-sets(omega, mu), res(mu, caratheodory-sets(omega, mu))))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `caratheodory-null-measurable` — forall([omega, mu, es], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) implies es subset omega implies mu(es) = 0 implies is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es))  _[warrant: reference]_

- `integral-additive` — forall([omega, ca, mu, f, g], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, g) implies integral(omega, ca, mu, ptwise-eplus(omega, f, g)) = eplus(integral(omega, ca, mu, f), integral(omega, ca, mu, g)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `integral-homogeneous` — forall([omega, ca, mu, f, c], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) implies c in rr implies 0 <= c implies integral(omega, ca, mu, ptwise-scale(omega, c, f)) = etimes(c, integral(omega, ca, mu, f)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `integral-in` — forall([omega, ca, mu, f], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) implies integral(omega, ca, mu, f) in rr-pos-star)  _[warrant: reference]_
- `integral-indicator` — forall([omega, ca, mu, a_], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies a_ in ca implies integral(omega, ca, mu, indicator(omega, a_)) = mu(a_))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `integral-infinite-on-null` — forall([omega, ca, mu, a_], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies a_ in ca implies mu(a_) = 0 implies integral(omega, ca, mu, ptwise-scale(omega, pos-inf, indicator(omega, a_))) = 0)  _[warrant: reference]_
- `integral-monotone` — forall([omega, ca, mu, f, g], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, g) implies ptwise-le(omega, f, g) implies integral(omega, ca, mu, f) <= integral(omega, ca, mu, g))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `integral-simple-approx` — forall([omega, ca, f], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) implies forsome([cf in fun(nn, fun(omega, rr-pos-star))], forall([n_ in nn], is-simple-fn(omega, ca, cf(n_))) and forall([n_ in nn], ptwise-le(omega, cf(n_), cf(succ(n_)))) and forall([x_ in omega], (ptwise-sup(omega, cf))(x_) = f(x_))))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `integral-sup-of-simple` — forall([omega, ca, mu, f], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) implies integral(omega, ca, mu, f) = esup({y_ in rr-pos-star: forsome([s_], is-simple-fn(omega, ca, s_) and ptwise-le(omega, s_, f) and y_ = integral(omega, ca, mu, s_))}))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `measurable-fn-eplus` — forall([omega, ca, f, g], is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, g) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, ptwise-eplus(omega, f, g)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `measurable-fn-etimes` — forall([omega, ca, f, g], is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, f) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, g) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, ptwise-etimes(omega, f, g)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `measurable-fn-liminf` — forall([omega, ca, cf], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies cf in fun(nn, fun(omega, rr-pos-star)) implies forall([n_ in nn], is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, cf(n_))) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, ptwise-liminf(omega, cf)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `measurable-fn-sup` — forall([omega, ca, cf], is-sigma-algebra(omega, ca) implies cf in fun(nn, fun(omega, rr-pos-star)) implies forall([n_ in nn], is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, cf(n_))) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, ptwise-sup(omega, cf)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `measure-continuity-from-above` — forall([omega, ca, mu, f_], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies f_ in fun(nn, ca) implies forall([n_ in nn], f_(succ(n_)) subset f_(n_)) implies mu(f_(0)) in rr implies mu(complement-in(omega, big-union(n_, nn, complement-in(omega, f_(n_))))) = einf(image(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_))), nn)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `measure-continuity-from-below` — forall([omega, ca, mu, f_], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies f_ in fun(nn, ca) implies forall([n_ in nn], f_(n_) subset f_(succ(n_))) implies mu(big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_))) = esup(image(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_))), nn)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `measure-countably-subadditive` — forall([omega, ca, mu, f_], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies f_ in fun(nn, ca) implies mu(big-union(n_, nn, f_(n_))) <= esum(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, mu(f_(n_)))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `measure-finitely-additive` — forall([omega, ca, mu, a_, b_], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies a_ in ca implies b_ in ca implies intersection(a_, b_) = empty-set implies mu(union(a_, b_)) = eplus(mu(a_), mu(b_)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `measure-monotone` — forall([omega, ca, mu, a_, b_], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies a_ in ca implies b_ in ca implies a_ subset b_ implies mu(a_) <= mu(b_))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `metrizable-iff-gauge-countable` — forall([s], is-top-space(s) implies is-metrizable-top-space(s) iff is-hausdorff(s) and is-gauge-countable(s))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `minkowski-l2` — forall([s], s in set and card(s) in nn implies forall([a in fun(s, rr), b in fun(s, rr)], sqrt(finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, (a(i) + b(i)) * (a(i) + b(i))), s)) <= sqrt(finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, a(i) * a(i)), s)) + sqrt(finsum(commutative-ring-additive-ag(rr-normed-field), vnb-lambda(i, s, b(i) * b(i)), s))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `monotone-convergence` — forall([omega, ca, mu, cf], is-measure(omega, ca, mu) implies cf in fun(nn, fun(omega, rr-pos-star)) implies forall([n_ in nn], is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, cf(n_))) implies forall([n_ in nn], ptwise-le(omega, cf(n_), cf(succ(n_)))) implies is-measurable-fn(omega, ca, ptwise-sup(omega, cf)) and integral(omega, ca, mu, ptwise-sup(omega, cf)) = esup(image(vnb-lambda(n_, nn, integral(omega, ca, mu, cf(n_))), nn)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `mul-defined-factors` — forall([a, b], a * b = a * b implies a = a and b = b)  _[warrant: informal]_
- `open-mapping-bounded-inverse` — forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt, gg], is-frechet-structure(m1, fam1) implies is-frechet-structure(m2, fam2) implies is-cont-lin(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) implies is-linear-map(m2, m1, gg) implies forall([x in vec(m1)], gg(tt(x)) = x) implies forall([y in vec(m2)], tt(gg(y)) = y) implies is-cont-lin(m2, fam2, m1, fam1, gg))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `open-mapping-theorem` — forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], is-frechet-structure(m1, fam1) implies is-frechet-structure(m2, fam2) implies is-cont-lin(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) implies forall([y in vec(m2)], forsome([x in vec(m1)], y = tt(x))) implies is-open-lin-map(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `outer-measure-binary-subadditive` — forall([omega, mu, a_, b_], is-outer-measure(omega, mu) implies a_ in power(omega) implies b_ in power(omega) implies mu(union(a_, b_)) <= eplus(mu(a_), mu(b_)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `permutation-recurrence` — forall([n in nn], card(permutations(succ(n))) = succ(n) * card(permutations(n)))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `recip-defined-nonzero` — forall([q], recip(q) = recip(q) implies not(q = 0))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `rpow-add` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies forall([b in qq, d in qq], rpow(a, b + d) = rpow(a, b) * rpow(a, d)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-mono-base` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies forall([c], c in rr and a <= c implies forall([b], b in qq and 0 <= b implies rpow(a, b) <= rpow(c, b))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-mono-exp-ge1` — forall([a], a in rr and 1 <= a implies forall([b in qq, d], d in qq and b <= d implies rpow(a, b) <= rpow(a, d)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-mono-exp-le1` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a and a <= 1 implies forall([b in qq, d], d in qq and b <= d implies rpow(a, d) <= rpow(a, b)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-mul-base` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies forall([c], c in rr and 0 < c implies forall([b in qq], rpow(a * c, b) = rpow(a, b) * rpow(c, b))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-nat` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies forall([n in nn], rpow(a, n) = a ^ n))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-neg` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies forall([b in qq], rpow(a, 0 - b) = recip(rpow(a, b))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-one` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies rpow(a, 1) = a)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-pos` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies forall([b in qq], rpow(a, b) in rr and 0 < rpow(a, b)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-pow` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies forall([b in qq, d in qq], rpow(rpow(a, b), d) = rpow(a, b * d)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-zero` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies rpow(a, 0) = 1)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-zero-base` — forall([b], b in qq and 0 < b implies rpow(0, b) = 0)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `rpow-zero-zero` — rpow(0, 0) = 1  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `sqrt-rpow` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies sqrt(a) = rpow(a, (/)(1, 2)))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `submodule-of-free-is-free` — forall([md], is-module(md) and is-euclidean-ring(scal(md)) implies forall([n in nn, u in mat(n, 1, vec(md))], generates(md, n, u) and rel-free(md, n, u) implies forall([sm], is-submodule(md, sm) implies forsome([k], (k in nn and k <= n) and forsome([w in mat(k, 1, vec(md))], spans(md, k, w, sm) and rel-free(md, k, w))))))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `summable-bound-converges` — forall([s, f, rad], is-complete(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and rad in fun(nn, rr) and series-converges(rad) and forall([k in nn], (dist(s))(f(k), f(succ(k))) <= rad(k)) implies converges(s, f))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `young-inequality` — forall([a], a in rr and 0 < a implies forall([b], b in rr and 0 < b implies forall([p], p in qq and 1 < p implies forall([q], q in qq and 1 < q implies (/)(1, p) + (/)(1, q) = 1 implies a * b <= (/)(rpow(a, p), p) + (/)(rpow(b, q), q)))))  _[warrant: well-known]_

## Stated without proof

Not proved in VNB; split by *origin*.  "Axiom" is reserved for the first group — it is NOT the umbrella for this section.

### Axioms — the fixed VNB base

The foundational axioms of VNB set theory (make-vnb-base-theory core + theorem-library/axioms).  Fixed, though not finite (separation/replacement are schemas).  Everything else rests on these.

> **The axioms below are NOT the whole trusted base.**  The set- & class-formation *schemas* (separation `{x∈A|p}`, comprehension `{x|p}`, indexed union `⋃_{z∈A}body`) carry a formula schema in their body and so live in the proof checker as primitive inference rules, not as formulas in this table.  They are stated, with their premises and conclusions, in `KERNEL-RULES.md`.  The trusted base is these axioms **plus** the kernel rules.

- `app-graph` — forall([fn_, arg_], fn_(arg_) == iota(val_, [arg_, val_] in fn_))
- `apply-tupling-1` — forall([f, a], f(a) == f([a]))
- `apply-tupling-2` — forall([f, a, b], f(a, b) == f([a, b]))
- `apply-tupling-3` — forall([f, a, b, c], f(a, b, c) == f([a, b, c]))
- `burali-forti` — not(ord in set)
- `cartesian-set-iff` — forall([a, b], cartesian(a, b) in set iff a in set and b in set)
- `cc-add-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in cc and b in cc and c in cc implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
- `cc-add-closed` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a + b in cc)
- `cc-add-comm` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a + b = b + a)
- `cc-add-zero` — forall([a in cc], a + 0 = a)
- `cc-conjugate-add` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies conjugate(a + b) = conjugate(a) + conjugate(b))
- `cc-conjugate-closed` — forall([a in cc], conjugate(a) in cc)
- `cc-conjugate-fixes-rr` — forall([a in rr], conjugate(a) = a)  _[warrant: reference]_
- `cc-conjugate-i` — conjugate(1i) = -1i  _[warrant: reference]_
- `cc-conjugate-involution` — forall([a in cc], conjugate(conjugate(a)) = a)
- `cc-conjugate-mul` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies conjugate(a * b) = conjugate(a) * conjugate(b))
- `cc-distributive` — forall([a, b, c], a in cc and b in cc and c in cc implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
- `cc-generated-by-rr` — forall([z in cc], forsome([x in rr, y in rr], z = x + y * 1i))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `cc-i-in` — 1i in cc  _[warrant: reference]_
- `cc-i-squared` — 1i * 1i = -1  _[warrant: reference]_
- `cc-is-set` — cc in set
- `cc-mul-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in cc and b in cc and c in cc implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
- `cc-mul-closed` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a * b in cc)
- `cc-mul-comm` — forall([a, b], a in cc and b in cc implies a * b = b * a)
- `cc-neg-closed` — forall([a in cc], -a in cc)
- `cc-neg-inverse` — forall([a in cc], a + -a = 0)
- `cc-one-in` — 1 in cc
- `cc-one-mul` — forall([a in cc], 1 * a = a)
- `cc-recip-closed` — forall([a], a in cc and not(a = 0) implies recip(a) in cc)
- `cc-recip-inverse` — forall([a], a in cc and not(a = 0) implies a * recip(a) = 1)
- `cc-self-conj-nonneg` — forall([a in cc], a * conjugate(a) in rr and 0 <= a * conjugate(a))
- `cc-self-conj-real` — forall([a in cc], a * conjugate(a) in rr)
- `cc-zero-in` — 0 in cc
- `choice-axiom` — forall([a], forsome([x], x in a) implies choice(a) in a)
- `class-extensionality` — forall([a, b], forall([x], x in a iff x in b) implies a = b)
- `complement-in-membership` — forall([a, b, x], x in complement-in(a, b) iff x in a and not(x in b))
- `complement-in-set-closure` — forall([a, b], a in set implies complement-in(a, b) in set)
- `dom-fun-membership` — forall([a, f in fun(a), x], x in dom(f) iff x in a)
- `dom-membership` — forall([f, x], x in dom(f) iff x in set and f(x) = f(x))
- `dom-of-fun` — forall([a, f], f in fun(a) and a in set implies dom(f) in set and dom(f) = a)
- `empty-set-has-no-members` — forall([x], not(x in empty-set))
- `empty-set-is-set` — empty-set in set
- `eq-subst-membership` — forall([a, b, s], a = b and b in s implies a in s)
- `equality-symmetry` — forall([a, b], a = b implies b = a)
- `equality-transitivity` — forall([a, b, c], a = b and b = c implies a = c)
- `extensionality` — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies a = b iff forall([x], x in a iff x in b))
- `fun-apply-type` — forall([f, a, b, x], f in fun(a, b) and x in a implies f(x) in b)
- `fun-codomain-iff` — forall([a, b, f], f in fun(a, b) iff f in fun(a) and forall([x in a], f(x) in b))
- `fun-domain-apply-def` — forall([a, f, x], f in fun(a) implies f(x) = f(x) iff x in a)
- `fun-domain-elements-are-sets` — forall([a, f], f in fun(a) and a in set implies f in set)
- `fun-domain-extensionality` — forall([a, f, g], f in fun(a) implies g in fun(a) implies forall([x in a], f(x) = g(x)) implies f = g)
- `fun-elements-are-sets` — forall([a, b, f], f in fun(a, b) and a in set implies f in set)
- `fun-no-junk` — forall([a, f in fun(a), z], z in f iff forsome([x, y], z = [x, y] and x in a and f(x) = y))
- `fun-set-iff` — forall([a, b], fun(a, b) in set iff a in set and b in set)
- `image-set` — forall([phi, s in set], image(phi, s) in set)
- `intersection-decompose` — forall([x, as], x in intersection(restvar(as)) iff splice(and, e, as, x in e))
- `intersection-membership` — forall([a, b, x], x in intersection(a, b) iff x in a and x in b)
- `intersection-set-closure` — forall([a, b], a in set or b in set implies intersection(a, b) in set)
- `is-fun-def` — forall([f], is-fun(f) iff forsome([a in set], f in fun(a)))
- `length-in-nn` — forall([a, l in tuples(a)], length(l) in nn)
- `length-of-empty` — length([]) = 0
- `limit-ord-is-sup` — forall([lambda], limit-ord(lambda) implies sup-ord(ord-segment(lambda)) = lambda)
- `list-sethood` — forall([a, l], a in set and l in tuples(a) implies l in set)
- `make-set-empty` — {} = empty-set
- `make-set-membership` — forall([x, l], x in make-set(l) iff x in set and forsome([i in nn], 1 <= i and i <= length(l) and nth(i, l) = x))
- `make-set-sethood` — forall([a, l], l in tuples(a) and a in set implies make-set(l) in set)
- `membership-implies-sethood` — forall([a, b], a in b implies a in set)
- `neg-inf-lower-bound` — forall([x in rr-star], neg-inf <= x)
- `neg-inf-not-in-rr` — not(neg-inf in rr)
- `nn-add-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in nn and b in nn and c in nn implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
- `nn-add-closed` — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a + b in nn)
- `nn-add-comm` — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a + b = b + a)
- `nn-add-zero` — forall([a in nn], a + 0 = a)
- `nn-distributive` — forall([a, b, c], a in nn and b in nn and c in nn implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
- `nn-induction` — forall([c], 0 in c and forall([n], n in nn and n in c implies succ(n) in c) implies forall([n in nn], n in c))
- `nn-is-set` — nn in set
- `nn-mul-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in nn and b in nn and c in nn implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
- `nn-mul-closed` — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a * b in nn)
- `nn-mul-comm` — forall([a, b], a in nn and b in nn implies a * b = b * a)
- `nn-one-mul` — forall([a in nn], 1 * a = a)
- `nn-subset-ord` — forall([n in nn], n in ord)
- `nn-subset-zz` — forall([n in nn], n in zz)
- `nn-succ-closed` — forall([n in nn], succ(n) in nn)
- `nn-zero-in` — 0 in nn
- `nth-in-range` — forall([a, i, l], i in nn and l in tuples(a) and 1 <= i and i <= length(l) implies nth(i, l) in a)
- `ord-le-antisymm` — forall([alpha, beta], ord-le(alpha, beta) and ord-le(beta, alpha) implies alpha = beta)
- `ord-le-closure` — forall([alpha, beta], ord-le(alpha, beta) implies alpha in ord and beta in ord)
- `ord-le-nn-compat` — forall([m, n], m in nn and n in nn implies ord-le(m, n) iff m <= n)
- `ord-le-refl` — forall([alpha in ord], ord-le(alpha, alpha))
- `ord-le-total` — forall([alpha, beta], alpha in ord and beta in ord implies ord-le(alpha, beta) or ord-le(beta, alpha))
- `ord-le-trans` — forall([alpha, beta, gamma], ord-le(alpha, beta) and ord-le(beta, gamma) implies ord-le(alpha, gamma))
- `ord-segment-is-set` — forall([alpha in ord], ord-segment(alpha) in set)
- `ord-segment-limit` — forall([lambda], limit-ord(lambda) implies forall([x], ord-lt(x, lambda) implies forsome([beta], ord-lt(x, beta) and ord-lt(beta, lambda))))
- `ord-segment-membership` — forall([alpha, x], alpha in ord implies x in ord-segment(alpha) iff ord-lt(x, alpha))
- `ord-segment-succ` — forall([alpha in ord, x], x in ord-segment(succ_ord(alpha)) iff x in ord-segment(alpha) or x = alpha)
- `ord-segment-zero` — ord-segment(0) = empty-set
- `ord-succ-above` — forall([alpha in ord], ord-lt(alpha, succ_ord(alpha)))
- `ord-succ-immediate` — forall([alpha, beta], alpha in ord and beta in ord and ord-lt(alpha, beta) implies ord-le(succ_ord(alpha), beta))
- `ord-succ-in` — forall([alpha in ord], succ_ord(alpha) in ord)
- `ord-succ-injective` — forall([alpha, beta], alpha in ord and beta in ord and succ_ord(alpha) = succ_ord(beta) implies alpha = beta)
- `ord-succ-nn` — forall([n in nn], succ_ord(n) = succ(n))
- `ord-zero-least` — forall([alpha in ord], ord-le(0, alpha))
- `pairing` — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies pair(a, b) in set)
- `pairing-membership` — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies forall([x], x in pair(a, b) iff x = a or x = b))
- `partial-fun-binary-membership` — forall([a, c, f], f in partial-fun(a, c) iff forsome([b in power(a)], f in fun(b, c)))
- `partial-fun-binary-sethood` — forall([a, c], a in set and c in set implies partial-fun(a, c) in set)
- `partial-fun-membership` — forall([a, f], f in partial-fun(a) iff forsome([b in power(a)], f in fun(b)))
- `pos-inf-above-reals` — forall([x in rr], not(pos-inf <= x))
- `pos-inf-upper-bound` — forall([x in rr-star], x <= pos-inf)
- `power-neg` — forall([x, n], x in cc and n in nn and not(x = 0) implies x ^ (-n) = recip(x ^ n))
- `power-set` — forall([a in set], power(a) in set)
- `power-set-membership` — forall([a, x], x in power(a) iff x in set and forall([z in x], z in a))
- `power-succ` — forall([x, n], x in cc and n in nn implies x ^ succ(n) = x * x ^ n)
- `power-typing-nonneg` — forall([x, n], x in cc and n in nn implies x ^ n in cc)
- `power-zero` — forall([x in cc], x ^ 0 = 1)
- `qq-add-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in qq and b in qq and c in qq implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
- `qq-add-closed` — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a + b in qq)
- `qq-add-comm` — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a + b = b + a)
- `qq-add-zero` — forall([a in qq], a + 0 = a)
- `qq-distributive` — forall([a, b, c], a in qq and b in qq and c in qq implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
- `qq-is-fraction` — forall([q in qq], forsome([a in zz, b in zz], not(b = 0) and q = a * recip(b)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `qq-is-set` — qq in set
- `qq-mul-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in qq and b in qq and c in qq implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
- `qq-mul-closed` — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a * b in qq)
- `qq-mul-comm` — forall([a, b], a in qq and b in qq implies a * b = b * a)
- `qq-neg-closed` — forall([a in qq], -a in qq)
- `qq-neg-inverse` — forall([a in qq], a + -a = 0)
- `qq-one-in` — 1 in qq
- `qq-one-mul` — forall([a in qq], 1 * a = a)
- `qq-recip-closed` — forall([a], a in qq and not(a = 0) implies recip(a) in qq)
- `qq-recip-inverse` — forall([a], a in qq and not(a = 0) implies a * recip(a) = 1)
- `qq-subset-rr` — forall([n in qq], n in rr)
- `qq-zero-in` — 0 in qq
- `quasi-eq-def` — forall([a, b], a = a implies a == b iff a = b)
- `quasi-eq-reflexivity` — forall([a], a == a)
- `quasi-eq-subst-membership` — forall([a, b, s], a == b and b in s implies a in s)  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `quasi-eq-symmetry` — forall([a, b], a == b implies b == a)
- `quasi-eq-transitivity` — forall([a, b, c], a == b and b == c implies a == c)
- `res-apply` — forall([a, b, f, x], f in fun(a) and b subset a and x in b implies (res(f, b))(x) = f(x))
- `res-codomain` — forall([a, b, c, f], f in fun(a, c) and b subset a implies res(f, b) in fun(b, c))
- `res-typing` — forall([a, b, f], f in fun(a) and b subset a implies res(f, b) in fun(b))
- `rr-add-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in rr and b in rr and c in rr implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
- `rr-add-closed` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a + b in rr)
- `rr-add-comm` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a + b = b + a)
- `rr-add-zero` — forall([a in rr], a + 0 = a)
- `rr-distributive` — forall([a, b, c], a in rr and b in rr and c in rr implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
- `rr-is-set` — rr in set
- `rr-leq-add-compat` — forall([a, b, c], a in rr and b in rr and c in rr implies a <= b implies a + c <= b + c)
- `rr-leq-antisymmetric` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a <= b and b <= a implies a = b)
- `rr-leq-mul-nonneg` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies 0 <= a and 0 <= b implies 0 <= a * b)
- `rr-leq-reflexive` — forall([a in rr], a <= a)
- `rr-leq-total` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a <= b or b <= a)
- `rr-leq-transitive` — forall([a, b, c], a in rr and b in rr and c in rr implies a <= b and b <= c implies a <= c)
- `rr-mul-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in rr and b in rr and c in rr implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
- `rr-mul-closed` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a * b in rr)
- `rr-mul-comm` — forall([a, b], a in rr and b in rr implies a * b = b * a)
- `rr-neg-closed` — forall([a in rr], -a in rr)
- `rr-neg-inverse` — forall([a in rr], a + -a = 0)
- `rr-one-in` — 1 in rr
- `rr-one-mul` — forall([a in rr], 1 * a = a)
- `rr-recip-closed` — forall([a], a in rr and not(a = 0) implies recip(a) in rr)
- `rr-recip-inverse` — forall([a], a in rr and not(a = 0) implies a * recip(a) = 1)
- `rr-subset-cc` — forall([n in rr], n in cc)
- `rr-sup-in` — forall([s], s subset rr implies forsome([x], x in s) implies rr-bounded-above(s) implies sup(s) in rr)  _[warrant: reference]_
- `rr-sup-least` — forall([s], s subset rr implies forsome([x], x in s) implies rr-bounded-above(s) implies forall([b], rr-upper-bound(s, b) implies sup(s) <= b))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `rr-sup-upper` — forall([s], s subset rr implies forsome([x], x in s) implies rr-bounded-above(s) implies rr-upper-bound(s, sup(s)))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `rr-zero-in` — 0 in rr
- `subset-def` — forall([a, b], a subset b iff forall([x in a], x in b))
- `subset-set` — forall([a], a subset set)
- `sup-ord-empty` — sup-ord(empty-set) = 0
- `sup-ord-in` — forall([a], a in set and forall([x in a], x in ord) implies sup-ord(a) in ord)
- `sup-ord-least` — forall([a], a in set and forall([x in a], x in ord) implies forall([beta], beta in ord and forall([alpha in a], ord-le(alpha, beta)) implies ord-le(sup-ord(a), beta)))
- `sup-ord-succ-segment` — forall([alpha in ord], sup-ord(ord-segment(succ_ord(alpha))) = alpha)
- `sup-ord-upper` — forall([a], a in set and forall([x in a], x in ord) implies forall([alpha in a], ord-le(alpha, sup-ord(a))))
- `transfinite-induction` — forall([c], forall([alpha], alpha in ord and forall([beta], ord-lt(beta, alpha) implies beta in c) implies alpha in c) implies forall([alpha in ord], alpha in c))
- `tuple-cons-decompose` — forall([a, n, l], n in nn and l in tuples(a) and length(l) = succ(n) implies forsome([x in a, m in tuples(a)], length(m) = n and l = cons(x, m)))
- `tuple-length-zero` — forall([a, l], l in tuples(a) and length(l) = 0 implies l = [])
- `tuples-sethood` — forall([a in set], tuples(a) in set)
- `union-decompose` — forall([x, as], x in union(restvar(as)) iff splice(or, e, as, x in e))
- `union-membership` — forall([a, b, x], x in union(a, b) iff x in a or x in b)
- `union-set-closure` — forall([a, b], a in set and b in set implies union(a, b) in set)
- `zz-add-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in zz and b in zz and c in zz implies (a + b) + c = a + (b + c))
- `zz-add-closed` — forall([a, b], a in zz and b in zz implies a + b in zz)
- `zz-add-comm` — forall([a, b], a in zz and b in zz implies a + b = b + a)
- `zz-add-zero` — forall([a in zz], a + 0 = a)
- `zz-distributive` — forall([a, b, c], a in zz and b in zz and c in zz implies a * (b + c) = a * b + a * c)
- `zz-generated-by-nn` — forall([a in zz], forsome([n in nn], a = n or a = -n))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `zz-is-set` — zz in set
- `zz-mul-assoc` — forall([a, b, c], a in zz and b in zz and c in zz implies (a * b) * c = a * (b * c))
- `zz-mul-closed` — forall([a, b], a in zz and b in zz implies a * b in zz)
- `zz-mul-comm` — forall([a, b], a in zz and b in zz implies a * b = b * a)
- `zz-neg-closed` — forall([a in zz], -a in zz)
- `zz-neg-inverse` — forall([a in zz], a + -a = 0)
- `zz-one-in` — 1 in zz
- `zz-one-mul` — forall([a in zz], 1 * a = a)
- `zz-subset-qq` — forall([n in zz], n in qq)
- `zz-zero-in` — 0 in zz

### Definitions — conservative extensions

Emitted by def-/declare- forms (IS-X folding, accessor laws, view typing).  Name new vocabulary; add no strength.

- `<` — forall([x, y], x < y iff x <= y and not(x = y))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid` — forall([r], is-abelian-group(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(r)))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-commutative-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(commutative-ring-additive-ag(r))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-commutative-ring-additive-ag-normed-field-as-commutative-ring` — forall([r], is-normed-field(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(commutative-ring-additive-ag(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(r)))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-field-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(field-additive-ag(r))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-module-vector-ag` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(module-vector-ag(r))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-module-vector-ag-complex-inner-product-space-as-module` — forall([r], is-complex-inner-product-space(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(module-vector-ag(complex-inner-product-space-as-module(r)))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-module-vector-ag-normed-vector-space-as-module` — forall([r], is-normed-vector-space(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(module-vector-ag(normed-vector-space-as-module(r)))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-normed-ag-as-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-normed-ag(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(r))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-normed-ag-as-abelian-group-normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag` — forall([r], is-normed-vector-space(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(normed-ag-as-abelian-group(normed-vector-space-as-normed-ag(r)))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-normed-field-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-normed-field(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(normed-field-additive-ag(r))))
- `abelian-group-as-monoid-is-monoid-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies is-monoid(abelian-group-as-monoid(ring-additive-ag(r))))
- `abelian-group-assoc-module-vector-ag` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r), c in vec(r)], (vadd(r))((vadd(r))(a, b), c) = (vadd(r))(a, (vadd(r))(b, c))))
- `abelian-group-class` — forall([s], s in abelian-group iff is-abelian-group(s))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-module-vector-ag` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, a) = a implies a = vzero(r)))
- `abelian-group-idempotent-is-id-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, a) = a implies a = zero(r)))
- `abelian-group-inverse-unique-module-vector-ag` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r), b in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, b) = vzero(r) implies b = (vneg(r))(a)))
- `abelian-group-is-group-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies is-group(ring-additive-ag(r)))
- `abelian-group-opr-comm-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, b) = (add(r))(b, a)))
- `abelian-group-right-id-module-vector-ag` — forall([r], is-module(r) implies forall([a in vec(r)], (vadd(r))(a, vzero(r)) = a))
- `ag-cancel-right-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), b in carr(r), c in carr(r)], (add(r))(a, c) = (add(r))(b, c) implies a = b))
- `bijection-membership-iff` — forall([x, y, phi], phi in bijection(x, y) iff phi in fun(x, y) and forall([a in x, b in x], phi(a) = phi(b) implies a = b) and forall([w in y], forsome([z in x], phi(z) = w)))
- `binary-divide-def` — forall([a, b], (/)(a, b) == a * recip(b))
- `binary-minus-def` — forall([a, b], a - b == a + -b)
- `binneg-apply` — forall([x], binneg(x) == -x)
- `binplus-apply` — forall([x, y], binplus(x, y) == x + y)
- `bintimes-apply` — forall([x, y], bintimes(x, y) == x * y)
- `c-metric-space-class` — forall([s], s in c-metric-space iff is-c-metric-space(s))
- `caratheodory-sets-membership` — forall([omega, mu, es_], es_ in caratheodory-sets(omega, mu) iff es_ in power(omega) and is-caratheodory-measurable(omega, mu, es_))
- `cc-coords-def` — cc-coords == vnb-lambda(zv_, cc, [real-part(zv_), imag-part(zv_)])
- `cc-ms-def` — cc-ms = [cc, vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(cc, cc), magnitude(x - y))]
- `cc-ms@dist` — dist(cc-ms) == vnb-lambda([x, y], cartesian(cc, cc), magnitude(x - y))
- `cc-ms@pts` — pts(cc-ms) == cc
- `cc-normed-field-def` — cc-normed-field = [cc, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, cc, -x_), 0, 1, vnb-lambda(x_, cc, magnitude(x_))]
- `cc-normed-field@add` — add(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ + y_)
- `cc-normed-field@carr` — carr(cc-normed-field) == cc
- `cc-normed-field@fnrm` — fnrm(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, cc, magnitude(x_))
- `cc-normed-field@mul` — mul(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(cc, cc), x_ * y_)
- `cc-normed-field@neg` — neg(cc-normed-field) == vnb-lambda(x_, cc, -x_)
- `cc-normed-field@one` — one(cc-normed-field) == 1
- `cc-normed-field@zero` — zero(cc-normed-field) == 0
- `cc-of-pair-def` — cc-of-pair == vnb-lambda(pv_, cartesian(rr, rr), nth(1, pv_) + nth(2, pv_) * 1i)
- `cc-series-converges` — forall([f], cc-series-converges(f) iff converges(cc-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, cc-series-partial-sum(f, k))))
- `cc-series-converges-to` — forall([f, l], cc-series-converges-to(f, l) iff converges-to(cc-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, cc-series-partial-sum(f, k)), l))
- `cips-act-type` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v)], (act(v))(a_, x_) in vec(v)))
- `cips-ip-add-left` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v), z_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))((vadd(v))(x_, y_), z_) = (ip(v))(x_, z_) + (ip(v))(y_, z_)))
- `cips-ip-conj-sym` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(y_, x_) = conjugate((ip(v))(x_, y_))))
- `cips-ip-definite` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, x_) = 0 iff x_ = vzero(v)))
- `cips-ip-homog-left` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([a_ in cc, x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))((act(v))(a_, x_), y_) = a_ * (ip(v))(x_, y_)))
- `cips-ip-self-nonneg` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], 0 <= (ip(v))(x_, x_)))
- `cips-ip-self-real` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, x_) in rr))
- `cips-ip-type` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, y_) in cc))
- `cips-ip-zero-vector` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (ip(v))(x_, x_) = 0 implies x_ = vzero(v)))
- `cips-scal-carrier` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies carr(scal(v)) = cc)
- `cips-vadd-type` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v), y_ in vec(v)], (vadd(v))(x_, y_) in vec(v)))
- `cips-vadd-vzero` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies forall([x_ in vec(v)], (vadd(v))(vzero(v), x_) = x_))
- `cips-vzero-in` — forall([v], is-complex-inner-product-space(v) implies vzero(v) in vec(v))
- `cluster-point` — forall([s, f, x], cluster-point(s, f, x) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and x in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forall([m in nn], forsome([n in nn], m <= n and (dist(s))(f(n), x) < eps))))
- `comb-kk-succ` — forall([r, x, y, m in nn], comb-kk(r, x, y, succ(m)) == vnb-lambda(k, zz, (add(r))((mul(r))(x, (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k - 1)), (mul(r))(y, (comb-kk(r, x, y, m))(k)))))
- `comb-kk-zero` — forall([r, x, y], comb-kk(r, x, y, 0) == vnb-lambda(k, zz, if(k = 0, one(r), zero(r))))
- `comm-monoid-class` — forall([s], s in comm-monoid iff is-comm-monoid(s))
- `commutative-ring-additive-ag-is-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies is-abelian-group(commutative-ring-additive-ag(r)))
- `commutative-ring-additive-ag-is-abelian-group-normed-field-as-commutative-ring` — forall([r], is-normed-field(r) implies is-abelian-group(commutative-ring-additive-ag(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(r))))
- `commutative-ring-class` — forall([s], s in commutative-ring iff is-commutative-ring(s))
- `commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm-is-comm-monoid` — forall([r], is-commutative-ring(r) implies is-comm-monoid(commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(r)))
- `commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm-is-comm-monoid-normed-field-as-commutative-ring` — forall([r], is-normed-field(r) implies is-comm-monoid(commutative-ring-multiplicative-cm(normed-field-as-commutative-ring(r))))
- `complete-cauchy-converges` — forall([s], is-complete(s) implies forall([f], is-cauchy-seq(s, f) implies converges(s, f)))
- `complex-inner-product-space-as-module-is-module` — forall([r], is-complex-inner-product-space(r) implies is-module(complex-inner-product-space-as-module(r)))
- `complex-inner-product-space-class` — forall([s], s in complex-inner-product-space iff is-complex-inner-product-space(s))
- `cons-in-tuples` — forall([a, x, l], x in a and l in tuples(a) implies cons(x, l) in tuples(a))
- `converges` — forall([s, f], converges(s, f) iff forsome([l], converges-to(s, f, l)))
- `converges-along` — forall([s, g, b, p], converges-along(s, g, b, p) iff is-metric-space(s) and g in fun(nn, pts(s)) and p in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([i in nn], i in b implies n <= i implies (dist(s))(g(i), p) <= eps))))
- `converges-on` — forall([s, fam, dseq], converges-on(s, fam, dseq) iff is-metric-space(s) and fam in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and dseq in fun(nn, pts(s)) and forall([m in nn], converges(rr-ms, vnb-lambda(k, nn, (fam(k))(dseq(m))))))
- `converges-to` — forall([s, f, l], converges-to(s, f, l) iff is-metric-space(s) and f in fun(nn, pts(s)) and l in pts(s) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([n in nn], forall([n_ in nn], n <= n_ implies (dist(s))(f(n_), l) <= eps))))
- `converges-uniformly` — forall([s, seq, g], converges-uniformly(s, seq, g) iff is-metric-space(s) and seq in fun(nn, fun(pts(s), rr)) and g in fun(pts(s), rr) and forall([eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([k], k in nn and cap <= k implies forall([x in pts(s)], abs((seq(k))(x) - g(x)) < eps)))))
- `cseq-equiv` — forall([m, f, g], cseq-equiv(m, f, g) iff converges-to(rr-ms, dist-seq(m, f, g), 0))
- `dc-iter-succ` — forall([s, a, nxt, n in nn], dc-iter(s, a, nxt, succ(n)) == choice({y_ in s: y_ in nxt(n, dc-iter(s, a, nxt, n))}))
- `dc-iter-zero` — forall([s, a, nxt], dc-iter(s, a, nxt, 0) == a)
- `deg-bound` — forall([a, p, n], deg-bound(a, p, n) iff forall([k_ in nn], n < k_ implies p(k_) = zero(a)))
- `det-cofactor` — forall([r, n, a], is-ring(r) implies n in nn implies a in mat(succ(n), succ(n), carr(r)) implies det(r, succ(n), a) == finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), vnb-lambda(j, interval(1, succ(n)), (mul(r))(mpow(ring-multiplicative-monoid(r), (neg(r))(one(r)), succ(j)), (mul(r))(entry(a, 1, j), det(r, n, minor(a, 1, j, n))))), interval(1, succ(n))))
- `det-zero` — forall([r, a], det(r, 0, a) == one(r))
- `dual-carrier` — forall([m], vec(dual(m)) == dual-vec(m))
- `dual-norm-value` — forall([m, f in dual-vec(m)], (vnrm(dual(m)))(f) == dual-norm(m, f))
- `dual-scal` — forall([m], scal(dual(m)) == rr-normed-field)
- `dual-vec-membership` — forall([m, f], f in dual-vec(m) iff f in fun(vec(m), rr) and is-bounded-linear-functional(m, f))
- `econverges-to` — forall([f, l], econverges-to(f, l) iff f in fun(nn, rr-pos-star) and l in rr-pos-star and eliminf(f) = l and elimsup(f) = l)
- `ell-one-def` — ell-one = {x_ in sqn(cc): series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(x_(k_))))}
- `ell-two-def` — ell-two = {x_ in sqn(cc): series-converges(vnb-lambda(k_, nn, magnitude(x_(k_)) * magnitude(x_(k_))))}
- `empty-in-tuples` — forall([a], [] in tuples(a))
- `eplus-def` — eplus == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr-pos-star, rr-pos-star), if(x_ = pos-inf or y_ = pos-inf, pos-inf, binplus(x_, y_)))
- `etimes-def` — etimes == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(rr-pos-star, rr-pos-star), if(x_ = pos-inf or y_ = pos-inf, if(x_ = 0 or y_ = 0, 0, pos-inf), bintimes(x_, y_)))
- `euclidean-ring-class` — forall([s], s in euclidean-ring iff is-euclidean-ring(s))
- `even` — forall([x], even(x) iff forsome([k in zz], x = 2 * k))
- `even-def` — forall([x], even(x) iff forsome([k in zz], x = 2 * k))
- `extends-on` — forall([s, g, f], extends-on(s, g, f) iff forall([x_ in s], g(x_) = f(x_)))
- `factorial-succ` — forall([k in nn], factorial(succ(k)) = succ(k) * factorial(k))
- `factorial-zero` — factorial(0) = succ(0)
- `falling-succ` — forall([n, k in nn], falling(n, succ(k)) == (n - k) * falling(n, k))
- `falling-zero` — forall([n], falling(n, 0) == succ(0))
- `fam-of-list-apply` — forall([l, i], i in nn and succ(i) <= length(l) implies (fam-of-list(l))(i) = nth(succ(i), l))
- `field-additive-ag-is-abelian-group` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-abelian-group(field-additive-ag(r)))
- `field-as-euclidean-ring-is-euclidean-ring` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-euclidean-ring(field-as-euclidean-ring(r)))
- `field-as-integral-domain-is-integral-domain` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-integral-domain(field-as-integral-domain(r)))
- `field-class` — forall([s], s in field iff is-field(s))
- `field-multiplicative-group-is-group` — forall([r], is-field(r) implies is-group(field-multiplicative-group(r)))
- `field-non-zero-carrier` — forall([s], is-field(s) implies non-zero(s) = difference(carr(s), singleton(zero(s))))
- `field-ring-class` — forall([s], s in field-ring iff is-field-ring(s))
- `finsum-all-id-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(r))], forall([z in s], f(z) = zero(r)) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s) = zero(r))))
- `finsum-single-support-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(r)), i0 in s], forall([j in s], not(j = i0) implies f(j) = zero(r)) implies finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s) = f(i0))))
- `finsum-type-ring-additive-ag` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([s in set], card(s) in nn implies forall([f in fun(s, carr(r))], finsum(ring-additive-ag(r), f, s) in carr(r))))
- `fr-cauchy` — forall([m, fam, seq], fr-cauchy(m, fam, seq) iff forall([k in nn, eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([i], i in nn and cap <= i implies forall([j], j in nn and cap <= j implies (fam(k))((vadd(m))(seq(i), (vneg(m))(seq(j)))) < eps)))))
- `fr-conv` — forall([m, fam, seq, lim], fr-conv(m, fam, seq, lim) iff forall([k in nn, eps], pos-rr(eps) implies forsome([cap in nn], forall([i], i in nn and cap <= i implies (fam(k))((vadd(m))(seq(i), (vneg(m))(lim))) < eps))))
- `generates` — forall([md, n, u], generates(md, n, u) iff forall([x_ in vec(md)], forsome([c_ in mat(1, n, carr(scal(md)))], x_ = lincomb(md, n, c_, u))))
- `good-sub` — forall([m, s, f, t], good-sub(m, s, f, t) iff forsome([g_], npe(m, s, f, t, g_)))
- `group-class` — forall([s], s in group iff is-group(s))
- `has-closed-graph` — forall([m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt], has-closed-graph(m1, fam1, m2, fam2, tt) iff is-linear-map(m1, m2, tt) and forall([seq in fun(nn, vec(m1)), p1 in vec(m1), p2 in vec(m2)], fr-conv(m1, fam1, seq, p1) and fr-conv(m2, fam2, vnb-lambda(i, nn, tt(seq(i))), p2) implies p2 = tt(p1)))
- `has-deriv-at` — forall([f, a, l], has-deriv-at(f, a, l) iff forsome([ice_], pos-rr(ice_) and is-diff-on(rr-normed-field, ooint(a - ice_, a + ice_), restrict(f, ooint(a - ice_, a + ice_)), a, l)))
- `has-deriv-at-within` — forall([f, w, x, l], has-deriv-at-within(f, w, x, l) iff restrict(f, w) in fun(w, rr) and x in w and l in rr and forsome([osp_ in fun(w, rr)], is-continuous-at(subspace-ms(rr-ms, w), rr-ms, osp_, x) and osp_(x) = l and forall([ost_ in w], f(ost_) - f(x) = osp_(ost_) * (ost_ - x))))
- `has-div-remainder` — forall([s, dg], has-div-remainder(s, dg) iff forall([a_ in carr(s), b in carr(s)], not(b = zero(s)) implies forsome([q in carr(s), r in carr(s)], a_ = (add(s))((mul(s))(q, b), r) and (r = zero(s) or succ(dg(r)) <= dg(b)))))
