- `zz-is-gcd` — forall([a, b, d], zz-is-gcd(a, b, d) iff zz-divides(d, a) and zz-divides(d, b) and forall([e_ in zz], zz-divides(e_, a) implies zz-divides(e_, b) implies zz-divides(e_, d)))
- `zz-ring-def` — zz-ring = [zz, vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ + y_), vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ * y_), vnb-lambda(x_, zz, -x_), 0, 1]
- `zz-ring@add` — add(zz-ring) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ + y_)
- `zz-ring@carr` — carr(zz-ring) == zz
- `zz-ring@mul` — mul(zz-ring) == vnb-lambda([x_, y_], cartesian(zz, zz), x_ * y_)
- `zz-ring@neg` — neg(zz-ring) == vnb-lambda(x_, zz, -x_)
- `zz-ring@one` — one(zz-ring) == 1
- `zz-ring@zero` — zero(zz-ring) == 0

### Assertions — accepted without proof

Genuine mathematical content with no machine proof — the warrant candidates.

- `bijection-set-iff` — forall([x, y], x in set and y in set implies bijection(x, y) in set)  _[warrant: informal]_
- `delete-at-above-k` — forall([h, k, i], i in nn and k in nn and not(i in ord-segment(k)) implies (delete-at(h, k))(i) = h(succ(i)))
- `delete-at-below-k` — forall([h, k, i in ord-segment(k)], (delete-at(h, k))(i) == h(i))
- `delete-at-in-fun` — forall([b, h, k], h in fun(nn, b) implies delete-at(h, k) in fun(nn, b))
- `delete-at-is-bijection` — forall([n, k, h], n in nn and k in ord-segment(succ(n)) and h in bijection(ord-segment(succ(n)), ord-segment(succ(n))) and h(k) = n implies delete-at(h, k) in bijection(ord-segment(n), ord-segment(n)))
- `injection-in-fun` — forall([x, y, phi in injection(x, y)], phi in fun(x, y))
- `injection-injective` — forall([x, y, phi in injection(x, y), a in x, b in x], phi(a) = phi(b) implies a = b)
- `injection-set-iff` — forall([x, y], x in set and y in set implies injection(x, y) in set)
- `mpow-mult` — forall([m], is-comm-monoid(m) implies forall([x in carr(m), y in carr(m), n in nn], mpow(m, (opr(m))(x, y), n) = (opr(m))(mpow(m, x, n), mpow(m, y, n))))  _[warrant: well-known]_
- `nary-plus-2-list` — forall([x, y], x + y == reduce(binplus, fam-of-list([x, y]), 2))
- `nary-plus-3` — forall([x, y, z], x + y + z == binplus(binplus(x, y), z))
- `nary-plus-3-list` — forall([x, y, z], x + y + z == reduce(binplus, fam-of-list([x, y, z]), 3))
- `nary-plus-4` — forall([w, x, y, z], w + x + y + z == binplus(binplus(binplus(w, x), y), z))
- `nary-plus-4-list` — forall([w, x, y, z], w + x + y + z == reduce(binplus, fam-of-list([w, x, y, z]), 4))
- `nary-plus-5` — forall([v, w, x, y, z], v + w + x + y + z == binplus(binplus(binplus(binplus(v, w), x), y), z))
- `nary-plus-5-list` — forall([v, w, x, y, z], v + w + x + y + z == reduce(binplus, fam-of-list([v, w, x, y, z]), 5))
- `nary-times-2-list` — forall([x, y], x * y == reduce(bintimes, fam-of-list([x, y]), 2))
- `nary-times-3` — forall([x, y, z], x * y * z == bintimes(bintimes(x, y), z))
- `nary-times-3-list` — forall([x, y, z], x * y * z == reduce(bintimes, fam-of-list([x, y, z]), 3))
- `nary-times-4` — forall([w, x, y, z], w * x * y * z == bintimes(bintimes(bintimes(w, x), y), z))
- `nary-times-4-list` — forall([w, x, y, z], w * x * y * z == reduce(bintimes, fam-of-list([w, x, y, z]), 4))
- `nary-times-5` — forall([v, w, x, y, z], v * w * x * y * z == bintimes(bintimes(bintimes(bintimes(v, w), x), y), z))
- `nary-times-5-list` — forall([v, w, x, y, z], v * w * x * y * z == reduce(bintimes, fam-of-list([v, w, x, y, z]), 5))
- `normed-ag-nrm-definite` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], (nrm(s))(a) = 0 iff a = iden(s)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-nonneg` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s)], 0 <= (nrm(s))(a)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-subadditive` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s)], (nrm(s))((opr(s))(a, b)) <= (nrm(s))(a) + (nrm(s))(b)))
- `normed-ag-nrm-type` — forall([s], is-normed-ag(s) implies nrm(s) in fun(carr(s), rr))
- `normed-field-mul-inverses` — forall([s], is-normed-field(s) implies forall([a], a in carr(s) and not(a = zero(s)) implies forsome([b in carr(s)], not(b = zero(s)) and (mul(s))(a, b) = one(s))))
- `normed-field-zero-not-one` — forall([s], is-normed-field(s) implies not(zero(s) = one(s)))
- `prod-ord-singleton` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(m))], prod-ord(m, f, 1) = f(0)))
- `prod-ord-type` — forall([m], is-monoid(m) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(m)), n in nn], prod-ord(m, f, n) in carr(m)))
- `qq-dense-in-rr` — forall([eps, x], pos-rr(eps) implies x in rr implies forsome([a in qq], a - eps < x and x < a + eps))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `qq-is-commutative-ring` — is-commutative-ring(qq-ring)
- `qq-is-integral-domain` — is-integral-domain(qq-ring)
- `ring-prod-is-ring` — forall([x], is-ring(x) implies forall([y], is-ring(y) implies is-ring(ring-prod(x, y))))
- `ring-prod-n-is-ring` — forall([f, n in nn], forall([i in nn], is-ring(f(i))) implies is-ring(ring-prod-n(f, n)))
- `semigroup-assoc` — forall([s], is-semigroup(s) implies forall([a in carr(s), b in carr(s), c in carr(s)], (opr(s))((opr(s))(a, b), c) = (opr(s))(a, (opr(s))(b, c))))
- `sum-ag-as-reduce` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(ag)), n], n in nn and 1 <= n implies sum-ag(ag, f, n) = reduce(opr(ag), f, n)))
- `sum-ag-singleton` — forall([ag], is-abelian-group(ag) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(ag))], sum-ag(ag, f, 1) = f(0)))
- `sum-left-scalar` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([a in carr(r), f in fun(nn, carr(r)), n in nn], sum(r, vnb-lambda(i, nn, (mul(r))(a, f(i))), n) = (mul(r))(a, sum(r, f, n))))
- `sum-singleton` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(r))], sum(r, f, 1) = f(0)))
- `sum-type` — forall([r], is-ring(r) implies forall([f in fun(nn, carr(r)), n in nn], sum(r, f, n) in carr(r)))
- `sums-to-unique` — forall([grp], is-normed-ag(grp) implies forall([f in fun(dom(f), carr(grp)), r1 in carr(grp), r2 in carr(grp)], sums-to(grp, f, r1) and sums-to(grp, f, r2) implies r1 = r2))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `totally-bounded-has-cauchy-subseq-rad` — forall([s], totally-bounded(s) implies forall([f in fun(nn, pts(s)), rad], null-rr-seq(rad) implies forsome([phi], strictly-mono-nn(phi) and is-cauchy-seq(s, subseq(f, phi)))))  _[warrant: reference]_
- `zero-ring-is-ring` — is-ring(zero-ring)
- `zz-act-assoc` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([j in zz, k in zz, a in carr(g)], zz-act(g, j * k, a) = zz-act(g, j, zz-act(g, k, a))))  _[warrant: informal]_
- `zz-act-distrib` — forall([g], is-abelian-group(g) implies forall([k in zz, a in carr(g), b in carr(g)], zz-act(g, k, (opr(g))(a, b)) = (opr(g))(zz-act(g, k, a), zz-act(g, k, b))))  _[warrant: informal]_
